




|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

dīghanikāyau


pāthikavaggapāḷi


1. pāthikasuttaṃ

sunakkhattavatthu

1. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā mal‍laisu viharati anupiyaṃ nāma anuppiyaṃ nāma (syā॰) mal‍lānaṃ nigamau| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya anupiyaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| atha khau bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘atippagau khau tāva anupiyāyaṃ anupiyaṃ (ka॰) piṇḍāya carituṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ yaina bhaggavagauttassa paribbājakassa ārāmau, yaina bhaggavagauttau paribbājakau tainupasaṅkamaiyya’’nti|

2. atha  khau bhagavā yaina bhaggavagauttassa paribbājakassa ārāmau, yaina bhaggavagauttau paribbājakau tainupasaṅkami| atha khau bhaggavagauttau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aitu khau, bhantai, bhagavā| svāgataṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau| cirassaṃ khau, bhantai, bhagavā imaṃ pariyāyamakāsi yadidaṃ idhāgamanāya| nisīdatu, bhantai, bhagavā, idamāsanaṃ pañ‍ñatta’’nti| nisīdi bhagavā pañ‍ñattai āsanai| bhaggavagauttaupi khau paribbājakau añ‍ñataraṃ  nīcaṃ āsanaṃ gahaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau bhaggavagauttau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘purimāni, bhantai, divasāni  purimatarāni sunakkhattau licchaviputtau yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā maṃ aitadavauca – ‘pac‍cakkhātau dāni mayā, bhaggava, bhagavā| na dānāhaṃ bhagavantaṃ uddissa viharāmī’ti| kac‍caitaṃ, bhantai, tathaiva, yathā sunakkhattau licchaviputtau avacā’’ti? ‘‘tathaiva khau aitaṃ, bhaggava, yathā sunakkhattau licchaviputtau avaca’’|

3. purimāni, bhaggava, divasāni purimatarāni sunakkhattau licchaviputtau yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā maṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau, bhaggava, sunakkhattau licchaviputtau maṃ aitadavauca – ‘pac‍cakkhāmi dānāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ| na dānāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ uddissa viharissāmī’ti| ‘aivaṃ vuttai, ahaṃ, bhaggava, sunakkhattaṃ licchaviputtaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘api nu tāhaṃ, sunakkhatta, aivaṃ avacaṃ, aihi tvaṃ, sunakkhatta, mamaṃ uddissa viharāhī’ti? ‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’| ‘tvaṃ  vā pana maṃ aivaṃ avaca – ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ uddissa viharissāmī’ti? ‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’| ‘iti kira, sunakkhatta, naivāhaṃ taṃ vadāmi – aihi tvaṃ, sunakkhatta, mamaṃ uddissa viharāhīti| napi kira maṃ tvaṃ vadaisi – ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ uddissa viharissāmīti| aivaṃ santai, maughapurisa, kau santau kaṃ pac‍cācikkhasi? passa, maughapurisa, yāvañ‍ca  yāva ca (ka॰) tai idaṃ aparaddha’nti|

4. ‘na hi pana mai, bhantai, bhagavā uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ karautī’ti| ‘api nu tāhaṃ, sunakkhatta, aivaṃ avacaṃ – aihi tvaṃ, sunakkhatta, mamaṃ uddissa viharāhi, ahaṃ tai uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ karissāmī’ti? ‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’| ‘tvaṃ vā pana maṃ aivaṃ avaca – ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ uddissa viharissāmi, bhagavā mai uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ karissatī’ti? ‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’| ‘iti kira, sunakkhatta, naivāhaṃ taṃ vadāmi – aihi tvaṃ, sunakkhatta, mamaṃ uddissa viharāhi, ahaṃ tai uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ karissāmī’ti; napi kira maṃ tvaṃ vadaisi – ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ uddissa viharissāmi, bhagavā mai uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ karissatī’ti| aivaṃ santai, maughapurisa, kau santau kaṃ pac‍cācikkhasi? taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, sunakkhatta, katai vā uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyai akatai vā uttarimanussadhammā  iddhipāṭihāriyai yassatthāya mayā dhammau daisitau sau niyyāti tak‍karassa sammā dukkhakkhayāyā’ti? ‘katai  vā, bhantai, uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyai akatai vā uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyai yassatthāya bhagavatā dhammau daisitau sau niyyāti tak‍karassa sammā dukkhakkhayāyā’ti| ‘iti kira, sunakkhatta, katai vā uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyai, akatai vā uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyai, yassatthāya mayā dhammau daisitau, sau niyyāti tak‍karassa  sammā dukkhakkhayāya| tatra, sunakkhatta, kiṃ uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ kataṃ karissati? passa, maughapurisa, yāvañ‍ca tai idaṃ aparaddha’nti|

5. ‘na hi pana mai, bhantai, bhagavā aggañ‍ñaṃ pañ‍ñapaitī’ti pañ‍ñāpaitīti (pī॰)? ‘api nu tāhaṃ, sunakkhatta, aivaṃ avacaṃ – aihi tvaṃ, sunakkhatta, mamaṃ uddissa viharāhi, ahaṃ tai aggañ‍ñaṃ pañ‍ñapaissāmī’ti? ‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’| ‘tvaṃ vā pana maṃ aivaṃ avaca – ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ uddissa viharissāmi, bhagavā mai aggañ‍ñaṃ pañ‍ñapaissatī’ti? ‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’| ‘iti kira, sunakkhatta, naivāhaṃ taṃ vadāmi – aihi tvaṃ, sunakkhatta, mamaṃ uddissa viharāhi, ahaṃ tai aggañ‍ñaṃ pañ‍ñapaissāmīti| napi kira maṃ tvaṃ vadaisi – ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ uddissa viharissāmi, bhagavā mai aggañ‍ñaṃ pañ‍ñapaissatī’ti| aivaṃ santai, maughapurisa, kau santau kaṃ pac‍cācikkhasi? taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, sunakkhatta, pañ‍ñattai vā aggañ‍ñai, apañ‍ñattai vā aggañ‍ñai, yassatthāya mayā dhammau daisitau, sau niyyāti tak‍karassa sammā dukkhakkhayāyā’ti? ‘pañ‍ñattai vā, bhantai, aggañ‍ñai, apañ‍ñattai vā aggañ‍ñai, yassatthāya bhagavatā dhammau daisitau, sau niyyāti tak‍karassa sammā dukkhakkhayāyā’ti| ‘iti  kira, sunakkhatta, pañ‍ñattai vā aggañ‍ñai, apañ‍ñattai vā aggañ‍ñai, yassatthāya mayā dhammau daisitau, sau niyyāti tak‍karassa sammā dukkhakkhayāya| tatra, sunakkhatta, kiṃ aggañ‍ñaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ  karissati? passa, maughapurisa, yāvañ‍ca tai idaṃ aparaddhaṃ’|

6. ‘anaikapariyāyaina khau tai, sunakkhatta, mama vaṇṇau bhāsitau vaj‍jigāmai – itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau  laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| iti khau tai, sunakkhatta, anaikapariyāyaina mama vaṇṇau bhāsitau vaj‍jigāmai|

‘anaikapariyāyaina  khau tai, sunakkhatta, dhammassa vaṇṇau bhāsitau vaj‍jigāmai – svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau sandiṭṭhikau akālikau aihipassikau aupanaiyyikau pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhīti| iti khau tai, sunakkhatta, anaikapariyāyaina dhammassa vaṇṇau bhāsitau vaj‍jigāmai|

‘anaikapariyāyaina khau tai, sunakkhatta, saṅghassa vaṇṇau bhāsitau vaj‍jigāmai – suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, ujuppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, ñāyappaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, sāmīcippaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, yadidaṃ cattāri purisayugāni aṭṭha purisapuggalā, aisa bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, āhunaiyyau pāhunaiyyau dakkhiṇaiyyau añ‍jalikaraṇīyau anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassāti| iti khau tai, sunakkhatta, anaikapariyāyaina saṅghassa vaṇṇau bhāsitau vaj‍jigāmai|

‘āraucayāmi khau tai, sunakkhatta, paṭivaidayāmi khau tai, sunakkhatta| bhavissanti khau tai, sunakkhatta, vattārau, nau visahi sunakkhattau licchaviputtau samaṇai gautamai brahmacariyaṃ carituṃ, sau avisahantau  sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattauti| iti khau tai, sunakkhatta, bhavissanti vattārau’ti|

aivaṃ pi  khau, bhaggava, sunakkhattau licchaviputtau mayā vuc‍camānau apak‍kamaiva imasmā dhammavinayā, yathā taṃ āpāyikau nairayikau|

kaurakkhattiyavatthu

7. ‘‘aikamidāhaṃ, bhaggava, samayaṃ thūlūsu bumūsu (sī॰ pī॰) viharāmi uttarakā nāma thūlūnaṃ nigamau| atha khvāhaṃ, bhaggava, pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya sunakkhattaina licchaviputtaina pacchāsamaṇaina uttarakaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisiṃ| taina khau pana samayaina acailau kaurakkhattiyau kuk‍kuravatikau catuk‍kuṇḍikau catukuṇḍikau (sī॰ pī॰) catukauṇḍikau (syā॰ ka॰) chamānikiṇṇaṃ bhakkhasaṃ mukhainaiva khādati, mukhainaiva bhuñ‍jati| addasā khau, bhaggava, sunakkhattau licchaviputtau acailaṃ kaurakkhattiyaṃ kuk‍kuravatikaṃ  catuk‍kuṇḍikaṃ chamānikiṇṇaṃ bhakkhasaṃ mukhainaiva khādantaṃ mukhainaiva bhuñ‍jantaṃ| disvānassa aitadahausi – ‘sādhurūpau vata, bhau, ayaṃ arahaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) samaṇau catuk‍kuṇḍikau chamānikiṇṇaṃ bhakkhasaṃ mukhainaiva khādati, mukhainaiva bhuñ‍jatī’ti|

‘‘atha khvāhaṃ, bhaggava, sunakkhattassa licchaviputtassa caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya sunakkhattaṃ  licchaviputtaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘tvampi nāma, maughapurisa, samaṇau sakyaputtiyau maughapurisa sakyaputtiyau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) paṭijānissasī’ti! ‘kiṃ pana maṃ, bhantai, bhagavā aivamāha – ‘tvampi  nāma, maughapurisa, samaṇau sakyaputtiyau maughapurisa sakyaputtiyau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) paṭijānissasī’ti? ‘nanu tai, sunakkhatta, imaṃ acailaṃ kaurakkhattiyaṃ kuk‍kuravatikaṃ catuk‍kuṇḍikaṃ chamānikiṇṇaṃ bhakkhasaṃ mukhainaiva khādantaṃ mukhainaiva  bhuñ‍jantaṃ disvāna aitadahausi – sādhurūpau vata, bhau, ayaṃ samaṇau catuk‍kuṇḍikau chamānikiṇṇaṃ bhakkhasaṃ mukhainaiva khādati, mukhainaiva bhuñ‍jatī’ti? ‘aivaṃ, bhantai| kiṃ pana, bhantai, bhagavā arahattassa maccharāyatī’ti? ‘na khau ahaṃ, maughapurisa, arahattassa maccharāyāmi| api ca, tuyhaivaitaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ, taṃ pajaha| mā tai ahausi dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya| yaṃ khau panaitaṃ, sunakkhatta, mañ‍ñasi acailaṃ kaurakkhattiyaṃ – sādhurūpau ayaṃ samaṇauti mañ‍ñasi ‘‘acailau kaurakhattiyau sādhurūpau arahaṃ samaṇauti’’ (syā॰)| sau sattamaṃ divasaṃ alasakaina kālaṅkarissati| kālaṅkatau kālakatau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) ca kālakañ‍cikā kālakañ‍jā (sī॰ pī॰), kālakañ‍jikā (syā॰) nāma asurā sabbanihīnau asurakāyau, tatra upapaj‍jissati| kālaṅkatañ‍ca naṃ bīraṇatthambakai susānai chaḍḍaissanti| ākaṅkhamānau ca tvaṃ, sunakkhatta, acailaṃ kaurakkhattiyaṃ upasaṅkamitvā pucchaiyyāsi – jānāsi, āvusau kaurakkhattiya acaila kaurakhattiya (ka॰), attanau gatinti? ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, sunakkhatta, vij‍jati yaṃ tai acailau kaurakkhattiyau byākarissati – jānāmi, āvusau sunakkhatta, attanau gatiṃ; kālakañ‍cikā nāma asurā sabbanihīnau asurakāyau, tatrāmhi upapan‍nauti|

‘‘atha khau, bhaggava, sunakkhattau licchaviputtau yaina acailau kaurakkhattiyau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā acailaṃ kaurakkhattiyaṃ aitadavauca  – ‘byākatau khausi, āvusau kaurakkhattiya, samaṇaina gautamaina – acailau kaurakkhattiyau sattamaṃ divasaṃ alasakaina kālaṅkarissati| kālaṅkatau  ca kālakañ‍cikā nāma asurā sabbanihīnau asurakāyau, tatra upapaj‍jissati| kālaṅkatañ‍ca naṃ bīraṇatthambakai susānai chaḍḍaissantī’ti| yaina tvaṃ, āvusau kaurakkhattiya, mattaṃ mattañ‍ca bhattaṃ bhuñ‍jaiyyāsi, mattaṃ mattañ‍ca pānīyaṃ pivaiyyāsi| yathā samaṇassa gautamassa micchā assa vacana’nti|

8. ‘‘atha khau, bhaggava, sunakkhattau licchaviputtau aikadvīhikāya sattarattindivāni gaṇaisi, yathā taṃ tathāgatassa asaddahamānau| atha khau, bhaggava, acailau kaurakkhattiyau sattamaṃ divasaṃ alasakaina kālamakāsi| kālaṅkatau ca kālakañ‍cikā nāma asurā sabbanihīnau asurakāyau, tatra upapaj‍ji| kālaṅkatañ‍ca naṃ bīraṇatthambakai susānai chaḍḍaisuṃ|

9. ‘‘assausi  khau, bhaggava, sunakkhattau licchaviputtau – ‘acailau kira kaurakkhattiyau alasakaina kālaṅkatau bīraṇatthambakai susānai chaḍḍitau’ti| atha khau, bhaggava, sunakkhattau licchaviputtau yaina bīraṇatthambakaṃ susānaṃ, yaina acailau kaurakkhattiyau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā acailaṃ kaurakkhattiyaṃ tikkhattuṃ pāṇinā ākauṭaisi – ‘jānāsi, āvusau kaurakkhattiya, attanau gati’nti? atha khau, bhaggava, acailau kaurakkhattiyau pāṇinā piṭṭhiṃ paripuñchantau vuṭṭhāsi| ‘jānāmi, āvusau sunakkhatta, attanau gatiṃ| kālakañ‍cikā nāma asurā sabbanihīnau asurakāyau, tatrāmhi upapan‍nau’ti vatvā tatthaiva uttānau papati paripati (syā॰ ka॰)|

10. ‘‘atha khau, bhaggava, sunakkhattau licchaviputtau yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā maṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau ahaṃ, bhaggava, sunakkhattaṃ licchaviputtaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, sunakkhatta, yathaiva tai ahaṃ acailaṃ kaurakkhattiyaṃ ārabbha byākāsiṃ, tathaiva taṃ vipākaṃ, añ‍ñathā vā’ti? ‘yathaiva mai, bhantai, bhagavā acailaṃ kaurakkhattiyaṃ ārabbha byākāsi, tathaiva taṃ vipākaṃ, nau añ‍ñathā’ti| ‘taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, sunakkhatta, yadi aivaṃ santai kataṃ vā hauti uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ, akataṃ vāti? ‘addhā khau, bhantai, aivaṃ santai kataṃ hauti uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ, nau akata’nti| ‘aivampi khau maṃ tvaṃ, maughapurisa, uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ karauntaṃ aivaṃ vadaisi – na hi pana mai, bhantai, bhagavā uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ karautīti| passa, maughapurisa, yāvañ‍ca tai idaṃ aparaddha’nti| ‘‘aivampi khau, bhaggava, sunakkhattau licchaviputtau mayā vuc‍camānau apak‍kamaiva imasmā dhammavinayā, yathā taṃ āpāyikau nairayikau|

acailakaḷāramaṭṭakavatthu

11. ‘‘aikamidāhaṃ, bhaggava, samayaṃ vaisāliyaṃ viharāmi mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina acailau kaḷāramaṭṭakau vaisāliyaṃ paṭivasati lābhaggappattau caiva yasaggappattau ca vaj‍jigāmai| tassa sattavatapadāni sattavattapadāni (syā॰ pī॰) samattāni samādin‍nāni haunti – ‘yāvajīvaṃ acailakau assaṃ, na vatthaṃ paridahaiyyaṃ, yāvajīvaṃ brahmacārī assaṃ, na maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivaiyyaṃ, yāvajīvaṃ surāmaṃsainaiva yāpaiyyaṃ, na audanakummāsaṃ  bhuñ‍jaiyyaṃ| puratthimaina vaisāliṃ udainaṃ nāma caitiyaṃ, taṃ nātik‍kamaiyyaṃ, dakkhiṇaina vaisāliṃ gautamakaṃ nāma caitiyaṃ, taṃ nātik‍kamaiyyaṃ, pacchimaina vaisāliṃ sattambaṃ nāma caitiyaṃ, taṃ  nātik‍kamaiyyaṃ, uttaraina vaisāliṃ bahuputtaṃ  nāma bahuputtakaṃ nāma (syā॰) caitiyaṃ taṃ nātik‍kamaiyya’nti| sau imaisaṃ sattan‍naṃ vatapadānaṃ samādānahaitu lābhaggappattau caiva yasaggappattau ca vaj‍jigāmai|

12. ‘‘atha khau, bhaggava, sunakkhattau licchaviputtau yaina acailau kaḷāramaṭṭakau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā acailaṃ kaḷāramaṭṭakaṃ pañhaṃ apucchi| tassa acailau kaḷāramaṭṭakau pañhaṃ puṭṭhau na sampāyāsi| asampāyantau kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātvākāsi| atha khau, bhaggava, sunakkhattassa licchaviputtassa aitadahausi – ‘sādhurūpaṃ vata bhau arahantaṃ samaṇaṃ āsādimhasai asādiyimhasai (syā॰)| mā vata nau ahausi dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāyā’ti|

13. ‘‘atha  khau, bhaggava, sunakkhattau licchaviputtau yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā maṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau ahaṃ, bhaggava, sunakkhattaṃ licchaviputtaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘tvampi nāma, maughapurisa, samaṇau sakyaputtiyau paṭijānissasī’ti! ‘kiṃ pana maṃ, bhantai, bhagavā aivamāha – tvampi nāma, maughapurisa, samaṇau sakyaputtiyau paṭijānissasī’ti? ‘nanu tvaṃ, sunakkhatta, acailaṃ kaḷāramaṭṭakaṃ upasaṅkamitvā pañhaṃ apucchi| tassa tai acailau kaḷāramaṭṭakau pañhaṃ puṭṭhau na sampāyāsi| asampāyantau kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca  appac‍cayañ‍ca pātvākāsi| tassa tai aitadahausi – ‘‘sādhurūpaṃ vata, bhau, arahantaṃ samaṇaṃ āsādimhasai| mā vata nau ahausi dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāyā’ti| ‘aivaṃ, bhantai| kiṃ pana, bhantai, bhagavā arahattassa maccharāyatī’ti? ‘na  khau ahaṃ, maughapurisa, arahattassa maccharāyāmi, api ca tuyhaivaitaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ, taṃ pajaha| mā tai ahausi dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya| yaṃ khau panaitaṃ, sunakkhatta, mañ‍ñasi acailaṃ kaḷāramaṭṭakaṃ – sādhurūpau ayaṃ arahaṃ (syā॰) samaṇauti, sau nacirassaiva parihitau sānucārikau vicarantau audanakummāsaṃ bhuñ‍jamānau sabbānaiva vaisāliyāni caitiyāni samatik‍kamitvā yasā nihīnau yasānikiṇṇau (ka॰) kālaṃ karissatī’ti|

‘‘‘atha khau, bhaggava, acailau kaḷāramaṭṭakau nacirassaiva parihitau sānucārikau vicarantau audanakummāsaṃ bhuñ‍jamānau sabbānaiva vaisāliyāni caitiyāni samatik‍kamitvā yasā nihīnau kālamakāsi|

14. ‘‘assausi khau, bhaggava, sunakkhattau licchaviputtau – ‘acailau kira kaḷāramaṭṭakau parihitau sānucārikau vicarantau audanakummāsaṃ bhuñ‍jamānau sabbānaiva vaisāliyāni caitiyāni samatik‍kamitvā yasā nihīnau kālaṅkatau’ti| atha khau, bhaggava, sunakkhattau  licchaviputtau yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā maṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau ahaṃ, bhaggava, sunakkhattaṃ licchaviputtaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, sunakkhatta, yathaiva tai ahaṃ acailaṃ kaḷāramaṭṭakaṃ ārabbha byākāsiṃ, tathaiva taṃ vipākaṃ, añ‍ñathā vā’ti? ‘yathaiva  mai, bhantai, bhagavā acailaṃ kaḷāramaṭṭakaṃ ārabbha byākāsi, tathaiva taṃ vipākaṃ, nau  añ‍ñathā’ti| ‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, sunakkhatta, yadi aivaṃ santai kataṃ  vā hauti uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ akataṃ vā’ti? ‘addhā khau, bhantai, aivaṃ santai kataṃ hauti uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ, nau akata’nti| ‘aivampi khau maṃ tvaṃ, maughapurisa, uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ karauntaṃ aivaṃ vadaisi – na hi pana mai, bhantai, bhagavā uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ karautī’’ti| passa, maughapurisa, yāvañ‍ca tai idaṃ aparaddha’nti| ‘‘aiva’mpi khau, bhaggava, sunakkhattau licchaviputtau mayā vuc‍camānau apak‍kamaiva imasmā dhammavinayā, yathā taṃ āpāyikau nairayikau|

acailapāthikaputtavatthu

15. ‘‘aikamidāhaṃ, bhaggava, samayaṃ tatthaiva vaisāliyaṃ viharāmi mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina acailau pāthikaputtau pāṭikaputtau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) vaisāliyaṃ paṭivasati lābhaggappattau caiva yasaggappattau ca vaj‍jigāmai| sau vaisāliyaṃ parisati aivaṃ vācaṃ bhāsati – ‘samaṇaupi gautamau ñāṇavādau, ahampi ñāṇavādau| ñāṇavādau khau pana ñāṇavādaina arahati uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ dassaituṃ| samaṇau gautamau upaḍḍhapathaṃ āgacchaiyya, ahampi upaḍḍhapathaṃ gacchaiyyaṃ| tai tattha ubhaupi uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ karaiyyāma| aikaṃ cai samaṇau gautamau uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ karissati, dvāhaṃ karissāmi| dvai cai samaṇau gautamau uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyāni  karissati, cattārāhaṃ karissāmi| cattāri cai samaṇau gautamau uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyāni karissati, aṭṭhāhaṃ karissāmi| iti yāvatakaṃ yāvatakaṃ samaṇau gautamau uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ karissati, taddiguṇaṃ taddiguṇāhaṃ karissāmī’ti|

16. ‘‘atha khau, bhaggava, sunakkhattau licchaviputtau yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā maṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau, bhaggava, sunakkhattau licchaviputtau maṃ aitadavauca – ‘acailau, bhantai, pāthikaputtau vaisāliyaṃ paṭivasati lābhaggappattau caiva yasaggappattau ca vaj‍jigāmai| sau vaisāliyaṃ parisati aivaṃ vācaṃ bhāsati – samaṇaupi  gautamau  ñāṇavādau, ahampi ñāṇavādau| ñāṇavādau khau pana ñāṇavādaina arahati uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ dassaituṃ| samaṇau gautamau upaḍḍhapathaṃ āgacchaiyya, ahampi upaḍḍhapathaṃ gacchaiyyaṃ| tai tattha ubhaupi uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ karaiyyāma| aikaṃ cai samaṇau gautamau uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ karissati, dvāhaṃ karissāmi| dvai cai samaṇau gautamau uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyāni karissati, cattārāhaṃ karissāmi| cattāri cai samaṇau gautamau uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyāni karissati, aṭṭhāhaṃ karissāmi| iti yāvatakaṃ yāvatakaṃ samaṇau gautamau uttari manussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ karissati, taddiguṇaṃ taddiguṇāhaṃ karissāmī’’ti|

‘‘aivaṃ vuttai, ahaṃ, bhaggava, sunakkhattaṃ licchaviputtaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘abhabbau khau, sunakkhatta, acailau pāthikaputtau taṃ vācaṃ appahāya taṃ cittaṃ appahāya taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaj‍jitvā mama sammukhībhāvaṃ āgantuṃ| sacaipissa aivamassa – ahaṃ taṃ vācaṃ appahāya taṃ cittaṃ appahāya taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaj‍jitvā samaṇassa gautamassa sammukhībhāvaṃ gacchaiyyanti, muddhāpi  tassa vipataiyyā’ti|

17. ‘rakkhataitaṃ, bhantai, bhagavā vācaṃ, rakkhataitaṃ sugatau vāca’nti| ‘kiṃ  pana maṃ tvaṃ, sunakkhatta, aivaṃ vadaisi – rakkhataitaṃ, bhantai, bhagavā vācaṃ, rakkhataitaṃ sugatau vāca’nti? ‘bhagavatā cassa, bhantai, aisā vācā aikaṃsaina audhāritā auvāditā (ka॰) – abhabbau acailau pāthikaputtau taṃ vācaṃ appahāya taṃ cittaṃ appahāya taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaj‍jitvā mama sammukhībhāvaṃ āgantuṃ| sacaipissa aivamassa – ahaṃ taṃ vācaṃ appahāya taṃ cittaṃ appahāya taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaj‍jitvā samaṇassa gautamassa sammukhībhāvaṃ gacchaiyyanti, muddhāpi tassa vipataiyyāti| acailau ca, bhantai, pāthikaputtau virūparūpaina bhagavatau sammukhībhāvaṃ āgacchaiyya, tadassa bhagavatau musā’ti|

18. ‘api nu, sunakkhatta, tathāgatau taṃ vācaṃ bhāsaiyya yā sā vācā dvayagāminī’ti? ‘kiṃ pana, bhantai, bhagavatā acailau pāthikaputtau caitasā  caitau paric‍ca viditau – abhabbau acailau pāthikaputtau taṃ vācaṃ appahāya taṃ cittaṃ appahāya taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaj‍jitvā mama sammukhībhāvaṃ āgantuṃ| sacaipissa aivamassa – ahaṃ taṃ vācaṃ appahāya taṃ cittaṃ appahāya taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaj‍jitvā samaṇassa gautamassa sammukhībhāvaṃ gacchaiyyanti, muddhāpi tassa vipataiyyā’ti?

‘udāhu, daivatā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ – abhabbau, bhantai, acailau pāthikaputtau taṃ vācaṃ appahāya taṃ cittaṃ appahāya taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaj‍jitvā bhagavatau sammukhībhāvaṃ āgantuṃ| sacaipissa aivamassa – ahaṃ taṃ vācaṃ appahāya taṃ cittaṃ appahāya taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaj‍jitvā samaṇassa gautamassa sammukhībhāvaṃ gacchaiyyanti, muddhāpi tassa vipataiyyā’ti?

19. ‘caitasā caitau paric‍ca viditau caiva mai, sunakkhatta, acailau pāthikaputtau abhabbau acailau pāthikaputtau taṃ vācaṃ appahāya taṃ cittaṃ appahāya taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaj‍jitvā mama sammukhībhāvaṃ āgantuṃ| sacaipissa aivamassa – ahaṃ taṃ vācaṃ appahāya taṃ cittaṃ appahāya taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaj‍jitvā samaṇassa gautamassa sammukhībhāvaṃ gacchaiyyanti, muddhāpi tassa vipataiyyā’ti|

‘daivatāpi mai aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ – abhabbau, bhantai, acailau pāthikaputtau taṃ vācaṃ appahāya taṃ cittaṃ appahāya taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaj‍jitvā bhagavatau sammukhībhāvaṃ āgantuṃ| sacaipissa aivamassa – ahaṃ taṃ vācaṃ appahāya taṃ cittaṃ appahāya taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaj‍jitvā samaṇassa gautamassa sammukhībhāvaṃ gacchaiyyanti, muddhāpi tassa vipataiyyā’ti|

‘ajitaupi nāma licchavīnaṃ saināpati adhunā kālaṅkatau tāvatiṃsakāyaṃ upapan‍nau| saupi maṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivamāraucaisi – alaj‍jī, bhantai, acailau pāthikaputtau; musāvādī, bhantai, acailau pāthikaputtau| mampi, bhantai, acailau pāthikaputtau byākāsi vaj‍jigāmai – ajitau licchavīnaṃ saināpati mahānirayaṃ upapan‍nauti| na khau panāhaṃ, bhantai, mahānirayaṃ upapan‍nau; tāvatiṃsakāyamhi upapan‍nau| alaj‍jī, bhantai, acailau pāthikaputtau; musāvādī, bhantai, acailau pāthikaputtau; abhabbau ca, bhantai, acailau pāthikaputtau taṃ vācaṃ appahāya taṃ cittaṃ appahāya taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaj‍jitvā bhagavatau sammukhībhāvaṃ āgantuṃ| sacaipissa  aivamassa – ahaṃ taṃ vācaṃ appahāya taṃ cittaṃ appahāya taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaj‍jitvā samaṇassa gautamassa sammukhībhāvaṃ gacchaiyyanti, muddhāpi tassa vipataiyyā’ti|

‘iti khau, sunakkhatta, caitasā caitau paric‍ca viditau caiva mai acailau pāthikaputtau abhabbau acailau pāthikaputtau taṃ vācaṃ appahāya taṃ cittaṃ appahāya taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaj‍jitvā  mama sammukhībhāvaṃ āgantuṃ| sacaipissa aivamassa – ahaṃ taṃ vācaṃ appahāya taṃ cittaṃ appahāya taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaj‍jitvā samaṇassa gautamassa sammukhībhāvaṃ gacchaiyyanti, muddhāpi tassa vipataiyyāti| daivatāpi mai aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ – abhabbau, bhantai, acailau pāthikaputtau taṃ vācaṃ appahāya taṃ cittaṃ appahāya taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaj‍jitvā bhagavatau sammukhībhāvaṃ āgantuṃ| sacaipissa aivamassa – ahaṃ taṃ vācaṃ appahāya taṃ cittaṃ appahāya taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaj‍jitvā samaṇassa gautamassa sammukhībhāvaṃ gacchaiyyanti, muddhāpi tassa vipataiyyā’ti|

‘sau khau panāhaṃ, sunakkhatta, vaisāliyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātappaṭik‍kantau yaina acailassa pāthikaputtassa ārāmau tainupasaṅkamissāmi divāvihārāya| yassadāni tvaṃ, sunakkhatta, icchasi, tassa āraucaihī’ti|

iddhipāṭihāriyakathā

20. ‘‘atha  khvāhaṃ atha khau svāhaṃ (syā॰), bhaggava, pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya vaisāliṃ piṇḍāya pāvisiṃ| vaisāliyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātappaṭik‍kantau yaina acailassa pāthikaputtassa ārāmau tainupasaṅkamiṃ divāvihārāya| atha khau, bhaggava, sunakkhattau licchaviputtau taramānarūpau vaisāliṃ pavisitvā yaina abhiñ‍ñātā abhiñ‍ñātā licchavī tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā abhiñ‍ñātai abhiñ‍ñātai licchavī aitadavauca – ‘aisāvusau, bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātappaṭik‍kantau yaina acailassa pāthikaputtassa ārāmau tainupasaṅkami divāvihārāya| abhik‍kamathāyasmantau abhik‍kamathāyasmantau, sādhurūpānaṃ samaṇānaṃ uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ bhavissatī’ti| atha khau, bhaggava, abhiñ‍ñātānaṃ abhiñ‍ñātānaṃ licchavīnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘sādhurūpānaṃ kira, bhau, samaṇānaṃ uttarimanussadhammā  iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ bhavissati; handa vata, bhau, gacchāmā’ti| yaina ca abhiñ‍ñātā abhiñ‍ñātā brāhmaṇamahāsālā gahapatinaicayikā nānātitthiyā nānātitthiya (syā॰) samaṇabrāhmaṇā tainupasaṅkami| upasaṅkamitvā abhiñ‍ñātai abhiñ‍ñātai nānātitthiyai nānātitthiya (syā॰) samaṇabrāhmaṇai aitadavauca – ‘aisāvusau, bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātappaṭik‍kantau yaina acailassa pāthikaputtassa ārāmau tainupasaṅkami divāvihārāya| abhik‍kamathāyasmantau abhik‍kamathāyasmantau, sādhurūpānaṃ samaṇānaṃ uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ bhavissatī’ti| atha  khau, bhaggava, abhiñ‍ñātānaṃ abhiñ‍ñātānaṃ nānātitthiyānaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ aitadahausi – ‘sādhurūpānaṃ kira, bhau, samaṇānaṃ uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ bhavissati; handa vata, bhau, gacchāmā’ti|

‘‘atha  khau, bhaggava, abhiñ‍ñātā abhiñ‍ñātā licchavī, abhiñ‍ñātā abhiñ‍ñātā ca brāhmaṇamahāsālā gahapatinaicayikā nānātitthiyā samaṇabrāhmaṇā yaina acailassa pāthikaputtassa ārāmau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu| sā aisā, bhaggava, parisā mahā hauti parisā hauti (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) anaikasatā anaikasahassā|

21. ‘‘assausi khau, bhaggava, acailau pāthikaputtau – ‘abhik‍kantā kira abhiñ‍ñātā abhiñ‍ñātā licchavī, abhik‍kantā abhiñ‍ñātā abhiñ‍ñātā ca brāhmaṇamahāsālā gahapatinaicayikā nānātitthiyā samaṇabrāhmaṇā| samaṇaupi gautamau mayhaṃ ārāmai divāvihāraṃ nisin‍nau’ti| sutvānassa bhayaṃ chambhitattaṃ laumahaṃsau udapādi| atha khau, bhaggava, acailau pāthikaputtau  bhītau saṃviggau laumahaṭṭhajātau yaina tindukakhāṇuparibbājakārāmau tainupasaṅkami|

‘‘assausi khau, bhaggava, sā parisā – ‘acailau kira pāthikaputtau bhītau saṃviggau laumahaṭṭhajātau yaina tindukakhāṇuparibbājakārāmau  tainupasaṅkantau’ti tainupasaṅkamantau (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰)| atha khau, bhaggava, sā parisā añ‍ñataraṃ purisaṃ āmantaisi –

‘aihi tvaṃ, bhau purisa, yaina tindukakhāṇuparibbājakārāmau, yaina acailau pāthikaputtau tainupasaṅkama| upasaṅkamitvā acailaṃ pāthikaputtaṃ aivaṃ vadaihi – abhik‍kamāvusau, pāthikaputta, abhik‍kantā abhiñ‍ñātā abhiñ‍ñātā licchavī, abhik‍kantā abhiñ‍ñātā abhiñ‍ñātā ca brāhmaṇamahāsālā gahapatinaicayikā nānātitthiyā samaṇabrāhmaṇā, samaṇaupi gautamau āyasmatau ārāmai divāvihāraṃ nisin‍nau; bhāsitā khau pana tai aisā, āvusau pāthikaputta, vaisāliyaṃ parisati vācā samaṇaupi gautamau ñāṇavādau, ahampi ñāṇavādau| ñāṇavādau khau pana ñāṇavādaina arahati uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ dassaituṃ| samaṇau  gautamau upaḍḍhapathaṃ āgacchaiyya ahampi upaḍḍhapathaṃ gacchaiyyaṃ| tai tattha ubhaupi uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ karaiyyāma| aikaṃ cai samaṇau gautamau uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ karissati, dvāhaṃ karissāmi| dvai cai samaṇau gautamau uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyāni karissati, cattārāhaṃ karissāmi| cattāri cai samaṇau gautamau  uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyāni karissati, aṭṭhāhaṃ karissāmi| iti yāvatakaṃ yāvatakaṃ samaṇau gautamau uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ karissati, taddiguṇaṃ taddiguṇāhaṃ karissāmī’ti abhik‍kamassaiva abhik‍kamayaiva (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) khau; āvusau pāthikaputta, upaḍḍhapathaṃ| sabbapaṭhamaṃyaiva āgantvā samaṇau gautamau āyasmatau ārāmai divāvihāraṃ nisin‍nau’ti|

22. ‘‘aivaṃ, bhauti khau, bhaggava, sau purisau tassā parisāya paṭissutvā yaina tindukakhāṇuparibbājakārāmau, yaina acailau pāthikaputtau tainupasaṅkami| upasaṅkamitvā acailaṃ pāthikaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘abhik‍kamāvusau pāthikaputta, abhik‍kantā abhiñ‍ñātā abhiñ‍ñātā licchavī, abhik‍kantā abhiñ‍ñātā abhiñ‍ñātā ca brāhmaṇamahāsālā gahapatinaicayikā nānātitthiyā samaṇabrāhmaṇā| samaṇaupi gautamau āyasmatau ārāmai divāvihāraṃ nisin‍nau| bhāsitā  khau pana tai aisā, āvusau pāthikaputta, vaisāliyaṃ parisati vācā – samaṇaupi gautamau ñāṇavādau; ahampi ñāṇavādau| ñāṇavādau khau pana ñāṇavādaina arahati uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ dassaituṃ…pai॰… taddiguṇaṃ taddiguṇāhaṃ karissāmīti| abhik‍kamassaiva khau, āvusau pāthikaputta, upaḍḍhapathaṃ| sabbapaṭhamaṃyaiva āgantvā samaṇau gautamau āyasmatau ārāmai divāvihāraṃ nisin‍nau’ti|

‘‘aivaṃ vuttai, bhaggava, acailau pāthikaputtau ‘āyāmi āvusau, āyāmi  āvusau’ti vatvā tatthaiva saṃsappati saṃsabbati (ka॰), na sak‍kauti āsanāpi vuṭṭhātuṃ| atha khau sau, bhaggava, purisau acailaṃ pāthikaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘kiṃ su nāma tai, āvusau pāthikaputta, pāvaḷā su nāma tai pīṭhakasmiṃ al‍līnā, pīṭhakaṃ su nāma tai pāvaḷāsu al‍līnaṃ? āyāmi āvusau, āyāmi āvusauti vatvā tatthaiva saṃsappasi, na sak‍kausi āsanāpi vuṭṭhātu’nti| aivampi khau, bhaggava, vuc‍camānau acailau pāthikaputtau ‘āyāmi āvusau, āyāmi āvusau’ti vatvā tatthaiva saṃsappati, na sak‍kauti āsanāpi vuṭṭhātuṃ|

23. ‘‘yadā khau sau, bhaggava, purisau añ‍ñāsi – ‘parābhūtarūpau ayaṃ acailau pāthikaputtau| āyāmi āvusau, āyāmi āvusauti vatvā tatthaiva saṃsappati, na sak‍kauti āsanāpi vuṭṭhātu’nti| atha taṃ parisaṃ āgantvā aivamāraucaisi – ‘parābhūtarūpau, bhau parābhūtarūpau bhau ayaṃ (syā॰ ka॰), parābhūtarūpau (sī॰ pī॰), acailau pāthikaputtau| āyāmi āvusau, āyāmi āvusauti vatvā tatthaiva saṃsappati, na sak‍kauti āsanāpi vuṭṭhātu’nti| aivaṃ vuttai, ahaṃ, bhaggava, taṃ parisaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘abhabbau khau, āvusau, acailau pāthikaputtau taṃ vācaṃ appahāya taṃ cittaṃ appahāya taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaj‍jitvā  mama sammukhībhāvaṃ āgantuṃ| sacaipissa aivamassa – ‘ahaṃ taṃ vācaṃ appahāya taṃ cittaṃ appahāya taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaj‍jitvā samaṇassa gautamassa sammukhībhāvaṃ gacchaiyya’nti, muddhāpi tassa vipataiyyāti|

paṭhamabhāṇavārau niṭṭhitau|

24. ‘‘atha  khau, bhaggava, añ‍ñatarau licchavimahāmattau uṭṭhāyāsanā taṃ parisaṃ aitadavauca – ‘taina hi, bhau, muhuttaṃ tāva āgamaitha, yāvāhaṃ gacchāmi  pac‍cāgacchāmi (?)| appaiva nāma ahampi sak‍kuṇaiyyaṃ acailaṃ pāthikaputtaṃ imaṃ parisaṃ ānaitu’nti|

‘‘atha khau sau, bhaggava, licchavimahāmattau yaina tindukakhāṇuparibbājakārāmau, yaina acailau pāthikaputtau tainupasaṅkami| upasaṅkamitvā acailaṃ pāthikaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘abhik‍kamāvusau pāthikaputta, abhik‍kantaṃ tai saiyyau, abhik‍kantā abhiñ‍ñātā abhiñ‍ñātā  licchavī, abhik‍kantā abhiñ‍ñātā abhiñ‍ñātā ca brāhmaṇamahāsālā gahapatinaicayikā nānātitthiyā samaṇabrāhmaṇā| samaṇaupi gautamau āyasmatau ārāmai divāvihāraṃ nisin‍nau| bhāsitā khau pana tai aisā, āvusau pāthikaputta, vaisāliyaṃ parisati vācā – samaṇaupi gautamau ñāṇavādau…pai॰… taddiguṇaṃ taddiguṇāhaṃ karissāmīti| abhik‍kamassaiva khau, āvusau pāthikaputta, upaḍḍhapathaṃ| sabbapaṭhamaṃyaiva āgantvā samaṇau gautamau āyasmatau ārāmai divāvihāraṃ nisin‍nau| bhāsitā khau panaisā, āvusau pāthikaputta, samaṇaina gautamaina parisati vācā – abhabbau khau acailau pāthikaputtau taṃ vācaṃ appahāya taṃ cittaṃ appahāya taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaj‍jitvā mama sammukhībhāvaṃ āgantuṃ| sacaipissa aivamassa – ahaṃ taṃ vācaṃ appahāya taṃ cittaṃ appahāya taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaj‍jitvā samaṇassa gautamassa sammukhībhāvaṃ gacchaiyyanti, muddhāpi tassa vipataiyyāti| abhik‍kamāvusau pāthikaputta, abhik‍kamanainaiva tai jayaṃ karissāma, samaṇassa gautamassa parājaya’nti|

‘‘aivaṃ vuttai, bhaggava, acailau pāthikaputtau ‘āyāmi āvusau, āyāmi āvusau’ti vatvā tatthaiva saṃsappati, na sak‍kauti  āsanāpi vuṭṭhātuṃ| atha khau sau, bhaggava, licchavimahāmattau acailaṃ pāthikaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘kiṃ su nāma tai, āvusau pāthikaputta, pāvaḷā su nāma tai pīṭhakasmiṃ al‍līnā, pīṭhakaṃ su nāma tai pāvaḷāsu al‍līnaṃ? āyāmi āvusau, āyāmi āvusauti vatvā tatthaiva saṃsappasi, na sak‍kausi āsanāpi vuṭṭhātu’nti| aivampi khau, bhaggava, vuc‍camānau acailau pāthikaputtau ‘āyāmi āvusau, āyāmi āvusau’ti vatvā tatthaiva saṃsappati, na sak‍kauti āsanāpi vuṭṭhātuṃ|

25. ‘‘yadā khau sau, bhaggava, licchavimahāmattau añ‍ñāsi – ‘parābhūtarūpau ayaṃ acailau pāthikaputtau āyāmi āvusau, āyāmi  āvusauti vatvā tatthaiva saṃsappati, na sak‍kauti āsanāpi vuṭṭhātu’nti| atha taṃ parisaṃ āgantvā aivamāraucaisi – ‘parābhūtarūpau, bhau parābhūtarūpau (sī॰ pī॰), parābhūtarūpau ayaṃ (syā॰), acailau pāthikaputtau āyāmi āvusau, āyāmi āvusauti vatvā tatthaiva saṃsappati, na sak‍kauti āsanāpi vuṭṭhātu’nti| aivaṃ vuttai, ahaṃ, bhaggava, taṃ parisaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘abhabbau khau, āvusau, acailau pāthikaputtau taṃ vācaṃ appahāya taṃ cittaṃ appahāya taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaj‍jitvā mama sammukhībhāvaṃ āgantuṃ| sacaipissa aivamassa – ahaṃ taṃ vācaṃ appahāya taṃ cittaṃ appahāya taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaj‍jitvā samaṇassa gautamassa sammukhībhāvaṃ gacchaiyyanti, muddhāpi tassa vipataiyya| sacai pāyasmantānaṃ licchavīnaṃ aivamassa – mayaṃ acailaṃ pāthikaputtaṃ varattāhi yāhi varattāhi (syā॰ ka॰) bandhitvā gauyugaihi āviñchaiyyāmāti āviñ‍jaiyyāmāti (syā॰), āvijjhaiyyāmāti (sī॰ pī॰), tā varattā chij‍jaiyyuṃ pāthikaputtau vā| abhabbau pana acailau pāthikaputtau taṃ vācaṃ appahāya taṃ cittaṃ appahāya taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaj‍jitvā mama sammukhībhāvaṃ āgantuṃ| sacaipissa  aivamassa – ahaṃ taṃ vācaṃ appahāya taṃ cittaṃ appahāya taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaj‍jitvā samaṇassa gautamassa sammukhībhāvaṃ gacchaiyyanti, muddhāpi tassa vipataiyyā’ti|

26. ‘‘atha khau, bhaggava, jāliyau dārupattikantaivāsī uṭṭhāyāsanā taṃ parisaṃ aitadavauca – ‘taina hi, bhau, muhuttaṃ tāva āgamaitha, yāvāhaṃ gacchāmi; appaiva nāma ahampi sak‍kuṇaiyyaṃ acailaṃ pāthikaputtaṃ imaṃ parisaṃ ānaitu’’nti|

‘‘atha  khau, bhaggava, jāliyau dārupattikantaivāsī yaina tindukakhāṇuparibbājakārāmau, yaina acailau pāthikaputtau tainupasaṅkami| upasaṅkamitvā acailaṃ pāthikaputtaṃ  aitadavauca – ‘abhik‍kamāvusau pāthikaputta, abhik‍kantaṃ tai saiyyau| abhik‍kantā abhiñ‍ñātā abhiñ‍ñātā licchavī, abhik‍kantā abhiñ‍ñātā abhiñ‍ñātā ca brāhmaṇamahāsālā gahapatinaicayikā nānātitthiyā samaṇabrāhmaṇā| samaṇaupi gautamau āyasmatau ārāmai divāvihāraṃ nisin‍nau| bhāsitā khau pana tai aisā, āvusau pāthikaputta, vaisāliyaṃ parisati vācā – samaṇaupi gautamau ñāṇavādau…pai॰… taddiguṇaṃ taddiguṇāhaṃ karissāmīti| abhik‍kamassaiva, khau āvusau pāthikaputta, upaḍḍhapathaṃ| sabbapaṭhamaṃyaiva āgantvā samaṇau gautamau āyasmatau ārāmai divāvihāraṃ  nisin‍nau| bhāsitā khau panaisā, āvusau pāthikaputta, samaṇaina gautamaina parisati vācā – abhabbau acailau pāthikaputtau taṃ vācaṃ appahāya taṃ cittaṃ appahāya taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaj‍jitvā mama sammukhībhāvaṃ āgantuṃ| sacaipissa aivamassa – ahaṃ taṃ vācaṃ appahāya taṃ cittaṃ appahāya taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaj‍jitvā samaṇassa gautamassa sammukhībhāvaṃ gacchaiyyanti, muddhāpi tassa vipataiyya| sacai pāyasmantānaṃ licchavīnaṃ aivamassa – mayaṃ acailaṃ pāthikaputtaṃ varattāhi bandhitvā gauyugaihi āviñchaiyyāmāti| tā varattā chij‍jaiyyuṃ pāthikaputtau vā| abhabbau pana acailau pāthikaputtau taṃ vācaṃ appahāya taṃ cittaṃ appahāya taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaj‍jitvā mama sammukhībhāvaṃ āgantuṃ| sacaipissa aivamassa – ahaṃ taṃ vācaṃ appahāya taṃ cittaṃ appahāya taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaj‍jitvā samaṇassa gautamassa sammukhībhāvaṃ āgacchaiyyanti, muddhāpi tassa vipataiyyāti| abhik‍kamāvusau pāthikaputta, abhik‍kamanainaiva tai jayaṃ karissāma, samaṇassa gautamassa parājaya’nti|

‘‘aivaṃ  vuttai, bhaggava, acailau pāthikaputtau  ‘āyāmi āvusau, āyāmi āvusau’ti vatvā tatthaiva saṃsappati, na sak‍kauti āsanāpi vuṭṭhātuṃ| atha khau, bhaggava, jāliyau dārupattikantaivāsī acailaṃ pāthikaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘kiṃ su nāma tai, āvusau pāthikaputta, pāvaḷā su nāma tai pīṭhakasmiṃ al‍līnā, pīṭhakaṃ su nāma tai pāvaḷāsu al‍līnaṃ? āyāmi āvusau, āyāmi āvusauti vatvā tatthaiva  saṃsappasi, na sak‍kausi āsanāpi vuṭṭhātu’nti| aivampi khau, bhaggava, vuc‍camānau acailau pāthikaputtau ‘‘āyāmi āvusau, āyāmi āvusau’’ti vatvā tatthaiva saṃsappati, na sak‍kauti āsanāpi vuṭṭhātunti|

27. ‘‘yadā khau, bhaggava, jāliyau dārupattikantaivāsī añ‍ñāsi – ‘parābhūtarūpau ayaṃ acailau pāthikaputtau ‘āyāmi āvusau, āyāmi āvusauti vatvā tatthaiva saṃsappati, na sak‍kauti āsanāpi vuṭṭhātu’nti, atha naṃ aitadavauca –

‘bhūtapubbaṃ, āvusau pāthikaputta, sīhassa migarañ‍ñau aitadahausi – yaṃnūnāhaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ vanasaṇḍaṃ nissāya āsayaṃ kappaiyyaṃ| tatrāsayaṃ kappaitvā sāyanhasamayaṃ āsayā nikkhamaiyyaṃ, āsayā nikkhamitvā vijambhaiyyaṃ, vijambhitvā samantā catuddisā anuvilaukaiyyaṃ, samantā catuddisā anuvilaukaitvā tikkhattuṃ sīhanādaṃ nadaiyyaṃ, tikkhattuṃ sīhanādaṃ naditvā gaucarāya pak‍kamaiyyaṃ| sau varaṃ varaṃ migasaṃghai migasaṃghaṃ (syā॰ ka॰) vadhitvā mudumaṃsāni mudumaṃsāni bhakkhayitvā tamaiva āsayaṃ ajjhupaiyya’nti|

‘atha  khau, āvusau, sau sīhau migarājā añ‍ñataraṃ vanasaṇḍaṃ nissāya āsayaṃ kappaisi| tatrāsayaṃ kappaitvā sāyanhasamayaṃ  āsayā nikkhami, āsayā nikkhamitvā vijambhi, vijambhitvā samantā catuddisā anuvilaukaisi, samantā catuddisā anuvilaukaitvā tikkhattuṃ sīhanādaṃ nadi, tikkhattuṃ sīhanādaṃ naditvā gaucarāya pak‍kāmi| sau varaṃ varaṃ migasaṅghai vadhitvā mudumaṃsāni mudumaṃsāni bhakkhayitvā tamaiva āsayaṃ ajjhupaisi|

28. ‘tassaiva  khau, āvusau pāthikaputta, sīhassa migarañ‍ñau vighāsasaṃvaḍḍhau jarasiṅgālau jarasigālau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) dittau caiva balavā ca| atha khau, āvusau, tassa jarasiṅgālassa aitadahausi – kau cāhaṃ, kau sīhau migarājā| yaṃnūnāhampi añ‍ñataraṃ vanasaṇḍaṃ nissāya āsayaṃ kappaiyyaṃ| tatrāsayaṃ kappaitvā sāyanhasamayaṃ āsayā nikkhamaiyyaṃ, āsayā  nikkhamitvā vijambhaiyyaṃ, vijambhitvā samantā catuddisā anuvilaukaiyyaṃ, samantā catuddisā anuvilaukaitvā tikkhattuṃ sīhanādaṃ nadaiyyaṃ, tikkhattuṃ sīhanādaṃ naditvā gaucarāya pak‍kamaiyyaṃ| sau varaṃ varaṃ migasaṅghai vadhitvā mudumaṃsāni mudumaṃsāni bhakkhayitvā tamaiva āsayaṃ ajjhupaiyya’nti|

‘atha khau sau, āvusau, jarasiṅgālau añ‍ñataraṃ vanasaṇḍaṃ nissāya āsayaṃ kappaisi| tatrāsayaṃ kappaitvā sāyanhasamayaṃ āsayā nikkhami, āsayā nikkhamitvā vijambhi, vijambhitvā samantā catuddisā anuvilaukaisi, samantā catuddisā anuvilaukaitvā tikkhattuṃ sīhanādaṃ nadissāmīti siṅgālakaṃyaiva anadi bhairaṇḍakaṃyaiva bhaidaṇḍakaṃyaiva (ka॰) anadi, kai ca chavai siṅgālai, kai pana sīhanādaiti sīhanādai (?)|

‘aivamaiva khau tvaṃ, āvusau pāthikaputta, sugatāpadānaisu jīvamānau sugatātirittāni bhuñ‍jamānau tathāgatai  arahantai sammāsambuddhai āsādaitabbaṃ mañ‍ñasi| kai ca chavai pāthikaputtai, kā ca tathāgatānaṃ arahantānaṃ sammāsambuddhānaṃ āsādanā’ti|

29. ‘‘yatau khau, bhaggava, jāliyau dārupattikantaivāsī iminā aupammaina naiva asakkhi acailaṃ pāthikaputtaṃ tamhā āsanā cāvaituṃ| atha naṃ aitadavauca –

‘sīhauti  attānaṃ samaikkhiyāna,

amañ‍ñi kautthu migarājāhamasmi|

tathaiva tamaiva (syā॰) sau siṅgālakaṃ anadi,

kai ca chavai siṅgālai kai pana sīhanādai’ti||

‘aivamaiva  khau tvaṃ, āvusau pāthikaputta, sugatāpadānaisu jīvamānau sugatātirittāni bhuñ‍jamānau tathāgatai arahantai sammāsambuddhai āsādaitabbaṃ mañ‍ñasi| kai ca chavai pāthikaputtai, kā ca tathāgatānaṃ arahantānaṃ sammāsambuddhānaṃ āsādanā’ti|

30. ‘‘yatau khau, bhaggava, jāliyau dārupattikantaivāsī imināpi aupammaina naiva asakkhi acailaṃ pāthikaputtaṃ tamhā āsanā cāvaituṃ| atha naṃ aitadavauca –

‘añ‍ñaṃ  anucaṅkamanaṃ, attānaṃ vighāsai samaikkhiya|

yāva attānaṃ na passati, kautthu tāva byagghauti mañ‍ñati||

tathaiva  sau siṅgālakaṃ anadi|

kai ca chavai siṅgālai kai pana sīhanādai’ti||

‘aivamaiva khau tvaṃ, āvusau pāthikaputta, sugatāpadānaisu jīvamānau sugatātirittāni bhuñ‍jamānau tathāgatai arahantai sammāsambuddhai āsādaitabbaṃ mañ‍ñasi| kai ca chavai pāthikaputtai, kā ca tathāgatānaṃ arahantānaṃ sammāsambuddhānaṃ āsādanā’ti|

31. ‘‘yatau khau, bhaggava, jāliyau dārupattikantaivāsī imināpi  aupammaina naiva asakkhi acailaṃ pāthikaputtaṃ tamhā āsanā cāvaituṃ| atha naṃ aitadavauca –

‘bhutvāna bhaikai bhiṅgai (ka॰) khalamūsikāyau,

kaṭasīsu khittāni ca kauṇapāni kūṇapāni (syā॰)|

mahāvanai suñ‍ñavanai vivaḍḍhau,

amañ‍ñi kautthu migarājāhamasmi||

tathaiva sau siṅgālakaṃ anadi|

kai ca chavai siṅgālai kai pana sīhanādai’ti||

‘aivamaiva khau tvaṃ, āvusau pāthikaputta, sugatāpadānaisu jīvamānau sugatātirittāni bhuñ‍jamānau  tathāgatai arahantai sammāsambuddhai āsādaitabbaṃ mañ‍ñasi| kai ca chavai pāthikaputtai, kā ca tathāgatānaṃ arahantānaṃ sammāsambuddhānaṃ āsādanā’ti|

32. ‘‘yatau khau, bhaggava, jāliyau dārupattikantaivāsī imināpi aupammaina naiva asakkhi  acailaṃ pāthikaputtaṃ tamhā āsanā cāvaituṃ| atha taṃ parisaṃ āgantvā aivamāraucaisi – ‘parābhūtarūpau, bhau, acailau pāthikaputtau āyāmi āvusau, āyāmi āvusauti vatvā tatthaiva saṃsappati, na sak‍kauti āsanāpi vuṭṭhātu’nti|

33. ‘‘aivaṃ  vuttai, ahaṃ, bhaggava, taṃ parisaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘abhabbau khau, āvusau, acailau pāthikaputtau taṃ vācaṃ appahāya taṃ cittaṃ appahāya taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaj‍jitvā mama sammukhībhāvaṃ āgantuṃ| sacaipissa aivamassa – ahaṃ taṃ vācaṃ appahāya taṃ cittaṃ appahāya taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaj‍jitvā samaṇassa gautamassa sammukhībhāvaṃ gacchaiyyanti, muddhāpi tassa vipataiyya| sacaipāyasmantānaṃ licchavīnaṃ aivamassa – mayaṃ acailaṃ pāthikaputtaṃ varattāhi bandhitvā nāgaihi gauyugaihi (sabbattha) aṭṭhakathā passitabbā āviñchaiyyāmāti| tā varattā chij‍jaiyyuṃ pāthikaputtau vā| abhabbau pana acailau pāthikaputtau taṃ vācaṃ appahāya taṃ cittaṃ appahāya taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaj‍jitvā mama sammukhībhāvaṃ āgantuṃ| sacaipissa aivamassa – ahaṃ taṃ vācaṃ appahāya taṃ cittaṃ appahāya taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaj‍jitvā samaṇassa gautamassa sammukhībhāvaṃ gacchaiyyanti, muddhāpi tassa vipataiyyā’ti|

34. ‘‘atha khvāhaṃ, bhaggava, taṃ parisaṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisiṃ samādapaisiṃ samuttaijaisiṃ sampahaṃsaisiṃ, taṃ parisaṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaitvā samādapaitvā samuttaijaitvā sampahaṃsaitvā mahābandhanā maukkhaṃ karitvā caturāsītipāṇasahassāni mahāviduggā uddharitvā taijaudhātuṃ samāpaj‍jitvā sattatālaṃ vaihāsaṃ abbhuggantvā añ‍ñaṃ sattatālampi ac‍ciṃ aggiṃ (syā॰) abhinimminitvā paj‍jalitvā dhūmāyitvā  dhūpāyitvā (sī॰ pī॰) mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhāsiṃ|

35. ‘‘atha khau, bhaggava, sunakkhattau licchaviputtau yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā maṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau ahaṃ, bhaggava, sunakkhattaṃ licchaviputtaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, sunakkhatta, yathaiva tai ahaṃ acailaṃ pāthikaputtaṃ ārabbha byākāsiṃ, tathaiva taṃ vipākaṃ añ‍ñathā vā’ti? ‘yathaiva mai, bhantai, bhagavā acailaṃ pāthikaputtaṃ ārabbha byākāsi, tathaiva taṃ vipākaṃ, nau añ‍ñathā’ti|

‘taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, sunakkhatta, yadi aivaṃ santai kataṃ vā hauti uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ, akataṃ vā’ti? ‘addhā khau, bhantai, aivaṃ santai kataṃ hauti uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ, nau akata’nti| ‘aivampi khau  maṃ tvaṃ, maughapurisa, uttarimanussadhammā  iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ karauntaṃ aivaṃ vadaisi – na hi pana mai, bhantai, bhagavā uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ karautīti| passa, maughapurisa, yāvañ‍ca tai idaṃ aparaddhaṃ’ti|

‘‘aivampi khau, bhaggava, sunakkhattau licchaviputtau mayā vuc‍camānau apak‍kamaiva imasmā dhammavinayā, yathā taṃ āpāyikau nairayikau|

aggañ‍ñapañ‍ñattikathā

36. ‘‘aggañ‍ñañ‍cāhaṃ, bhaggava, pajānāmi| tañ‍ca pajānāmi ‘‘tañ‍capajānāmī’’ti idaṃ syāpautthakainatthi, tatau ca uttaritaraṃ pajānāmi, tañ‍ca pajānaṃ pajānanaṃ (syā॰ ka॰) aṭṭhakathāsaṃvaṇṇanā passitabbā na parāmasāmi, aparāmasatau ca mai pac‍cattañ‍ñaiva nibbuti viditā, yadabhijānaṃ tathāgatau nau anayaṃ āpaj‍jati|

37. ‘‘santi, bhaggava, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā issarakuttaṃ brahmakuttaṃ ācariyakaṃ aggañ‍ñaṃ pañ‍ñapainti| tyāhaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘sac‍caṃ kira tumhai āyasmantau issarakuttaṃ brahmakuttaṃ ācariyakaṃ aggañ‍ñaṃ pañ‍ñapaithā’ti? tai ca mai aivaṃ puṭṭhā, ‘āmau’ti āmāti (syā॰) paṭijānanti| tyāhaṃ aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘kathaṃvihitakaṃ pana kathaṃ vihitakaṃnau pana (ka॰) tumhai āyasmantau issarakuttaṃ brahmakuttaṃ ācariyakaṃ aggañ‍ñaṃ pañ‍ñapaithā’ti? tai mayā puṭṭhā na sampāyanti, asampāyantā mamañ‍ñaiva paṭipucchanti| taisāhaṃ puṭṭhau byākaraumi –

38. ‘hauti khau sau, āvusau, samayau yaṃ kadāci karahaci dīghassa addhunau ac‍cayaina ayaṃ laukau saṃvaṭṭati| saṃvaṭṭamānai laukai yaibhuyyaina sattā ābhassarasaṃvattanikā haunti| tai tattha haunti manaumayā pītibhakkhā sayaṃpabhā antalikkhacarā subhaṭṭhāyinau ciraṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ tiṭṭhanti|

‘hauti khau sau, āvusau, samayau yaṃ kadāci karahaci dīghassa addhunau ac‍cayaina ayaṃ laukau vivaṭṭati| vivaṭṭamānai laukai suñ‍ñaṃ brahmavimānaṃ pātubhavati| atha khau atha (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) añ‍ñatarau  sattau āyukkhayā vā puñ‍ñakkhayā vā ābhassarakāyā cavitvā suñ‍ñaṃ brahmavimānaṃ upapaj‍jati| sau  tattha hauti manaumayau pītibhakkhau sayaṃpabhau antalikkhacarau subhaṭṭhāyī, ciraṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ tiṭṭhati|

‘tassa tattha aikakassa dīgharattaṃ nivusitattā anabhirati paritassanā uppaj‍jati – ahau vata añ‍ñaipi sattā itthattaṃ āgacchaiyyunti| atha añ‍ñaipi sattā āyukkhayā vā puñ‍ñakkhayā vā  ābhassarakāyā cavitvā brahmavimānaṃ upapaj‍janti tassa sattassa sahabyataṃ| taipi tattha haunti manaumayā pītibhakkhā sayaṃpabhā antalikkhacarā subhaṭṭhāyinau, ciraṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ tiṭṭhanti|

39. ‘tatrāvusau, yau sau sattau paṭhamaṃ upapan‍nau, tassa aivaṃ hauti – ahamasmi brahmā mahābrahmā abhibhū anabhibhūtau añ‍ñadatthudasau vasavattī issarau kattā nimmātā saiṭṭhau sajitā sañ‍jitā (sī॰ pī॰), saj‍jitā (syā॰ kaṃ॰) vasī pitā bhūtabhabyānaṃ, mayā imai sattā nimmitā| taṃ kissa haitu? mamañhi pubbai aitadahausi – ahau vata añ‍ñaipi sattā itthattaṃ āgacchaiyyunti; iti mama ca manaupaṇidhi| imai ca sattā itthattaṃ āgatāti|

‘yaipi tai sattā pacchā upapan‍nā, taisampi aivaṃ hauti – ayaṃ khau bhavaṃ brahmā mahābrahmā abhibhū anabhibhūtau añ‍ñadatthudasau vasavattī issarau kattā nimmātā saiṭṭhau sajitā vasī pitā bhūtabhabyānaṃ; iminā mayaṃ bhautā brahmunā nimmitā| taṃ kissa haitu? imañhi mayaṃ addasāma idha paṭhamaṃ upapan‍naṃ; mayaṃ panāmha pacchā upapan‍nāti|

40. ‘tatrāvusau, yau sau sattau paṭhamaṃ upapan‍nau, sau dīghāyukatarau ca hauti vaṇṇavantatarau ca mahaisakkhatarau ca| yai pana tai sattā pacchā upapan‍nā, tai appāyukatarā ca haunti dubbaṇṇatarā ca appaisakkhatarā ca|

‘ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, āvusau, vij‍jati, yaṃ añ‍ñatarau sattau tamhā kāyā cavitvā itthattaṃ āgacchati| itthattaṃ āgatau samānau agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajati| agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau samānau  ātappamanvāya padhānamanvāya  anuyaugamanvāya appamādamanvāya sammāmanasikāramanvāya tathārūpaṃ caitausamādhiṃ phusati, yathāsamāhitai cittai taṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati; tatau paraṃ nānussarati|

‘sau  aivamāha – yau khau sau bhavaṃ brahmā mahābrahmā abhibhū anabhibhūtau añ‍ñadatthudasau vasavattī issarau kattā nimmātā saiṭṭhau sajitā vasī pitā bhūtabhabyānaṃ, yaina mayaṃ bhautā brahmunā nimmitā| sau nic‍cau dhuvau sassatau dīghāyukau (syā॰ ka॰) sassatau avipariṇāmadhammau sassatisamaṃ tathaiva ṭhassati| yai pana mayaṃ ahumhā taina bhautā brahmunā nimmitā, tai mayaṃ anic‍cā addhuvā addhuvā asassatā (syā॰ ka॰) appāyukā cavanadhammā itthattaṃ āgatā’ti| aivaṃvihitakaṃ nau tumhai āyasmantau issarakuttaṃ brahmakuttaṃ ācariyakaṃ aggañ‍ñaṃ pañ‍ñapaithāti| ‘tai aivamāhaṃsu – aivaṃ khau nau, āvusau gautama, sutaṃ, yathaivāyasmā gautamau āhā’ti| ‘‘aggañ‍ñañ‍cāhaṃ, bhaggava, pajānāmi| tañ‍ca pajānāmi, tatau ca uttaritaraṃ pajānāmi, tañ‍ca pajānaṃ na parāmasāmi, aparāmasatau ca mai pac‍cattañ‍ñaiva nibbuti viditā| yadabhijānaṃ tathāgatau nau anayaṃ āpaj‍jati|

41. ‘‘santi, bhaggava, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā khiḍḍāpadausikaṃ ācariyakaṃ aggañ‍ñaṃ pañ‍ñapainti| tyāhaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘sac‍caṃ kira tumhai āyasmantau khiḍḍāpadausikaṃ ācariyakaṃ aggañ‍ñaṃ pañ‍ñapaithā’ti? tai ca mai aivaṃ puṭṭhā ‘āmau’ti paṭijānanti| tyāhaṃ  aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘kathaṃvihitakaṃ pana tumhai āyasmantau khiḍḍāpadausikaṃ ācariyakaṃ aggañ‍ñaṃ pañ‍ñapaithā’ti? tai mayā puṭṭhā na sampāyanti, asampāyantā mamañ‍ñaiva paṭipucchanti, taisāhaṃ puṭṭhau byākaraumi  –

42. ‘santāvusau, khiḍḍāpadausikā nāma daivā| tai ativailaṃ hassakhiḍḍāratidhammasamāpan‍nā hasakhiḍḍāratidhammasamāpan‍nā (ka॰) viharanti| taisaṃ ativailaṃ hassakhiḍḍāratidhammasamāpan‍nānaṃ viharataṃ sati sammussati, satiyā sammausā satiyā sammausāya (syā॰) tai daivā tamhā kāyā cavanti|

‘ṭhānaṃ  khau panaitaṃ, āvusau, vij‍jati, yaṃ añ‍ñatarau sattau tamhā kāyā cavitvā itthattaṃ āgacchati, itthattaṃ āgatau samānau agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajati, agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau samānau ātappamanvāya padhānamanvāya anuyaugamanvāya appamādamanvāya sammāmanasikāramanvāya tathārūpaṃ caitausamādhiṃ phusati, yathāsamāhitai cittai taṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati; tatau paraṃ nānussarati|

‘sau aivamāha – yai khau tai bhauntau daivā na khiḍḍāpadausikā tai na ativailaṃ hassakhiḍḍāratidhammasamāpan‍nā viharanti| taisaṃ nātivailaṃ hassakhiḍḍāratidhammasamāpan‍nānaṃ viharataṃ sati na sammussati, satiyā asammausā tai daivā tamhā kāyā na cavanti, nic‍cā dhuvā sassatā  avipariṇāmadhammā sassatisamaṃ tathaiva ṭhassanti| yai pana mayaṃ ahumhā khiḍḍāpadausikā tai mayaṃ ativailaṃ hassakhiḍḍāratidhammasamāpan‍nā viharimhā, taisaṃ nau ativailaṃ hassakhiḍḍāratidhammasamāpan‍nānaṃ viharataṃ sati sammussati, satiyā sammausā aivaṃ sammausā aiva (sī॰ pī॰) sammausā tai (syā॰ ka॰) mayaṃ tamhā kāyā cutā, anic‍cā addhuvā appāyukā cavanadhammā itthattaṃ āgatāti| aivaṃvihitakaṃ nau  tumhai āyasmantau khiḍḍāpadausikaṃ ācariyakaṃ aggañ‍ñaṃ pañ‍ñapaithā’ti| ‘tai  aivamāhaṃsu – aivaṃ khau nau, āvusau gautama, sutaṃ, yathaivāyasmā gautamau āhā’ti| ‘‘aggañ‍ñañ‍cāhaṃ, bhaggava, pajānāmi…pai॰… yadabhijānaṃ tathāgatau nau anayaṃ āpaj‍jati|

43. ‘‘santi, bhaggava, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā manaupadausikaṃ ācariyakaṃ aggañ‍ñaṃ pañ‍ñapainti| tyāhaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘sac‍caṃ kira tumhai āyasmantau manaupadausikaṃ ācariyakaṃ aggañ‍ñaṃ pañ‍ñapaithā’ti? tai ca mai aivaṃ puṭṭhā ‘āmau’ti paṭijānanti| tyāhaṃ aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘kathaṃvihitakaṃ pana tumhai āyasmantau manaupadausikaṃ ācariyakaṃ aggañ‍ñaṃ pañ‍ñapaithā’ti? tai mayā puṭṭhā na sampāyanti, asampāyantā mamañ‍ñaiva paṭipucchanti| taisāhaṃ puṭṭhau byākaraumi –

44. ‘santāvusau, manaupadausikā nāma daivā| tai ativailaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ upanijjhāyanti| tai ativailaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ upanijjhāyantā añ‍ñamañ‍ñamhi cittāni  padūsainti| tai añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ paduṭṭhacittā kilantakāyā kilantacittā| tai daivā tamhā kāyā cavanti|

‘ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, āvusau, vij‍jati, yaṃ añ‍ñatarau sattau tamhā kāyā cavitvā itthattaṃ āgacchati| itthattaṃ āgatau samānau agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajati| agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau samānau ātappamanvāya padhānamanvāya anuyaugamanvāya appamādamanvāya sammāmanasikāramanvāya tathārūpaṃ caitausamādhiṃ phusati, yathāsamāhitai cittai taṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, tatau paraṃ nānussarati|

‘sau aivamāha – yai khau tai bhauntau daivā na manaupadausikā tai nātivailaṃ  añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ upanijjhāyanti| tai nātivailaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ upanijjhāyantā añ‍ñamañ‍ñamhi cittāni nappadūsainti| tai añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ appaduṭṭhacittā akilantakāyā akilantacittā| tai daivā tamhā  akilantacittā tamhā (ka॰) kāyā  na cavanti, nic‍cā dhuvā sassatā avipariṇāmadhammā sassatisamaṃ tathaiva ṭhassanti| yai pana mayaṃ ahumhā manaupadausikā, tai mayaṃ ativailaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ upanijjhāyimhā| tai mayaṃ ativailaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ upanijjhāyantā añ‍ñamañ‍ñamhi cittāni padūsimhā padausiyimhā (syā॰), padūsayimhā (?)| tai mayaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ paduṭṭhacittā kilantakāyā kilantacittā| aivaṃ mayaṃ kilantacittāaiva mayaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), kilantacittā (ka॰) tamhā kāyā cutā, anic‍cā addhuvā appāyukā cavanadhammā itthattaṃ āgatāti| aivaṃvihitakaṃ nau tumhai āyasmantau manaupadausikaṃ ācariyakaṃ aggañ‍ñaṃ pañ‍ñapaithā’ti| ‘tai aivamāhaṃsu – aivaṃ khau nau, āvusau gautama, sutaṃ, yathaivāyasmā gautamau āhā’ti| ‘‘aggañ‍ñañ‍cāhaṃ, bhaggava, pajānāmi…pai॰… yadabhijānaṃ tathāgatau nau anayaṃ āpaj‍jati|

45. ‘‘santi, bhaggava, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ ācariyakaṃ aggañ‍ñaṃ pañ‍ñapainti| tyāhaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘sac‍caṃ kira tumhai āyasmantau adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ ācariyakaṃ aggañ‍ñaṃ pañ‍ñapaithā’ti? tai ca mai aivaṃ puṭṭhā ‘āmau’ti paṭijānanti| tyāhaṃ aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘kathaṃvihitakaṃ pana tumhai āyasmantau adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ ācariyakaṃ aggañ‍ñaṃ pañ‍ñapaithā’ti? tai  mayā puṭṭhā na  sampāyanti, asampāyantā mamañ‍ñaiva paṭipucchanti| taisāhaṃ puṭṭhau byākaraumi –

46. ‘santāvusau, asañ‍ñasattā nāma daivā| sañ‍ñuppādā ca pana tai daivā tamhā kāyā cavanti|

‘ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, āvusau, vij‍jati| yaṃ añ‍ñatarau sattau tamhā kāyā cavitvā itthattaṃ āgacchati| itthattaṃ āgatau samānau agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajati| agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau samānau ātappamanvāya padhānamanvāya anuyaugamanvāya appamādamanvāya sammāmanasikāramanvāya tathārūpaṃ caitausamādhiṃ phusati, yathāsamāhitai cittai taṃ idaṃ padaṃ brahmajālasuttai na dissati| aivaṃ (pī॰ ka॰) sañ‍ñuppādaṃ anussarati, tatau paraṃ nānussarati|

‘sau aivamāha – adhic‍casamuppan‍nau attā ca laukau ca| taṃ kissa haitu? ahañhi  pubbai nāhausiṃ, saumhi aitarahi ahutvā santatāya sattakāya (sī॰ pī॰), sattāya (ka॰ sī॰) pariṇatauti| aivaṃvihitakaṃ nau tumhai āyasmantau adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ ācariyakaṃ aggañ‍ñaṃ pañ‍ñapaithā’ti? ‘tai aivamāhaṃsu – aivaṃ khau nau, āvusau gautama, sutaṃ yathaivāyasmā gautamau āhā’ti| ‘‘aggañ‍ñañ‍cāhaṃ, bhaggava, pajānāmi tañ‍ca  pajānāmi, tatau ca uttaritaraṃ pajānāmi, tañ‍ca pajānaṃ na parāmasāmi, aparāmasatau ca mai pac‍cattañ‍ñaiva nibbuti viditā| yadabhijānaṃ tathāgatau nau anayaṃ āpaj‍jati|

47. ‘‘aivaṃvādiṃ khau maṃ, bhaggava, aivamakkhāyiṃ aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā asatā tucchā musā abhūtaina abbhācikkhanti – ‘viparītau samaṇau gautamau bhikkhavau ca| samaṇau gautamau aivamāha – yasmiṃ samayai subhaṃ vimaukkhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, sabbaṃ tasmiṃ samayai asubhantvaiva asubhantaiva (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) pajānātī’ti sañ‍jānātīti (sī॰ pī॰)| na  khau panāhaṃ, bhaggava, aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘yasmiṃ samayai subhaṃ vimaukkhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, sabbaṃ tasmiṃ samayai asubhantvaiva pajānātī’ti| aivañ‍ca khvāhaṃ, bhaggava, vadāmi – ‘yasmiṃ samayai subhaṃ vimaukkhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, subhantvaiva tasmiṃ samayai pajānātī’ti|

‘‘tai  ca, bhantai, viparītā, yai bhagavantaṃ viparītatau dahanti bhikkhavau ca| aivaṃpasan‍nau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavati| pahauti  mai bhagavā tathā dhammaṃ daisaituṃ, yathā ahaṃ subhaṃ vimaukkhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya’’nti|

48. ‘‘duk‍karaṃ khau aitaṃ, bhaggava, tayā añ‍ñadiṭṭhikaina añ‍ñakhantikaina añ‍ñarucikaina añ‍ñatrāyaugaina añ‍ñatrācariyakaina subhaṃ vimaukkhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharituṃ| iṅgha tvaṃ, bhaggava, yau ca tai ayaṃ mayi pasādau, tamaiva tvaṃ sādhukamanurakkhā’’ti| ‘‘sacai taṃ, bhantai, mayā duk‍karaṃ añ‍ñadiṭṭhikaina añ‍ñakhantikaina añ‍ñarucikaina añ‍ñatrāyaugaina añ‍ñatrācariyakaina subhaṃ vimaukkhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharituṃ| yau ca mai ayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavati pasādau, tamaivāhaṃ sādhukamanurakkhissāmī’’ti| idamavauca bhagavā| attamanau bhaggavagauttau paribbājakau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandīti|

pāthikasuttaṃ pāṭikasuttantaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) niṭṭhitaṃ paṭhamaṃ|



2. udumbarikasuttaṃ

nigraudhaparibbājakavatthu

49. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati gijjhakūṭai pabbatai| taina khau pana samayaina nigraudhau paribbājakau udumbarikāya paribbājakārāmai paṭivasati mahatiyā paribbājakaparisāya saddhiṃ tiṃsamattaihi paribbājakasataihi| atha khau sandhānau gahapati divā divassa divādivassaiva (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) rājagahā nikkhami bhagavantaṃ dassanāya| atha khau sandhānassa gahapatissa aitadahausi – ‘‘akālau khau bhagavantaṃ dassanāya| paṭisal‍līnau bhagavā| manaubhāvanīyānampi bhikkhūnaṃ asamayau dassanāya| paṭisal‍līnā manaubhāvanīyā bhikkhū| yaṃnūnāhaṃ yaina udumbarikāya paribbājakārāmau, yaina nigraudhau paribbājakau tainupasaṅkamaiyya’’nti| atha khau sandhānau gahapati yaina udumbarikāya paribbājakārāmau, tainupasaṅkami|

50. taina khau pana samayaina nigraudhau paribbājakau mahatiyā paribbājakaparisāya saddhiṃ nisin‍nau hauti un‍nādiniyā uc‍cāsaddamahāsaddāya anaikavihitaṃ tiracchānakathaṃ kathaintiyā| saiyyathidaṃ – rājakathaṃ caurakathaṃ  mahāmattakathaṃ sainākathaṃ bhayakathaṃ yuddhakathaṃ an‍nakathaṃ pānakathaṃ vatthakathaṃ sayanakathaṃ mālākathaṃ gandhakathaṃ ñātikathaṃ yānakathaṃ gāmakathaṃ nigamakathaṃ nagarakathaṃ janapadakathaṃ itthikathaṃ sūrakathaṃ visikhākathaṃ kumbhaṭṭhānakathaṃ pubbapaitakathaṃ nānattakathaṃ laukakkhāyikaṃ samuddakkhāyikaṃ itibhavābhavakathaṃ  iti vā|

51. addasā khau nigraudhau paribbājakau sandhānaṃ gahapatiṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvā sakaṃ parisaṃ saṇṭhāpaisi – ‘‘appasaddā bhauntau hauntu, mā bhauntau saddamakattha| ayaṃ samaṇassa gautamassa sāvakau āgacchati sandhānau gahapati| yāvatā khau pana samaṇassa gautamassa sāvakā gihī audātavasanā rājagahai paṭivasanti, ayaṃ taisaṃ añ‍ñatarau sandhānau gahapati| appasaddakāmā  khau panaitai āyasmantau appasaddavinītā, appasaddassa vaṇṇavādinau| appaiva nāma appasaddaṃ parisaṃ viditvā upasaṅkamitabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyyā’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai tai paribbājakā tuṇhī ahaisuṃ|

52. atha khau sandhānau gahapati yaina nigraudhau paribbājakau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā nigraudhaina paribbājakaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sandhānau gahapati nigraudhaṃ paribbājakaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘añ‍ñathā khau imai bhauntau añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā saṅgamma samāgamma un‍nādinau uc‍cāsaddamahāsaddā anaikavihitaṃ  tiracchānakathaṃ anuyuttā viharanti| saiyyathidaṃ – rājakathaṃ…pai॰… itibhavābhavakathaṃ iti vā| añ‍ñathā khau ca (sī॰ pī॰) pana sau bhagavā arañ‍ñavanapatthāni pantāni saināsanāni paṭisaivati appasaddāni appanigghausāni vijanavātāni manussarāhassaiyyakāni paṭisal‍lānasāruppānī’’ti|

53. aivaṃ vuttai nigraudhau paribbājakau sandhānaṃ gahapatiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yagghai  gahapati, jānaiyyāsi, kaina samaṇau gautamau saddhiṃ sal‍lapati, kaina sākacchaṃ samāpaj‍jati, kaina pañ‍ñāvaiyyattiyaṃ samāpaj‍jati? suñ‍ñāgārahatā samaṇassa gautamassa pañ‍ñā aparisāvacarau samaṇau gautamau nālaṃ sal‍lāpāya| sau antamantānaiva saivati antapantānaiva (syā॰)| saiyyathāpi nāma gaukāṇā pariyantacārinī antamantānaiva saivati| aivamaiva suñ‍ñāgārahatā samaṇassa gautamassa pañ‍ñā; aparisāvacarau samaṇau gautamau; nālaṃ sal‍lāpāya| sau antamantānaiva saivati| iṅgha, gahapati, samaṇau gautamau imaṃ parisaṃ āgacchaiyya, aikapañhainaiva naṃ saṃsādaiyyāma saṃharaiyyāma (ka॰), tucchakumbhīva naṃ mañ‍ñai auraudhaiyyāmā’’ti|

54. assausi khau bhagavā dibbāya sautadhātuyā visuddhāya atik‍kantamānusikāya sandhānassa gahapatissa nigraudhaina paribbājakaina saddhiṃ imaṃ kathāsal‍lāpaṃ| atha khau bhagavā gijjhakūṭā pabbatā aurauhitvā yaina sumāgadhāya  tīrai mauranivāpau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā  sumāgadhāya tīrai mauranivāpai abbhaukāsai caṅkami| addasā khau nigraudhau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ sumāgadhāya tīrai mauranivāpai abbhaukāsai caṅkamantaṃ| disvāna sakaṃ parisaṃ saṇṭhāpaisi – ‘‘appasaddā bhauntau hauntu, mā bhauntau saddamakattha, ayaṃ samaṇau gautamau sumāgadhāya tīrai mauranivāpai abbhaukāsai caṅkamati| appasaddakāmau khau pana sau āyasmā, appasaddassa vaṇṇavādī| appaiva  nāma appasaddaṃ parisaṃ viditvā upasaṅkamitabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyya| sacai samaṇau gautamau  imaṃ parisaṃ āgacchaiyya, imaṃ taṃ pañhaṃ pucchaiyyāma – ‘kau nāma sau, bhantai, bhagavatau dhammau, yaina bhagavā sāvakai vinaiti, yaina bhagavatā sāvakā vinītā assāsappattā paṭijānanti ajjhāsayaṃ ādibrahmacariya’nti? aivaṃ vuttai tai paribbājakā tuṇhī ahaisuṃ|

tapaujigucchāvādau

55. atha khau bhagavā yaina nigraudhau paribbājakau tainupasaṅkami| atha khau nigraudhau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aitu khau, bhantai, bhagavā, svāgataṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau| cirassaṃ khau, bhantai, bhagavā imaṃ pariyāyamakāsi yadidaṃ idhāgamanāya| nisīdatu, bhantai, bhagavā, idamāsanaṃ pañ‍ñatta’’nti| nisīdi bhagavā pañ‍ñattai āsanai| nigraudhaupi khau paribbājakau añ‍ñataraṃ nīcāsanaṃ gahaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau nigraudhaṃ paribbājakaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘kāya nuttha, nigraudha, aitarahi kathāya san‍nisin‍nā, kā ca pana vau antarākathā vippakatā’’ti? aivaṃ vuttai, nigraudhau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca, ‘‘idha mayaṃ, bhantai, addasāma bhagavantaṃ sumāgadhāya tīrai mauranivāpai abbhaukāsai caṅkamantaṃ, disvāna aivaṃ  avaucumhā – ‘sacai samaṇau gautamau imaṃ parisaṃ āgacchaiyya, imaṃ taṃ pañhaṃ pucchaiyyāma – kau nāma sau, bhantai, bhagavatau dhammau, yaina bhagavā sāvakai vinaiti, yaina bhagavatā sāvakā vinītā assāsappattā  paṭijānanti ajjhāsayaṃ ādibrahmacariya’nti? ayaṃ khau nau, bhantai, antarākathā vippakatā; atha bhagavā anuppattau’’ti|

56. ‘‘duj‍jānaṃ khau aitaṃ, nigraudha, tayā añ‍ñadiṭṭhikaina añ‍ñakhantikaina añ‍ñarucikaina añ‍ñatrāyaugaina añ‍ñatrācariyakaina, yaināhaṃ sāvakai vinaimi, yaina mayā sāvakā vinītā assāsappattā paṭijānanti ajjhāsayaṃ ādibrahmacariyaṃ| iṅgha tvaṃ maṃ, nigraudha, sakai ācariyakai adhijaigucchai pañhaṃ puccha – ‘kathaṃ santā nu khau, bhantai, tapaujigucchā paripuṇṇā hauti, kathaṃ aparipuṇṇā’ti? aivaṃ vuttai tai paribbājakā un‍nādinau uc‍cāsaddamahāsaddā ahaisuṃ – ‘‘acchariyaṃ vata bhau, abbhutaṃ vata bhau, samaṇassa gautamassa mahiddhikatā mahānubhāvatā, yatra hi nāma sakavādaṃ ṭhapaissati, paravādaina pavāraissatī’’ti|

57. atha khau nigraudhau paribbājakau tai paribbājakai appasaddai katvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mayaṃ khau, bhantai, tapaujigucchāvādā taraujigucchaṃsāraudā (ka॰) tapaujigucchāsārā tapaujigucchāal‍līnā viharāma| kathaṃ santā nu khau, bhantai, tapaujigucchā paripuṇṇā hauti, kathaṃ aparipuṇṇā’’ti?

‘‘idha, nigraudha, tapassī acailakau hauti muttācārau, hatthāpalaikhanau hatthāvalaikhanau (syā॰), na aihibhaddantikau, na tiṭṭhabhaddantikau, nābhihaṭaṃ, na uddissakataṃ, na nimantanaṃ sādiyati, sau na kumbhimukhā paṭiggaṇhāti, na kaḷaupimukhā paṭiggaṇhāti, na aiḷakamantaraṃ, na daṇḍamantaraṃ, na musalamantaraṃ, na dvin‍naṃ bhuñ‍jamānānaṃ, na gabbhiniyā, na pāyamānāya, na  purisantaragatāya, na saṅkittīsu, na yattha sā upaṭṭhitau hauti, na yattha makkhikā saṇḍasaṇḍacārinī, na macchaṃ, na maṃsaṃ, na suraṃ, na mairayaṃ, na thusaudakaṃ pivati, sau aikāgārikau vā hauti aikālaupikau, dvāgārikau vā hauti dvālaupikau, sattāgārikau vā hauti sattālaupikau, aikissāpi dattiyā yāpaiti, dvīhipi dattīhi yāpaiti, sattahipi dattīhi yāpaiti; aikāhikampi āhāraṃ āhāraiti, dvīhikampi dvāhikaṃpi (sī॰ syā॰) āhāraṃ āhāraiti, sattāhikampi āhāraṃ āhāraiti, iti aivarūpaṃ addhamāsikampi pariyāyabhattabhaujanānuyaugamanuyuttau viharati| sau sākabhakkhau vā hauti, sāmākabhakkhau vā hauti, nīvārabhakkhau vā hauti, daddulabhakkhau vā hauti, haṭabhakkhau vā hauti, kaṇabhakkhau vā hauti, ācāmabhakkhau vā hauti, piñ‍ñākabhakkhau vā hauti, tiṇabhakkhau vā hauti, gaumayabhakkhau vā hauti; vanamūlaphalāhārau yāpaiti pavattaphalabhaujī| sau sāṇānipi dhāraiti, masāṇānipi dhāraiti, chavadussānipi dhāraiti, paṃsukūlānipi dhāraiti, tirīṭānipi dhāraiti, ajinampi dhāraiti, ajinakkhipampi dhāraiti, kusacīrampi dhāraiti, vākacīrampi dhāraiti, phalakacīrampi dhāraiti, kaisakambalampi dhāraiti, vāḷakambalampi dhāraiti, ulūkapakkhampi dhāraiti, kaisamassulaucakaupi hauti kaisamassulaucanānuyaugamanuyuttau, ubbhaṭṭhakaupi ubhaṭṭhakaupi (syā॰), ubbhaṭṭhikaupi (ka॰) hauti āsanapaṭikkhittau, uk‍kuṭikaupi hauti uk‍kuṭikappadhānamanuyuttau, kaṇṭakāpassayikaupi hauti kaṇṭakāpassayai saiyyaṃ kappaiti, phalakasaiyyampi kappaiti, thaṇḍilasaiyyampi kappaiti, aikapassayikaupi hauti rajaujal‍ladharau, abbhaukāsikaupi  hauti yathāsanthatikau, vaikaṭikaupi hauti vikaṭabhaujanānuyaugamanuyuttau, apānakaupi hauti apānakattamanuyuttau, sāyatatiyakampi udakaurauhanānuyaugamanuyuttau viharati| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, nigraudha, yadi aivaṃ santai tapaujigucchā paripuṇṇā vā hauti aparipuṇṇā vā’’ti? ‘‘addhā khau, bhantai, aivaṃ santai tapaujigucchā paripuṇṇā hauti, nau aparipuṇṇā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ paripuṇṇāyapi khau ahaṃ, nigraudha, tapaujigucchāya anaikavihitai upak‍kilaisai vadāmī’’ti|

upak‍kilaisau

58. ‘‘yathā  kathaṃ pana, bhantai, bhagavā aivaṃ paripuṇṇāya tapaujigucchāya anaikavihitai upak‍kilaisai vadatī’’ti? ‘‘idha, nigraudha, tapassī tapaṃ samādiyati, sau taina tapasā attamanau hauti paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| yampi, nigraudha, tapassī tapaṃ samādiyati, sau taina tapasā attamanau hauti paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| ayampi khau, nigraudha, tapassinau upak‍kilaisau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, nigraudha, tapassī tapaṃ samādiyati, sau taina tapasā attānuk‍kaṃsaiti paraṃ vambhaiti| yampi, nigraudha, tapassī tapaṃ samādiyati, sau taina tapasā attānuk‍kaṃsaiti paraṃ vambhaiti| ayampi khau, nigraudha, tapassinau upak‍kilaisau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, nigraudha, tapassī tapaṃ samādiyati, sau taina tapasā maj‍jati mucchati pamādamāpaj‍jati madamāpaj‍jati (syā॰)| yampi, nigraudha, tapassī tapaṃ samādiyati, sau  taina tapasā maj‍jati mucchati  pamādamāpaj‍jati| ayampi khau, nigraudha, tapassinau upak‍kilaisau hauti|

59. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, nigraudha, tapassī tapaṃ samādiyati, sau taina tapasā lābhasak‍kārasilaukaṃ  abhinibbattaiti, sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina attamanau hauti paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| yampi, nigraudha, tapassī tapaṃ samādiyati, sau taina tapasā lābhasak‍kārasilaukaṃ abhinibbattaiti, sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina attamanau hauti paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| ayampi khau, nigraudha, tapassinau upak‍kilaisau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, nigraudha, tapassī tapaṃ samādiyati, sau taina tapasā lābhasak‍kārasilaukaṃ abhinibbattaiti, sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina attānuk‍kaṃsaiti paraṃ vambhaiti| yampi, nigraudha, tapassī tapaṃ samādiyati, sau taina tapasā lābhasak‍kārasilaukaṃ abhinibbattaiti, sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina attānuk‍kaṃsaiti paraṃ vambhaiti| ayampi khau, nigraudha, tapassinau upak‍kilaisau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, nigraudha, tapassī tapaṃ samādiyati, sau taina tapasā lābhasak‍kārasilaukaṃ abhinibbattaiti, sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina maj‍jati mucchati pamādamāpaj‍jati| yampi, nigraudha, tapassī tapaṃ samādiyati, sau taina tapasā lābhasak‍kārasilaukaṃ abhinibbattaiti, sau taina  lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina maj‍jati mucchati pamādamāpaj‍jati| ayampi khau, nigraudha, tapassinau upak‍kilaisau hauti|

60. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, nigraudha, tapassī bhaujanaisu vaudāsaṃ āpaj‍jati – ‘idaṃ mai khamati, idaṃ mai nakkhamatī’ti| sau yañ‍ca yaṃ hi (sī॰ pī॰) khvassa nakkhamati, taṃ sāpaikkhau pajahati| yaṃ panassa khamati, taṃ gadhitau gathitau (sī॰ pī॰) mucchitau ajjhāpan‍nau anādīnavadassāvī anissaraṇapañ‍ñau paribhuñ‍jati…pai॰… ayampi khau, nigraudha, tapassinau upak‍kilaisau hauti|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, nigraudha, tapassī tapaṃ samādiyati lābhasak‍kārasilaukanikantihaitu – ‘sak‍karissanti maṃ rājānau rājamahāmattā khattiyā brāhmaṇā  gahapatikā titthiyā’ti…pai॰… ayampi khau, nigraudha, tapassinau upak‍kilaisau hauti|

61. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, nigraudha, tapassī añ‍ñataraṃ samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā apasādaitā apasāraitā (ka॰) hauti – ‘kiṃ panāyaṃ sambahulājīvau bahulājīvau (sī॰ pī॰) sabbaṃ saṃbhakkhaiti| saiyyathidaṃ – mūlabījaṃ khandhabījaṃ phaḷubījaṃ aggabījaṃ bījabījamaiva pañ‍camaṃ, asanivicak‍kaṃ dantakūṭaṃ, samaṇappavādainā’ti…pai॰… ayampi khau, nigraudha, tapassinau upak‍kilaisau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, nigraudha, tapassī passati añ‍ñataraṃ samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā kulaisu sak‍kariyamānaṃ garukariyamānaṃ māniyamānaṃ pūjiyamānaṃ| disvā tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘imañhi nāma sambahulājīvaṃ kulaisu sak‍karaunti garuṃ karaunti mānainti pūjainti| maṃ pana tapassiṃ lūkhājīviṃ kulaisu na sak‍karaunti na garuṃ karaunti na mānainti na pūjaintī’ti, iti sau issāmacchariyaṃ kulaisu uppādaitā hauti…pai॰… ayampi khau, nigraudha, tapassinau upak‍kilaisau hauti|

62. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, nigraudha, tapassī āpāthakanisādī hauti…pai॰… ayampi khau, nigraudha, tapassinau upak‍kilaisau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, nigraudha, tapassī attānaṃ adassayamānau kulaisu carati – ‘idampi mai tapasmiṃ idampi mai tapasmi’nti…pai॰… ayampi khau, nigraudha, tapassinau upak‍kilaisau hauti|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, nigraudha, tapassī kiñ‍cidaiva paṭicchan‍naṃ saivati| sau ‘khamati tai ida’nti puṭṭhau samānau akkhamamānaṃ āha – ‘khamatī’ti| khamamānaṃ  āha – ‘nakkhamatī’ti| iti sau sampajānamusā bhāsitā hauti…pai॰… ayampi khau, nigraudha, tapassinau upak‍kilaisau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, nigraudha, tapassī tathāgatassa vā tathāgatasāvakassa vā dhammaṃ daisaintassa santaṃyaiva pariyāyaṃ anuñ‍ñaiyyaṃ nānujānāti…pai॰… ayampi khau, nigraudha, tapassinau upak‍kilaisau hauti|

63. ‘‘puna  caparaṃ, nigraudha, tapassī kaudhanau hauti upanāhī| yampi, nigraudha, tapassī kaudhanau hauti upanāhī| ayampi khau, nigraudha, tapassinau upak‍kilaisau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, nigraudha, tapassī makkhī hauti paḷāsī palāsī (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) …pai॰… issukī hauti maccharī… saṭhau hauti māyāvī… thaddhau hauti atimānī… pāpicchau hauti pāpikānaṃ icchānaṃ vasaṃ gatau… micchādiṭṭhikau hauti antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā saman‍nāgatau… sandiṭṭhiparāmāsī hauti ādhānaggāhī duppaṭinissaggī| yampi, nigraudha, tapassī sandiṭṭhiparāmāsī hauti ādhānaggāhī duppaṭinissaggī| ayampi khau, nigraudha, tapassinau upak‍kilaisau hauti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, nigraudha, yadimai tapaujigucchā tapaujigucchāya (?) upak‍kilaisā vā anupak‍kilaisā vā’’ti? ‘‘addhā khau imai, bhantai, tapaujigucchā tapaujigucchāya (?) upak‍kilaisā upak‍kilaisā hauti (ka॰), nau anupak‍kilaisā| ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, bhantai, vij‍jati yaṃ idhaikac‍cau tapassī sabbaihaiva imaihi upak‍kilaisaihi saman‍nāgatau assa; kau pana vādau añ‍ñatarañ‍ñatarainā’’ti|

parisuddhapapaṭikappattakathā

64. ‘‘idha, nigraudha, tapassī tapaṃ samādiyati, sau taina tapasā na attamanau hauti na paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| yampi, nigraudha, tapassī tapaṃ  samādiyati, sau taina tapasā na attamanau  hauti na paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| aivaṃ sau tasmiṃ ṭhānai parisuddhau hauti|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, nigraudha, tapassī tapaṃ samādiyati, sau taina tapasā na attānuk‍kaṃsaiti na paraṃ vambhaiti…pai॰… aivaṃ sau tasmiṃ ṭhānai parisuddhau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, nigraudha, tapassī tapaṃ samādiyati, sau taina tapasā na maj‍jati na mucchati na pamādamāpaj‍jati…pai॰… aivaṃ sau tasmiṃ ṭhānai parisuddhau hauti|

65. ‘‘puna  caparaṃ, nigraudha, tapassī tapaṃ samādiyati, sau taina tapasā lābhasak‍kārasilaukaṃ abhinibbattaiti, sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina na attamanau hauti na paripuṇṇasaṅkappau…pai॰… aivaṃ sau tasmiṃ ṭhānai parisuddhau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, nigraudha, tapassī tapaṃ samādiyati, sau taina tapasā lābhasak‍kārasilaukaṃ abhinibbattaiti, sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina na attānuk‍kaṃsaiti na paraṃ vambhaiti…pai॰… aivaṃ sau tasmiṃ ṭhānai parisuddhau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, nigraudha, tapassī tapaṃ samādiyati, sau taina tapasā lābhasak‍kārasilaukaṃ abhinibbattaiti, sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina na maj‍jati na mucchati na pamādamāpaj‍jati…pai॰… aivaṃ sau tasmiṃ ṭhānai parisuddhau hauti|

66. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, nigraudha, tapassī bhaujanaisu na vaudāsaṃ āpaj‍jati – ‘idaṃ mai khamati, idaṃ mai nakkhamatī’ti| sau yañ‍ca khvassa nakkhamati, taṃ anapaikkhau pajahati| yaṃ panassa khamati, taṃ agadhitau amucchitau anajjhāpan‍nau ādīnavadassāvī nissaraṇapañ‍ñau paribhuñ‍jati…pai॰… aivaṃ sau tasmiṃ ṭhānai parisuddhau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, nigraudha, tapassī na tapaṃ samādiyati lābhasak‍kārasilaukanikantihaitu – ‘sak‍karissanti maṃ rājānau rājamahāmattā khattiyā brāhmaṇā gahapatikā titthiyā’ti…pai॰… aivaṃ sau tasmiṃ ṭhānai parisuddhau hauti|

67. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, nigraudha, tapassī añ‍ñataraṃ samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā nāpasādaitā hauti – ‘kiṃ panāyaṃ sambahulājīvau  sabbaṃ saṃbhakkhaiti| saiyyathidaṃ – mūlabījaṃ khandhabījaṃ phaḷubījaṃ  aggabījaṃ bījabījamaiva pañ‍camaṃ, asanivicak‍kaṃ dantakūṭaṃ, samaṇappavādainā’ti…pai॰… aivaṃ sau tasmiṃ ṭhānai parisuddhau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, nigraudha, tapassī passati añ‍ñataraṃ samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā kulaisu sak‍kariyamānaṃ garu kariyamānaṃ māniyamānaṃ pūjiyamānaṃ| disvā tassa na aivaṃ  hauti – ‘imañhi nāma sambahulājīvaṃ kulaisu sak‍karaunti garuṃ karaunti mānainti pūjainti| maṃ pana tapassiṃ lūkhājīviṃ kulaisu na sak‍karaunti na garuṃ karaunti na mānainti na pūjaintī’ti, iti sau issāmacchariyaṃ kulaisu nuppādaitā hauti…pai॰… aivaṃ sau tasmiṃ ṭhānai parisuddhau hauti|

68. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, nigraudha, tapassī na āpāthakanisādī hauti…pai॰… aivaṃ sau tasmiṃ ṭhānai parisuddhau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, nigraudha, tapassī na attānaṃ adassayamānau kulaisu carati – ‘idampi mai tapasmiṃ, idampi mai tapasmi’nti…pai॰… aivaṃ sau tasmiṃ ṭhānai parisuddhau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, nigraudha, tapassī na  kañ‍cidaiva paṭicchan‍naṃ saivati, sau – ‘khamati tai ida’nti puṭṭhau samānau akkhamamānaṃ āha – ‘nakkhamatī’ti| khamamānaṃ āha – ‘khamatī’ti| iti sau sampajānamusā na bhāsitā hauti…pai॰… aivaṃ sau tasmiṃ ṭhānai parisuddhau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, nigraudha, tapassī tathāgatassa vā tathāgatasāvakassa vā dhammaṃ daisaintassa santaṃyaiva pariyāyaṃ anuñ‍ñaiyyaṃ anujānāti…pai॰… aivaṃ sau tasmiṃ ṭhānai parisuddhau hauti|

69. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, nigraudha, tapassī ak‍kaudhanau hauti anupanāhī| yampi, nigraudha, tapassī ak‍kaudhanau hauti anupanāhī aivaṃ sau tasmiṃ ṭhānai parisuddhau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, nigraudha, tapassī amakkhī hauti apaḷāsī…pai॰… anissukī hauti amaccharī… asaṭhau hauti amāyāvī… atthaddhau hauti anatimānī…  na pāpicchau hauti na pāpikānaṃ icchānaṃ vasaṃ gatau… na micchādiṭṭhikau hauti na antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā saman‍nāgatau… na sandiṭṭhiparāmāsī hauti na ādhānaggāhī suppaṭinissaggī| yampi, nigraudha, tapassī  na sandiṭṭhiparāmāsī hauti na ādhānaggāhī suppaṭinissaggī| aivaṃ sau tasmiṃ ṭhānai parisuddhau hauti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, nigraudha, yadi aivaṃ santai tapaujigucchā parisuddhā vā hauti aparisuddhā vā’’ti? ‘‘addhā khau, bhantai, aivaṃ santai tapaujigucchā parisuddhā  hauti nau aparisuddhā, aggappattā ca sārappattā cā’’ti| ‘‘na khau, nigraudha, aittāvatā tapaujigucchā aggappattā ca hauti sārappattā ca; api ca khau papaṭikappattā pappaṭikapattā (ka॰) hautī’’ti|

parisuddhatacappattakathā

70. ‘‘kittāvatā  pana, bhantai, tapaujigucchā aggappattā ca hauti sārappattā ca? sādhu mai, bhantai, bhagavā tapaujigucchāya aggañ‍ñaiva pāpaitu, sārañ‍ñaiva pāpaitū’’ti| ‘‘idha, nigraudha, tapassī cātuyāmasaṃvarasaṃvutau hauti| kathañ‍ca, nigraudha, tapassī cātuyāmasaṃvarasaṃvutau hauti? idha, nigraudha, tapassī na pāṇaṃ atipātaiti atipāpaiti (ka॰ sī॰ pī॰ ka॰), na pāṇaṃ atipātayati, na pāṇamatipātayatau samanuñ‍ñau hauti| na adin‍naṃ ādiyati, na adin‍naṃ ādiyāpaiti, na adin‍naṃ ādiyatau samanuñ‍ñau hauti| na musā bhaṇati, na musā bhaṇāpaiti, na musā bhaṇatau samanuñ‍ñau hauti| na bhāvitamāsīsati na bhāvitamāsiṃ sati (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), na bhāvitamāsīsāpaiti, na bhāvitamāsīsatau samanuñ‍ñau hauti| aivaṃ khau, nigraudha, tapassī cātuyāmasaṃvarasaṃvutau hauti|

‘‘yatau khau, nigraudha, tapassī cātuyāmasaṃvarasaṃvutau hauti, aduṃ cassa hauti tapassitāya| sau abhiharati nau hīnāyāvattati| sau vivittaṃ saināsanaṃ bhajati arañ‍ñaṃ rukkhamūlaṃ pabbataṃ kandaraṃ giriguhaṃ susānaṃ vanapatthaṃ abbhaukāsaṃ palālapuñ‍jaṃ| sau pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātappaṭik‍kantau nisīdati pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| sau abhijjhaṃ laukai pahāya vigatābhijjhaina caitasā viharati, abhijjhāya cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| byāpādappadausaṃ pahāya abyāpan‍nacittau viharati sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī, byāpādappadausā cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| thinamiddhaṃ thīnamiddhaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) pahāya vigatathinamiddhau viharati ālaukasañ‍ñī satau sampajānau, thinamiddhā cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ  pahāya anuddhatau viharati ajjhattaṃ vūpasantacittau, uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cā cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| vicikicchaṃ pahāya tiṇṇavicikicchau viharati akathaṃkathī kusalaisu dhammaisu, vicikicchāya cittaṃ parisaudhaiti|

71. ‘‘sau  imai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahāya caitasau upak‍kilaisai pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇai maittāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati| tathā dutiyaṃ| tathā tatiyaṃ| tathā catutthaṃ| iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ maittāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina  appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina pharitvā viharati| karuṇāsahagataina caitasā…pai॰… muditāsahagataina caitasā…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati| tathā dutiyaṃ| tathā tatiyaṃ| tathā catutthaṃ| iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina pharitvā viharati|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, nigraudha| yadi aivaṃ santai tapaujigucchā parisuddhā vā hauti aparisuddhā vā’’ti? ‘‘addhā khau, bhantai, aivaṃ santai tapaujigucchā parisuddhā hauti nau aparisuddhā, aggappattā ca sārappattā cā’’ti| ‘‘na khau, nigraudha, aittāvatā tapaujigucchā aggappattā ca hauti sārappattā ca; api ca khau tacappattā hautī’’ti|

parisuddhaphaigguppattakathā

72. ‘‘kittāvatā pana, bhantai, tapaujigucchā aggappattā ca hauti sārappattā ca? sādhu mai, bhantai, bhagavā tapaujigucchāya aggañ‍ñaiva  pāpaitu, sārañ‍ñaiva pāpaitū’’ti| ‘‘idha, nigraudha, tapassī cātuyāmasaṃvarasaṃvutau hauti| kathañ‍ca, nigraudha, tapassī cātuyāmasaṃvarasaṃvutau hauti…pai॰… yatau khau, nigraudha, tapassī cātuyāmasaṃvarasaṃvutau hauti, aduṃ cassa hauti tapassitāya| sau abhiharati nau hīnāyāvattati| sau vivittaṃ saināsanaṃ bhajati…pai॰… sau imai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahāya caitasau upak‍kilaisai pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇai maittāsahagataina caitasā…pai॰… karuṇāsahagataina caitasā…pai॰… muditāsahagataina caitasā…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina pharitvā viharati| sau anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau tissaupi jātiyau catassaupi jātiyau pañ‍capi jātiyau dasapi jātiyau vīsampi jātiyau tiṃsampi jātiyau cattālīsampi  jātiyau pañ‍ñāsampi jātiyau jātisatampi jātisahassampi  jātisatasahassampi anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭakappai anaikaipi vivaṭṭakappai anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭakappai – ‘amutrāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau amutra udapādiṃ, tatrāpāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau  aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau idhūpapan‍nau’ti| iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, nigraudha, yadi aivaṃ santai tapaujigucchā parisuddhā vā hauti aparisuddhā vā’’ti? ‘‘addhā khau, bhantai, aivaṃ santai tapaujigucchā parisuddhā  hauti, nau aparisuddhā, aggappattā ca sārappattā cā’’ti| ‘‘na khau, nigraudha, aittāvatā tapaujigucchā aggappattā ca hauti sārappattā ca; api ca khau phaigguppattā hautī’’ti|

parisuddhaaggappattasārappattakathā

73. ‘‘kittāvatā pana, bhantai, tapaujigucchā aggappattā ca hauti sārappattā ca? sādhu mai, bhantai, bhagavā tapaujigucchāya aggañ‍ñaiva pāpaitu, sārañ‍ñaiva pāpaitū’’ti| ‘‘idha, nigraudha, tapassī cātuyāmasaṃvarasaṃvutau hauti| kathañ‍ca, nigraudha, tapassī cātuyāmasaṃvarasaṃvutau hauti…pai॰… yatau khau, nigraudha, tapassī cātuyāmasaṃvarasaṃvutau hauti, aduṃ cassa hauti tapassitāya| sau abhiharati nau hīnāyāvattati| sau vivittaṃ saināsanaṃ bhajati…pai॰… sau imai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahāya caitasau upak‍kilaisai pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇai maittāsahagataina caitasā…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina pharitvā viharati| sau anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati| saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau tissaupi jātiyau catassaupi jātiyau pañ‍capi jātiyau…pai॰… iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ  anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati| sau dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai sugatai duggatai, yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti – ‘imai vata bhauntau sattā kāyaduc‍caritaina  saman‍nāgatā vacīduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā manauduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ upavādakā micchādiṭṭhikā micchādiṭṭhikammasamādānā| tai kāyassa bhaidā  paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapan‍nā| imai vā pana bhauntau sattā kāyasucaritaina saman‍nāgatā vacīsucaritaina saman‍nāgatā manausucaritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ anupavādakā sammādiṭṭhikā sammādiṭṭhikammasamādānā| tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍nā’ti| iti dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai sugatai duggatai, yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti|

‘‘taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, nigraudha, yadi aivaṃ santai tapaujigucchā parisuddhā vā hauti aparisuddhā vā’’ti? ‘‘addhā khau, bhantai, aivaṃ santai tapaujigucchā parisuddhā hauti nau aparisuddhā, aggappattā ca sārappattā cā’’ti|

74. ‘‘aittāvatā khau, nigraudha, tapaujigucchā aggappattā ca hauti sārappattā ca| iti khau, nigraudha iti nigraudha (syā॰), yaṃ maṃ tvaṃ avacāsi – ‘kau nāma sau, bhantai, bhagavatau dhammau, yaina bhagavā sāvakai vinaiti, yaina bhagavatā sāvakā vinītā assāsappattā paṭijānanti ajjhāsayaṃ ādibrahmacariya’nti| iti khau taṃ, nigraudha, ṭhānaṃ uttaritarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca, yaināhaṃ sāvakai vinaimi, yaina mayā sāvakā vinītā assāsappattā paṭijānanti ajjhāsayaṃ ādibrahmacariya’’nti|

aivaṃ vuttai, tai paribbājakā un‍nādinau uc‍cāsaddamahāsaddā ahaisuṃ – ‘‘aittha mayaṃ anassāma sācariyakā, na mayaṃ itau bhiyyau uttaritaraṃ pajānāmā’’ti|

nigraudhassa pajjhāyanaṃ
