75. yadā  añ‍ñāsi sandhānau gahapati – ‘‘añ‍ñadatthu khau dānimai añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ sussūsanti, sautaṃ audahanti, añ‍ñācittaṃ upaṭṭhāpaintī’’ti| atha atha naṃ (ka॰) nigraudhaṃ paribbājakaṃ aitadavauca  – ‘‘iti khau, bhantai nigraudha, yaṃ maṃ tvaṃ avacāsi – ‘yagghai, gahapati, jānaiyyāsi, kaina samaṇau gautamau saddhiṃ sal‍lapati, kaina sākacchaṃ samāpaj‍jati, kaina pañ‍ñāvaiyyattiyaṃ samāpaj‍jati, suñ‍ñāgārahatā samaṇassa gautamassa pañ‍ñā, aparisāvacarau samaṇau gautamau nālaṃ sal‍lāpāya, sau antamantānaiva saivati; saiyyathāpi nāma gaukāṇā pariyantacārinī antamantānaiva saivati| aivamaiva suñ‍ñāgārahatā samaṇassa gautamassa pañ‍ñā, aparisāvacarau samaṇau gautamau nālaṃ sal‍lāpāya; sau antamantānaiva saivati; iṅgha, gahapati, samaṇau gautamau imaṃ parisaṃ āgacchaiyya, aikapañhainaiva naṃ saṃsādaiyyāma, tucchakumbhīva naṃ mañ‍ñai auraudhaiyyāmā’ti| ayaṃ khau sau, bhantai, bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau idhānuppattau, aparisāvacaraṃ pana naṃ karautha, gaukāṇaṃ pariyantacāriniṃ karautha, aikapañhainaiva naṃ saṃsādaitha, tucchakumbhīva naṃ auraudhaithā’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, nigraudhau paribbājakau tuṇhībhūtau maṅkubhūtau pattakkhandhau adhaumukhau pajjhāyantau  appaṭibhānau nisīdi|

76. atha khau bhagavā nigraudhaṃ paribbājakaṃ tuṇhībhūtaṃ maṅkubhūtaṃ pattakkhandhaṃ adhaumukhaṃ pajjhāyantaṃ appaṭibhānaṃ viditvā nigraudhaṃ paribbājakaṃ  aitadavauca – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, nigraudha, bhāsitā tai aisā vācā’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhantai, bhāsitā mai aisā vācā, yathābālaina yathāmūḷhaina yathāakusalainā’’ti| ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, nigraudha| kinti tai sutaṃ paribbājakānaṃ vuḍḍhānaṃ mahal‍lakānaṃ ācariyapācariyānaṃ bhāsamānānaṃ – ‘yai tai ahaisuṃ atītamaddhānaṃ arahantau sammāsambuddhā, aivaṃ su tai bhagavantau saṃgamma samāgamma un‍nādinau uc‍cāsaddamahāsaddā anaikavihitaṃ tiracchānakathaṃ anuyuttā viharanti| saiyyathidaṃ – rājakathaṃ caurakathaṃ…pai॰… itibhavābhavakathaṃ iti vā| saiyyathāpi tvaṃ aitarahi sācariyakau| udāhu, aivaṃ su tai bhagavantau arañ‍ñavanapatthāni pantāni saināsanāni paṭisaivanti appasaddāni appanigghausāni vijanavātāni manussarāhassaiyyakāni paṭisal‍lānasāruppāni, saiyyathāpāhaṃ aitarahī’ti|

‘‘sutaṃ maitaṃ, bhantai| paribbājakānaṃ vuḍḍhānaṃ mahal‍lakānaṃ ācariyapācariyānaṃ bhāsamānānaṃ – ‘yai tai ahaisuṃ atītamaddhānaṃ arahantau sammāsambuddhā, na aivaṃ su nāssu (sī॰ pī॰) tai bhagavantau saṃgamma samāgamma un‍nādinau uc‍cāsaddamahāsaddā anaikavihitaṃ tiracchānakathaṃ anuyuttā viharanti| saiyyathidaṃ – rājakathaṃ caurakathaṃ…pai॰… itibhavābhavakathaṃ iti vā, saiyyathāpāhaṃ aitarahi sācariyakau| aivaṃ su tai bhagavantau arañ‍ñavanapatthāni pantāni saināsanāni paṭisaivanti appasaddāni appanigghausāni vijanavātāni manussarāhassaiyyakāni paṭisal‍lānasāruppāni, saiyyathāpi bhagavā aitarahī’’’ti|

‘‘tassa tai, nigraudha, viñ‍ñussa satau mahal‍lakassa na aitadahausi – ‘buddhau sau bhagavā baudhāya  dhammaṃ daisaiti, dantau sau bhagavā damathāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, santau sau bhagavā samathāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, tiṇṇau sau bhagavā taraṇāya  dhammaṃ daisaiti, parinibbutau sau bhagavā parinibbānāya dhammaṃ daisaitī’’’ti?

brahmacariyapariyausānasacchikiriyā

77. aivaṃ vuttai, nigraudhau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ac‍cayau maṃ, bhantai, ac‍cagamā yathābālaṃ yathāmūḷhaṃ yathāakusalaṃ, yvāhaṃ aivaṃ bhagavantaṃ avacāsiṃ| tassa mai, bhantai, bhagavā ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau paṭiggaṇhātu āyatiṃ saṃvarāyā’’ti| ‘‘taggha tvaṃ taṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), nigraudha, ac‍cayau  ac‍cagamā yathābālaṃ yathāmūḷhaṃ yathāakusalaṃ, yau maṃ tvaṃ aivaṃ avacāsi| yatau ca khau tvaṃ, nigraudha, ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau disvā yathādhammaṃ paṭikarausi, taṃ tai mayaṃ paṭiggaṇhāma| vuddhi haisā, nigraudha, ariyassa vinayai, yau ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau disvā yathādhammaṃ paṭikarauti āyatiṃ saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| ahaṃ khau pana, nigraudha, aivaṃ vadāmi –

‘aitu viñ‍ñū purisau asaṭhau amāyāvī ujujātikau, ahamanusāsāmi ahaṃ dhammaṃ daisaimi| yathānusiṭṭhaṃ tathā yathānusiṭṭhaṃ (?) paṭipaj‍jamānau, yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti, tadanuttaraṃ brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharissati sattavassāni| tiṭṭhantu, nigraudha, satta vassāni| aitu viñ‍ñū purisau asaṭhau amāyāvī ujujātikau, ahamanusāsāmi ahaṃ dhammaṃ daisaimi| yathānusiṭṭhaṃ tathā paṭipaj‍jamānau, yassatthāya kulaputtā  sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ  pabbajanti, tadanuttaraṃ brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharissati cha vassāni| pañ‍ca vassāni… cattāri vassāni… tīṇi vassāni… dvai vassāni… aikaṃ vassaṃ| tiṭṭhatu, nigraudha, aikaṃ vassaṃ| aitu viñ‍ñū purisau asaṭhau amāyāvī ujujātikau ahamanusāsāmi ahaṃ dhammaṃ daisaimi| yathānusiṭṭhaṃ tathā paṭipaj‍jamānau, yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti, tadanuttaraṃ brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharissati satta māsāni| tiṭṭhantu, nigraudha, satta māsāni… cha māsāni… pañ‍ca māsāni … cattāri māsāni… tīṇi māsāni… dvai māsāni… aikaṃ māsaṃ… aḍḍhamāsaṃ| tiṭṭhatu, nigraudha, aḍḍhamāsau| aitu viñ‍ñū purisau asaṭhau amāyāvī ujujātikau, ahamanusāsāmi ahaṃ dhammaṃ daisaimi| yathānusiṭṭhaṃ tathā paṭipaj‍jamānau, yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti, tadanuttaraṃ brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharissati sattāhaṃ’|

paribbājakānaṃ pajjhāyanaṃ

78. ‘‘siyā khau pana tai, nigraudha, aivamassa – ‘antaivāsikamyatā nau samaṇau gautamau aivamāhā’ti| na khau panaitaṃ, nigraudha, aivaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| yau aiva vau tai (sī॰ syā॰) ācariyau, sau aiva vau ācariyau hautu| siyā khau pana tai, nigraudha, aivamassa – ‘uddaisā nau cāvaitukāmau samaṇau gautamau aivamāhā’ti| na khau panaitaṃ, nigraudha, aivaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| yau aiva vau uddaisau sau aiva vau uddaisau hautu| siyā khau pana tai, nigraudha, aivamassa – ‘ājīvā nau cāvaitukāmau  samaṇau gautamau aivamāhā’ti| na khau panaitaṃ, nigraudha, aivaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| yau aiva vau ājīvau, sau aiva vau ājīvau hautu| siyā khau pana tai, nigraudha, aivamassa – ‘yai nau dhammā akusalā akusalasaṅkhātā sācariyakānaṃ, taisu patiṭṭhāpaitukāmau samaṇau gautamau aivamāhā’ti| na khau panaitaṃ, nigraudha, aivaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| akusalā caiva vau tai dhammā vaudhammā (ka॰), tai dhammā (syā॰) hauntu akusalasaṅkhātā ca sācariyakānaṃ| siyā khau pana tai, nigraudha, aivamassa – ‘yai nau dhammā kusalā kusalasaṅkhātā sācariyakānaṃ, taihi vivaicaitukāmau samaṇau gautamau aivamāhā’ti| na khau panaitaṃ, nigraudha, aivaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| kusalā caiva vau tai dhammā hauntu kusalasaṅkhātā ca sācariyakānaṃ| iti khvāhaṃ, nigraudha, naiva antaivāsikamyatā aivaṃ vadāmi, napi uddaisā cāvaitukāmau aivaṃ  vadāmi, napi ājīvā cāvaitukāmau aivaṃ vadāmi, napi yai vau dhammā napi yai khau dhammā (sī॰), napi yai tai dhammā (syā॰), napi yai ca vau dhammā (ka॰) akusalā akusalasaṅkhātā sācariyakānaṃ, taisu patiṭṭhāpaitukāmau aivaṃ vadāmi, napi yai vau dhammā napi yai khau dhammā (sī॰), napi yai tai dhammā (syā॰), napi yai ca vau dhammā (ka॰) kusalā kusalasaṅkhātā sācariyakānaṃ, taihi vivaicaitukāmau aivaṃ vadāmi| santi ca khau, nigraudha, akusalā dhammā appahīnā saṃkilaisikā paunaubbhavikā paunaubhavikā (ka॰) sadarā saddarā (pī॰ ka॰), sadarathā (syā॰ ka॰) dukkhavipākā āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇiyā, yaisāhaṃ pahānāya dhammaṃ daisaimi| yathāpaṭipan‍nānaṃ vau saṃkilaisikā dhammā pahīyissanti, vaudānīyā dhammā abhivaḍḍhissanti, pañ‍ñāpāripūriṃ vaipul‍lattañ‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai  sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharissathā’’ti|

79. aivaṃ vuttai, tai paribbājakā tuṇhībhūtā maṅkubhūtā pattakkhandhā adhaumukhā pajjhāyantā appaṭibhānā nisīdiṃsu yathā taṃ māraina pariyuṭṭhitacittā| atha khau bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘sabbai pimai maughapurisā phuṭṭhā pāpimatā| yatra hi nāma aikassapi na aivaṃ bhavissati – ‘handa mayaṃ añ‍ñāṇatthampi samaṇai gautamai brahmacariyaṃ carāma, kiṃ karissati sattāhau’’’ti? atha khau bhagavā udumbarikāya paribbājakārāmai sīhanādaṃ naditvā vaihāsaṃ abbhuggantvā gijjhakūṭai pabbatai pac‍cupaṭṭhāsi pac‍cuṭṭhāsi (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰)| sandhānau pana gahapati tāvadaiva rājagahaṃ pāvisīti|

udumbarikasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dutiyaṃ|



3. cak‍kavattisuttaṃ

attadīpasaraṇatā

80. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā magadhaisu viharati mātulāyaṃ| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhaddantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘attadīpā, bhikkhavai, viharatha attasaraṇā anañ‍ñasaraṇā, dhammadīpā dhammasaraṇā anañ‍ñasaraṇā| kathañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu attadīpau viharati attasaraṇau anañ‍ñasaraṇau, dhammadīpau dhammasaraṇau anañ‍ñasaraṇau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī…pai॰… cittai cittānupassī…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu attadīpau viharati attasaraṇau anañ‍ñasaraṇau, dhammadīpau dhammasaraṇau anañ‍ñasaraṇau|

‘‘gaucarai, bhikkhavai, caratha sakai paittikai visayai| gaucarai, bhikkhavai, carataṃ sakai paittikai visayai na lacchati mārau autāraṃ, na lacchati mārau ārammaṇaṃ āramaṇaṃ (?)| kusalānaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammānaṃ samādānahaitu aivamidaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ pavaḍḍhati|

daḷhanaimicak‍kavattirājā

81. ‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, rājā daḷhanaimi nāma ahausi cak‍kavattī cak‍kavatti (syā॰ pī॰) dhammikau dhammarājā cāturantau vijitāvī janapadatthāvariyappattau sattaratanasaman‍nāgatau| tassimāni satta ratanāni  ahaisuṃ saiyyathidaṃ – cak‍karatanaṃu  hatthiratanaṃ assaratanaṃ maṇiratanaṃ itthiratanaṃ gahapatiratanaṃ pariṇāyakaratanamaiva sattamaṃ| parausahassaṃ khau panassa puttā ahaisuṃ sūrā vīraṅgarūpā parasainappamaddanā| sau imaṃ pathaviṃ sāgarapariyantaṃ adaṇḍaina asatthaina dhammaina dhammaina samaina (syā॰ ka॰) abhivijiya ajjhāvasi|

82. ‘‘atha  khau, bhikkhavai, rājā daḷhanaimi bahun‍naṃ vassānaṃ bahun‍naṃ vassasatānaṃ bahun‍naṃ vassasahassānaṃ ac‍cayaina añ‍ñataraṃ purisaṃ āmantaisi – ‘yadā tvaṃ, ambhau purisa, passaiyyāsi dibbaṃ cak‍karatanaṃ ausak‍kitaṃ ṭhānā cutaṃ, atha mai āraucaiyyāsī’ti| ‘aivaṃ, daivā’ti khau, bhikkhavai, sau purisau rañ‍ñau daḷhanaimissa pac‍cassausi| addasā khau, bhikkhavai, sau purisau bahun‍naṃ vassānaṃ bahun‍naṃ vassasatānaṃ bahun‍naṃ vassasahassānaṃ ac‍cayaina dibbaṃ cak‍karatanaṃ ausak‍kitaṃ ṭhānā cutaṃ, disvāna yaina rājā daḷhanaimi tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā rājānaṃ daḷhanaimiṃ aitadavauca – ‘yagghai, daiva, jānaiyyāsi, dibbaṃ tai cak‍karatanaṃ ausak‍kitaṃ ṭhānā cuta’nti| atha khau, bhikkhavai, rājā daḷhanaimi jaiṭṭhaputtaṃ kumāraṃ āmantāpaitvā āmantaitvā (syā॰ ka॰) aitadavauca – ‘dibbaṃ kira mai, tāta kumāra, cak‍karatanaṃ ausak‍kitaṃ ṭhānā cutaṃ| sutaṃ khau pana maitaṃ – yassa rañ‍ñau cak‍kavattissa dibbaṃ cak‍karatanaṃ ausak‍kati ṭhānā cavati, na dāni taina rañ‍ñā ciraṃ jīvitabbaṃ hautīti| bhuttā khau pana  mai mānusakā kāmā, samayau dāni mai dibbai kāmai pariyaisituṃ| aihi tvaṃ, tāta kumāra, imaṃ samuddapariyantaṃ pathaviṃ paṭipaj‍ja| ahaṃ pana kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā  agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajissāmī’ti|

83. ‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, rājā daḷhanaimi jaiṭṭhaputtaṃ kumāraṃ sādhukaṃ raj‍jai samanusāsitvā kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaji| sattāhapabbajitai khau pana, bhikkhavai, rājisimhi dibbaṃ cak‍karatanaṃ antaradhāyi|

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatarau purisau yaina rājā khattiyau muddhābhisittau muddhāvasittau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) aivamuparipi tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā rājānaṃ khattiyaṃ muddhābhisittaṃ aitadavauca – ‘yagghai, daiva, jānaiyyāsi, dibbaṃ cak‍karatanaṃ antarahita’nti| atha khau, bhikkhavai, rājā khattiyau muddhābhisittau dibbai cak‍karatanai antarahitai anattamanau ahausi, anattamanatañ‍ca paṭisaṃvaidaisi| sau yaina rājisi tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā rājisiṃ aitadavauca – ‘yagghai, daiva, jānaiyyāsi, dibbaṃ cak‍karatanaṃ antarahita’nti| aivaṃ vuttai, bhikkhavai, rājisi rājānaṃ khattiyaṃ  muddhābhisittaṃ aitadavauca – ‘mā khau tvaṃ, tāta, dibbai  cak‍karatanai antarahitai anattamanau ahausi, mā anattamanatañ‍ca paṭisaṃvaidaisi, na hi tai, tāta, dibbaṃ cak‍karatanaṃ paittikaṃ dāyaj‍jaṃ| iṅgha tvaṃ, tāta, ariyai cak‍kavattivattai vattāhi| ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ vij‍jati, yaṃ tai ariyai cak‍kavattivattai vattamānassa tadahupausathai pan‍narasai sīsaṃnhātassa sīsaṃ nahātassa (sī॰ pī॰), sīsanhātassa (syā॰) upausathikassa uparipāsādavaragatassa dibbaṃ cak‍karatanaṃ pātubhavissati sahassāraṃ sanaimikaṃ sanābhikaṃ sabbākāraparipūra’nti|

cak‍kavattiariyavattaṃ

84. ‘‘‘katamaṃ  pana taṃ, daiva, ariyaṃ cak‍kavattivatta’nti? ‘taina hi tvaṃ, tāta, dhammaṃyaiva nissāya dhammaṃ sak‍karauntau dhammaṃ garuṃ karauntau garukarauntau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) dhammaṃ mānaintau dhammaṃ pūjaintau dhammaṃ apacāyamānau dhammaddhajau dhammakaitu dhammādhipataiyyau dhammikaṃ rakkhāvaraṇaguttiṃ saṃvidahassu antaujanasmiṃ balakāyasmiṃ khattiyaisu anuyantaisu anuyuttaisu (sī॰ pī॰) brāhmaṇagahapatikaisu naigamajānapadaisu samaṇabrāhmaṇaisu migapakkhīsu| mā ca tai, tāta, vijitai adhammakārau pavattittha| yai ca tai, tāta, vijitai adhanā assu, taisañ‍ca dhanamanuppadaiyyāsi dhanamanuppadaj‍jaiyyāsi (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰)| yai ca tai, tāta, vijitai samaṇabrāhmaṇā madappamādā paṭiviratā khantisaurac‍cai niviṭṭhā aikamattānaṃ damainti, aikamattānaṃ samainti, aikamattānaṃ parinibbāpainti, tai kālaina kālaṃ upasaṅkamitvā paripucchaiyyāsi pariggaṇhaiyyāsi – ‘‘kiṃ, bhantai, kusalaṃ, kiṃ akusalaṃ, kiṃ sāvaj‍jaṃ, kiṃ anavaj‍jaṃ, kiṃ saivitabbaṃ, kiṃ na saivitabbaṃ, kiṃ mai karīyamānaṃ dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya assa, kiṃ vā pana mai karīyamānaṃ dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya assā’’ti? taisaṃ sutvā yaṃ akusalaṃ taṃ abhinivaj‍jaiyyāsi, yaṃ kusalaṃ taṃ samādāya vattaiyyāsi| idaṃ khau, tāta, taṃ ariyaṃ cak‍kavattivatta’nti|

cak‍karatanapātubhāvau

85. ‘‘‘aivaṃ, daivā’ti khau, bhikkhavai, rājā khattiyau muddhābhisittau rājisissa paṭissutvā ariyai cak‍kavattivattai ariyaṃ cak‍kavattivattaṃ (ka॰) vatti| tassa ariyai cak‍kavattivattai vattamānassa tadahupausathai pan‍narasai sīsaṃnhātassa upausathikassa  uparipāsādavaragatassa dibbaṃ cak‍karatanaṃ pāturahausi sahassāraṃ sanaimikaṃ sanābhikaṃ sabbākāraparipūraṃ| disvāna  rañ‍ñau khattiyassa muddhābhisittassa aitadahausi – ‘sutaṃ khau pana maitaṃ – yassa rañ‍ñau khattiyassa muddhābhisittassa tadahupausathai  pan‍narasai sīsaṃnhātassa upausathikassa uparipāsādavaragatassa dibbaṃ cak‍karatanaṃ pātubhavati sahassāraṃ sanaimikaṃ sanābhikaṃ sabbākāraparipūraṃ, sau hauti rājā cak‍kavattī’ti| assaṃ nu khau ahaṃ rājā cak‍kavattīti|

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, rājā khattiyau muddhābhisittau uṭṭhāyāsanā aikaṃsaṃ utarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā vāmaina hatthaina bhiṅkāraṃ gahaitvā dakkhiṇaina hatthaina cak‍karatanaṃ abbhuk‍kiri – ‘pavattatu bhavaṃ cak‍karatanaṃ, abhivijinātu bhavaṃ cak‍karatana’nti|

‘‘atha khau taṃ, bhikkhavai, cak‍karatanaṃ puratthimaṃ disaṃ pavatti, anvadaiva rājā cak‍kavattī saddhiṃ caturaṅginiyā saināya| yasmiṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai, padaisai cak‍karatanaṃ patiṭṭhāsi, tattha rājā cak‍kavattī vāsaṃ upagacchi saddhiṃ caturaṅginiyā saināya| yai khau pana, bhikkhavai, puratthimāya disāya paṭirājānau, tai rājānaṃ cak‍kavattiṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘aihi khau, mahārāja, svāgataṃ tai sāgataṃ (sī॰ pī॰) mahārāja, sakaṃ tai, mahārāja, anusāsa, mahārājā’ti| rājā cak‍kavattī aivamāha – ‘pāṇau na hantabbau, adin‍naṃ nādātabbaṃ, kāmaisumicchā na caritabbā, musā na bhāsitabbā, maj‍jaṃ na pātabbaṃ, yathābhuttañ‍ca bhuñ‍jathā’ti| yai  khau pana, bhikkhavai, puratthimāya disāya paṭirājānau, tai rañ‍ñau cak‍kavattissa anuyantā anuyuttā (sī॰ pī॰) ahaisuṃ|

86. ‘‘atha khau taṃ, bhikkhavai, cak‍karatanaṃ puratthimaṃ samuddaṃ ajjhaugāhaitvā ajjhaugahaitvā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) pac‍cuttaritvā dakkhiṇaṃ disaṃ pavatti…pai॰… dakkhiṇaṃ samuddaṃ ajjhaugāhaitvā pac‍cuttaritvā pacchimaṃ disaṃ pavatti, anvadaiva rājā cak‍kavattī saddhiṃ caturaṅginiyā saināya| yasmiṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai, padaisai cak‍karatanaṃ patiṭṭhāsi, tattha rājā cak‍kavattī vāsaṃ upagacchi saddhiṃ caturaṅginiyā saināya| yai khau pana, bhikkhavai, pacchimāya disāya paṭirājānau, tai rājānaṃ cak‍kavattiṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘aihi khau, mahārāja, svāgataṃ tai, mahārāja, sakaṃ tai, mahārāja, anusāsa, mahārājā’ti| rājā cak‍kavattī  aivamāha – ‘pāṇau na hantabbau, adin‍naṃ nādātabbaṃ, kāmaisumicchā na caritabbā, musā na bhāsitabbā, maj‍jaṃ na pātabbaṃ, yathābhuttañ‍ca bhuñ‍jathā’ti| yai khau pana, bhikkhavai, pacchimāya disāya paṭirājānau, tai rañ‍ñau cak‍kavattissa anuyantā  ahaisuṃ|

87. ‘‘atha khau taṃ, bhikkhavai, cak‍karatanaṃ pacchimaṃ samuddaṃ ajjhaugāhaitvā pac‍cuttaritvā uttaraṃ disaṃ pavatti, anvadaiva rājā cak‍kavattī saddhiṃ caturaṅginiyā saināya| yasmiṃ  khau pana, bhikkhavai, padaisai cak‍karatanaṃ patiṭṭhāsi, tattha rājā cak‍kavattī vāsaṃ upagacchi saddhiṃ caturaṅginiyā saināya| yai khau pana, bhikkhavai, uttarāya disāya paṭirājānau, tai rājānaṃ cak‍kavattiṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘aihi khau, mahārāja, svāgataṃ tai, mahārāja, sakaṃ tai, mahārāja, anusāsa, mahārājā’ti| rājā cak‍kavattī aivamāha – ‘pāṇau na hantabbau, adin‍naṃ nādātabbaṃ, kāmaisumicchā na caritabbā, musā na bhāsitabbā, maj‍jaṃ na pātabbaṃ, yathābhuttañ‍ca bhuñ‍jathā’ti| yai khau pana, bhikkhavai, uttarāya disāya paṭirājānau, tai rañ‍ñau cak‍kavattissa anuyantā ahaisuṃ|

‘‘atha khau taṃ, bhikkhavai, cak‍karatanaṃ samuddapariyantaṃ pathaviṃ abhivijinitvā tamaiva rājadhāniṃ pac‍cāgantvā rañ‍ñau cak‍kavattissa antaipuradvārai atthakaraṇapamukhai aḍḍakaraṇapamukhai (ka॰) akkhāhataṃ mañ‍ñai aṭṭhāsi rañ‍ñau cak‍kavattissa antaipuraṃ upasaubhayamānaṃ|

dutiyādicak‍kavattikathā

88. ‘‘dutiyaupi khau, bhikkhavai, rājā cak‍kavattī…pai॰… tatiyaupi khau, bhikkhavai, rājā cak‍kavattī… catutthaupi khau, bhikkhavai, rājā cak‍kavattī… pañ‍camaupi khau, bhikkhavai, rājā cak‍kavattī… chaṭṭhaupi khau, bhikkhavai, rājā cak‍kavattī… sattamaupi khau, bhikkhavai, rājā cak‍kavattī bahun‍naṃ vassānaṃ bahun‍naṃ vassasatānaṃ bahun‍naṃ vassasahassānaṃ ac‍cayaina añ‍ñataraṃ purisaṃ āmantaisi – ‘yadā tvaṃ, ambhau purisa, passaiyyāsi dibbaṃ cak‍karatanaṃ ausak‍kitaṃ ṭhānā cutaṃ, atha mai āraucaiyyāsī’ti| ‘aivaṃ, daivā’ti khau, bhikkhavai, sau purisau rañ‍ñau cak‍kavattissa pac‍cassausi| addasā khau, bhikkhavai, sau purisau bahun‍naṃ vassānaṃ bahun‍naṃ vassasatānaṃ bahun‍naṃ vassasahassānaṃ ac‍cayaina dibbaṃ cak‍karatanaṃ ausak‍kitaṃ  ṭhānā cutaṃ| disvāna yaina rājā cak‍kavattī tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā rājānaṃ cak‍kavattiṃ aitadavauca – ‘yagghai, daiva, jānaiyyāsi, dibbaṃ tai cak‍karatanaṃ ausak‍kitaṃ ṭhānā cuta’nti?

89. ‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, rājā cak‍kavattī jaiṭṭhaputtaṃ kumāraṃ āmantāpaitvā aitadavauca – ‘dibbaṃ kira mai, tāta kumāra, cak‍karatanaṃ ausak‍kitaṃ, ṭhānā cutaṃ, sutaṃ khau pana maitaṃ – yassa rañ‍ñau cak‍kavattissa dibbaṃ cak‍karatanaṃ ausak‍kati, ṭhānā cavati, na dāni taina rañ‍ñā ciraṃ jīvitabbaṃ hautīti| bhuttā khau pana mai mānusakā kāmā, samayau dāni mai dibbai kāmai pariyaisituṃ, aihi tvaṃ, tāta kumāra, imaṃ samuddapariyantaṃ pathaviṃ paṭipaj‍ja| ahaṃ pana kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajissāmī’ti|

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, rājā cak‍kavattī jaiṭṭhaputtaṃ kumāraṃ sādhukaṃ raj‍jai samanusāsitvā kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaji| sattāhapabbajitai khau pana, bhikkhavai, rājisimhi dibbaṃ cak‍karatanaṃ antaradhāyi|

90. ‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatarau purisau yaina rājā khattiyau muddhābhisittau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā rājānaṃ khattiyaṃ muddhābhisittaṃ aitadavauca – ‘yagghai, daiva, jānaiyyāsi, dibbaṃ cak‍karatanaṃ antarahita’nti? atha khau, bhikkhavai, rājā khattiyau muddhābhisittau dibbai cak‍karatanai antarahitai anattamanau ahausi| anattamanatañ‍ca paṭisaṃvaidaisi; nau ca khau rājisiṃ upasaṅkamitvā ariyaṃ cak‍kavattivattaṃ pucchi| sau samatainaiva sudaṃ janapadaṃ pasāsati| tassa samataina janapadaṃ pasāsatau pubbaināparaṃ  janapadā na pabbanti, yathā taṃ pubbakānaṃ rājūnaṃ ariyai cak‍kavattivattai vattamānānaṃ|

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, amac‍cā pārisaj‍jā gaṇakamahāmattā anīkaṭṭhā dauvārikā mantassājīvinau san‍nipatitvā rājānaṃ khattiyaṃ muddhābhisittaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘na  khau tai, daiva, samataina (sudaṃ) janapadaṃ pasāsatau pubbaināparaṃ  janapadā pabbanti, yathā taṃ pubbakānaṃ rājūnaṃ ariyai cak‍kavattivattai vattamānānaṃ| saṃvij‍janti khau tai, daiva, vijitai amac‍cā pārisaj‍jā gaṇakamahāmattā anīkaṭṭhā dauvārikā mantassājīvinau mayañ‍caiva añ‍ñai ca añ‍ñai ca paṇḍitai samaṇabrāhmaṇai pucchaiyyāsi (ka॰) yai mayaṃ ariyaṃ cak‍kavattivattaṃ dhāraima| iṅgha tvaṃ, daiva, amhai ariyaṃ cak‍kavattivattaṃ puccha| tassa tai mayaṃ ariyaṃ cak‍kavattivattaṃ puṭṭhā byākarissāmā’ti|

āyuvaṇṇādipariyānikathā

91. ‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, rājā khattiyau muddhābhisittau amac‍cai pārisaj‍jai gaṇakamahāmattai anīkaṭṭhai dauvārikai mantassājīvinau san‍nipātaitvā ariyaṃ cak‍kavattivattaṃ pucchi| tassa tai ariyaṃ cak‍kavattivattaṃ puṭṭhā byākariṃsu| taisaṃ sutvā dhammikañhi khau rakkhāvaraṇaguttiṃ saṃvidahi, nau ca khau adhanānaṃ dhanamanuppadāsi| adhanānaṃ dhanai ananuppadiyamānai dāliddiyaṃ vaipul‍lamagamāsi| dāliddiyai vaipul‍laṃ gatai añ‍ñatarau purisau paraisaṃ adin‍naṃ  thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyi| tamainaṃ aggahaisuṃ| gahaitvā rañ‍ñau khattiyassa muddhābhisittassa dassaisuṃ – ‘ayaṃ, daiva, purisau paraisaṃ adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyī’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, bhikkhavai, rājā khattiyau muddhābhisittau  taṃ purisaṃ aitadavauca – ‘sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, ambhau purisa, paraisaṃ adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyī’ti ādiyasīti (syā॰)? ‘sac‍caṃ, daivā’ti| ‘kiṃ kāraṇā’ti? ‘na hi, daiva, jīvāmī’ti| atha  khau, bhikkhavai, rājā khattiyau muddhābhisittau tassa purisassa dhanamanuppadāsi – ‘iminā tvaṃ, ambhau purisa, dhanaina attanā ca jīvāhi, mātāpitarau ca pausaihi, puttadārañ‍ca pausaihi, kammantai ca payaujaihi, samaṇabrāhmaṇaisu samaṇaisu brāhmaṇaisu (bahūsu) uddhaggikaṃ dakkhiṇaṃ patiṭṭhāpaihi sauvaggikaṃ sukhavipākaṃ saggasaṃvattanika’nti| ‘aivaṃ, daivā’ti khau, bhikkhavai, sau purisau rañ‍ñau khattiyassa muddhābhisittassa pac‍cassausi|

‘‘añ‍ñataraupi khau, bhikkhavai, purisau paraisaṃ adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyi| tamainaṃ aggahaisuṃ| gahaitvā rañ‍ñau khattiyassa muddhābhisittassa dassaisuṃ – ‘ayaṃ, daiva, purisau paraisaṃ adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyī’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, bhikkhavai, rājā khattiyau muddhābhisittau taṃ purisaṃ aitadavauca – ‘sac‍caṃ  kira tvaṃ, ambhau purisa, paraisaṃ adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyī’ti? ‘sac‍caṃ, daivā’ti| ‘kiṃ kāraṇā’ti? ‘na hi, daiva, jīvāmī’ti| atha khau, bhikkhavai, rājā khattiyau muddhābhisittau tassa purisassa dhanamanuppadāsi – ‘iminā tvaṃ, ambhau purisa, dhanaina attanā ca jīvāhi, mātāpitarau ca pausaihi, puttadārañ‍ca pausaihi, kammantai ca payaujaihi, samaṇabrāhmaṇaisu uddhaggikaṃ dakkhiṇaṃ patiṭṭhāpaihi sauvaggikaṃ sukhavipākaṃ saggasaṃvattanika’nti| ‘aivaṃ, daivā’ti khau, bhikkhavai, sau purisau rañ‍ñau khattiyassa muddhābhisittassa pac‍cassausi|

92. ‘‘assausuṃ khau, bhikkhavai, manussā – ‘yai kira, bhau, paraisaṃ adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyanti, taisaṃ rājā dhanamanuppadaitī’ti| sutvāna taisaṃ aitadahausi – ‘yaṃnūna mayampi paraisaṃ adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyaiyyāmā’ti| atha khau, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatarau purisau paraisaṃ adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyi| tamainaṃ aggahaisuṃ| gahaitvā rañ‍ñau khattiyassa muddhābhisittassa dassaisuṃ – ‘ayaṃ, daiva, purisau paraisaṃ adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyī’ti| aivaṃ  vuttai, bhikkhavai, rājā khattiyau muddhābhisittau taṃ purisaṃ aitadavauca – ‘sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, ambhau purisa, paraisaṃ adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyī’ti? ‘sac‍caṃ, daivā’ti| ‘kiṃ kāraṇā’ti? ‘na hi, daiva, jīvāmī’ti| atha khau, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau khattiyassa muddhābhisittassa aitadahausi – ‘sacai khau ahaṃ yau yau paraisaṃ adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyissati, tassa tassa dhanamanuppadassāmi, aivamidaṃ  adin‍nādānaṃ pavaḍḍhissati| yaṃnūnāhaṃ imaṃ purisaṃ sunisaidhaṃ nisaidhaiyyaṃ, mūlaghac‍caṃ mūlaghacchaṃ (syā॰), mūlachaij‍ja (ka॰) karaiyyaṃ, sīsamassa chindaiyya’nti| atha khau, bhikkhavai, rājā khattiyau muddhābhisittau purisai āṇāpaisi – ‘taina hi, bhaṇai, imaṃ purisaṃ daḷhāya raj‍juyā pacchābāhaṃ pacchābāhuṃ (syā॰) gāḷhabandhanaṃ bandhitvā khuramuṇḍaṃ karitvā kharassaraina paṇavaina rathikāya rathikaṃ siṅghāṭakaina siṅghāṭakaṃ parinaitvā dakkhiṇaina dvāraina nikkhamitvā dakkhiṇatau nagarassa sunisaidhaṃ nisaidhaitha, mūlaghac‍caṃ karautha, sīsamassa chindathā’ti| ‘aivaṃ, daivā’ti  khau, bhikkhavai, tai purisā rañ‍ñau khattiyassa muddhābhisittassa paṭissutvā taṃ purisaṃ daḷhāya raj‍juyā pacchābāhaṃ gāḷhabandhanaṃ bandhitvā khuramuṇḍaṃ karitvā kharassaraina paṇavaina rathikāya rathikaṃ siṅghāṭakaina siṅghāṭakaṃ parinaitvā  dakkhiṇaina dvāraina nikkhamitvā dakkhiṇatau nagarassa sunisaidhaṃ nisaidhaisuṃ, mūlaghac‍caṃ akaṃsu, sīsamassa chindiṃsu|

93. ‘‘assausuṃ khau, bhikkhavai, manussā – ‘yai kira, bhau, paraisaṃ adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyanti, tai rājā sunisaidhaṃ nisaidhaiti, mūlaghac‍caṃ karauti, sīsāni taisaṃ chindatī’ti| sutvāna taisaṃ aitadahausi – ‘yaṃnūna mayampi tiṇhāni satthāni kārāpaissāma kārāpaiyyāma (syā॰ pī॰) kārāpaiyyāmāti (ka॰ sī॰), tiṇhāni satthāni kārāpaitvā yaisaṃ adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyissāma, tai sunisaidhaṃ  nisaidhaissāma, mūlaghac‍caṃ karissāma, sīsāni taisaṃ chindissāmā’ti| tai tiṇhāni satthāni kārāpaisuṃ, tiṇhāni satthāni kārāpaitvā gāmaghātampi upak‍kamiṃsu kātuṃ, nigamaghātampi upak‍kamiṃsu kātuṃ, nagaraghātampi upak‍kamiṃsu kātuṃ, panthaduhanampi panthadūhanaṃpi (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) upak‍kamiṃsu kātuṃ| yaisaṃ tai adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyanti, tai sunisaidhaṃ nisaidhainti, mūlaghac‍caṃ karaunti, sīsāni taisaṃ chindanti|

94. ‘‘iti khau, bhikkhavai, adhanānaṃ dhanai ananuppadiyamānai dāliddiyaṃ vaipul‍lamagamāsi, dāliddiyai vaipul‍laṃ gatai adin‍nādānaṃ vaipul‍lamagamāsi, adin‍nādānai vaipul‍laṃ gatai satthaṃ vaipul‍lamagamāsi, satthai  vaipul‍laṃ gatai pāṇātipātau vaipul‍lamagamāsi, pāṇātipātai vaipul‍laṃ gatai taisaṃ sattānaṃ āyupi parihāyi, vaṇṇaupi parihāyi| taisaṃ āyunāpi parihāyamānānaṃ vaṇṇainapi parihāyamānānaṃ asītivassasahassāyukānaṃ manussānaṃ cattārīsavassasahassāyukā puttā ahaisuṃ|

‘‘cattārīsavassasahassāyukaisu, bhikkhavai, manussaisu añ‍ñatarau purisau paraisaṃ adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyi| tamainaṃ aggahaisuṃ| gahaitvā rañ‍ñau khattiyassa muddhābhisittassa dassaisuṃ – ‘ayaṃ, daiva, purisau paraisaṃ adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyī’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, bhikkhavai, rājā khattiyau  muddhābhisittau taṃ purisaṃ aitadavauca – ‘sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, ambhau purisa, paraisaṃ adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyī’ti? ‘na hi, daivā’ti sampajānamusā abhāsi|

95. ‘‘iti  khau, bhikkhavai, adhanānaṃ dhanai ananuppadiyamānai dāliddiyaṃ vaipul‍lamagamāsi| dāliddiyai vaipul‍laṃ gatai adin‍nādānaṃ vaipul‍lamagamāsi, adin‍nādānai vaipul‍laṃ gatai satthaṃ vaipul‍lamagamāsi| satthai vaipul‍laṃ gatai pāṇātipātau vaipul‍lamagamāsi, pāṇātipātai vaipul‍laṃ gatai musāvādau vaipul‍lamagamāsi, musāvādai vaipul‍laṃ gatai taisaṃ sattānaṃ āyupi parihāyi, vaṇṇaupi parihāyi| taisaṃ āyunāpi parihāyamānānaṃ vaṇṇainapi parihāyamānānaṃ cattārīsavassasahassāyukānaṃ manussānaṃ vīsativassasahassāyukā puttā ahaisuṃ|

‘‘vīsativassasahassāyukaisu, bhikkhavai, manussaisu añ‍ñatarau purisau paraisaṃ adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyi| tamainaṃ añ‍ñatarau purisau rañ‍ñau khattiyassa muddhābhisittassa āraucaisi – ‘itthan‍nāmau, daiva, purisau paraisaṃ adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyī’ti paisuñ‍ñamakāsi|

96. ‘‘iti khau, bhikkhavai, adhanānaṃ dhanai ananuppadiyamānai dāliddiyaṃ vaipul‍lamagamāsi| dāliddiyai vaipul‍laṃ gatai adin‍nādānaṃ vaipul‍lamagamāsi, adin‍nādānai vaipul‍laṃ gatai satthaṃ vaipul‍lamagamāsi, satthai vaipul‍laṃ gatai pāṇātipātau vaipul‍lamagamāsi, pāṇātipātai vaipul‍laṃ gatai musāvādau vaipul‍lamagamāsi, musāvādai vaipul‍laṃ gatai pisuṇā vācā vaipul‍lamagamāsi, pisuṇāya vācāya vaipul‍laṃ gatāya taisaṃ sattānaṃ āyupi parihāyi, vaṇṇaupi parihāyi| taisaṃ āyunāpi parihāyamānānaṃ vaṇṇainapi parihāyamānānaṃ vīsativassasahassāyukānaṃ manussānaṃ dasavassasahassāyukā puttā ahaisuṃ|

‘‘dasavassasahassāyukaisu, bhikkhavai, manussaisu aikidaṃ sattā vaṇṇavantau haunti, aikidaṃ sattā dubbaṇṇā| tattha yai tai sattā dubbaṇṇā, tai vaṇṇavantai sattai abhijjhāyantā paraisaṃ dāraisu cārittaṃ āpaj‍jiṃsu|

97. ‘‘iti khau, bhikkhavai, adhanānaṃ dhanai ananuppadiyamānai dāliddiyaṃ vaipul‍lamagamāsi| dāliddiyai vaipul‍laṃ gatai…pai॰… kāmaisumicchācārau vaipul‍lamagamāsi, kāmaisumicchācārai vaipul‍laṃ gatai taisaṃ sattānaṃ āyupi parihāyi, vaṇṇaupi parihāyi| taisaṃ āyunāpi  parihāyamānānaṃ vaṇṇainapi  parihāyamānānaṃ dasavassasahassāyukānaṃ manussānaṃ pañ‍cavassasahassāyukā puttā ahaisuṃ|

98. ‘‘pañ‍cavassasahassāyukaisu, bhikkhavai, manussaisu dvai dhammā vaipul‍lamagamaṃsu – pharusāvācā samphappalāpau ca| dvīsu dhammaisu vaipul‍laṃ gataisu taisaṃ sattānaṃ āyupi parihāyi, vaṇṇaupi parihāyi| taisaṃ āyunāpi parihāyamānānaṃ vaṇṇainapi parihāyamānānaṃ pañ‍cavassasahassāyukānaṃ  manussānaṃ appaikac‍cai aḍḍhataiyyavassasahassāyukā, appaikac‍cai dvaivassasahassāyukā puttā ahaisuṃ|

99. ‘‘aḍḍhataiyyavassasahassāyukaisu, bhikkhavai, manussaisu abhijjhābyāpādā vaipul‍lamagamaṃsu| abhijjhābyāpādaisu vaipul‍laṃ gataisu taisaṃ sattānaṃ āyupi parihāyi, vaṇṇaupi parihāyi| taisaṃ āyunāpi parihāyamānānaṃ vaṇṇainapi parihāyamānānaṃ aḍḍhataiyyavassasahassāyukānaṃ manussānaṃ vassasahassāyukā puttā ahaisuṃ|

100. ‘‘vassasahassāyukaisu, bhikkhavai, manussaisu micchādiṭṭhi vaipul‍lamagamāsi| micchādiṭṭhiyā vaipul‍laṃ gatāya taisaṃ sattānaṃ āyupi parihāyi, vaṇṇaupi parihāyi| taisaṃ āyunāpi parihāyamānānaṃ vaṇṇainapi parihāyamānānaṃ vassasahassāyukānaṃ manussānaṃ pañ‍cavassasatāyukā puttā ahaisuṃ|

101. ‘‘pañ‍cavassasatāyukaisu, bhikkhavai, manussaisu tayau dhammā vaipul‍lamagamaṃsu| adhammarāgau visamalaubhau micchādhammau| tīsu dhammaisu vaipul‍laṃ gataisu taisaṃ sattānaṃ āyupi parihāyi, vaṇṇaupi parihāyi| taisaṃ āyunāpi parihāyamānānaṃ vaṇṇainapi parihāyamānānaṃ pañ‍cavassasatāyukānaṃ manussānaṃ appaikac‍cai aḍḍhataiyyavassasatāyukā, appaikac‍cai dvaivassasatāyukā puttā ahaisuṃ|

‘‘aḍḍhataiyyavassasatāyukaisu, bhikkhavai, manussaisu imai dhammā vaipul‍lamagamaṃsu| amattaiyyatā apaittaiyyatā asāmañ‍ñatā abrahmañ‍ñatā na kulai jaiṭṭhāpacāyitā|

102. ‘‘iti  khau, bhikkhavai, adhanānaṃ dhanai ananuppadiyamānai dāliddiyaṃ vaipul‍lamagamāsi| dāliddiyai vaipul‍laṃ gatai adin‍nādānaṃ vaipul‍lamagamāsi| adin‍nādānai vaipul‍laṃ gatai  satthaṃ vaipul‍lamagamāsi| satthai vaipul‍laṃ gatai pāṇātipātau vaipul‍lamagamāsi| pāṇātipātai vaipul‍laṃ gatai musāvādau vaipul‍lamagamāsi| musāvādai vaipul‍laṃ gatai pisuṇā vācā vaipul‍lamagamāsi| pisuṇāya vācāya vaipul‍laṃ gatāya kāmaisumicchācārau vaipul‍lamagamāsi| kāmaisumicchācārai vaipul‍laṃ gatai  dvai dhammā vaipul‍lamagamaṃsu, pharusā vācā samphappalāpau ca| dvīsu dhammaisu vaipul‍laṃ gataisu abhijjhābyāpādā vaipul‍lamagamaṃsu| abhijjhābyāpādaisu vaipul‍laṃ gataisu micchādiṭṭhi vaipul‍lamagamāsi| micchādiṭṭhiyā vaipul‍laṃ gatāya tayau dhammā vaipul‍lamagamaṃsu, adhammarāgau visamalaubhau micchādhammau| tīsu dhammaisu vaipul‍laṃ gataisu imai dhammā vaipul‍lamagamaṃsu, amattaiyyatā apaittaiyyatā asāmañ‍ñatā abrahmañ‍ñatā na kulai jaiṭṭhāpacāyitā| imaisu dhammaisu vaipul‍laṃ gataisu taisaṃ sattānaṃ āyupi parihāyi, vaṇṇaupi parihāyi| taisaṃ āyunāpi parihāyamānānaṃ vaṇṇainapi parihāyamānānaṃ aḍḍhataiyyavassasatāyukānaṃ manussānaṃ vassasatāyukā puttā ahaisuṃ|

dasavassāyukasamayau

103. ‘‘bhavissati, bhikkhavai, sau samayau, yaṃ imaisaṃ manussānaṃ dasavassāyukā puttā bhavissanti| dasavassāyukaisu, bhikkhavai, manussaisu pañ‍cavassikā pañ‍camāsikā (ka॰ sī॰) kumārikā alaṃpataiyyā bhavissanti| dasavassāyukaisu, bhikkhavai, manussaisu imāni rasāni antaradhāyissanti, saiyyathidaṃ, sappi navanītaṃ tailaṃ madhu phāṇitaṃ lauṇaṃ| dasavassāyukaisu, bhikkhavai, manussaisu kudrūsakau aggaṃ bhaujanānaṃ aggabhaujanaṃ (syā॰) bhavissati| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, aitarahi sālimaṃsaudanau aggaṃ bhaujanānaṃ; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, dasavassāyukaisu manussaisu kudrūsakau aggaṃ bhaujanānaṃ bhavissati|

‘‘dasavassāyukaisu, bhikkhavai, manussaisu dasa kusalakammapathā sabbaina sabbaṃ antaradhāyissanti, dasa akusalakammapathā atibyādippissanti ativiya dippissanti (syā॰ pī॰), ativyādippissanti (sī॰)| dasavassāyukaisu, bhikkhavai, manussaisu kusalantipi na bhavissati, kutau pana kusalassa kārakau| dasavassāyukaisu, bhikkhavai, manussaisu yai tai bhavissanti amattaiyyā  apaittaiyyā asāmañ‍ñā abrahmañ‍ñā na kulai jaiṭṭhāpacāyinau, tai puj‍jā ca bhavissanti pāsaṃsā ca| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, aitarahi mattaiyyā paittaiyyā sāmañ‍ñā brahmañ‍ñā kulai jaiṭṭhāpacāyinau puj‍jā ca pāsaṃsā ca; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, dasavassāyukaisu manussaisu yai tai bhavissanti amattaiyyā apaittaiyyā asāmañ‍ñā abrahmañ‍ñā na kulai jaiṭṭhāpacāyinau, tai puj‍jā ca bhavissanti pāsaṃsā ca|

‘‘dasavassāyukaisu, bhikkhavai, manussaisu na bhavissati mātāti  vā mātucchāti vā mātulānīti vā ācariyabhariyāti vā garūnaṃ dārāti vā| sambhaidaṃ laukau gamissati yathā ajaiḷakā kuk‍kuṭasūkarā sauṇasiṅgālā sauṇasigālā (sī॰ pī॰)|

‘‘dasavassāyukaisu, bhikkhavai, manussaisu taisaṃ sattānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñamhi tibbau āghātau pac‍cupaṭṭhitau bhavissati tibbau byāpādau tibbau manaupadausau tibbaṃ vadhakacittaṃ| mātupi puttamhi puttassapi mātari; pitupi puttamhi puttassapi pitari; bhātupi bhaginiyā bhaginiyāpi bhātari tibbau āghātau pac‍cupaṭṭhitau bhavissati tibbau byāpādau tibbau manaupadausau tibbaṃ vadhakacittaṃ| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, māgavikassa migaṃ disvā tibbau āghātau pac‍cupaṭṭhitau hauti tibbau byāpādau tibbau manaupadausau tibbaṃ vadhakacittaṃ; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, dasavassāyukaisu manussaisu taisaṃ sattānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñamhi tibbau āghātau pac‍cupaṭṭhitau bhavissati tibbau byāpādau tibbau manaupadausau tibbaṃ vadhakacittaṃ| mātupi puttamhi puttassapi mātari; pitupi puttamhi puttassapi pitari; bhātupi bhaginiyā bhaginiyāpi bhātari tibbau āghātau  pac‍cupaṭṭhitau bhavissati tibbau byāpādau tibbau manaupadausau tibbaṃ vadhakacittaṃ|

104. ‘‘dasavassāyukaisu, bhikkhavai, manussaisu sattāhaṃ satthantarakappau bhavissati| tai añ‍ñamañ‍ñamhi migasañ‍ñaṃ paṭilabhissanti| taisaṃ tiṇhāni  satthāni  hatthaisu pātubhavissanti| tai tiṇhaina satthaina ‘aisa migau aisa migau’ti añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ jīvitā vauraupaissanti|

‘‘atha khau taisaṃ, bhikkhavai, sattānaṃ aikac‍cānaṃ aivaṃ bhavissati – ‘mā ca mayaṃ kañ‍ci kiñ‍ci (ka॰), mā ca amhai kauci, yaṃnūna mayaṃ tiṇagahanaṃ vā vanagahanaṃ vā rukkhagahanaṃ vā nadīviduggaṃ vā pabbatavisamaṃ vā pavisitvā vanamūlaphalāhārā yāpaiyyāmā’ti| tai tiṇagahanaṃ vā vanagahanaṃ vā rukkhagahanaṃ vā nadīviduggaṃ vā pabbatavisamaṃ vā tai tiṇagahanaṃ vanagahanaṃ rukkhagahanaṃ nadīviduggaṃ pabbatavisamaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) pavisitvā sattāhaṃ vanamūlaphalāhārā yāpaissanti| tai tassa sattāhassa ac‍cayaina tiṇagahanā vanagahanā rukkhagahanā nadīviduggā pabbatavisamā nikkhamitvā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ āliṅgitvā sabhāgāyissanti samassāsissanti – ‘diṭṭhā, bhau, sattā jīvasi, diṭṭhā, bhau, sattā jīvasī’ti|

āyuvaṇṇādivaḍḍhanakathā

105. ‘‘atha  khau taisaṃ, bhikkhavai, sattānaṃ aivaṃ bhavissati – ‘mayaṃ khau akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samādānahaitu aivarūpaṃ āyataṃ ñātikkhayaṃ pattā| yaṃnūna mayaṃ kusalaṃ karaiyyāma| kiṃ kusalaṃ karaiyyāma? yaṃnūna mayaṃ pāṇātipātā viramaiyyāma, idaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ samādāya vattaiyyāmā’ti| tai pāṇātipātā viramissanti, idaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ samādāya vattissanti| tai kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samādānahaitu āyunāpi vaḍḍhissanti, vaṇṇainapi vaḍḍhissanti| taisaṃ āyunāpi vaḍḍhamānānaṃ vaṇṇainapi vaḍḍhamānānaṃ dasavassāyukānaṃ manussānaṃ vīsativassāyukā puttā bhavissanti|

‘‘atha khau taisaṃ, bhikkhavai, sattānaṃ aivaṃ bhavissati – ‘mayaṃ khau kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ  samādānahaitu āyunāpi vaḍḍhāma, vaṇṇainapi vaḍḍhāma| yaṃnūna mayaṃ bhiyyausaumattāya kusalaṃ karaiyyāma| kiṃ kusalaṃ karaiyyāma? yaṃnūna mayaṃ adin‍nādānā viramaiyyāma… kāmaisumicchācārā viramaiyyāma… musāvādā viramaiyyāma… pisuṇāya vācāya viramaiyyāma… pharusāya vācāya viramaiyyāma… samphappalāpā viramaiyyāma… abhijjhaṃ pajahaiyyāma… byāpādaṃ pajahaiyyāma… micchādiṭṭhiṃ pajahaiyyāma… tayau dhammai pajahaiyyāma – adhammarāgaṃ visamalaubhaṃ micchādhammaṃ… yaṃnūna mayaṃ mattaiyyā assāma paittaiyyā sāmañ‍ñā brahmañ‍ñā kulai jaiṭṭhāpacāyinau, idaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ samādāya vattaiyyāmā’ti| tai mattaiyyā bhavissanti paittaiyyā sāmañ‍ñā  brahmañ‍ñā kulai jaiṭṭhāpacāyinau, idaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ samādāya vattissanti|

‘‘tai kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samādānahaitu āyunāpi vaḍḍhissanti, vaṇṇainapi vaḍḍhissanti| taisaṃ āyunāpi vaḍḍhamānānaṃ vaṇṇainapi vaḍḍhamānānaṃ vīsativassāyukānaṃ manussānaṃ cattārīsavassāyukā puttā bhavissanti… cattārīsavassāyukānaṃ manussānaṃ asītivassāyukā puttā bhavissanti… asītivassāyukānaṃ manussānaṃ saṭṭhivassasatāyukā puttā bhavissanti… saṭṭhivassasatāyukānaṃ manussānaṃ vīsatitivassasatāyukā puttā bhavissanti… vīsatitivassasatāyukānaṃ manussānaṃ cattārīsachabbassasatāyukā puttā bhavissanti| cattārīsachabbassasatāyukānaṃ manussānaṃ dvaivassasahassāyukā puttā bhavissanti… dvaivassasahassāyukānaṃ manussānaṃ cattārivassasahassāyukā  puttā bhavissanti… cattārivassasahassāyukānaṃ manussānaṃ aṭṭhavassasahassāyukā puttā bhavissanti… aṭṭhavassasahassāyukānaṃ manussānaṃ vīsativassasahassāyukā puttā bhavissanti… vīsativassasahassāyukānaṃ  manussānaṃ cattārīsavassasahassāyukā  puttā bhavissanti… cattārīsavassasahassāyukānaṃ manussānaṃ asītivassasahassāyukā puttā bhavissanti… asītivassasahassāyukaisu, bhikkhavai, manussaisu pañ‍cavassasatikā kumārikā alaṃpataiyyā bhavissanti|

saṅkharājauppatti

106. ‘‘asītivassasahassāyukaisu, bhikkhavai, manussaisu tayau ābādhā bhavissanti, icchā, anasanaṃ, jarā| asītivassasahassāyukaisu, bhikkhavai, manussaisu ayaṃ jambudīpau iddhau caiva bhavissati phītau ca, kuk‍kuṭasampātikā gāmanigamarājadhāniyau gāmanigamajanapadā rājadhāniyau (ka॰)| asītivassasahassāyukaisu, bhikkhavai, manussaisu ayaṃ jambudīpau avīci mañ‍ñai phuṭau bhavissati manussaihi, saiyyathāpi naḷavanaṃ vā saravanaṃ sāravanaṃ (syā॰) vā| asītivassasahassāyukaisu, bhikkhavai, manussaisu ayaṃ bārāṇasī kaitumatī nāma rājadhānī bhavissati iddhā caiva phītā ca bahujanā ca ākiṇṇamanussā ca subhikkhā ca| asītivassasahassāyukaisu, bhikkhavai, manussaisu  imasmiṃ jambudīpai caturāsītinagarasahassāni bhavissanti kaitumatīrājadhānīpamukhāni| asītivassasahassāyukaisu, bhikkhavai, manussaisu kaitumatiyā rājadhāniyā saṅkhau nāma rājā uppaj‍jissati cak‍kavattī dhammikau dhammarājā cāturantau  vijitāvī janapadatthāvariyappattau sattaratanasaman‍nāgatau| tassimāni satta ratanāni bhavissanti, saiyyathidaṃ, cak‍karatanaṃ hatthiratanaṃ assaratanaṃ maṇiratanaṃ itthiratanaṃ gahapatiratanaṃ pariṇāyakaratanamaiva sattamaṃ| parausahassaṃ khau panassa puttā bhavissanti sūrā vīraṅgarūpā parasainappamaddanā| sau imaṃ pathaviṃ sāgarapariyantaṃ adaṇḍaina asatthaina dhammaina abhivijiya ajjhāvasissati|

maittaiyyabuddhuppādau

107. ‘‘asītivassasahassāyukaisu, bhikkhavai, manussaisu maittaiyyau  nāma bhagavā laukai uppaj‍jissati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā| saiyyathāpāhamaitarahi laukai uppan‍nau arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā| sau imaṃ laukaṃ sadaivakaṃ  samārakaṃ sabrahmakaṃ sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiṃ pajaṃ sadaivamanussaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaissati, saiyyathāpāhamaitarahi imaṃ laukaṃ sadaivakaṃ samārakaṃ sabrahmakaṃ sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiṃ pajaṃ sadaivamanussaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaimi| sau dhammaṃ daisaissati ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaissati; saiyyathāpāhamaitarahi dhammaṃ daisaimi ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaimi| sau anaikasahassaṃ anaikasatasahassaṃ (ka॰) bhikkhusaṃghaṃ  pariharissati, saiyyathāpāhamaitarahi anaikasataṃ bhikkhusaṃghaṃ pariharāmi|

108. ‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, saṅkhau nāma rājā yau sau yūpau rañ‍ñā mahāpanādaina kārāpitau| taṃ yūpaṃ ussāpaitvā ajjhāvasitvā taṃ datvā  vissaj‍jitvā samaṇabrāhmaṇakapaṇaddhikavaṇibbakayācakānaṃ dānaṃ datvā maittaiyyassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa santikai kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajissati| sau aivaṃ pabbajitau samānau aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau nacirassaiva yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā  anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti, tadanuttaraṃ brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharissati|

109. ‘‘attadīpā, bhikkhavai, viharatha attasaraṇā anañ‍ñasaraṇā, dhammadīpā dhammasaraṇā anañ‍ñasaraṇā| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu attadīpau viharati attasaraṇau anañ‍ñasaraṇau dhammadīpau dhammasaraṇau anañ‍ñasaraṇau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī…pai॰… cittai cittānupassī…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| aivaṃ  khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu attadīpau viharati attasaraṇau anañ‍ñasaraṇau dhammadīpau dhammasaraṇau anañ‍ñasaraṇau|

bhikkhunauāyuvaṇṇādivaḍḍhanakathā

110. ‘‘gaucarai, bhikkhavai, caratha sakai paittikai visayai| gaucarai, bhikkhavai, carantā sakai paittikai visayai āyunāpi vaḍḍhissatha, vaṇṇainapi vaḍḍhissatha, sukhainapi vaḍḍhissatha, bhaugainapi vaḍḍhissatha, balainapi vaḍḍhissatha|

‘‘kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau āyusmiṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, vīriyasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, cittasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, vīmaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti| sau imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā ākaṅkhamānau kappaṃ vā tiṭṭhaiyya kappāvasaisaṃ vā| idaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau āyusmiṃ|

‘‘kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau vaṇṇasmiṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlavā hauti, pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau  viharati ācāragaucarasampan‍nau, aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu  bhayadassāvī, samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu| idaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau vaṇṇasmiṃ|

‘‘kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau sukhasmiṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| idaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau, sukhasmiṃ|

‘‘kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau bhaugasmiṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu maittāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā  viharati tathā dutiyaṃ| tathā tatiyaṃ| tathā catutthaṃ| iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ maittāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina pharitvā viharati| karuṇāsahagataina caitasā…pai॰… muditāsahagataina caitasā…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati| tathā dutiyaṃ| tathā tatiyaṃ| tathā catutthaṃ| iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina pharitvā viharati| idaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau bhaugasmiṃ|

‘‘kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau balasmiṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| idaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau balasmiṃ|

‘‘nāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñaṃ aikabalampi samanupassāmi yaṃ aivaṃ duppasahaṃ, yathayidaṃ, bhikkhavai, mārabalaṃ| kusalānaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammānaṃ samādānahaitu aivamidaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ pavaḍḍhatī’’ti| idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

cak‍kavattisuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ tatiyaṃ|



4. aggañ‍ñasuttaṃ

vāsaiṭṭhabhāradvājā

111. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati pubbārāmai migāramātupāsādai| taina khau pana samayaina vāsaiṭṭhabhāradvājā bhikkhūsu parivasanti bhikkhubhāvaṃ ākaṅkhamānā| atha khau bhagavā sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau pāsādā aurauhitvā pāsādapacchāyāyaṃ pāsādacchāyāyaṃ (ka॰) abbhaukāsai caṅkamati|

112. addasā khau vāsaiṭṭhau bhagavantaṃ sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitaṃ pāsādā aurauhitvā pāsādapacchāyāyaṃ abbhaukāsai caṅkamantaṃ| disvāna bhāradvājaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘ayaṃ, āvusau bhāradvāja, bhagavā sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau pāsādā aurauhitvā pāsādapacchāyāyaṃ abbhaukāsai caṅkamati| āyāmāvusau bhāradvāja, yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamissāma; appaiva nāma labhaiyyāma bhagavatau santikā sammukhā (syā॰ ka॰) dhammiṃ kathaṃ savanāyā’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau bhāradvājau vāsaiṭṭhassa pac‍cassausi|

113. atha khau vāsaiṭṭhabhāradvājā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā bhagavantaṃ caṅkamantaṃ anucaṅkamiṃsu| atha khau bhagavā vāsaiṭṭhaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘tumhai  khvattha, vāsaiṭṭha, brāhmaṇajac‍cā brāhmaṇakulīnā brāhmaṇakulā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitā, kac‍ci vau, vāsaiṭṭha, brāhmaṇā na ak‍kausanti na paribhāsantī’’ti? ‘‘taggha nau, bhantai, brāhmaṇā ak‍kausanti paribhāsanti attarūpāya paribhāsāya  paripuṇṇāya, nau aparipuṇṇāyā’’ti| ‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana vau, vāsaiṭṭha, brāhmaṇā ak‍kausanti paribhāsanti attarūpāya paribhāsāya paripuṇṇāya, nau aparipuṇṇāyā’’ti? ‘‘brāhmaṇā, bhantai, aivamāhaṃsu – ‘brāhmaṇauva saiṭṭhau vaṇṇau, hīnā añ‍ñai vaṇṇā hīnau añ‍ñau vaṇṇau (sī॰ pī॰ ma॰ ni॰ 2 madhurasutta)| brāhmaṇauva suk‍kau vaṇṇau, kaṇhā  añ‍ñai vaṇṇā kaṇhau añ‍ñau vaṇṇau (sī॰ pī॰ ma॰ ni॰ 2 madhurasutta)| brāhmaṇāva sujjhanti, nau abrāhmaṇā| brāhmaṇāva brāhmaṇā (syā॰) brahmunau puttā aurasā mukhatau jātā brahmajā brahmanimmitā brahmadāyādā| tai tumhai saiṭṭhaṃ vaṇṇaṃ hitvā hīnamattha vaṇṇaṃ ajjhupagatā, yadidaṃ muṇḍakai samaṇakai ibbhai kaṇhai bandhupādāpac‍cai| tayidaṃ na sādhu, tayidaṃ nappatirūpaṃ, yaṃ tumhai saiṭṭhaṃ vaṇṇaṃ hitvā hīnamattha vaṇṇaṃ ajjhupagatā yadidaṃ muṇḍakai samaṇakai ibbhai kaṇhai bandhupādāpac‍cai’ti| aivaṃ khau nau, bhantai, brāhmaṇā ak‍kausanti paribhāsanti attarūpāya paribhāsāya paripuṇṇāya, nau aparipuṇṇāyā’’ti|

114. ‘‘taggha vau, vāsaiṭṭha, brāhmaṇā paurāṇaṃ assarantā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘brāhmaṇauva saiṭṭhau vaṇṇau, hīnā añ‍ñai vaṇṇā; brāhmaṇauva suk‍kau vaṇṇau, kaṇhā añ‍ñai vaṇṇā; brāhmaṇāva sujjhanti, nau abrāhmaṇā; brāhmaṇāva brahmunau puttā aurasā mukhatau jātā brahmajā brahmanimmitā brahmadāyādā’ti| dissanti khau pana, vāsaiṭṭha, brāhmaṇānaṃ brāhmaṇiyau utuniyaupi gabbhiniyaupi vijāyamānāpi  pāyamānāpi| tai ca brāhmaṇā yaunijāva samānā aivamāhaṃsu  – ‘brāhmaṇauva saiṭṭhau vaṇṇau, hīnā añ‍ñai vaṇṇā; brāhmaṇauva suk‍kau vaṇṇau, kaṇhā añ‍ñai vaṇṇā; brāhmaṇāva sujjhanti, nau abrāhmaṇā; brāhmaṇāva brahmunau puttā aurasā mukhatau jātā brahmajā brahmanimmitā brahmadāyādā’ti| tai tai ca (syā॰ ka॰) brahmānañ‍caiva abbhācikkhanti, musā ca bhāsanti, bahuñ‍ca apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavanti|

catuvaṇṇasuddhi

115. ‘‘cattāraumai, vāsaiṭṭha, vaṇṇā – khattiyā, brāhmaṇā, vaissā, suddā| khattiyaupi khau, vāsaiṭṭha, idhaikac‍cau pāṇātipātī hauti adin‍nādāyī kāmaisumicchācārī musāvādī pisuṇavācau pharusavācau samphappalāpī abhijjhālu byāpan‍nacittau micchādiṭṭhī| iti khau, vāsaiṭṭha, yaimai dhammā akusalā akusalasaṅkhātā sāvaj‍jā sāvaj‍jasaṅkhātā asaivitabbā asaivitabbasaṅkhātā naalamariyā naalamariyasaṅkhātā kaṇhā kaṇhavipākā viñ‍ñugarahitā, khattiyaipi tai khau vāsaiṭṭha (ka॰) idhaikac‍cai sandissanti| brāhmaṇaupi khau, vāsaiṭṭha…pai॰… vaissaupi khau, vāsaiṭṭha…pai॰… suddaupi khau, vāsaiṭṭha, idhaikac‍cau  pāṇātipātī hauti adin‍nādāyī kāmaisumicchācārī musāvādī pisuṇavācau pharusavācau samphappalāpī abhijjhālu byāpan‍nacittau micchādiṭṭhī| iti khau, vāsaiṭṭha, yaimai dhammā akusalā akusalasaṅkhātā…pai॰… kaṇhā kaṇhavipākā viñ‍ñugarahitā; suddaipi tai idhaikac‍cai sandissanti|

‘‘khattiyaupi  khau, vāsaiṭṭha, idhaikac‍cau pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau, kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau, musāvādā paṭiviratau, pisuṇāya vācāya paṭiviratau, pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratau, samphappalāpā paṭiviratau, anabhijjhālu abyāpan‍nacittau, sammādiṭṭhī| iti khau, vāsaiṭṭha, yaimai dhammā kusalā kusalasaṅkhātā anavaj‍jā  anavaj‍jasaṅkhātā saivitabbā saivitabbasaṅkhātā alamariyā alamariyasaṅkhātā suk‍kā suk‍kavipākā viñ‍ñuppasatthā, khattiyaipi tai idhaikac‍cai sandissanti| brāhmaṇaupi khau, vāsaiṭṭha…pai॰… vaissaupi khau, vāsaiṭṭha…pai॰… suddaupi khau, vāsaiṭṭha, idhaikac‍cau pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti…pai॰… anabhijjhālu, abyāpan‍nacittau, sammādiṭṭhī| iti khau, vāsaiṭṭha, yaimai dhammā kusalā kusalasaṅkhātā anavaj‍jā anavaj‍jasaṅkhātā saivitabbā saivitabbasaṅkhātā alamariyā alamariyasaṅkhātā suk‍kā suk‍kavipākā viñ‍ñuppasatthā; suddaipi tai idhaikac‍cai sandissanti|

116. ‘‘imaisu khau, vāsaiṭṭha, catūsu vaṇṇaisu aivaṃ ubhayavaukiṇṇaisu vattamānaisu kaṇhasuk‍kaisu dhammaisu viñ‍ñugarahitaisu caiva viñ‍ñuppasatthaisu ca yadaittha brāhmaṇā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘brāhmaṇauva saiṭṭhau vaṇṇau, hīnā añ‍ñai vaṇṇā; brāhmaṇauva suk‍kau vaṇṇau, kaṇhā añ‍ñai vaṇṇā; brāhmaṇāva sujjhanti, nau abrāhmaṇā; brāhmaṇāva brahmunau puttā aurasā mukhatau jātā brahmajā brahmanimmitā brahmadāyādā’ti| taṃ taisaṃ viñ‍ñū nānujānanti| taṃ kissa haitu? imaisañhi, vāsaiṭṭha, catun‍naṃ vaṇṇānaṃ yau hauti bhikkhu arahaṃ khīṇāsavau vusitavā katakaraṇīyau auhitabhārau anuppattasadatthau parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanau sammadañ‍ñāvimuttau, sau naisaṃ aggamakkhāyati dhammainaiva, nau adhammaina| dhammau hi, vāsaiṭṭha, saiṭṭhau janaitasmiṃ, diṭṭhai caiva dhammai abhisamparāyañ‍ca|

117. ‘‘tadamināpaitaṃ, vāsaiṭṭha, pariyāyaina vaiditabbaṃ, yathā dhammauva saiṭṭhau janaitasmiṃ, diṭṭhai caiva dhammai abhisamparāyañ‍ca|

‘‘jānāti  khau khau pana (ka॰), vāsaiṭṭha, rājā pasainadi kausalau – ‘samaṇau gautamau anantarā anuttarau (bahūsu) sakyakulā pabbajitau’ti| sakyā khau pana, vāsaiṭṭha, rañ‍ñau pasainadissa kausalassa anuyuttā anantarā anuyantā (syā॰), anantarā anuyuttā (ka॰) bhavanti| karaunti khau, vāsaiṭṭha, sakyā rañ‍ñai pasainadimhi kausalai nipac‍cakāraṃ abhivādanaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ añ‍jalikammaṃ sāmīcikammaṃ| iti khau, vāsaiṭṭha, yaṃ karaunti sakyā rañ‍ñai pasainadimhi kausalai nipac‍cakāraṃ abhivādanaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ añ‍jalikammaṃ sāmīcikammaṃ, karauti  taṃ  rājā pasainadi kausalau tathāgatai nipac‍cakāraṃ abhivādanaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ añ‍jalikammaṃ sāmīcikammaṃ, na naṃ nanu (bahūsu) ‘sujātau samaṇau gautamau, duj‍jātauhamasmi| balavā samaṇau gautamau, dubbalauhamasmi| pāsādikau samaṇau gautamau, dubbaṇṇauhamasmi| mahaisakkhau samaṇau gautamau, appaisakkhauhamasmī’ti| atha khau naṃ dhammaṃyaiva sak‍karauntau dhammaṃ garuṃ karauntau dhammaṃ mānaintau dhammaṃ pūjaintau dhammaṃ apacāyamānau aivaṃ rājā pasainadi kausalau tathāgatai nipac‍cakāraṃ karauti, abhivādanaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ añ‍jalikammaṃ sāmīcikammaṃ| imināpi khau aitaṃ, vāsaiṭṭha, pariyāyaina vaiditabbaṃ, yathā dhammauva saiṭṭhau janaitasmiṃ, diṭṭhai caiva dhammai abhisamparāyañ‍ca|

118. ‘‘tumhai khvattha, vāsaiṭṭha, nānājac‍cā nānānāmā nānāgauttā nānākulā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitā| ‘kai tumhai’ti – puṭṭhā  samānā ‘samaṇā sakyaputtiyāmhā’ti – paṭijānātha| yassa khau panassa, vāsaiṭṭha, tathāgatai saddhā niviṭṭhā mūlajātā patiṭṭhitā daḷhā asaṃhāriyā samaṇaina vā brāhmaṇaina vā daivaina vā māraina vā brahmunā vā kainaci vā laukasmiṃ, tassaitaṃ kal‍laṃ vacanāya – ‘bhagavataumhi puttau aurasau mukhatau jātau dhammajau dhammanimmitau dhammadāyādau’ti| taṃ kissa haitu? tathāgatassa haitaṃ, vāsaiṭṭha, adhivacanaṃ ‘dhammakāyau’ itipi, ‘brahmakāyau’ itipi, ‘dhammabhūtau’ itipi, ‘brahmabhūtau’ itipi|

119. ‘‘hauti khau sau, vāsaiṭṭha, samayau yaṃ kadāci karahaci dīghassa addhunau ac‍cayaina ayaṃ laukau saṃvaṭṭati| saṃvaṭṭamānai laukai yaibhuyyaina sattā ābhassarasaṃvattanikā haunti| tai tattha haunti  manaumayā pītibhakkhā sayaṃpabhā antalikkhacarā subhaṭṭhāyinau ciraṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ tiṭṭhanti|

‘‘hauti khau sau, vāsaiṭṭha, samayau yaṃ kadāci karahaci dīghassa addhunau ac‍cayaina ayaṃ laukau vivaṭṭati| vivaṭṭamānai laukai yaibhuyyaina sattā ābhassarakāyā  cavitvā itthattaṃ āgacchanti| taidha haunti manaumayā pītibhakkhā sayaṃpabhā antalikkhacarā subhaṭṭhāyinau ciraṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ tiṭṭhanti|

rasapathavipātubhāvau

120. ‘‘aikaudakībhūtaṃ khau pana, vāsaiṭṭha, taina samayaina hauti andhakārau andhakāratimisā| na candimasūriyā pañ‍ñāyanti, na nakkhattāni tārakarūpāni pañ‍ñāyanti, na rattindivā pañ‍ñāyanti, na māsaḍḍhamāsā pañ‍ñāyanti, na utusaṃvaccharā pañ‍ñāyanti, na itthipumā pañ‍ñāyanti, sattā sattātvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchanti| atha khau taisaṃ, vāsaiṭṭha, sattānaṃ kadāci karahaci dīghassa addhunau ac‍cayaina rasapathavī udakasmiṃ samatani samatāni (bahūsu); saiyyathāpi nāma payasau tattassa payatattassa (syā॰) nibbāyamānassa upari santānakaṃ hauti, aivamaiva pāturahausi| sā ahausi vaṇṇasampan‍nā gandhasampan‍nā rasasampan‍nā, saiyyathāpi nāma sampan‍naṃ vā sappi sampan‍naṃ vā navanītaṃ aivaṃvaṇṇā ahausi| saiyyathāpi nāma khuddamadhuṃ khuddaṃ madhuṃ (ka॰ sī॰) anaiḷakaṃ anailakaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), aivamassādā ahausi| atha khau, vāsaiṭṭha, añ‍ñatarau sattau laulajātikau – ‘ambhau, kimaividaṃ bhavissatī’ti rasapathaviṃ aṅguliyā sāyi| tassa rasapathaviṃ aṅguliyā sāyatau acchādaisi, taṇhā cassa auk‍kami| añ‍ñaipi khau, vāsaiṭṭha, sattā tassa sattassa diṭṭhānugatiṃ āpaj‍jamānā rasapathaviṃ aṅguliyā sāyiṃsu| taisaṃ rasapathaviṃ aṅguliyā sāyataṃ acchādaisi, taṇhā ca taisaṃ auk‍kami|

candimasūriyādipātubhāvau

121. ‘‘atha khau tai, vāsaiṭṭha, sattā rasapathaviṃ hatthaihi āluppakārakaṃ upak‍kamiṃsu paribhuñ‍jituṃ| yatau khau  tai yatau khau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), vāsaiṭṭha, sattā rasapathaviṃ  hatthaihi āluppakārakaṃ upak‍kamiṃsu paribhuñ‍jituṃ| atha taisaṃ sattānaṃ sayaṃpabhā antaradhāyi| sayaṃpabhāya antarahitāya candimasūriyā pāturahaisuṃ| candimasūriyaisu pātubhūtaisu nakkhattāni tārakarūpāni pāturahaisuṃ| nakkhattaisu tārakarūpaisu pātubhūtaisu rattindivā pañ‍ñāyiṃsu| rattindivaisu pañ‍ñāyamānaisu māsaḍḍhamāsā pañ‍ñāyiṃsu| māsaḍḍhamāsaisu pañ‍ñāyamānaisu utusaṃvaccharā pañ‍ñāyiṃsu| aittāvatā khau, vāsaiṭṭha, ayaṃ laukau puna vivaṭṭau hauti|

122. ‘‘atha khau tai, vāsaiṭṭha, sattā rasapathaviṃ paribhuñ‍jantā taṃbhakkhā tabbhakkhā (syā॰) tadāhārā ciraṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu| yathā yathā khau tai, vāsaiṭṭha, sattā rasapathaviṃ paribhuñ‍jantā taṃbhakkhā tadāhārā ciraṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu, tathā tathā taisaṃ sattānaṃ (rasapathaviṃ paribhuñ‍jantānaṃ) ( ) sī॰ syā॰ pī॰ pautthakaisu natthi kharattañ‍caiva kāyasmiṃ auk‍kami, vaṇṇavaivaṇṇatā vaṇṇavaivaj‍jatā (ṭīkā) ca pañ‍ñāyittha| aikidaṃ sattā vaṇṇavantau haunti, aikidaṃ sattā dubbaṇṇā| tattha yai tai sattā vaṇṇavantau, tai dubbaṇṇai sattai atimañ‍ñanti – ‘mayamaitaihi vaṇṇavantatarā, amhaihaitai dubbaṇṇatarā’ti| taisaṃ vaṇṇātimānapac‍cayā mānātimānajātikānaṃ rasapathavī antaradhāyi| rasāya pathaviyā antarahitāya san‍nipatiṃsu| san‍nipatitvā anutthuniṃsu – ‘ahau rasaṃ, ahau rasa’nti! tadaitarahipi  manussā kañ‍cidaiva surasaṃ sādhurasaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) labhitvā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘ahau rasaṃ, ahau rasa’nti! tadaiva paurāṇaṃ aggañ‍ñaṃ akkharaṃ anusaranti, na tvaivassa atthaṃ ājānanti|

bhūmipappaṭakapātubhāvau

123. ‘‘atha khau taisaṃ, vāsaiṭṭha, sattānaṃ rasāya pathaviyā  antarahitāya bhūmipappaṭakau pāturahausi| saiyyathāpi nāma ahicchattakau, aivamaiva pāturahausi| sau ahausi vaṇṇasampan‍nau gandhasampan‍nau rasasampan‍nau, saiyyathāpi nāma sampan‍naṃ vā sappi sampan‍naṃ vā navanītaṃ aivaṃvaṇṇau ahausi| saiyyathāpi nāma khuddamadhuṃ anaiḷakaṃ, aivamassādau  ahausi|

‘‘atha  khau tai, vāsaiṭṭha, sattā bhūmipappaṭakaṃ upak‍kamiṃsu paribhuñ‍jituṃ| tai taṃ paribhuñ‍jantā taṃbhakkhā tadāhārā ciraṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu| yathā yathā khau tai, vāsaiṭṭha, sattā bhūmipappaṭakaṃ paribhuñ‍jantā taṃbhakkhā tadāhārā ciraṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu, tathā tathā taisaṃ sattānaṃ bhiyyausau mattāya kharattañ‍caiva kāyasmiṃ auk‍kami, vaṇṇavaivaṇṇatā ca pañ‍ñāyittha| aikidaṃ sattā vaṇṇavantau haunti, aikidaṃ sattā dubbaṇṇā| tattha yai tai sattā vaṇṇavantau, tai dubbaṇṇai sattai atimañ‍ñanti – ‘mayamaitaihi vaṇṇavantatarā, amhaihaitai dubbaṇṇatarā’ti| taisaṃ vaṇṇātimānapac‍cayā mānātimānajātikānaṃ bhūmipappaṭakau antaradhāyi|

padālatāpātubhāvau

124. ‘‘bhūmipappaṭakai antarahitai padālatā saddālatā (sī॰) pāturahausi, saiyyathāpi nāma kalambukā kalambakā (syā॰), aivamaiva pāturahausi| sā ahausi vaṇṇasampan‍nā gandhasampan‍nā rasasampan‍nā, saiyyathāpi nāma sampan‍naṃ vā sappi sampan‍naṃ vā navanītaṃ aivaṃvaṇṇā ahausi| saiyyathāpi nāma khuddamadhuṃ anaiḷakaṃ, aivamassādā ahausi|

‘‘atha khau tai, vāsaiṭṭha, sattā padālataṃ upak‍kamiṃsu paribhuñ‍jituṃ| tai taṃ paribhuñ‍jantā taṃbhakkhā tadāhārā ciraṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu| yathā yathā khau tai, vāsaiṭṭha, sattā padālataṃ paribhuñ‍jantā taṃbhakkhā tadāhārā ciraṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu, tathā tathā taisaṃ sattānaṃ bhiyyausaumattāya kharattañ‍caiva kāyasmiṃ auk‍kami, vaṇṇavaivaṇṇatā ca pañ‍ñāyittha| aikidaṃ  sattā  vaṇṇavantau haunti, aikidaṃ sattā dubbaṇṇā| tattha yai tai sattā vaṇṇavantau, tai dubbaṇṇai sattai  atimañ‍ñanti – ‘mayamaitaihi vaṇṇavantatarā, amhaihaitai dubbaṇṇatarā’ti| taisaṃ vaṇṇātimānapac‍cayā mānātimānajātikānaṃ padālatā antaradhāyi|

‘‘padālatāya antarahitāya san‍nipatiṃsu| san‍nipatitvā anutthuniṃsu – ‘ahu vata nau, ahāyi vata nau padālatā’ti! tadaitarahipi manussā kainaci kainacidaiva (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) dukkhadhammaina phuṭṭhā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘ahu vata nau, ahāyi  vata nau’ti! tadaiva paurāṇaṃ aggañ‍ñaṃ akkharaṃ anusaranti, na tvaivassa atthaṃ ājānanti|

akaṭṭhapākasālipātubhāvau

125. ‘‘atha khau taisaṃ, vāsaiṭṭha, sattānaṃ padālatāya antarahitāya akaṭṭhapākau sāli pāturahausi akaṇau athusau suddhau sugandhau taṇḍulapphalau| yaṃ taṃ sāyaṃ sāyamāsāya āharanti, pātau taṃ hauti pak‍kaṃ paṭivirūḷhaṃ| yaṃ taṃ pātau pātarāsāya āharanti, sāyaṃ taṃ hauti pak‍kaṃ paṭivirūḷhaṃ; nāpadānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati| atha khau tai, vāsaiṭṭha, sattā akaṭṭhapākaṃ sāliṃ paribhuñ‍jantā taṃbhakkhā tadāhārā ciraṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu|

itthipurisaliṅgapātubhāvau

126. ‘‘yathā yathā khau tai, vāsaiṭṭha, sattā akaṭṭhapākaṃ sāliṃ paribhuñ‍jantā taṃbhakkhā tadāhārā ciraṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu, tathā tathā taisaṃ sattānaṃ bhiyyausaumattāya kharattañ‍caiva kāyasmiṃ auk‍kami, vaṇṇavaivaṇṇatā ca pañ‍ñāyittha, itthiyā ca itthiliṅgaṃ pāturahausi purisassa ca purisaliṅgaṃ| itthī  ca purisaṃ ativailaṃ upanijjhāyati purisau ca itthiṃ| taisaṃ ativailaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ upanijjhāyataṃ sārāgau udapādi, pariḷāhau kāyasmiṃ auk‍kami| tai pariḷāhapac‍cayā maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaiviṃsu|

‘‘yai khau pana tai, vāsaiṭṭha, taina samayaina sattā passanti maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivantai, añ‍ñai paṃsuṃ khipanti, añ‍ñai saiṭṭhiṃ khipanti, añ‍ñai gaumayaṃ khipanti – ‘nassa asuci vasali (syā॰), vasalī (ka॰), nassa asucī’ti| ‘kathañhi nāma sattau sattassa aivarūpaṃ karissatī’ti! tadaitarahipi manussā aikac‍caisu janapadaisu vadhuyā nibbuyhamānāya nivayhamānāya, niggayhamānāya (ka॰) añ‍ñai paṃsuṃ khipanti, añ‍ñai saiṭṭhiṃ khipanti, añ‍ñai  gaumayaṃ khipanti| tadaiva paurāṇaṃ aggañ‍ñaṃ akkharaṃ anusaranti, na tvaivassa atthaṃ ājānanti|

maithunadhammasamācārau

127. ‘‘adhammasammataṃ  khau pana adhammasammataṃ taṃ khau pana (syā॰), adhammasammataṃ khau pana taṃ (?), vāsaiṭṭha, taina samayaina hauti, tadaitarahi dhammasammataṃ| yai khau pana, vāsaiṭṭha, taina samayaina sattā maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivanti, tai māsampi dvaimāsampi na labhanti gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā pavisituṃ| yatau khau tai, vāsaiṭṭha, sattā tasmiṃ asaddhammai ativailaṃ pātabyataṃ āpaj‍jiṃsu| atha agārāni upak‍kamiṃsu kātuṃ tassaiva asaddhammassa paṭicchādanatthaṃ| atha khau, vāsaiṭṭha, añ‍ñatarassa sattassa alasajātikassa aitadahausi – ‘ambhau, kimaivāhaṃ vihañ‍ñāmi sāliṃ āharantau sāyaṃ sāyamāsāya pātau pātarāsāya! yaṃnūnāhaṃ sāliṃ āharaiyyaṃ sakiṃdaiva sakiṃdaiva (ka॰) sāyapātarāsāyā’ti!

‘‘atha khau sau, vāsaiṭṭha, sattau sāliṃ āhāsi sakiṃdaiva sāyapātarāsāya| atha khau, vāsaiṭṭha, añ‍ñatarau sattau yaina sau sattau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ sattaṃ aitadavauca – ‘aihi, bhau satta, sālāhāraṃ gamissāmā’ti| ‘alaṃ, bhau satta, āhatau āhaṭau (syā॰) mai sāli sakiṃdaiva sāyapātarāsāyā’ti| atha khau sau, vāsaiṭṭha, sattau tassa sattassa diṭṭhānugatiṃ āpaj‍jamānau sāliṃ āhāsi sakiṃdaiva dvīhāya| ‘aivampi kira, bhau, sādhū’ti|

‘‘atha khau, vāsaiṭṭha, añ‍ñatarau sattau yaina sau sattau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā  taṃ sattaṃ aitadavauca – ‘aihi, bhau satta, sālāhāraṃ gamissāmā’ti| ‘alaṃ, bhau satta, āhatau mai sāli sakiṃdaiva dvīhāyā’ti| atha khau sau, vāsaiṭṭha, sattau tassa sattassa diṭṭhānugatiṃ āpaj‍jamānau sāliṃ āhāsi sakiṃdaiva catūhāya, ‘aivampi kira, bhau, sādhū’ti|

‘‘atha khau, vāsaiṭṭha, añ‍ñatarau sattau yaina sau sattau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ sattaṃ aitadavauca – ‘aihi, bhau satta, sālāhāraṃ gamissāmā’ti| ‘alaṃ, bhau satta, āhatau mai sāli sakidaiva catūhāyā’ti| atha khau sau, vāsaiṭṭha, sattau tassa sattassa  diṭṭhānugatiṃ āpaj‍jamānau sāliṃ āhāsi sakidaiva aṭṭhāhāya, ‘aivampi kira, bhau, sādhū’ti|

‘‘yatau khau tai, vāsaiṭṭha, sattā san‍nidhikārakaṃ sāliṃ upak‍kamiṃsu paribhuñ‍jituṃ| atha kaṇaupi  taṇḍulaṃ pariyaunandhi, thusaupi taṇḍulaṃ pariyaunandhi; lūnampi nappaṭivirūḷhaṃ, apadānaṃ pañ‍ñāyittha, saṇḍasaṇḍā sālayau aṭṭhaṃsu|

sālivibhāgau

128. ‘‘atha khau tai, vāsaiṭṭha, sattā san‍nipatiṃsu, san‍nipatitvā anutthuniṃsu – ‘pāpakā vata, bhau, dhammā sattaisu pātubhūtā| mayañhi pubbai manaumayā ahumhā pītibhakkhā sayaṃpabhā antalikkhacarā subhaṭṭhāyinau, ciraṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ aṭṭhamhā| taisaṃ nau amhākaṃ kadāci karahaci dīghassa addhunau ac‍cayaina rasapathavī udakasmiṃ samatani| sā ahausi vaṇṇasampan‍nā gandhasampan‍nā rasasampan‍nā| tai mayaṃ rasapathaviṃ hatthaihi āluppakārakaṃ upak‍kamimha paribhuñ‍jituṃ, taisaṃ nau rasapathaviṃ hatthaihi āluppakārakaṃ upak‍kamataṃ paribhuñ‍jituṃ sayaṃpabhā antaradhāyi| sayaṃpabhāya antarahitāya candimasūriyā pāturahaisuṃ, candimasūriyaisu pātubhūtaisu nakkhattāni tārakarūpāni  pāturahaisuṃ, nakkhattaisu tārakarūpaisu pātubhūtaisu rattindivā pañ‍ñāyiṃsu, rattindivaisu pañ‍ñāyamānaisu māsaḍḍhamāsā pañ‍ñāyiṃsu| māsaḍḍhamāsaisu pañ‍ñāyamānaisu utusaṃvaccharā pañ‍ñāyiṃsu| tai mayaṃ rasapathaviṃ paribhuñ‍jantā taṃbhakkhā tadāhārā ciraṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ aṭṭhamhā| taisaṃ nau pāpakānaṃyaiva akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pātubhāvā rasapathavī antaradhāyi| rasapathaviyā antarahitāya bhūmipappaṭakau pāturahausi| sau ahausi vaṇṇasampan‍nau gandhasampan‍nau rasasampan‍nau| tai mayaṃ bhūmipappaṭakaṃ upak‍kamimha paribhuñ‍jituṃ| tai mayaṃ taṃ paribhuñ‍jantā taṃbhakkhā tadāhārā ciraṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ aṭṭhamhā| taisaṃ nau pāpakānaṃyaiva akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pātubhāvā  bhūmipappaṭakau antaradhāyi| bhūmipappaṭakai antarahitai padālatā pāturahausi| sā ahausi vaṇṇasampan‍nā gandhasampan‍nā rasasampan‍nā| tai mayaṃ padālataṃ upak‍kamimha paribhuñ‍jituṃ| tai mayaṃ taṃ paribhuñ‍jantā taṃbhakkhā tadāhārā ciraṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ aṭṭhamhā| taisaṃ nau pāpakānaṃyaiva akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pātubhāvā  padālatā antaradhāyi| padālatāya antarahitāya akaṭṭhapākau sāli pāturahausi akaṇau athusau suddhau sugandhau taṇḍulapphalau| yaṃ taṃ sāyaṃ sāyamāsāya āharāma, pātau taṃ hauti pak‍kaṃ paṭivirūḷhaṃ| yaṃ taṃ pātau pātarāsāya āharāma, sāyaṃ taṃ hauti pak‍kaṃ paṭivirūḷhaṃ| nāpadānaṃ pañ‍ñāyittha| tai mayaṃ akaṭṭhapākaṃ sāliṃ paribhuñ‍jantā taṃbhakkhā tadāhārā ciraṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ aṭṭhamhā| taisaṃ nau pāpakānaṃyaiva akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pātubhāvā kaṇaupi taṇḍulaṃ pariyaunandhi, thusaupi taṇḍulaṃ pariyaunandhi, lūnampi nappaṭivirūḷhaṃ, apadānaṃ pañ‍ñāyittha, saṇḍasaṇḍā  sālayau ṭhitā| yaṃnūna mayaṃ  sāliṃ vibhajaiyyāma, mariyādaṃ ṭhapaiyyāmā’ti! atha khau tai, vāsaiṭṭha, sattā sāliṃ vibhajiṃsu, mariyādaṃ ṭhapaisuṃ|

129. ‘‘atha khau, vāsaiṭṭha, añ‍ñatarau sattau laulajātikau sakaṃ bhāgaṃ parirakkhantau añ‍ñataraṃ añ‍ñassa (?) bhāgaṃ adin‍naṃ ādiyitvā paribhuñ‍ji| tamainaṃ aggahaisuṃ, gahaitvā aitadavaucuṃ – ‘pāpakaṃ vata, bhau satta, karausi, yatra hi nāma sakaṃ bhāgaṃ parirakkhantau añ‍ñataraṃ bhāgaṃ adin‍naṃ ādiyitvā paribhuñ‍jasi| māssu, bhau satta, punapi aivarūpamakāsī’ti| ‘aivaṃ, bhau’ti khau, vāsaiṭṭha, sau sattau taisaṃ sattānaṃ pac‍cassausi| dutiyampi khau, vāsaiṭṭha, sau sattau…pai॰… tatiyampi khau, vāsaiṭṭha, sau sattau sakaṃ bhāgaṃ parirakkhantau añ‍ñataraṃ bhāgaṃ adin‍naṃ ādiyitvā paribhuñ‍ji| tamainaṃ aggahaisuṃ, gahaitvā aitadavaucuṃ – ‘pāpakaṃ vata, bhau satta, karausi, yatra hi nāma sakaṃ bhāgaṃ parirakkhantau añ‍ñataraṃ bhāgaṃ adin‍naṃ  ādiyitvā paribhuñ‍jasi| māssu, bhau satta, punapi aivarūpamakāsī’ti| añ‍ñai pāṇinā pahariṃsu, añ‍ñai laiḍḍunā pahariṃsu, añ‍ñai daṇḍaina pahariṃsu| tadaggai khau, vāsaiṭṭha, adin‍nādānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, garahā pañ‍ñāyati, musāvādau pañ‍ñāyati, daṇḍādānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati|

mahāsammatarājā

130. ‘‘atha khau tai, vāsaiṭṭha, sattā san‍nipatiṃsu, san‍nipatitvā anutthuniṃsu – ‘pāpakā vata bhau dhammā sattaisu pātubhūtā, yatra hi nāma  adin‍nādānaṃ pañ‍ñāyissati, garahā pañ‍ñāyissati, musāvādau pañ‍ñāyissati, daṇḍādānaṃ pañ‍ñāyissati| yaṃnūna mayaṃ aikaṃ sattaṃ samman‍naiyyāma, yau nau sammā khīyitabbaṃ khīyaiyya, sammā garahitabbaṃ garahaiyya, sammā pabbājaitabbaṃ pabbājaiyya| mayaṃ panassa sālīnaṃ bhāgaṃ anuppadassāmā’ti|

‘‘atha  khau tai, vāsaiṭṭha, sattā yau naisaṃ sattau abhirūpatarau ca dassanīyatarau ca pāsādikatarau ca mahaisakkhatarau ca taṃ sattaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aitadavaucuṃ – ‘aihi, bhau satta, sammā khīyitabbaṃ khīya, sammā garahitabbaṃ garaha, sammā pabbājaitabbaṃ  pabbājaihi| mayaṃ pana tai sālīnaṃ bhāgaṃ anuppadassāmā’ti| ‘aivaṃ, bhau’ti khau, vāsaiṭṭha, sau sattau taisaṃ sattānaṃ paṭissuṇitvā sammā khīyitabbaṃ khīyi, sammā garahitabbaṃ garahi, sammā pabbājaitabbaṃ pabbājaisi| tai panassa sālīnaṃ bhāgaṃ anuppadaṃsu|

131. ‘‘mahājanasammatauti  khau, vāsaiṭṭha, ‘mahāsammatau, mahāsammatau’ tvaiva paṭhamaṃ akkharaṃ upanibbattaṃ| khaittānaṃ adhipatīti khau, vāsaiṭṭha, ‘khattiyau, khattiyau’ tvaiva dutiyaṃ akkharaṃ upanibbattaṃ| dhammaina parai rañ‍jaitīti khau, vāsaiṭṭha, ‘rājā, rājā’ tvaiva tatiyaṃ akkharaṃ upanibbattaṃ| iti khau, vāsaiṭṭha, aivamaitassa khattiyamaṇḍalassa paurāṇaina aggañ‍ñaina akkharaina abhinibbatti ahausi taisaṃyaiva sattānaṃ, anañ‍ñaisaṃ| sadisānaṃyaiva, nau asadisānaṃ| dhammainaiva, nau adhammaina| dhammau hi, vāsaiṭṭha, saiṭṭhau janaitasmiṃ diṭṭhai caiva dhammai abhisamparāyañ‍ca|

brāhmaṇamaṇḍalaṃ

132. ‘‘atha khau taisaṃ, vāsaiṭṭha, sattānaṃyaiva taisaṃ yaiva khau vāsaiṭṭha sattānaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) aikac‍cānaṃ aitadahausi – ‘pāpakā vata, bhau, dhammā sattaisu pātubhūtā, yatra hi nāma adin‍nādānaṃ pañ‍ñāyissati, garahā pañ‍ñāyissati, musāvādau pañ‍ñāyissati, daṇḍādānaṃ pañ‍ñāyissati, pabbājanaṃ pañ‍ñāyissati| yaṃnūna mayaṃ pāpakai akusalai dhammai vāhaiyyāmā’ti| tai pāpakai akusalai dhammai vāhaisuṃ| pāpakai akusalai dhammai vāhaintīti khau, vāsaiṭṭha, ‘brāhmaṇā, brāhmaṇā’ tvaiva paṭhamaṃ akkharaṃ upanibbattaṃ| tai arañ‍ñāyatanai paṇṇakuṭiyau  karitvā paṇṇakuṭīsu jhāyanti vītaṅgārā vītadhūmā pan‍namusalā  sāyaṃ sāyamāsāya pātau pātarāsāya gāmanigamarājadhāniyau ausaranti ghāsamaisamānā ghāsamaisanā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰)| tai ghāsaṃ paṭilabhitvā punadaiva arañ‍ñāyatanai paṇṇakuṭīsu jhāyanti| tamainaṃ manussā disvā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘imai khau, bhau, sattā arañ‍ñāyatanai paṇṇakuṭiyau karitvā paṇṇakuṭīsu jhāyanti, vītaṅgārā vītadhūmā pan‍namusalā sāyaṃ sāyamāsāya pātau pātarāsāya gāmanigamarājadhāniyau ausaranti ghāsamaisamānā| tai ghāsaṃ paṭilabhitvā punadaiva arañ‍ñāyatanai paṇṇakuṭīsu jhāyantī’ti, jhāyantīti khau paṇṇakuṭīsu jhāyanti jhāyantīti khau (sī॰ pī॰), paṇṇakuṭīsu jhāyantīti khau (ka॰), vāsaiṭṭha, ‘jhāyakā, jhāyakā’ tvaiva dutiyaṃ akkharaṃ upanibbattaṃ| taisaṃyaiva khau, vāsaiṭṭha, sattānaṃ aikac‍cai sattā arañ‍ñāyatanai paṇṇakuṭīsu taṃ jhānaṃ anabhisambhuṇamānā anabhisaṃbhūnamānā (katthaci) gāmasāmantaṃ nigamasāmantaṃ ausaritvā ganthai karauntā acchanti| tamainaṃ manussā disvā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘imai khau, bhau, sattā arañ‍ñāyatanai paṇṇakuṭīsu taṃ jhānaṃ anabhisambhuṇamānā gāmasāmantaṃ nigamasāmantaṃ ausaritvā ganthai karauntā acchanti, na dānimai jhāyantī’ti| na dānimai na dānimai jhāyantī na dānimai (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) jhāyantīti khau, vāsaiṭṭha, ‘ajjhāyakā ajjhāyakā’ tvaiva tatiyaṃ akkharaṃ upanibbattaṃ| hīnasammataṃ khau pana, vāsaiṭṭha, taina samayaina hauti, tadaitarahi saiṭṭhasammataṃ| iti khau, vāsaiṭṭha, aivamaitassa brāhmaṇamaṇḍalassa paurāṇaina aggañ‍ñaina akkharaina abhinibbatti ahausi taisaṃyaiva sattānaṃ, anañ‍ñaisaṃ sadisānaṃyaiva nau asadisānaṃ dhammainaiva, nau adhammaina| dhammau hi, vāsaiṭṭha, saiṭṭhau janaitasmiṃ diṭṭhai caiva dhammai abhisamparāyañ‍ca|

vaissamaṇḍalaṃ

133. ‘‘taisaṃyaiva  khau, vāsaiṭṭha, sattānaṃ aikac‍cai sattā maithunaṃ dhammaṃ samādāya visukammantai vissutakammantai (sī॰ pī॰), vissukammantai (ka॰ sī॰), visuṃ kammantai (syā॰ ka॰) payaujaisuṃ| maithunaṃ dhammaṃ samādāya visukammantai payaujaintīti khau, vāsaiṭṭha, ‘vaissā, vaissā’ tvaiva akkharaṃ upanibbattaṃ| iti khau, vāsaiṭṭha, aivamaitassa vaissamaṇḍalassa paurāṇaina aggañ‍ñaina akkharaina abhinibbatti ahausi taisañ‍ñaiva sattānaṃ anañ‍ñaisaṃ sadisānaṃyaiva, nau asadisānaṃ, dhammainaiva nau adhammaina| dhammau hi, vāsaiṭṭha, saiṭṭhau janaitasmiṃ diṭṭhai caiva dhammai abhisamparāyañ‍ca|

suddamaṇḍalaṃ

134. ‘‘taisañ‍ñaiva khau, vāsaiṭṭha, sattānaṃ yai tai sattā avasaisā tai luddācārā khuddācārā ahaisuṃ| luddācārā khuddācārāti khau, vāsaiṭṭha, ‘suddā, suddā’ tvaiva akkharaṃ upanibbattaṃ| iti khau, vāsaiṭṭha, aivamaitassa suddamaṇḍalassa paurāṇaina aggañ‍ñaina akkharaina abhinibbatti ahausi taisaṃyaiva sattānaṃ anañ‍ñaisaṃ, sadisānaṃyaiva nau asadisānaṃ, dhammainaiva, nau adhammaina| dhammau hi, vāsaiṭṭha, saiṭṭhau janaitasmiṃ diṭṭhai caiva dhammai abhisamparāyañ‍ca|

135. ‘‘ahu khau sau, vāsaiṭṭha, samayau, yaṃ khattiyaupi sakaṃ dhammaṃ garahamānau agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajati – ‘samaṇau bhavissāmī’ti| brāhmaṇaupi khau, vāsaiṭṭha…pai॰… vaissaupi khau, vāsaiṭṭha…pai॰… suddaupi khau, vāsaiṭṭha, sakaṃ dhammaṃ garahamānau  agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajati – ‘samaṇau bhavissāmī’ti| imaihi khau, vāsaiṭṭha, catūhi maṇḍalaihi samaṇamaṇḍalassa abhinibbatti ahausi, taisaṃyaiva sattānaṃ anañ‍ñaisaṃ, sadisānaṃyaiva nau asadisānaṃ, dhammainaiva nau adhammaina| dhammau hi, vāsaiṭṭha, saiṭṭhau janaitasmiṃ diṭṭhai caiva dhammai abhisamparāyañ‍ca|

duc‍caritādikathā

136. ‘‘khattiyaupi  khau, vāsaiṭṭha, kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ caritvā vācāya duc‍caritaṃ  caritvā manasā duc‍caritaṃ caritvā micchādiṭṭhikau micchādiṭṭhikammasamādānau idaṃ padaṃ sī॰ ipautthakaisu natthi micchādiṭṭhikammasamādānahaitu kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| brāhmaṇaupi khau, vāsaiṭṭha…pai॰… vaissaupi khau, vāsaiṭṭha… suddaupi khau, vāsaiṭṭha… samaṇaupi khau, vāsaiṭṭha, kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ caritvā vācāya duc‍caritaṃ caritvā manasā duc‍caritaṃ caritvā micchādiṭṭhikau micchādiṭṭhikammasamādānau micchādiṭṭhikammasamādānahaitu kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati|

‘‘khattiyaupi  khau, vāsaiṭṭha, kāyaina sucaritaṃ caritvā vācāya sucaritaṃ caritvā manasā sucaritaṃ caritvā sammādiṭṭhikau sammādiṭṭhikammasamādānau idaṃ padaṃ sī॰ pī॰ pautthakaisu natthi sammādiṭṭhikammasamādānahaitu kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| brāhmaṇaupi khau, vāsaiṭṭha…pai॰… vaissaupi khau, vāsaiṭṭha… suddaupi khau, vāsaiṭṭha… samaṇaupi khau, vāsaiṭṭha, kāyaina sucaritaṃ caritvā vācāya sucaritaṃ caritvā manasā sucaritaṃ caritvā sammādiṭṭhikau sammādiṭṭhikammasamādānau sammādiṭṭhikammasamādānahaitu kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati|

137. ‘‘khattiyaupi khau, vāsaiṭṭha, kāyaina dvayakārī, vācāya dvayakārī, manasā dvayakārī, vimissadiṭṭhikau vimissadiṭṭhikammasamādānau vimissadiṭṭhikammasamādānahaitu vimissadiṭṭhikau vimissakammasamādānau vimissakammasamādānahaitu (syā॰), vītimissadiṭṭhikau vītimissadiṭṭhikammasamādānahaitu (sī॰ pī॰) kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī hauti| brāhmaṇaupi khau, vāsaiṭṭha  …pai॰… vaissaupi khau, vāsaiṭṭha… suddaupi khau, vāsaiṭṭha… samaṇaupi khau, vāsaiṭṭha, kāyaina dvayakārī, vācāya dvayakārī, manasā dvayakārī, vimissadiṭṭhikau vimissadiṭṭhikammasamādānau vimissadiṭṭhikammasamādānahaitu kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī hauti|

baudhipakkhiyabhāvanā

138. ‘‘khattiyaupi khau, vāsaiṭṭha, kāyaina saṃvutau vācāya saṃvutau manasā saṃvutau sattan‍naṃ baudhipakkhiyānaṃ dhammānaṃ bhāvanamanvāya diṭṭhaiva dhammai parinibbāyati parinibbāti (ka॰)| brāhmaṇaupi khau, vāsaiṭṭha…pai॰… vaissaupi khau vāsaiṭṭha… suddaupi khau, vāsaiṭṭha … samaṇaupi khau, vāsaiṭṭha, kāyaina saṃvutau vācāya saṃvutau  manasā saṃvutau sattan‍naṃ baudhipakkhiyānaṃ dhammānaṃ bhāvanamanvāya diṭṭhaiva dhammai parinibbāyati|

139. ‘‘imaisañhi, vāsaiṭṭha, catun‍naṃ vaṇṇānaṃ yau hauti bhikkhu arahaṃ khīṇāsavau vusitavā katakaraṇīyau auhitabhārau anuppattasadatthau parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanau  sammadañ‍ñā vimuttau sau naisaṃ aggamakkhāyati dhammainaiva| nau adhammaina| dhammau hi, vāsaiṭṭha, saiṭṭhau janaitasmiṃ diṭṭhai caiva dhammai abhisamparāyañ‍ca|

140. ‘‘brahmunā paisā, vāsaiṭṭha, sanaṅkumāraina gāthā bhāsitā –

‘khattiyau saiṭṭhau janaitasmiṃ, yai gauttapaṭisārinau|

vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau, sau saiṭṭhau daivamānusai’ti||

‘‘sā khau panaisā, vāsaiṭṭha, brahmunā sanaṅkumāraina gāthā sugītā, nau duggītā| subhāsitā, nau dubbhāsitā| atthasaṃhitā, nau anatthasaṃhitā| anumatā mayā| ahampi, vāsaiṭṭha, aivaṃ vadāmi –

‘khattiyau  saiṭṭhau janaitasmiṃ, yai gauttapaṭisārinau|

vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau, sau saiṭṭhau daivamānusai’ti||

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā vāsaiṭṭhabhāradvājā bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

aggañ‍ñasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ catutthaṃ|



5. sampasādanīyasuttaṃ

sāriputtasīhanādau

141. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā nāḷandāyaṃ viharati pāvārikambavanai| atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aivaṃpasan‍nau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavati, na cāhu na ca bhavissati na caitarahi vij‍jati añ‍ñau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā bhagavatā bhiyyaubhiñ‍ñatarau yadidaṃ sambaudhiya’’nti|

142. ‘‘uḷārā khau tai ayaṃ, sāriputta, āsabhī vācā bhāsitā, aikaṃsau gahitau, sīhanādau naditau – ‘aivaṃpasan‍nau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavati; na cāhu na ca bhavissati na caitarahi vij‍jati añ‍ñau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā bhagavatā bhiyyaubhiñ‍ñatarau yadidaṃ sambaudhiya’nti| kiṃ tai kiṃ nu (sī॰ pī॰), kiṃ nu khau tai (syā॰), sāriputta, yai tai ahaisuṃ atītamaddhānaṃ arahantau sammāsambuddhā, sabbai tai bhagavantau caitasā caitau paric‍ca viditā – ‘aivaṃsīlā tai bhagavantau ahaisuṃ itipi, aivaṃdhammā tai bhagavantau ahaisuṃ itipi, aivaṃpañ‍ñā tai bhagavantau ahaisuṃ itipi, aivaṃvihārī tai bhagavantau ahaisuṃ itipi, aivaṃvimuttā tai bhagavantau ahaisuṃ itipī’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘kiṃ pana tai kiṃ pana (sī॰ pī॰), sāriputta, yai tai bhavissanti anāgatamaddhānaṃ arahantau sammāsambuddhā, sabbai tai bhagavantau caitasā caitau paric‍ca viditā, `aivaṃsīlā tai bhagavantau bhavissanti itipi, aivaṃdhammā…pai॰… aivaṃpañ‍ñā… aivaṃvihārī… aivaṃvimuttā tai bhagavantau bhavissanti itipī’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘kiṃ  pana tai kiṃ pana (sī॰ pī॰), sāriputta, ahaṃ aitarahi arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau caitasā caitau paric‍ca viditau – ‘aivaṃsīlau bhagavā itipi, aivaṃdhammau…pai॰… aivaṃpañ‍ñau … aivaṃvihārī… aivaṃvimuttau bhagavā itipī’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘aittha ca hi tai, sāriputta, atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naisu arahantaisu sammāsambuddhaisu caitaupariyañāṇaṃ natthi| atha kiṃ carahi tai ayaṃ, sāriputta, uḷārā āsabhī vācā bhāsitā, aikaṃsau gahitau, sīhanādau naditau – ‘aivaṃpasan‍nau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavati, na cāhu na ca bhavissati na caitarahi vij‍jati añ‍ñau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā bhagavatā bhiyyaubhiñ‍ñatarau yadidaṃ sambaudhiya’’’nti?

143. ‘‘na khau mai na khau panaitaṃ (syā॰ ka॰), bhantai, atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naisu arahantaisu sammāsambuddhaisu caitaupariyañāṇaṃ atthi| api ca, mai mai bhantai (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) dhammanvayau viditau| saiyyathāpi, bhantai, rañ‍ñau pac‍cantimaṃ nagaraṃ daḷhuddhāpaṃ daḷhuddāpaṃ (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) daḷhapākāratauraṇaṃ aikadvāraṃ| tatrassa dauvārikau paṇḍitau byattau maidhāvī añ‍ñātānaṃ nivāraitā, ñātānaṃ pavaisaitā| sau tassa nagarassa samantā anupariyāyapathaṃ anuk‍kamamānau na passaiyya pākārasandhiṃ vā pākāravivaraṃ vā antamasau biḷāranikkhamanamattampi| tassa aivamassa – ‘yai khau kaici auḷārikā pāṇā imaṃ nagaraṃ  pavisanti vā nikkhamanti vā, sabbai tai imināva dvāraina pavisanti vā nikkhamanti vā’ti| aivamaiva khau mai, bhantai, dhammanvayau viditau| yai tai, bhantai, ahaisuṃ atītamaddhānaṃ arahantau sammāsambuddhā, sabbai tai bhagavantau pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahāya caitasau upak‍kilaisai pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇai catūsu satipaṭṭhānaisu suppatiṭṭhitacittā, satta sambaujjhaṅgai yathābhūtaṃ bhāvaitvā anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambujjhiṃsu| yaipi tai, bhantai, bhavissanti anāgatamaddhānaṃ arahantau sammāsambuddhā, sabbai tai bhagavantau pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahāya caitasau upak‍kilaisai pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇai catūsu satipaṭṭhānaisu suppatiṭṭhitacittā, satta sambaujjhaṅgai yathābhūtaṃ bhāvaitvā anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambujjhissanti| bhagavāpi, bhantai, aitarahi arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahāya caitasau upak‍kilaisai pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇai catūsu satipaṭṭhānaisu suppatiṭṭhitacittau  satta sambaujjhaṅgai yathābhūtaṃ bhāvaitvā anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhau|

144. ‘‘idhāhaṃ, bhantai, yaina bhagavā  tainupasaṅkamiṃ dhammassavanāya| tassa mai, bhantai, bhagavā dhammaṃ daisaiti uttaruttaraṃ paṇītapaṇītaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kasappaṭibhāgaṃ| yathā yathā mai, bhantai, bhagavā dhammaṃ daisaisi uttaruttaraṃ paṇītapaṇītaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kasappaṭibhāgaṃ, tathā tathāhaṃ tasmiṃ dhammai abhiñ‍ñā  idhaikac‍caṃ dhammaṃ dhammaisu niṭṭhamagamaṃ; satthari pasīdiṃ – ‘sammāsambuddhau bhagavā, svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau, suppaṭipan‍nau sāvakasaṅghau’ti|

kusaladhammadaisanā

145. ‘‘aparaṃ  pana, bhantai, aitadānuttariyaṃ, yathā bhagavā dhammaṃ daisaiti kusalaisu dhammaisu| tatrimai kusalā dhammā saiyyathidaṃ, cattārau satipaṭṭhānā, cattārau sammappadhānā, cattārau iddhipādā, pañ‍cindriyāni, pañ‍ca balāni, satta baujjhaṅgā, ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau| idha, bhantai, bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| aitadānuttariyaṃ, bhantai, kusalaisu dhammaisu| taṃ bhagavā asaisamabhijānāti, taṃ bhagavatau asaisamabhijānatau uttari abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ natthi, yadabhijānaṃ añ‍ñau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā bhagavatā bhiyyaubhiñ‍ñatarau assa, yadidaṃ kusalaisu dhammaisu|

āyatanapaṇṇattidaisanā

146. ‘‘aparaṃ pana, bhantai, aitadānuttariyaṃ, yathā bhagavā dhammaṃ daisaiti āyatanapaṇṇattīsu| chayimāni, bhantai, ajjhattikabāhirāni āyatanāni| cakkhuñ‍caiva rūpā rūpāni (ka॰) ca, sautañ‍caiva saddā ca, ghānañ‍caiva gandhā ca, jivhā caiva rasā ca, kāyau caiva phauṭṭhabbā ca, manau caiva dhammā ca| aitadānuttariyaṃ, bhantai, āyatanapaṇṇattīsu| taṃ bhagavā asaisamabhijānāti, taṃ bhagavatau asaisamabhijānatau uttari abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ natthi, yadabhijānaṃ  añ‍ñau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā bhagavatā bhiyyaubhiñ‍ñatarau assa yadidaṃ āyatanapaṇṇattīsu|

gabbhāvak‍kantidaisanā

147. ‘‘aparaṃ  pana, bhantai, aitadānuttariyaṃ, yathā bhagavā dhammaṃ daisaiti gabbhāvak‍kantīsu| catassau imā, bhantai, gabbhāvak‍kantiyau| idha, bhantai, aikac‍cau asampajānau mātukucchiṃ auk‍kamati; asampajānau mātukucchismiṃ ṭhāti; asampajānau mātukucchimhā nikkhamati| ayaṃ paṭhamā gabbhāvak‍kanti|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhantai, idhaikac‍cau sampajānau mātukucchiṃ auk‍kamati; asampajānau mātukucchismiṃ ṭhāti; asampajānau mātukucchimhā nikkhamati| ayaṃ dutiyā gabbhāvak‍kanti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhantai, idhaikac‍cau sampajānau mātukucchiṃ auk‍kamati; sampajānau mātukucchismiṃ ṭhāti; asampajānau mātukucchimhā nikkhamati| ayaṃ tatiyā gabbhāvak‍kanti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhantai, idhaikac‍cau sampajānau mātukucchiṃ auk‍kamati; sampajānau mātukucchismiṃ ṭhāti; sampajānau mātukucchimhā nikkhamati| ayaṃ catutthā gabbhāvak‍kanti| aitadānuttariyaṃ, bhantai, gabbhāvak‍kantīsu|

ādaisanavidhādaisanā

148. ‘‘aparaṃ pana, bhantai, aitadānuttariyaṃ, yathā bhagavā dhammaṃ daisaiti ādaisanavidhāsu| catassau imā, bhantai, ādaisanavidhā| idha, bhantai, aikac‍cau nimittaina ādisati – ‘aivampi tai manau, itthampi tai manau, itipi tai citta’nti| sau  bahuṃ caipi ādisati, tathaiva taṃ hauti, nau añ‍ñathā| ayaṃ paṭhamā ādaisanavidhā|
