‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhantai, idhaikac‍cau na haiva khau nimittaina ādisati| api ca khau manussānaṃ vā amanussānaṃ vā daivatānaṃ vā saddaṃ sutvā ādisati – ‘aivampi tai manau, itthampi tai manau, itipi tai citta’nti| sau bahuṃ caipi ādisati, tathaiva taṃ hauti, nau añ‍ñathā| ayaṃ dutiyā ādaisanavidhā|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhantai, idhaikac‍cau na haiva khau nimittaina ādisati, nāpi manussānaṃ vā amanussānaṃ vā daivatānaṃ vā saddaṃ sutvā ādisati| api  ca khau vitak‍kayatau vicārayatau vitak‍kavipphārasaddaṃ sutvā ādisati  – ‘aivampi tai manau, itthampi tai manau, itipi tai citta’nti| sau bahuṃ caipi ādisati, tathaiva taṃ hauti, nau añ‍ñathā| ayaṃ tatiyā ādaisanavidhā|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhantai, idhaikac‍cau na haiva khau nimittaina ādisati, nāpi manussānaṃ vā amanussānaṃ vā daivatānaṃ vā saddaṃ sutvā ādisati, nāpi vitak‍kayatau vicārayatau vitak‍kavipphārasaddaṃ  sutvā ādisati| api ca khau avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhiṃ samāpan‍nassa vitak‍kavicārasamādhisamāpan‍nassa (syā॰ ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 3.61 passitabbaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāti – ‘yathā imassa bhautau manausaṅkhārā paṇihitā| tathā imassa cittassa anantarā imaṃ nāma vitak‍kaṃ vitak‍kaissatī’ti| sau bahuṃ caipi ādisati, tathaiva taṃ hauti, nau añ‍ñathā| ayaṃ catutthā ādaisanavidhā| aitadānuttariyaṃ, bhantai, ādaisanavidhāsu|

dassanasamāpattidaisanā

149. ‘‘aparaṃ pana, bhantai, aitadānuttariyaṃ, yathā bhagavā dhammaṃ daisaiti dassanasamāpattīsu| catassau imā, bhantai, dassanasamāpattiyau| idha, bhantai, aikac‍cau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā ātappamanvāya padhānamanvāya anuyaugamanvāya appamādamanvāya sammāmanasikāramanvāya tathārūpaṃ caitausamādhiṃ phusati, yathāsamāhitai cittai imamaiva kāyaṃ uddhaṃ pādatalā  adhau kaisamatthakā tacapariyantaṃ pūraṃ nānappakārassa asucinau pac‍cavaikkhati – ‘atthi imasmiṃ kāyai kaisā laumā nakhā dantā tacau maṃsaṃ nhāru aṭṭhi aṭṭhimiñ‍jaṃ vak‍kaṃ hadayaṃ yakanaṃ kilaumakaṃ pihakaṃ papphāsaṃ antaṃ antaguṇaṃ udariyaṃ karīsaṃ pittaṃ saimhaṃ pubbau lauhitaṃ saidau maidau assu vasā khaiḷau siṅghānikā lasikā mutta’nti| ayaṃ paṭhamā dassanasamāpatti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhantai, idhaikac‍cau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā ātappamanvāya…pai॰… tathārūpaṃ caitausamādhiṃ phusati, yathāsamāhitai cittai imamaiva kāyaṃ uddhaṃ pādatalā adhau kaisamatthakā tacapariyantaṃ pūraṃ nānappakārassa asucinau pac‍cavaikkhati – ‘atthi imasmiṃ kāyai kaisā  laumā…pai॰… lasikā mutta’nti| atik‍kamma ca purisassa chavimaṃsalauhitaṃ aṭṭhiṃ pac‍cavaikkhati| ayaṃ dutiyā dassanasamāpatti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhantai, idhaikac‍cau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā ātappamanvāya…pai॰… tathārūpaṃ caitausamādhiṃ phusati, yathāsamāhitai cittai imamaiva kāyaṃ uddhaṃ pādatalā adhau kaisamatthakā tacapariyantaṃ pūraṃ nānappakārassa asucinau pac‍cavaikkhati – ‘atthi imasmiṃ kāyai kaisā laumā…pai॰… lasikā mutta’nti| atik‍kamma ca purisassa chavimaṃsalauhitaṃ aṭṭhiṃ pac‍cavaikkhati| purisassa ca viñ‍ñāṇasautaṃ pajānāti, ubhayatau abbaucchin‍naṃ idha laukai patiṭṭhitañ‍ca paralaukai patiṭṭhitañ‍ca| ayaṃ tatiyā dassanasamāpatti|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhantai, idhaikac‍cau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā ātappamanvāya…pai॰… tathārūpaṃ caitausamādhiṃ phusati, yathāsamāhitai cittai imamaiva kāyaṃ uddhaṃ pādatalā adhau kaisamatthakā tacapariyantaṃ pūraṃ nānappakārassa asucinau pac‍cavaikkhati – ‘atthi imasmiṃ kāyai kaisā laumā…pai॰… lasikā mutta’nti| atik‍kamma ca purisassa  chavimaṃsalauhitaṃ aṭṭhiṃ pac‍cavaikkhati| purisassa ca viñ‍ñāṇasautaṃ pajānāti, ubhayatau abbaucchin‍naṃ idha laukai appatiṭṭhitañ‍ca paralaukai appatiṭṭhitañ‍ca| ayaṃ catutthā dassanasamāpatti| aitadānuttariyaṃ, bhantai, dassanasamāpattīsu|

puggalapaṇṇattidaisanā

150. ‘‘aparaṃ pana, bhantai, aitadānuttariyaṃ, yathā bhagavā dhammaṃ daisaiti puggalapaṇṇattīsu| sattimai, bhantai, puggalā| ubhataubhāgavimuttau pañ‍ñāvimuttau kāyasakkhī diṭṭhippattau saddhāvimuttau dhammānusārī saddhānusārī| aitadānuttariyaṃ, bhantai, puggalapaṇṇattīsu|

padhānadaisanā

151. ‘‘aparaṃ pana, bhantai, aitadānuttariyaṃ, yathā bhagavā dhammaṃ  daisaiti padhānaisu| sattimai, bhantai sambaujjhaṅgā satisambaujjhaṅgau dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau pītisambaujjhaṅgau passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau samādhisambaujjhaṅgau  upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau| aitadānuttariyaṃ, bhantai, padhānaisu|

paṭipadādaisanā

152. ‘‘aparaṃ pana, bhantai, aitadānuttariyaṃ, yathā bhagavā dhammaṃ daisaiti paṭipadāsu| catassau imā, bhantai, paṭipadā dukkhā paṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā, dukkhā paṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñā, sukhā paṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā, sukhā paṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñāti| tatra, bhantai, yāyaṃ paṭipadā dukkhā dandhābhiñ‍ñā, ayaṃ, bhantai, paṭipadā ubhayainaiva hīnā akkhāyati dukkhattā ca dandhattā ca| tatra, bhantai, yāyaṃ paṭipadā dukkhā khippābhiñ‍ñā, ayaṃ pana, bhantai, paṭipadā dukkhattā hīnā  akkhāyati| tatra, bhantai, yāyaṃ paṭipadā sukhā dandhābhiñ‍ñā, ayaṃ pana, bhantai, paṭipadā dandhattā hīnā akkhāyati| tatra, bhantai, yāyaṃ paṭipadā sukhā khippābhiñ‍ñā, ayaṃ pana, bhantai, paṭipadā ubhayainaiva paṇītā akkhāyati sukhattā ca khippattā ca| aitadānuttariyaṃ, bhantai, paṭipadāsu|

bhassasamācārādidaisanā

153. ‘‘aparaṃ pana, bhantai, aitadānuttariyaṃ, yathā bhagavā dhammaṃ daisaiti bhassasamācārai| idha, bhantai, aikac‍cau na caiva musāvādupasañhitaṃ vācaṃ bhāsati na ca vaibhūtiyaṃ na ca paisuṇiyaṃ na ca sārambhajaṃ jayāpaikkhau; mantā mantā ca vācaṃ bhāsati nidhānavatiṃ kālaina| aitadānuttariyaṃ, bhantai, bhassasamācārai|

‘‘aparaṃ pana, bhantai, aitadānuttariyaṃ, yathā bhagavā dhammaṃ daisaiti purisasīlasamācārai| idha, bhantai, aikac‍cau sac‍cau cassa saddhau ca, na ca kuhakau, na ca lapakau, na ca naimittikau, na ca nippaisikau, na ca lābhaina  lābhaṃ nijigīsanakau jijigiṃsanakau (syā॰), nijigiṃsitā (sī॰ pī॰), indriyaisu guttadvārau, bhaujanai mattañ‍ñū, samakārī, jāgariyānuyaugamanuyuttau, atanditau, āraddhavīriyau, jhāyī, satimā, kalyāṇapaṭibhānau, gatimā, dhitimā, matimā, na ca kāmaisu giddhau, satau ca nipakau ca| aitadānuttariyaṃ, bhantai, purisasīlasamācārai|

anusāsanavidhādaisanā

154. ‘‘aparaṃ  pana, bhantai, aitadānuttariyaṃ, yathā bhagavā dhammaṃ daisaiti anusāsanavidhāsu| catassau imā bhantai anusāsanavidhā – jānāti, bhantai, bhagavā aparaṃ puggalaṃ pac‍cattaṃ yaunisaumanasikārā ‘ayaṃ puggalau yathānusiṭṭhaṃ tathā paṭipaj‍jamānau tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā sautāpan‍nau bhavissati avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau’ti| jānāti, bhantai, bhagavā paraṃ puggalaṃ pac‍cattaṃ yaunisaumanasikārā – ‘ayaṃ puggalau yathānusiṭṭhaṃ tathā paṭipaj‍jamānau tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā rāgadausamauhānaṃ tanuttā sakadāgāmī bhavissati, sakidaiva imaṃ laukaṃ āgantvā dukkhassantaṃ karissatī’ti| jānāti, bhantai, bhagavā paraṃ puggalaṃ pac‍cattaṃ yaunisaumanasikārā – ‘ayaṃ puggalau yathānusiṭṭhaṃ tathā paṭipaj‍jamānau pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā aupapātikau bhavissati tattha parinibbāyī anāvattidhammau tasmā  laukā’ti| jānāti, bhantai, bhagavā paraṃ puggalaṃ pac‍cattaṃ yaunisaumanasikārā – ‘ayaṃ puggalau yathānusiṭṭhaṃ tathā paṭipaj‍jamānau āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharissatī’ti| aitadānuttariyaṃ, bhantai, anusāsanavidhāsu|

parapuggalavimuttiñāṇadaisanā

155. ‘‘aparaṃ  pana, bhantai, aitadānuttariyaṃ, yathā bhagavā dhammaṃ daisaiti parapuggalavimuttiñāṇai| jānāti, bhantai, bhagavā paraṃ puggalaṃ pac‍cattaṃ yaunisaumanasikārā – ‘ayaṃ puggalau tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā sautāpan‍nau bhavissati avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau’ti| jānāti, bhantai, bhagavā paraṃ puggalaṃ pac‍cattaṃ yaunisaumanasikārā  – ‘ayaṃ puggalau tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā rāgadausamauhānaṃ tanuttā sakadāgāmī bhavissati, sakidaiva imaṃ laukaṃ āgantvā dukkhassantaṃ karissatī’ti| jānāti, bhantai, bhagavā paraṃ puggalaṃ pac‍cattaṃ yaunisaumanasikārā – ‘ayaṃ puggalau pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā aupapātikau bhavissati tattha parinibbāyī anāvattidhammau tasmā laukā’ti| jānāti, bhantai, bhagavā paraṃ puggalaṃ pac‍cattaṃ yaunisaumanasikārā – ‘ayaṃ puggalau āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ  diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharissatī’ti| aitadānuttariyaṃ, bhantai, parapuggalavimuttiñāṇai|

sassatavādadaisanā

156. ‘‘aparaṃ pana, bhantai, aitadānuttariyaṃ, yathā bhagavā dhammaṃ daisaiti sassatavādaisu| tayaumai, bhantai, sassatavādā| idha, bhantai, aikac‍cau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā ātappamanvāya…pai॰… tathārūpaṃ caitausamādhiṃ phusati, yathāsamāhitai cittai anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati| saiyyathidaṃ, aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau tissaupi jātiyau catassaupi jātiyau pañ‍capi jātiyau dasapi jātiyau vīsampi jātiyau tiṃsampi jātiyau cattālīsampi jātiyau pañ‍ñāsampi jātiyau jātisatampi jātisahassampi jātisatasahassampi anaikānipi jātisatāni anaikānipi jātisahassāni anaikānipi jātisatasahassāni, ‘amutrāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī  aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau amutra udapādiṃ; tatrāpāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau  aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau idhūpapan‍nau’ti| iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati| sau aivamāha – ‘atītaṃpāhaṃ addhānaṃ jānāmi – saṃvaṭṭi vā laukau vivaṭṭi vāti| anāgataṃpāhaṃ addhānaṃ jānāmi – saṃvaṭṭissati vā laukau vivaṭṭissati vāti| sassatau attā ca laukau ca vañjhau kūṭaṭṭhau aisikaṭṭhāyiṭṭhitau| tai ca sattā sandhāvanti saṃsaranti cavanti upapaj‍janti, atthitvaiva sassatisama’nti| ayaṃ paṭhamau sassatavādau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhantai, idhaikac‍cau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā ātappamanvāya…pai॰… tathārūpaṃ caitausamādhiṃ phusati, yathāsamāhitai cittai anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati| saiyyathidaṃ, aikampi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭaṃ dvaipi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭāni tīṇipi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭāni cattāripi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭāni pañ‍capi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭāni dasapi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭāni, ‘amutrāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau  aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau amutra udapādiṃ; tatrāpāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau idhūpapan‍nau’ti| iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati| sau aivamāha – ‘atītaṃpāhaṃ addhānaṃ jānāmi saṃvaṭṭi vā laukau vivaṭṭi vāti| anāgataṃpāhaṃ addhānaṃ jānāmi  saṃvaṭṭissati vā laukau vivaṭṭissati vāti| sassatau attā ca laukau ca vañjhau kūṭaṭṭhau aisikaṭṭhāyiṭṭhitau| tai ca sattā sandhāvanti saṃsaranti cavanti upapaj‍janti, atthitvaiva sassatisama’nti| ayaṃ dutiyau sassatavādau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhantai, idhaikac‍cau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā ātappamanvāya…pai॰… tathārūpaṃ caitausamādhiṃ phusati, yathāsamāhitai cittai anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati| saiyyathidaṃ, dasapi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭāni vīsampi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭāni tiṃsampi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭāni cattālīsampi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭāni, ‘amutrāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau amutra udapādiṃ; tatrāpāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau idhūpapan‍nau’ti| iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati| sau aivamāha – ‘atītaṃpāhaṃ addhānaṃ jānāmi saṃvaṭṭipi laukau vivaṭṭipīti; anāgataṃpāhaṃ addhānaṃ jānāmi saṃvaṭṭissatipi laukau vivaṭṭissatipīti| sassatau attā ca laukau ca vañjhau kūṭaṭṭhau  aisikaṭṭhāyiṭṭhitau| tai ca sattā sandhāvanti saṃsaranti cavanti upapaj‍janti, atthitvaiva sassatisama’nti| ayaṃ tatiyau sassatavādau, aitadānuttariyaṃ, bhantai, sassatavādaisu|

pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇadaisanā

157. ‘‘aparaṃ pana, bhantai, aitadānuttariyaṃ, yathā bhagavā dhammaṃ daisaiti pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇai| idha, bhantai, aikac‍cau samaṇau vā  brāhmaṇau vā ātappamanvāya…pai॰… tathārūpaṃ caitausamādhiṃ phusati, yathāsamāhitai cittai anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati| saiyyathidaṃ, aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau tissaupi jātiyau catassaupi jātiyau pañ‍capi jātiyau dasapi jātiyau vīsampi jātiyau tiṃsampi jātiyau cattālīsampi  jātiyau pañ‍ñāsampi jātiyau jātisatampi jātisahassampi jātisatasahassampi anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭakappai anaikaipi vivaṭṭakappai  anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭakappai, ‘amutrāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau amutra udapādiṃ; tatrāpāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau idhūpapan‍nau’ti| iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati| santi, bhantai, daivā sattā (syā॰), yaisaṃ na sak‍kā gaṇanāya vā saṅkhānaina vā āyu saṅkhātuṃ| api ca, yasmiṃ yasmiṃ attabhāvai abhinivuṭṭhapubbau abhinivutthapubbau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) hauti yadi vā rūpīsu yadi vā arūpīsu yadi vā sañ‍ñīsu yadi vā asañ‍ñīsu yadi vā naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñīsu| iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati| aitadānuttariyaṃ, bhantai, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇai|

cutūpapātañāṇadaisanā

158. ‘‘aparaṃ pana, bhantai, aitadānuttariyaṃ, yathā bhagavā dhammaṃ daisaiti sattānaṃ cutūpapātañāṇai| idha, bhantai, aikac‍cau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā ātappamanvāya…pai॰… tathārūpaṃ caitausamādhiṃ phusati, yathāsamāhitai cittai dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti – ‘imai  vata bhauntau sattā kāyaduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā vacīduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā manauduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ upavādakā micchādiṭṭhikā micchādiṭṭhikammasamādānā| tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ  nirayaṃ upapan‍nā| imai vā pana bhauntau sattā kāyasucaritaina saman‍nāgatā vacīsucaritaina saman‍nāgatā manausucaritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ anupavādakā sammādiṭṭhikā sammādiṭṭhikammasamādānā| tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ  maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍nā’ti| iti dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai  suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti| aitadānuttariyaṃ, bhantai, sattānaṃ cutūpapātañāṇai|

iddhividhadaisanā

159. ‘‘aparaṃ pana, bhantai, aitadānuttariyaṃ, yathā bhagavā dhammaṃ daisaiti iddhividhāsu| dvaimā, bhantai, iddhividhāyau – atthi, bhantai, iddhi sāsavā saupadhikā, ‘nau ariyā’ti vuc‍cati| atthi, bhantai, iddhi anāsavā anupadhikā ‘ariyā’ti vuc‍cati| ‘‘katamā ca, bhantai, iddhi sāsavā saupadhikā, ‘nau ariyā’ti vuc‍cati? idha, bhantai, aikac‍cau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā ātappamanvāya…pai॰… tathārūpaṃ caitausamādhiṃ phusati, yathāsamāhitai cittai anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhauti| aikaupi hutvā bahudhā hauti, bahudhāpi hutvā aikau hauti; āvibhāvaṃ tiraubhāvaṃ  tiraukuṭṭaṃ tiraupākāraṃ tiraupabbataṃ asaj‍jamānau gacchati saiyyathāpi ākāsai| pathaviyāpi ummuj‍janimuj‍jaṃ karauti, saiyyathāpi udakai| udakaipi abhij‍jamānai gacchati, saiyyathāpi pathaviyaṃ| ākāsaipi pal‍laṅkaina kamati, saiyyathāpi pakkhī sakuṇau| imaipi candimasūriyai aivaṃmahiddhikai aivaṃmahānubhāvai pāṇinā parāmasati parimaj‍jati| yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaiti| ayaṃ, bhantai, iddhi sāsavā saupadhikā, ‘nau ariyā’ti vuc‍cati|

‘‘katamā pana, bhantai, iddhi anāsavā anupadhikā, ‘ariyā’ti vuc‍cati? idha, bhantai, bhikkhu sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘paṭikūlai appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharaiyya’nti, appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī tattha viharati| sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘appaṭikūlai paṭikūlasañ‍ñī  viharaiyya’nti, paṭikūlasañ‍ñī tattha viharati| sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘paṭikūlai ca appaṭikūlai ca appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharaiyya’nti, appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī tattha viharati| sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘paṭikūlai ca appaṭikūlai ca paṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharaiyya’nti, paṭikūlasañ‍ñī tattha viharati| sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘paṭikūlañ‍ca appaṭikūlañ‍ca tadubhayaṃ abhinivaj‍jaitvā upaikkhakau viharaiyyaṃ satau sampajānau’ti, upaikkhakau tattha viharati satau sampajānau| ayaṃ, bhantai, iddhi anāsavā anupadhikā ‘ariyā’ti vuc‍cati| aitadānuttariyaṃ, bhantai, iddhividhāsu| taṃ bhagavā asaisamabhijānāti, taṃ bhagavatau asaisamabhijānatau uttari abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ natthi, yadabhijānaṃ añ‍ñau samaṇau  vā brāhmaṇau vā bhagavatā bhiyyaubhiñ‍ñatarau assa yadidaṃ iddhividhāsu|

añ‍ñathāsatthuguṇadassanaṃ

160. ‘‘yaṃ  taṃ, bhantai, saddhaina kulaputtaina pattabbaṃ āraddhavīriyaina thāmavatā purisathāmaina purisavīriyaina purisaparak‍kamaina purisadhaurayhaina, anuppattaṃ taṃ bhagavatā| na ca, bhantai, bhagavā kāmaisu kāmasukhal‍likānuyaugamanuyuttau hīnaṃ gammaṃ pauthuj‍janikaṃ anariyaṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ, na ca attakilamathānuyaugamanuyuttau dukkhaṃ anariyaṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ| catun‍nañ‍ca bhagavā jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī akicchalābhī akasiralābhī|

anuyaugadānappakārau

161. ‘‘sacai maṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ pucchaiyya – ‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau sāriputta, ahaisuṃ atītamaddhānaṃ añ‍ñai samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā bhagavatā bhiyyaubhiñ‍ñatarā sambaudhiya’nti, aivaṃ puṭṭhau ahaṃ, bhantai, ‘nau’ti vadaiyyaṃ| ‘kiṃ panāvusau sāriputta, bhavissanti anāgatamaddhānaṃ añ‍ñai samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā bhagavatā bhiyyaubhiñ‍ñatarā sambaudhiya’nti, aivaṃ puṭṭhau ahaṃ, bhantai, ‘nau’ti vadaiyyaṃ| ‘kiṃ panāvusau sāriputta, atthaitarahi añ‍ñau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā bhagavatā bhiyyaubhiñ‍ñatarau sambaudhiya’nti, aivaṃ puṭṭhau ahaṃ, bhantai, ‘nau’ti vadaiyyaṃ|

‘‘sacai pana maṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ pucchaiyya – ‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau sāriputta, ahaisuṃ atītamaddhānaṃ añ‍ñai samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā bhagavatā samasamā sambaudhiya’nti, aivaṃ puṭṭhau ahaṃ, bhantai, ‘aiva’nti vadaiyyaṃ| ‘kiṃ panāvusau sāriputta, bhavissanti anāgatamaddhānaṃ añ‍ñai samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā bhagavatā samasamā sambaudhiya’nti, aivaṃ puṭṭhau ahaṃ, bhantai, ‘‘aiva’’nti vadaiyyaṃ| ‘kiṃ panāvusau sāriputta, atthaitarahi añ‍ñai samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā bhagavatā samasamā sambaudhiya’nti, aivaṃ puṭṭhau ahaṃ bhantai ‘nau’ti vadaiyyaṃ|

‘‘sacai pana maṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ pucchaiyya – ‘kiṃ panāyasmā sāriputtau aikac‍caṃ abbhanujānāti, aikac‍caṃ na abbhanujānātī’ti, aivaṃ puṭṭhau ahaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ  – ‘sammukhā maitaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatau sutaṃ, sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ‘‘ahaisuṃ atītamaddhānaṃ arahantau sammāsambuddhā mayā samasamā sambaudhiya’’nti| sammukhā maitaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatau sutaṃ, sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ‘‘bhavissanti anāgatamaddhānaṃ arahantau sammāsambuddhā mayā samasamā sambaudhiya’’nti| sammukhā maitaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatau sutaṃ sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ‘‘aṭṭhānamaitaṃ anavakāsau yaṃ aikissā laukadhātuyā dvai arahantau sammāsambuddhā apubbaṃ acarimaṃ uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jatī’’’ti|

‘‘kac‍cāhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ  puṭṭhau aivaṃ byākaramānau vuttavādī caiva bhagavatau haumi, na ca bhagavantaṃ abhūtaina abbhācikkhāmi, dhammassa cānudhammaṃ byākaraumi, na ca kauci sahadhammikau vādānuvādau vādānupātau (sī॰) gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchatī’’ti? ‘‘taggha tvaṃ, sāriputta, aivaṃ puṭṭhau aivaṃ byākaramānau vuttavādī caiva mai hausi, na ca maṃ abhūtaina abbhācikkhasi, dhammassa cānudhammaṃ byākarausi, na ca kauci sahadhammikau vādānuvādau gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchatī’’ti|

acchariyaabbhutaṃ

162. aivaṃ  vuttai, āyasmā udāyī bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhantai, abbhutaṃ, bhantai, tathāgatassa appicchatā santuṭṭhitā sal‍laikhatā| yatra hi nāma tathāgatau aivaṃmahiddhikau aivaṃmahānubhāvau, atha ca pana naivattānaṃ pātukarissati! aikamaikañ‍caipi itau, bhantai, dhammaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā attani samanupassaiyyuṃ, tai tāvatakainaiva paṭākaṃ pariharaiyyuṃ| acchariyaṃ, bhantai, abbhutaṃ, bhantai, tathāgatassa appicchatā santuṭṭhitā sal‍laikhatā| yatra hi nāma tathāgatau aivaṃ mahiddhikau aivaṃmahānubhāvau| atha ca pana naivattānaṃ pātukarissatī’’ti!

‘‘passa khau tvaṃ, udāyi, ‘tathāgatassa appicchatā santuṭṭhitā sal‍laikhatā| yatra hi nāma tathāgatau aivaṃmahiddhikau aivaṃmahānubhāvau, atha ca pana naivattānaṃ pātukarissati’! aikamaikañ‍caipi itau, udāyi, dhammaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā attani samanupassaiyyuṃ, tai tāvatakainaiva paṭākaṃ  pariharaiyyuṃ| passa khau tvaṃ, udāyi, ‘tathāgatassa appicchatā santuṭṭhitā sal‍laikhatā| yatra hi nāma tathāgatau aivaṃmahiddhikau aivaṃmahānubhāvau, atha ca pana naivattānaṃ pātukarissatī’’’ti!

163. atha  khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘tasmā tiha tvaṃ, sāriputta, imaṃ dhammapariyāyaṃ abhikkhaṇaṃ bhāsaiyyāsi bhikkhūnaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ upāsakānaṃ upāsikānaṃ| yaisampi hi, sāriputta, maughapurisānaṃ bhavissati tathāgatai kaṅkhā vā vimati vā, taisamimaṃ dhammapariyāyaṃ sutvā tathāgatai kaṅkhā vā vimati vā, sā pahīyissatī’’ti| iti hidaṃ āyasmā sāriputtau  bhagavatau sammukhā sampasādaṃ pavaidaisi| tasmā imassa vaiyyākaraṇassa sampasādanīyaṃ tvaiva adhivacananti|

sampasādanīyasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|



6. pāsādikasuttaṃ

164. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sak‍kaisu viharati vaidhañ‍ñā nāma sakyā, taisaṃ ambavanai pāsādai|

nigaṇṭhanāṭaputtakālaṅkiriyā

taina khau pana samayaina nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau nāthaputtau (sī॰ pī॰) pāvāyaṃ adhunākālaṅkatau hauti| tassa kālaṅkiriyāya bhin‍nā nigaṇṭhā dvaidhikajātā bhaṇḍanajātā kalahajātā vivādāpan‍nā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ mukhasattīhi vitudantā viharanti – ‘‘na tvaṃ imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ ājānāsi, ahaṃ imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ ājānāmi, kiṃ tvaṃ imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ ājānissasi? micchāpaṭipan‍nau tvamasi, ahamasmi sammāpaṭipan‍nau| sahitaṃ mai, asahitaṃ tai| puraivacanīyaṃ pacchā avaca, pacchāvacanīyaṃ purai avaca| adhiciṇṇaṃ tai viparāvattaṃ, āraupitau tai vādau, niggahitau tvamasi, cara vādappamaukkhāya, nibbaiṭhaihi vā sacai pahausī’’ti| vadhauyaiva khau vadhauyaivaikau (ka॰) mañ‍ñai nigaṇṭhaisu nāṭaputtiyaisu vattati anuvattati (syā॰ ka॰)| yaipi nigaṇṭhassa nāṭaputtassa sāvakā gihī audātavasanā, taipi tai taisu (ka॰) nigaṇṭhaisu nāṭaputtiyaisu nibbin‍narūpā nibbindarūpā (ka॰) virattarūpā paṭivānarūpā, yathā taṃ durakkhātai dhammavinayai duppavaiditai aniyyānikai anupasamasaṃvattanikai asammāsambuddhappavaiditai bhin‍nathūpai appaṭisaraṇai|

165. atha khau cundau samaṇuddaisau pāvāyaṃ vassaṃvuṭṭhau vassaṃvutthau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) yaina sāmagāmau, yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau  khau cundau samaṇuddaisau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘nigaṇṭhau, bhantai, nāṭaputtau pāvāyaṃ adhunākālaṅkatau| tassa kālaṅkiriyāya bhin‍nā nigaṇṭhā dvaidhikajātā…pai॰… bhin‍nathūpai appaṭisaraṇai’’ti|

aivaṃ  vuttai, āyasmā ānandau cundaṃ samaṇuddaisaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘atthi khau idaṃ, āvusau cunda, kathāpābhataṃ bhagavantaṃ dassanāya| āyāmāvusau  cunda, yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamissāma; upasaṅkamitvā aitamatthaṃ bhagavatau āraucaissāmā’’ti āraucaiyyāmāti (syā॰)| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau cundau samaṇuddaisau āyasmatau ānandassa pac‍cassausi|

atha khau āyasmā ca ānandau cundau ca samaṇuddaisau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ayaṃ, bhantai, cundau samaṇuddaisau aivamāha, ‘nigaṇṭhau, bhantai, nāṭaputtau pāvāyaṃ adhunākālaṅkatau, tassa kālaṅkiriyāya bhin‍nā nigaṇṭhā…pai॰… bhin‍nathūpai appaṭisaraṇai’’’ti|

asammāsambuddhappavaiditadhammavinayau

166. ‘‘aivaṃ haitaṃ, cunda, hauti durakkhātai dhammavinayai duppavaiditai  aniyyānikai anupasamasaṃvattanikai asammāsambuddhappavaiditai| idha, cunda, satthā ca hauti asammāsambuddhau, dhammau ca durakkhātau duppavaiditau aniyyānikau anupasamasaṃvattanikau asammāsambuddhappavaiditau, sāvakau ca tasmiṃ dhammai na dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau viharati na sāmīcippaṭipan‍nau na anudhammacārī, vauk‍kamma ca tamhā dhammā vattati| sau  aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘tassa tai, āvusau, lābhā, tassa tai suladdhaṃ, satthā ca tai asammāsambuddhau, dhammau ca durakkhātau duppavaiditau aniyyānikau anupasamasaṃvattanikau asammāsambuddhappavaiditau| tvañ‍ca tasmiṃ dhammai na dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau viharasi, na sāmīcippaṭipan‍nau, na anudhammacārī, vauk‍kamma ca tamhā dhammā vattasī’ti| iti khau, cunda, satthāpi tattha gārayhau, dhammaupi tattha gārayhau, sāvakau ca tattha aivaṃ pāsaṃsau| yau khau, cunda, aivarūpaṃ sāvakaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘aitāyasmā tathā paṭipaj‍jatu, yathā tai satthārā dhammau daisitau pañ‍ñattau’ti| yau ca samādapaiti samādāpaiti (sī॰ ṭṭha॰), yañ‍ca samādapaiti, yau ca samādapitau samādāpitau (sī॰ ṭṭha॰) tathattāya paṭipaj‍jati| sabbai tai bahuṃ apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavanti| taṃ kissa haitu? aivaṃ haitaṃ, cunda, hauti durakkhātai dhammavinayai duppavaiditai aniyyānikai anupasamasaṃvattanikai asammāsambuddhappavaiditai|

167. ‘‘idha  pana, cunda, satthā ca hauti asammāsambuddhau, dhammau ca durakkhātau duppavaiditau aniyyānikau anupasamasaṃvattanikau asammāsambuddhappavaiditau, sāvakau ca tasmiṃ dhammai  dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau viharati sāmīcippaṭipan‍nau anudhammacārī, samādāya taṃ dhammaṃ vattati| sau aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘tassa tai, āvusau, alābhā, tassa tai dul‍laddhaṃ, satthā ca tai asammāsambuddhau, dhammau ca durakkhātau duppavaiditau aniyyānikau anupasamasaṃvattanikau asammāsambuddhappavaiditau| tvañ‍ca  tasmiṃ dhammai dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau viharasi sāmīcippaṭipan‍nau anudhammacārī, samādāya taṃ dhammaṃ vattasī’ti| iti khau, cunda, satthāpi tattha gārayhau, dhammaupi tattha gārayhau, sāvakaupi tattha aivaṃ gārayhau| yau khau, cunda, aivarūpaṃ sāvakaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘addhāyasmā ñāyappaṭipan‍nau ñāyamārādhaissatī’ti| yau ca pasaṃsati, yañ‍ca pasaṃsati, yau ca pasaṃsitau bhiyyausau mattāya vīriyaṃ ārabhati| sabbai tai bahuṃ apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavanti| taṃ kissa haitu? aivañhaitaṃ, cunda, hauti durakkhātai dhammavinayai duppavaiditai aniyyānikai anupasamasaṃvattanikai asammāsambuddhappavaiditai|

sammāsambuddhappavaiditadhammavinayau

168. ‘‘idha pana, cunda, satthā ca hauti sammāsambuddhau, dhammau ca svākkhātau suppavaiditau niyyānikau upasamasaṃvattanikau sammāsambuddhappavaiditau, sāvakau ca tasmiṃ dhammai na dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau viharati, na sāmīcippaṭipan‍nau, na anudhammacārī, vauk‍kamma ca tamhā dhammā vattati| sau aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘tassa tai, āvusau, alābhā, tassa tai dul‍laddhaṃ, satthā ca tai sammāsambuddhau, dhammau ca svākkhātau suppavaiditau niyyānikau upasamasaṃvattanikau sammāsambuddhappavaiditau| tvañ‍ca tasmiṃ dhammai na dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau viharasi, na sāmīcippaṭipan‍nau, na anudhammacārī, vauk‍kamma ca tamhā dhammā vattasī’ti| iti khau, cunda, satthāpi tattha pāsaṃsau, dhammaupi tattha pāsaṃsau, sāvakau ca tattha aivaṃ gārayhau| yau khau, cunda, aivarūpaṃ  sāvakaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘aitāyasmā tathā paṭipaj‍jatu yathā tai satthārā dhammau daisitau pañ‍ñattau’ti| yau ca samādapaiti, yañ‍ca samādapaiti, yau ca samādapitau tathattāya paṭipaj‍jati| sabbai tai bahuṃ puñ‍ñaṃ pasavanti| taṃ kissa haitu? aivañhaitaṃ, cunda, hauti svākkhātai dhammavinayai suppavaiditai niyyānikai upasamasaṃvattanikai sammāsambuddhappavaiditai|

169. ‘‘idha  pana, cunda, satthā ca hauti sammāsambuddhau, dhammau ca svākkhātau suppavaiditau niyyānikau upasamasaṃvattanikau sammāsambuddhappavaiditau, sāvakau ca tasmiṃ dhammai dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau viharati sāmīcippaṭipan‍nau anudhammacārī, samādāya taṃ dhammaṃ vattati| sau aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘tassa tai, āvusau, lābhā, tassa tai suladdhaṃ, satthā ca tai satthā ca tai arahaṃ (syā॰) sammāsambuddhau, dhammau ca svākkhātau suppavaiditau niyyānikau upasamasaṃvattanikau sammāsambuddhappavaiditau| tvañ‍ca tasmiṃ dhammai dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau viharasi sāmīcippaṭipan‍nau anudhammacārī, samādāya taṃ dhammaṃ vattasī’ti| iti khau, cunda, satthāpi tattha pāsaṃsau, dhammaupi tattha pāsaṃsau, sāvakaupi tattha aivaṃ pāsaṃsau| yau khau, cunda, aivarūpaṃ sāvakaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘addhāyasmā ñāyappaṭipan‍nau ñāyamārādhaissatī’ti| yau ca pasaṃsati, yañ‍ca pasaṃsati, yau ca pasaṃsitau pasatthau (syā॰) bhiyyausau mattāya vīriyaṃ ārabhati| sabbai tai bahuṃ puñ‍ñaṃ pasavanti| taṃ kissa haitu? aivañhaitaṃ, cunda, hauti svākkhātai  dhammavinayai suppavaiditai niyyānikai upasamasaṃvattanikai sammāsambuddhappavaiditai|

sāvakānutappasatthu

170. ‘‘idha pana, cunda, satthā ca laukai udapādi arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau, dhammau ca svākkhātau suppavaiditau niyyānikau upasamasaṃvattanikau sammāsambuddhappavaiditau, aviñ‍ñāpitatthā cassa haunti sāvakā saddhammai, na ca taisaṃ kaivalaṃ paripūraṃ brahmacariyaṃ āvikataṃ hauti uttānīkataṃ sabbasaṅgāhapadakataṃ sappāṭihīrakataṃ  yāva daivamanussaihi suppakāsitaṃ| atha naisaṃ satthunau antaradhānaṃ hauti| aivarūpau khau, cunda, satthā sāvakānaṃ kālaṅkatau anutappau hauti| taṃ kissa haitu? satthā ca nau laukai udapādi arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau, dhammau ca svākkhātau suppavaiditau niyyānikau upasamasaṃvattanikau sammāsambuddhappavaiditau, aviñ‍ñāpitatthā camha saddhammai, na ca nau kaivalaṃ paripūraṃ brahmacariyaṃ āvikataṃ hauti  uttānīkataṃ sabbasaṅgāhapadakataṃ sappāṭihīrakataṃ yāva daivamanussaihi suppakāsitaṃ| atha nau satthunau antaradhānaṃ hautīti| aivarūpau khau, cunda, satthā sāvakānaṃ kālaṅkatau anutappau hauti|

sāvakānanutappasatthu

171. ‘‘idha pana, cunda, satthā ca laukai udapādi arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau| dhammau ca svākkhātau suppavaiditau niyyānikau upasamasaṃvattanikau sammāsambuddhappavaiditau| viñ‍ñāpitatthā cassa haunti  sāvakā saddhammai, kaivalañ‍ca taisaṃ paripūraṃ brahmacariyaṃ āvikataṃ hauti uttānīkataṃ sabbasaṅgāhapadakataṃ sappāṭihīrakataṃ yāva daivamanussaihi suppakāsitaṃ| atha naisaṃ satthunau antaradhānaṃ hauti| aivarūpau khau, cunda, satthā sāvakānaṃ kālaṅkatau ananutappau hauti| taṃ kissa haitu? satthā ca nau laukai udapādi arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau| dhammau ca svākkhātau suppavaiditau niyyānikau upasamasaṃvattanikau sammāsambuddhappavaiditau| viñ‍ñāpitatthā camha saddhammai, kaivalañ‍ca nau paripūraṃ brahmacariyaṃ āvikataṃ hauti uttānīkataṃ sabbasaṅgāhapadakataṃ sappāṭihīrakataṃ yāva daivamanussaihi suppakāsitaṃ| atha nau satthunau antaradhānaṃ hautīti| aivarūpau khau, cunda, satthā sāvakānaṃ kālaṅkatau ananutappau hauti|

brahmacariyaaparipūrādikathā

172. ‘‘aitaihi caipi, cunda, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ brahmacariyaṃ hauti, nau ca khau satthā hauti thairau rattañ‍ñū cirapabbajitau addhagatau vayauanuppattau| aivaṃ taṃ brahmacariyaṃ aparipūraṃ hauti tainaṅgaina|

‘‘yatau ca khau, cunda, aitaihi caiva aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ brahmacariyaṃ hauti, satthā ca hauti thairau rattañ‍ñū cirapabbajitau addhagatau vayauanuppattau| aivaṃ taṃ brahmacariyaṃ paripūraṃ hauti tainaṅgaina|

173. ‘‘aitaihi caipi, cunda, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ brahmacariyaṃ hauti, satthā ca hauti thairau rattañ‍ñū cirapabbajitau addhagatau vayauanuppattau, nau ca khvassa thairā bhikkhū sāvakā haunti viyattā vinītā visāradā pattayaugakkhaimā| alaṃ samakkhātuṃ saddhammassa, alaṃ uppan‍naṃ  parappavādaṃ sahadhammaihi suniggahitaṃ niggahaitvā sappāṭihāriyaṃ dhammaṃ daisaituṃ| aivaṃ taṃ brahmacariyaṃ aparipūraṃ hauti tainaṅgaina|

‘‘yatau ca khau, cunda, aitaihi caiva aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ brahmacariyaṃ hauti, satthā ca hauti thairau rattañ‍ñū cirapabbajitau addhagatau vayauanuppattau, thairā cassa bhikkhū sāvakā haunti viyattā vinītā visāradā pattayaugakkhaimā| alaṃ samakkhātuṃ saddhammassa, alaṃ uppan‍naṃ parappavādaṃ sahadhammaihi suniggahitaṃ niggahaitvā sappāṭihāriyaṃ dhammaṃ daisaituṃ| aivaṃ taṃ brahmacariyaṃ paripūraṃ hauti tainaṅgaina|

174. ‘‘aitaihi caipi, cunda, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ brahmacariyaṃ hauti, satthā ca hauti thairau rattañ‍ñū cirapabbajitau addhagatau vayauanuppattau, thairā cassa bhikkhū sāvakā haunti viyattā  vinītā visāradā pattayaugakkhaimā| alaṃ samakkhātuṃ saddhammassa, alaṃ uppan‍naṃ parappavādaṃ sahadhammaihi suniggahitaṃ niggahaitvā sappāṭihāriyaṃ dhammaṃ daisaituṃ| nau ca khvassa majjhimā bhikkhū sāvakā haunti…pai॰… majjhimā cassa bhikkhū sāvakā haunti, nau ca khvassa navā bhikkhū sāvakā haunti…pai॰… navā cassa bhikkhū sāvakā haunti, nau ca khvassa thairā bhikkhuniyau sāvikā haunti…pai॰… thairā cassa bhikkhuniyau sāvikā haunti, nau ca khvassa majjhimā  bhikkhuniyau sāvikā haunti…pai॰… majjhimā cassa bhikkhuniyau sāvikā haunti, nau ca khvassa navā bhikkhuniyau sāvikā haunti…pai॰… navā cassa bhikkhuniyau sāvikā haunti, nau ca khvassa upāsakā sāvakā haunti gihī audātavasanā brahmacārinau…pai॰… upāsakā cassa sāvakā haunti gihī audātavasanā brahmacārinau, nau ca khvassa upāsakā sāvakā haunti gihī audātavasanā kāmabhauginau…pai॰… upāsakā cassa sāvakā haunti gihī audātavasanā kāmabhauginau, nau ca khvassa upāsikā sāvikā haunti gihiniyau audātavasanā brahmacāriniyau…pai॰… upāsikā cassa sāvikā haunti gihiniyau audātavasanā brahmacāriniyau, nau ca khvassa upāsikā sāvikā haunti gihiniyau audātavasanā kāmabhauginiyau…pai॰… upāsikā cassa sāvikā haunti gihiniyau audātavasanā kāmabhauginiyau, nau ca khvassa brahmacariyaṃ hauti iddhañ‍caiva phītañ‍ca vitthārikaṃ bāhujañ‍ñaṃ puthubhūtaṃ yāva daivamanussaihi suppakāsitaṃ…pai॰… brahmacariyañ‍cassa hauti iddhañ‍caiva phītañ‍ca vitthārikaṃ bāhujañ‍ñaṃ puthubhūtaṃ yāva  daivamanussaihi suppakāsitaṃ, nau ca khau lābhaggayasaggappattaṃ| aivaṃ taṃ brahmacariyaṃ aparipūraṃ hauti tainaṅgaina|

‘‘yatau ca khau, cunda, aitaihi caiva aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ brahmacariyaṃ hauti, satthā ca hauti thairau rattañ‍ñū cirapabbajitau addhagatau vayauanuppattau, thairā cassa bhikkhū sāvakā haunti viyattā vinītā visāradā pattayaugakkhaimā| alaṃ samakkhātuṃ saddhammassa, alaṃ uppan‍naṃ parappavādaṃ sahadhammaihi suniggahitaṃ niggahaitvā sappāṭihāriyaṃ dhammaṃ daisaituṃ| majjhimā cassa bhikkhū sāvakā haunti…pai॰… navā cassa bhikkhū sāvakā haunti…pai॰… thairā cassa bhikkhuniyau sāvikā  haunti…pai॰… majjhimā cassa bhikkhuniyau sāvikā haunti…pai॰… navā cassa bhikkhuniyau sāvikā haunti…pai॰… upāsakā cassa sāvakā haunti…pai॰… gihī audātavasanā brahmacārinau| upāsakā cassa sāvakā haunti gihī audātavasanā kāmabhauginau…pai॰… upāsikā cassa sāvikā haunti gihiniyau audātavasanā brahmacāriniyau…pai॰… upāsikā cassa sāvikā haunti gihiniyau audātavasanā kāmabhauginiyau…pai॰… brahmacariyañ‍cassa hauti iddhañ‍caiva phītañ‍ca vitthārikaṃ  bāhujañ‍ñaṃ puthubhūtaṃ yāva daivamanussaihi suppakāsitaṃ, lābhaggappattañ‍ca yasaggappattañ‍ca| aivaṃ taṃ brahmacariyaṃ paripūraṃ hauti tainaṅgaina|

175. ‘‘ahaṃ khau pana, cunda, aitarahi satthā laukai uppan‍nau arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau| dhammau ca svākkhātau suppavaiditau niyyānikau upasamasaṃvattanikau sammāsambuddhappavaiditau| viñ‍ñāpitatthā ca mai sāvakā saddhammai, kaivalañ‍ca taisaṃ paripūraṃ brahmacariyaṃ āvikataṃ uttānīkataṃ sabbasaṅgāhapadakataṃ sappāṭihīrakataṃ yāva daivamanussaihi suppakāsitaṃ| ahaṃ khau pana, cunda, aitarahi satthā thairau rattañ‍ñū cirapabbajitau addhagatau vayauanuppattau|

‘‘santi khau pana mai, cunda, aitarahi thairā bhikkhū sāvakā haunti viyattā vinītā visāradā pattayaugakkhaimā| alaṃ samakkhātuṃ saddhammassa, alaṃ uppan‍naṃ parappavādaṃ sahadhammaihi suniggahitaṃ niggahaitvā sappāṭihāriyaṃ dhammaṃ daisaituṃ| santi khau pana mai, cunda, aitarahi majjhimā bhikkhū sāvakā…pai॰… santi khau pana mai, cunda, aitarahi navā bhikkhū sāvakā…pai॰… santi khau pana mai, cunda, aitarahi thairā bhikkhuniyau sāvikā…pai॰… santi khau  pana mai, cunda, aitarahi majjhimā bhikkhuniyau sāvikā…pai॰… santi khau pana mai, cunda, aitarahi navā bhikkhuniyau sāvikā…pai॰… santi khau pana mai, cunda, aitarahi upāsakā sāvakā gihī audātavasanā brahmacārinau…pai॰… santi khau pana mai, cunda, aitarahi upāsakā sāvakā gihī audātavasanā kāmabhauginau…pai॰… santi khau pana mai, cunda, aitarahi upāsikā sāvikā gihiniyau audātavasanā brahmacāriniyau…pai॰… santi khau pana  mai, cunda, aitarahi upāsikā sāvikā gihiniyau audātavasanā kāmabhauginiyau…pai॰… aitarahi khau pana mai, cunda, brahmacariyaṃ iddhañ‍caiva phītañ‍ca vitthārikaṃ bāhujañ‍ñaṃ puthubhūtaṃ yāva daivamanussaihi suppakāsitaṃ|

176. ‘‘yāvatā khau, cunda, aitarahi satthārau laukai uppan‍nā, nāhaṃ, cunda, añ‍ñaṃ aikasatthārampi samanupassāmi aivaṃlābhaggayasaggappattaṃ yatharivāhaṃ| yāvatā khau pana, cunda, aitarahi saṅghau vā gaṇau vā laukai uppan‍nau; nāhaṃ, cunda, añ‍ñaṃ aikaṃ saṃghampi samanupassāmi aivaṃlābhaggayasaggappattaṃ yatharivāyaṃ, cunda, bhikkhusaṅghau| yaṃ khau taṃ, cunda, sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘sabbākārasampan‍naṃ sabbākāraparipūraṃ anūnamanadhikaṃ svākkhātaṃ kaivalaṃ paripūraṃ brahmacariyaṃ suppakāsita’nti| idamaiva taṃ sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘sabbākārasampan‍naṃ…pai॰… suppakāsita’nti|

‘‘udakau  uddakau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) sudaṃ, cunda, rāmaputtau aivaṃ vācaṃ bhāsati – ‘passaṃ na passatī’ti| kiñ‍ca  passaṃ na passatīti? khurassa sādhunisitassa talamassa passati, dhārañ‍ca khvassa na passati| idaṃ vuc‍cati – ‘passaṃ na passatī’ti| yaṃ khau panaitaṃ, cunda, udakaina rāmaputtaina bhāsitaṃ hīnaṃ gammaṃ pauthuj‍janikaṃ anariyaṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ khuramaiva sandhāya| yañ‍ca taṃ yañ‍caitaṃ (syā॰ ka॰), cunda, sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘passaṃ  na passatī’ti, idamaiva taṃ idamaivaitaṃ (ka॰) sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘passaṃ na passatī’ti| kiñ‍ca passaṃ na passatīti? aivaṃ sabbākārasampan‍naṃ sabbākāraparipūraṃ anūnamanadhikaṃ svākkhātaṃ kaivalaṃ paripūraṃ brahmacariyaṃ suppakāsitanti, iti haitaṃ passati suppakāsitaṃ, iti haitaṃ na passatīti (syā॰ ka॰)| idamaittha apakaḍḍhaiyya, aivaṃ taṃ parisuddhataraṃ assāti, iti haitaṃ na passati na passatīti (syā॰ ka॰)| idamaittha upakaḍḍhaiyya, aivaṃ taṃ paripūraṃ parisuddhataraṃ (syā॰ ka॰), paripūrataraṃ (?) assāti, iti  haitaṃ na passati| idaṃ vuc‍cati cunda – ‘passaṃ na passatī’ti| yaṃ khau taṃ, cunda, sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘sabbākārasampan‍naṃ…pai॰… brahmacariyaṃ suppakāsita’nti| idamaiva taṃ sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘sabbākārasampan‍naṃ sabbākāraparipūraṃ anūnamanadhikaṃ svākkhātaṃ kaivalaṃ paripūraṃ brahmacariyaṃ suppakāsita’nti|

saṅgāyitabbadhammau

177. tasmātiha, cunda, yai vau mayā dhammā abhiñ‍ñā daisitā, tattha sabbaihaiva saṅgamma samāgamma atthaina atthaṃ byañ‍janaina byañ‍janaṃ saṅgāyitabbaṃ na vivaditabbaṃ, yathayidaṃ brahmacariyaṃ addhaniyaṃ assa ciraṭṭhitikaṃ, tadassa bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya laukānukampāya atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussānaṃ| katamai ca tai, cunda, dhammā mayā abhiñ‍ñā daisitā, yattha sabbaihaiva saṅgamma samāgamma atthaina atthaṃ byañ‍janaina byañ‍janaṃ saṅgāyitabbaṃ na vivaditabbaṃ, yathayidaṃ brahmacariyaṃ addhaniyaṃ assa ciraṭṭhitikaṃ, tadassa bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya laukānukampāya atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussānaṃ? saiyyathidaṃ – cattārau satipaṭṭhānā, cattārau sammappadhānā, cattārau iddhipādā, pañ‍cindriyāni, pañ‍ca balāni, satta baujjhaṅgā, ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau| imai khau tai, cunda, dhammā mayā abhiñ‍ñā daisitā| yattha sabbaihaiva saṅgamma samāgamma atthaina atthaṃ byañ‍janaina byañ‍janaṃ saṅgāyitabbaṃ na vivaditabbaṃ, yathayidaṃ brahmacariyaṃ addhaniyaṃ assa ciraṭṭhitikaṃ, tadassa bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya laukānukampāya atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussānaṃ|

sañ‍ñāpaitabbavidhi

178. ‘‘taisañ‍ca  vau, cunda, samaggānaṃ sammaudamānānaṃ avivadamānānaṃ sikkhataṃ sikkhitabbaṃ (bahūsu) añ‍ñatarau sabrahmacārī saṅghai dhammaṃ bhāsaiyya| tatra cai tumhākaṃ aivamassa – ‘ayaṃ khau āyasmā atthañ‍caiva micchā gaṇhāti, byañ‍janāni ca micchā raupaitī’ti| tassa naiva abhinanditabbaṃ na paṭik‍kausitabbaṃ, anabhinanditvā appaṭik‍kausitvā sau aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘imassa nu khau, āvusau, atthassa imāni vā byañ‍janāni aitāni vā byañ‍janāni katamāni aupāyikatarāni, imaisañ‍ca imaisaṃ vā (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰), imaisaṃ (sī॰) byañ‍janānaṃ ayaṃ vā atthau aisau vā atthau  katamau aupāyikatarau’ti? sau cai aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘imassa khau, āvusau, atthassa imānaiva byañ‍janāni aupāyikatarāni, yā caiva yañ‍caiva (sī॰ ka॰), ṭīkā aulaukaitabbā aitāni; imaisañ‍ca imaidaṃ (sabbattha) byañ‍janānaṃ ayamaiva atthau aupāyikatarau, yā caiva yañ‍caiva (sī॰ ka॰), ṭīkā aulaukaitabbā aisau’ti| sau naiva ussādaitabbau na apasādaitabbau, anussādaitvā anapasādaitvā svaiva sādhukaṃ sañ‍ñāpaitabbau tassa ca atthassa taisañ‍ca byañ‍janānaṃ nisantiyā|

179. ‘‘aparaupi cai, cunda, sabrahmacārī saṅghai dhammaṃ bhāsaiyya| tatra cai tumhākaṃ aivamassa – ‘ayaṃ khau āyasmā atthañhi khau micchā gaṇhāti byañ‍janāni sammā  raupaitī’ti| tassa naiva abhinanditabbaṃ na paṭik‍kausitabbaṃ, anabhinanditvā appaṭik‍kausitvā sau aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘imaisaṃ nu khau, āvusau, byañ‍janānaṃ ayaṃ vā atthau aisau vā atthau katamau aupāyikatarau’ti? sau cai aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘imaisaṃ khau, āvusau, byañ‍janānaṃ ayamaiva atthau aupāyikatarau, yā caiva aisau’ti| sau naiva ussādaitabbau na apasādaitabbau, anussādaitvā anapasādaitvā svaiva sādhukaṃ sañ‍ñāpaitabbau tassaiva atthassa nisantiyā|

180. ‘‘aparaupi cai, cunda, sabrahmacārī saṅghai dhammaṃ bhāsaiyya| tatra cai tumhākaṃ aivamassa – ‘ayaṃ khau āyasmā atthañhi khau sammā gaṇhāti byañ‍janāni micchā raupaitī’ti| tassa naiva abhinanditabbaṃ na paṭik‍kausitabbaṃ; anabhinanditvā appaṭik‍kausitvā sau  aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘imassa nu khau, āvusau, atthassa imāni vā byañ‍janāni aitāni vā byañ‍janāni katamāni aupāyikatarānī’ti? sau cai aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘imassa khau, āvusau, atthassa imānaiva byañ‍janāni aupayikatarāni, yāni caiva aitānī’ti| sau naiva ussādaitabbau na apasādaitabbau; anussādaitvā anapasādaitvā svaiva sādhukaṃ sañ‍ñāpaitabbau taisañ‍ñaiva byañ‍janānaṃ nisantiyā|

181. ‘‘aparaupi cai, cunda, sabrahmacārī saṅghai dhammaṃ bhāsaiyya| tatra cai tumhākaṃ aivamassa – ‘ayaṃ khau āyasmā atthañ‍caiva sammā gaṇhāti byañ‍janāni  ca sammā raupaitī’ti| tassa ‘sādhū’ti bhāsitaṃ abhinanditabbaṃ anumauditabbaṃ; tassa ‘sādhū’ti bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā sau aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘lābhā nau āvusau, suladdhaṃ nau āvusau, yai mayaṃ āyasmantaṃ tādisaṃ sabrahmacāriṃ passāma aivaṃ atthupaitaṃ byañ‍janupaita’nti|

pac‍cayānuñ‍ñātakāraṇaṃ

182. ‘‘na vau ahaṃ, cunda, diṭṭhadhammikānaṃyaiva āsavānaṃ  saṃvarāya dhammaṃ daisaimi| na panāhaṃ, cunda, samparāyikānaṃyaiva āsavānaṃ paṭighātāya dhammaṃ daisaimi| diṭṭhadhammikānaṃ caivāhaṃ, cunda, āsavānaṃ saṃvarāya dhammaṃ daisaimi; samparāyikānañ‍ca āsavānaṃ paṭighātāya| tasmātiha, cunda, yaṃ vau mayā cīvaraṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ, alaṃ vau taṃ – yāvadaiva sītassa paṭighātāya, uṇhassa paṭighātāya, ḍaṃsamakasavātātapasarīsapa siriṃsapa (syā॰) samphassānaṃ paṭighātāya, yāvadaiva hirikaupīnapaṭicchādanatthaṃ| yau  vau mayā piṇḍapātau anuñ‍ñātau, alaṃ vau sau yāvadaiva imassa kāyassa ṭhitiyā yāpanāya vihiṃsūparatiyā brahmacariyānuggahāya, iti purāṇañ‍ca vaidanaṃ paṭihaṅkhāmi, navañ‍ca vaidanaṃ na uppādaissāmi, yātrā ca mai bhavissati anavaj‍jatā ca phāsuvihārau ca cāti (bahūsu)| yaṃ vau mayā saināsanaṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ, alaṃ vau taṃ yāvadaiva sītassa paṭighātāya, uṇhassa paṭighātāya, ḍaṃsamakasavātātapasarīsapasamphassānaṃ paṭighātāya, yāvadaiva utuparissayavinaudana paṭisal‍lānārāmatthaṃ| yau vau mayā gilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍ja parikkhārau anuñ‍ñātau, alaṃ vau sau yāvadaiva uppan‍nānaṃ vaiyyābādhikānaṃ vaidanānaṃ paṭighātāya abyāpaj‍japaramatāya abyāpajjhaparamatāyāti (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), abyābajjhaparamatāya (?)|

sukhal‍likānuyaugau

183. ‘‘ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, cunda, vij‍jati yaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘sukhal‍likānuyaugamanuyuttā samaṇā sakyaputtiyā viharantī’ti| aivaṃvādinau vadamānā (syā॰), cunda, añ‍ñatitthiyā  paribbājakā aivamassu vacanīyā – ‘katamau sau, āvusau, sukhal‍likānuyaugau? sukhal‍likānuyaugā hi bahū anaikavihitā nānappakārakā’ti|

‘‘cattāraumai, cunda, sukhal‍likānuyaugā hīnā gammā pauthuj‍janikā anariyā anatthasaṃhitā na nibbidāya na virāgāya na niraudhāya na upasamāya na abhiñ‍ñāya na sambaudhāya na nibbānāya saṃvattanti| katamai cattārau?

‘‘idha, cunda, aikac‍cau bālau pāṇai vadhitvā vadhitvā attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaiti| ayaṃ paṭhamau sukhal‍likānuyaugau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, cunda, idhaikac‍cau  adin‍naṃ ādiyitvā ādiyitvā attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaiti| ayaṃ dutiyau sukhal‍likānuyaugau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, cunda, idhaikac‍cau musā bhaṇitvā bhaṇitvā attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaiti| ayaṃ tatiyau sukhal‍likānuyaugau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, cunda, idhaikac‍cau pañ‍cahi kāmaguṇaihi samappitau samaṅgībhūtau paricāraiti| ayaṃ catutthau sukhal‍likānuyaugau|

‘‘imai khau, cunda, cattārau sukhal‍likānuyaugā hīnā gammā pauthuj‍janikā anariyā anatthasaṃhitā na nibbidāya na virāgāya na niraudhāya na upasamāya na abhiñ‍ñāya na sambaudhāya na nibbānāya saṃvattanti|

‘‘ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, cunda, vij‍jati yaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘‘imai cattārau sukhal‍likānuyaugai anuyuttā samaṇā sakyaputtiyā viharantī’ti| tai vau tai (sī॰ pī॰) ‘māhaivaṃ’ tissu vacanīyā| na tai vau sammā vadamānā vadaiyyuṃ, abbhācikkhaiyyuṃ asatā abhūtaina|

184. ‘‘cattāraumai, cunda, sukhal‍likānuyaugā aikantanibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattanti| katamai cattārau?

‘‘idha, cunda, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ paṭhamau sukhal‍likānuyaugau|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, cunda, bhikkhu vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ dutiyau sukhal‍likānuyaugau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, cunda, bhikkhu pītiyā ca virāgā…pai॰… tatiyaṃ  jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ tatiyau sukhal‍likānuyaugau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, cunda, bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā  dukkhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ catutthau sukhal‍likānuyaugau|

‘‘imai khau, cunda, cattārau sukhal‍likānuyaugā aikantanibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattanti|

‘‘ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, cunda, vij‍jati yaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘‘imai cattārau sukhal‍likānuyaugai anuyuttā samaṇā sakyaputtiyā viharantī’ti| tai vau ‘aivaṃ’ tissu vacanīyā| sammā tai vau vadamānā vadaiyyuṃ, na tai vau abbhācikkhaiyyuṃ asatā abhūtaina|

sukhal‍likānuyaugānisaṃsau

185. ‘‘ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, cunda, vij‍jati, yaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘imai panāvusau, cattārau sukhal‍likānuyaugai anuyuttānaṃ viharataṃ kati phalāni katānisaṃsā pāṭikaṅkhā’ti? aivaṃvādinau, cunda, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivamassu vacanīyā – ‘imai khau, āvusau, cattārau sukhal‍likānuyaugai anuyuttānaṃ viharataṃ cattāri phalāni cattārau  ānisaṃsā pāṭikaṅkhā| katamai cattārau? idhāvusau, bhikkhu tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā sautāpan‍nau hauti avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau| idaṃ paṭhamaṃ phalaṃ, paṭhamau ānisaṃsau| puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā rāgadausamauhānaṃ tanuttā sakadāgāmī hauti, sakidaiva imaṃ laukaṃ āgantvā dukkhassantaṃ  karauti| idaṃ dutiyaṃ phalaṃ, dutiyau ānisaṃsau| puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā aupapātikau hauti, tattha parinibbāyī anāvattidhammau tasmā laukā| idaṃ tatiyaṃ phalaṃ, tatiyau ānisaṃsau| puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| idaṃ catutthaṃ phalaṃ catutthau ānisaṃsau| imai  khau, āvusau, cattārau sukhal‍likānuyaugai anuyuttānaṃ viharataṃ imāni cattāri phalāni, cattārau ānisaṃsā pāṭikaṅkhā’’ti|

khīṇāsavaabhabbaṭhānaṃ

186. ‘‘ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, cunda, vij‍jati yaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyā  paribbājakā aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘aṭṭhitadhammā samaṇā sakyaputtiyā viharantī’ti| aivaṃvādinau, cunda, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivamassu vacanīyā – ‘atthi khau, āvusau, taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina sāvakānaṃ dhammā daisitā pañ‍ñattā yāvajīvaṃ anatik‍kamanīyā| saiyyathāpi, āvusau, indakhīlau vā ayaukhīlau vā gambhīranaimau sunikhātau acalau asampavaidhī| aivamaiva khau, āvusau, taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina sāvakānaṃ dhammā daisitā pañ‍ñattā yāvajīvaṃ anatik‍kamanīyā| yau sau, āvusau, bhikkhu arahaṃ khīṇāsavau vusitavā katakaraṇīyau auhitabhārau anuppattasadatthau parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanau sammadañ‍ñā vimuttau, abhabbau sau nava ṭhānāni ajjhācarituṃ| abhabbau, āvusau, khīṇāsavau bhikkhu sañ‍cic‍ca  pāṇaṃ jīvitā vauraupaituṃ; abhabbau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyituṃ; abhabbau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivituṃ; abhabbau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu sampajānamusā bhāsituṃ; abhabbau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu san‍nidhikārakaṃ kāmai paribhuñ‍jituṃ saiyyathāpi pubbai āgārikabhūtau; abhabbau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu chandāgatiṃ gantuṃ; abhabbau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu dausāgatiṃ gantuṃ; abhabbau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu mauhāgatiṃ gantuṃ; abhabbau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu bhayāgatiṃ gantuṃ| yau sau, āvusau, bhikkhu arahaṃ khīṇāsavau vusitavā katakaraṇīyau auhitabhārau anuppattasadatthau parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanau sammadañ‍ñā vimuttau, abhabbau sau imāni nava ṭhānāni ajjhācaritu’’nti|

pañhābyākaraṇaṃ

187. ‘‘ṭhānaṃ  khau panaitaṃ, cunda, vij‍jati, yaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘atītaṃ khau addhānaṃ ārabbha samaṇau gautamau atīrakaṃ ñāṇadassanaṃ pañ‍ñapaiti, nau ca khau anāgataṃ addhānaṃ ārabbha atīrakaṃ ñāṇadassanaṃ pañ‍ñapaiti, tayidaṃ kiṃsu tayidaṃ kathaṃsū’ti? tai ca añ‍ñatitthiyā  paribbājakā añ‍ñavihitakaina ñāṇadassanaina añ‍ñavihitakaṃ ñāṇadassanaṃ pañ‍ñapaitabbaṃ mañ‍ñanti yathariva bālā abyattā| atītaṃ khau, cunda, addhānaṃ ārabbha tathāgatassa satānusāri ñāṇaṃ hauti; sau yāvatakaṃ ākaṅkhati tāvatakaṃ anussarati| anāgatañ‍ca khau addhānaṃ ārabbha tathāgatassa baudhijaṃ ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍jati – ‘ayamantimā  jāti, natthidāni punabbhavau’ti| ‘atītaṃ caipi, cunda, hauti abhūtaṃ atacchaṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ, na taṃ tathāgatau byākarauti| atītaṃ caipi, cunda, hauti bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ, tampi tathāgatau na byākarauti| atītaṃ caipi cunda, hauti bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ atthasaṃhitaṃ, tatra kālañ‍ñū tathāgatau hauti tassa pañhassa vaiyyākaraṇāya| anāgataṃ caipi, cunda, hauti abhūtaṃ atacchaṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ, na taṃ tathāgatau byākarauti…pai॰… tassa pañhassa vaiyyākaraṇāya| pac‍cuppan‍naṃ caipi, cunda, hauti abhūtaṃ atacchaṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ, na taṃ tathāgatau byākarauti| pac‍cuppan‍naṃ caipi, cunda, hauti bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ  anatthasaṃhitaṃ, tampi tathāgatau na byākarauti| pac‍cuppan‍naṃ caipi, cunda, hauti bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ atthasaṃhitaṃ, tatra kālañ‍ñū tathāgatau hauti tassa pañhassa vaiyyākaraṇāya|

188. ‘‘iti khau, cunda, atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naisu dhammaisu tathāgatau kālavādī kālavādī sac‍cavādī (syā॰) bhūtavādī atthavādī dhammavādī vinayavādī, tasmā ‘tathāgatau’ti vuc‍cati| yañ‍ca khau, cunda, sadaivakassa laukassa samārakassa sabrahmakassa sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya diṭṭhaṃ sutaṃ mutaṃ viñ‍ñātaṃ pattaṃ pariyaisitaṃ anuvicaritaṃ manasā, sabbaṃ tathāgataina abhisambuddhaṃ, tasmā ‘tathāgatau’ti vuc‍cati| yañ‍ca, cunda, rattiṃ tathāgatau anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambujjhati, yañ‍ca rattiṃ anupādisaisāya nibbānadhātuyā parinibbāyati, yaṃ aitasmiṃ antarai bhāsati  lapati niddisati| sabbaṃ taṃ tathaiva hauti nau añ‍ñathā, tasmā ‘tathāgatau’ti vuc‍cati| yathāvādī, cunda, tathāgatau tathākārī, yathākārī tathāvādī| iti yathāvādī tathākārī, yathākārī tathāvādī, tasmā ‘tathāgatau’ti vuc‍cati| sadaivakai laukai, cunda, samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya tathāgatau abhibhū anabhibhūtau añ‍ñadatthudasau vasavattī, tasmā ‘tathāgatau’ti vuc‍cati|

abyākataṭṭhānaṃ

189. ‘‘ṭhānaṃ  khau panaitaṃ, cunda, vij‍jati yaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau, hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti? aivaṃvādinau, cunda, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivamassu vacanīyā – ‘abyākataṃ khau, āvusau, bhagavatā  – ‘‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’’’nti|

‘‘ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, cunda, vij‍jati, yaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘kiṃ panāvusau, na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti? aivaṃvādinau, cunda, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivamassu vacanīyā – ‘aitampi khau, āvusau, bhagavatā abyākataṃ – ‘‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’’’nti|

‘‘ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, cunda, vij‍jati, yaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘kiṃ panāvusau, hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti? aivaṃvādinau, cunda, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivamassu vacanīyā – ‘abyākataṃ  khau aitaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā – ‘‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’’’nti|

‘‘ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, cunda, vij‍jati, yaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘kiṃ panāvusau, naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti? aivaṃvādinau, cunda, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivamassu vacanīyā – ‘aitampi khau, āvusau, bhagavatā abyākataṃ – ‘‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’’’nti|

‘‘ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, cunda, vij‍jati, yaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘kasmā panaitaṃ, āvusau, samaṇaina gautamaina abyākata’nti? aivaṃvādinau, cunda, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivamassu vacanīyā – ‘na haitaṃ, āvusau, atthasaṃhitaṃ na dhammasaṃhitaṃ  na ādibrahmacariyakaṃ na nibbidāya na virāgāya na niraudhāya na upasamāya na abhiñ‍ñāya na sambaudhāya na nibbānāya saṃvattati, tasmā taṃ bhagavatā abyākata’nti|

byākataṭṭhānaṃ

190. ‘‘ṭhānaṃ  khau panaitaṃ, cunda, vij‍jati, yaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘kiṃ panāvusau, samaṇaina gautamaina byākata’nti? aivaṃvādinau, cunda, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivamassu vacanīyā – ‘idaṃ dukkhanti khau, āvusau, bhagavatā byākataṃ, ayaṃ dukkhasamudayauti khau, āvusau, bhagavatā byākataṃ, ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhauti khau, āvusau, bhagavatā byākataṃ, ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadāti  khau, āvusau, bhagavatā byākata’nti|

‘‘ṭhānaṃ  khau panaitaṃ, cunda, vij‍jati, yaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘kasmā panaitaṃ, āvusau, samaṇaina gautamaina byākata’nti? aivaṃvādinau, cunda, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivamassu vacanīyā – ‘aitañhi, āvusau, atthasaṃhitaṃ, aitaṃ dhammasaṃhitaṃ, aitaṃ ādibrahmacariyakaṃ aikantanibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati| tasmā taṃ bhagavatā byākata’nti|

pubbantasahagatadiṭṭhinissayā

191. ‘‘yaipi tai, cunda, pubbantasahagatā diṭṭhinissayā, taipi vau mayā byākatā, yathā tai byākātabbā| yathā ca tai na byākātabbā, kiṃ vau ahaṃ tai tathā tattha (syā॰ ka॰) byākarissāmi? yaipi tai, cunda, aparantasahagatā diṭṭhinissayā, taipi vau mayā byākatā, yathā tai byākātabbā| yathā ca tai na byākātabbā, kiṃ vau ahaṃ tai tathā byākarissāmi? katamai ca tai, cunda, pubbantasahagatā diṭṭhinissayā, yai vau mayā byākatā, yathā tai byākātabbā| (yathā ca tai na byākātabbā, kiṃ vau ahaṃ tai tathā byākarissāmi) (yathā ca tai na byākātabbā) sabbattha? santi khau, cunda, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘sassatau attā ca laukau ca, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti| santi pana, cunda, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘asassatau attā ca laukau ca…pai॰… sassatau ca asassatau ca attā ca laukau ca… naiva sassatau nāsassatau attā ca laukau ca… sayaṃkatau  attā ca laukau ca… paraṃkatau attā  ca laukau ca… sayaṃkatau ca  paraṃkatau ca attā ca laukau ca… asayaṃkārau  aparaṃkārau adhic‍casamuppan‍nau attā ca laukau ca, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti| sassataṃ sukhadukkhaṃ… asassataṃ sukhadukkhaṃ… sassatañ‍ca asassatañ‍ca sukhadukkhaṃ… naivasassataṃ nāsassataṃ sukhadukkhaṃ… sayaṃkataṃ sukhadukkhaṃ… paraṃkataṃ sukhadukkhaṃ… sayaṃkatañ‍ca paraṃkatañ‍ca sukhadukkhaṃ… asayaṃkāraṃ aparaṃkāraṃ adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ sukhadukkhaṃ, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti|

192. ‘‘tatra, cunda, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘sassatau attā ca laukau ca, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti| tyāhaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘atthi nu khau idaṃ, āvusau, vuc‍cati – ‘‘sassatau attā ca laukau cā’’ti? yañ‍ca khau tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti| taṃ taisaṃ nānujānāmi| taṃ kissa haitu? añ‍ñathāsañ‍ñinaupi haittha, cunda, santaikai sattā| imāyapi khau ahaṃ, cunda, pañ‍ñattiyā naiva attanā samasamaṃ samanupassāmi kutau bhiyyau| atha khau ahamaiva tattha bhiyyau yadidaṃ adhipañ‍ñatti|

193. ‘‘tatra, cunda, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘asassatau attā ca laukau ca…pai॰… sassatau ca asassatau ca attā ca laukau ca… naivasassatau nāsassatau attā  ca laukau ca… sayaṃkatau attā ca laukau ca… paraṃkatau attā ca laukau ca… sayaṃkatau ca paraṃkatau ca attā ca laukau ca… asayaṃkārau aparaṃkārau adhic‍casamuppan‍nau attā ca laukau ca… sassataṃ sukhadukkhaṃ… asassataṃ  sukhadukkhaṃ… sassatañ‍ca asassatañ‍ca sukhadukkhaṃ… naivasassataṃ nāsassataṃ sukhadukkhaṃ… sayaṃkataṃ sukhadukkhaṃ… paraṃkataṃ sukhadukkhaṃ… sayaṃkatañ‍ca paraṃkatañ‍ca sukhadukkhaṃ… asayaṃkāraṃ aparaṃkāraṃ adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ sukhadukkhaṃ, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti| tyāhaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘atthi nu khau idaṃ, āvusau, vuc‍cati – ‘‘asayaṃkāraṃ aparaṃkāraṃ adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ sukhadukkha’’’nti? yañ‍ca khau tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti| taṃ taisaṃ nānujānāmi| taṃ kissa haitu? añ‍ñathāsañ‍ñinaupi haittha, cunda, santaikai sattā| imāyapi khau ahaṃ, cunda, pañ‍ñattiyā naiva attanā samasamaṃ samanupassāmi kutau bhiyyau| atha khau ahamaiva tattha bhiyyau yadidaṃ adhipañ‍ñatti| imai khau tai, cunda, pubbantasahagatā diṭṭhinissayā, yai vau mayā byākatā, yathā tai byākātabbā| yathā ca tai na byākātabbā, kiṃ vau ahaṃ tai tathā byākarissāmīti byākarissāmīti (sī॰ ka॰)?

aparantasahagatadiṭṭhinissayā

194. ‘‘katamai  ca tai, cunda, aparantasahagatā diṭṭhinissayā, yai vau mayā byākatā, yathā tai byākātabbā| (yathā ca tai na byākātabbā, kiṃ vau ahaṃ tai tathā byākarissāmī) ( ) aitthantarai pāṭhau sabbatthapi paripuṇṇau dissati? santi, cunda, aikai  samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘rūpī attā hauti araugau paraṃ maraṇā, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti| santi pana, cunda, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘arūpī attā hauti…pai॰… rūpī ca arūpī ca attā hauti… naivarūpī nārūpī attā hauti… sañ‍ñī  attā hauti… asañ‍ñī attā hauti… naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñī attā hauti… attā ucchij‍jati vinassati na hauti paraṃ maraṇā, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti| tatra, cunda, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘rūpī attā hauti araugau paraṃ maraṇā, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti| tyāhaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘atthi nu khau idaṃ, āvusau, vuc‍cati – ‘‘rūpī attā hauti araugau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti? yañ‍ca khau tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti| taṃ taisaṃ nānujānāmi| taṃ kissa haitu? añ‍ñathāsañ‍ñinaupi haittha, cunda, santaikai sattā| imāyapi khau ahaṃ, cunda, pañ‍ñattiyā naiva attanā samasamaṃ samanupassāmi kutau bhiyyau| atha khau ahamaiva tattha bhiyyau yadidaṃ adhipañ‍ñatti|

195. ‘‘tatra, cunda, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘arūpī attā hauti…pai॰… rūpī ca arūpī ca attā hauti… naivarūpīnārūpī attā hauti… sañ‍ñī attā hauti… asañ‍ñī attā hauti… naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñī  attā hauti… attā ucchij‍jati vinassati na hauti paraṃ maraṇā, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti| tyāhaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘atthi nu khau idaṃ, āvusau, vuc‍cati – ‘‘attā ucchij‍jati vinassati na hauti paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti? yañ‍ca khau tai, cunda, aivamāhaṃsu – ‘idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti| taṃ taisaṃ nānujānāmi| taṃ kissa haitu? añ‍ñathāsañ‍ñinaupi haittha, cunda, santaikai sattā| imāyapi khau ahaṃ, cunda, pañ‍ñattiyā naiva attanā  samasamaṃ samanupassāmi, kutau bhiyyau| atha khau ahamaiva tattha bhiyyau yadidaṃ adhipañ‍ñatti| imai khau tai, cunda, aparantasahagatā diṭṭhinissayā, yai vau mayā byākatā, yathā tai byākātabbā| yathā ca tai na byākātabbā, kiṃ vau ahaṃ tai tathā byākarissāmīti byākarissāmīti (sī॰ ka॰)?

196. ‘‘imaisañ‍ca, cunda, pubbantasahagatānaṃ diṭṭhinissayānaṃ imaisañ‍ca aparantasahagatānaṃ diṭṭhinissayānaṃ  pahānāya samatik‍kamāya aivaṃ mayā cattārau satipaṭṭhānā daisitā pañ‍ñattā| katamai cattārau? idha, cunda, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī…pai॰… cittai cittānupassī… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| imaisañ‍ca cunda, pubbantasahagatānaṃ diṭṭhinissayānaṃ imaisañ‍ca aparantasahagatānaṃ diṭṭhinissayānaṃ  pahānāya samatik‍kamāya| aivaṃ mayā imai cattārau satipaṭṭhānā daisitā pañ‍ñattā’’ti|

197. taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā upavāṇau bhagavatau piṭṭhitau ṭhitau hauti bhagavantaṃ bījayamānau| atha khau āyasmā upavāṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhantai, abbhutaṃ, bhantai! pāsādikau vatāyaṃ, bhantai, dhammapariyāyau; supāsādikau vatāyaṃ bhantai, dhammapariyāyau, kau nāmāyaṃ bhantai dhammapariyāyau’’ti? ‘‘tasmātiha tvaṃ, upavāṇa, imaṃ dhammapariyāyaṃ ‘pāsādikau’ tvaiva naṃ dhāraihī’’ti| idamavauca bhagavā| attamanau āyasmā upavāṇau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandīti|

pāsādikasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|



7. lakkhaṇasuttaṃ

dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇāni

198. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhaddantai’’ti bhadantaiti (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

199. ‘‘dvattiṃsimāni, bhikkhavai, mahāpurisassa mahāpurisalakkhaṇāni, yaihi saman‍nāgatassa mahāpurisassa dvaiva gatiyau bhavanti anañ‍ñā| sacai agāraṃ ajjhāvasati, rājā hauti cak‍kavattī dhammikau dhammarājā cāturantau vijitāvī janapadatthāvariyappattau sattaratanasaman‍nāgatau| tassimāni satta ratanāni bhavanti; saiyyathidaṃ, cak‍karatanaṃ hatthiratanaṃ assaratanaṃ maṇiratanaṃ itthiratanaṃ gahapatiratanaṃ pariṇāyakaratanamaiva sattamaṃ| parausahassaṃ khau panassa puttā bhavanti sūrā vīraṅgarūpā parasainappamaddanā| sau imaṃ pathaviṃ sāgarapariyantaṃ adaṇḍaina asatthaina dhammaina abhivijiya ajjhāvasati| sacai khau pana agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajati, arahaṃ hauti sammāsambuddhau laukai vivaṭṭacchadau vivaṭacchadau (syā॰ ka॰), vivattacchadau (sī॰ pī॰)|

200. ‘‘katamāni ca tāni, bhikkhavai, dvattiṃsa mahāpurisassa mahāpurisalakkhaṇāni, yaihi saman‍nāgatassa mahāpurisassa  dvaiva gatiyau bhavanti anañ‍ñā? sacai agāraṃ ajjhāvasati, rājā hauti cak‍kavattī…pai॰… sacai khau pana agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajati, arahaṃ hauti sammāsambuddhau  laukai vivaṭṭacchadau|

‘‘idha, bhikkhavai, mahāpurisau suppatiṭṭhitapādau hauti| yampi, bhikkhavai, mahāpurisau suppatiṭṭhitapādau hauti, idampi, bhikkhavai, mahāpurisassa mahāpurisalakkhaṇaṃ bhavati|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, mahāpurisassa haiṭṭhāpādatalaisu cak‍kāni jātāni haunti sahassārāni sanaimikāni sanābhikāni sabbākāraparipūrāni sabbākāraparipūrāni suvibhattantarāni (sī॰ pī॰)| yampi, bhikkhavai, mahāpurisassa haiṭṭhāpādatalaisu cak‍kāni jātāni haunti sahassārāni sanaimikāni sanābhikāni sabbākāraparipūrāni, idampi, bhikkhavai, mahāpurisassa mahāpurisalakkhaṇaṃ bhavati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, mahāpurisau āyatapaṇhi hauti…pai॰… dīghaṅguli hauti… mudutalunahatthapādau hauti… jālahatthapādau hauti… ussaṅkhapādau hauti… aiṇijaṅghau hauti… ṭhitakauva anaunamantau ubhauhi pāṇitalaihi jaṇṇukāni parimasati parimaj‍jati… kausauhitavatthaguyhau hauti… suvaṇṇavaṇṇau hauti kañ‍canasan‍nibhattacau… sukhumacchavi hauti, sukhumattā chaviyā rajaujal‍laṃ kāyai na upalimpati… aikaikalaumau  hauti, aikaikāni laumāni laumakūpaisu jātāni… uddhaggalaumau hauti, uddhaggāni laumāni jātāni nīlāni añ‍janavaṇṇāni kuṇḍalāvaṭṭāni kuṇḍalāvattāni (bahūsu) dakkhiṇāvaṭṭakajātāni dakkhiṇāvattakajātāni (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) … brahmujugattau hauti… sattussadau hauti… sīhapubbaddhakāyau hauti… citantaraṃsau pitantaraṃsau (syā॰) hauti… nigraudhaparimaṇḍalau hauti, yāvatak‍vassa kāyau tāvatak‍vassa byāmau yāvatak‍vassa byāmau tāvatak‍vassa kāyau… samavaṭṭakkhandhau hauti… rasaggasaggī hauti… sīhahanu hauti… cattālīsadantau hauti … samadantau hauti… aviraḷadantau hauti… susuk‍kadāṭhau hauti… pahūtajivhau hauti… brahmassarau hauti karavīkabhāṇī… abhinīlanaittau hauti… gaupakhumau hauti… uṇṇā bhamukantarai jātā hauti, audātā mudutūlasan‍nibhā| yampi, bhikkhavai, mahāpurisassa uṇṇā bhamukantarai jātā hauti, audātā mudutūlasan‍nibhā, idampi, bhikkhavai, mahāpurisassa mahāpurisalakkhaṇaṃ bhavati|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, mahāpurisau uṇhīsasīsau hauti| yampi, bhikkhavai, mahāpurisau uṇhīsasīsau hauti, idampi, bhikkhavai, mahāpurisassa mahāpurisalakkhaṇaṃ bhavati|

‘‘imāni khau tāni, bhikkhavai, dvattiṃsa mahāpurisassa mahāpurisalakkhaṇāni, yaihi saman‍nāgatassa mahāpurisassa dvaiva gatiyau bhavanti anañ‍ñā| sacai  agāraṃ ajjhāvasati, rājā hauti cak‍kavattī…pai॰… sacai khau pana agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajati, arahaṃ hauti sammāsambuddhau laukai vivaṭṭacchadau|

‘‘imāni khau, bhikkhavai, dvattiṃsa mahāpurisassa mahāpurisalakkhaṇāni bāhirakāpi isayau  dhārainti, nau ca khau tai jānanti – ‘imassa kammassa kaṭattā idaṃ lakkhaṇaṃ paṭilabhatī’ti|

(1) suppatiṭṭhitapādatālakkhaṇaṃ

201. ‘‘yampi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau purimaṃ jātiṃ purimaṃ bhavaṃ purimaṃ nikaitaṃ pubbai manussabhūtau samānau daḷhasamādānau ahausi kusalaisu dhammaisu, avatthitasamādānau kāyasucaritai vacīsucaritai manausucaritai dānasaṃvibhāgai sīlasamādānai upausathupavāsai mattaiyyatāya paittaiyyatāya sāmañ‍ñatāya  brahmañ‍ñatāya kulai jaiṭṭhāpacāyitāya añ‍ñatarañ‍ñataraisu ca adhikusalaisu dhammaisu| sau tassa kammassa kaṭattā upacitattā ussan‍nattā vipulattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| sau tattha añ‍ñai daivai dasahi ṭhānaihi adhiggaṇhāti dibbaina āyunā dibbaina vaṇṇaina dibbaina sukhaina dibbaina yasaina dibbaina ādhipataiyyaina dibbaihi rūpaihi dibbaihi saddaihi dibbaihi gandhaihi dibbaihi rasaihi dibbaihi phauṭṭhabbaihi| sau tatau cutau itthattaṃ āgatau samānau imaṃ mahāpurisalakkhaṇaṃ paṭilabhati| suppatiṭṭhitapādau hauti| samaṃ pādaṃ bhūmiyaṃ nikkhipati, samaṃ uddharati, samaṃ sabbāvantaihi pādatalaihi bhūmiṃ phusati|

202. ‘‘sau taina lakkhaṇaina saman‍nāgatau sacai agāraṃ ajjhāvasati, rājā hauti cak‍kavattī dhammikau dhammarājā cāturantau vijitāvī janapadatthāvariyappattau sattaratanasaman‍nāgatau| tassimāni satta ratanāni bhavanti; saiyyathidaṃ, cak‍karatanaṃ hatthiratanaṃ assaratanaṃ maṇiratanaṃ itthiratanaṃ gahapatiratanaṃ pariṇāyakaratanamaiva sattamaṃ| parausahassaṃ khau panassa puttā bhavanti sūrā vīraṅgarūpā parasainappamaddanā| sau imaṃ pathaviṃ sāgarapariyantaṃ akhilamanimittamakaṇṭakaṃ iddhaṃ phītaṃ khaimaṃ sivaṃ nirabbudaṃ adaṇḍaina asatthaina dhammaina abhivijiya ajjhāvasati| rājā samānau kiṃ labhati? akkhambhiyau avikkhambhiyau (sī॰ pī॰) hauti kainaci manussabhūtaina  pac‍catthikaina pac‍cāmittaina| rājā samānau idaṃ labhati| ‘‘sacai khau pana agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajati, arahaṃ hauti sammāsambuddhau laukai vivaṭṭacchadau| buddhau samānau kiṃ labhati? akkhambhiyau hauti abbhantaraihi vā bāhiraihi vā pac‍catthikaihi pac‍cāmittaihi rāgaina vā dausaina vā mauhaina vā samaṇaina vā  brāhmaṇaina vā daivaina vā māraina vā brahmunā vā kainaci vā laukasmiṃ| buddhau samānau idaṃ labhati’’| aitamatthaṃ bhagavā avauca|

203. tatthaitaṃ  vuc‍cati –

‘‘sac‍cai ca dhammai ca damai ca saṃyamai,

saucaiyyasīlālayupausathaisu ca|

dānai ahiṃsāya asāhasai ratau,

daḷhaṃ samādāya samattamācari samantamācari (syā॰ ka॰)||

‘‘sau taina kammaina divaṃ samak‍kami apak‍kami (syā॰ ka॰),

sukhañ‍ca khiḍḍāratiyau ca anvabhi aṃnvabhi (ṭīkā)|

tatau cavitvā punarāgatau idha,

samaihi pādaihi phusī vasundharaṃ||

‘‘byākaṃsu vaiyyañ‍janikā samāgatā,

samappatiṭṭhassa na hauti khambhanā|

gihissa vā pabbajitassa vā puna pana (syā॰),

taṃ lakkhaṇaṃ bhavati tadatthajautakaṃ||

‘‘akkhambhiyau  hauti agāramāvasaṃ,

parābhibhū sattubhi nappamaddanau|

manussabhūtainidha hauti kainaci,

akkhambhiyau tassa phalaina kammunau||

‘‘sacai  ca pabbaj‍jamupaiti tādisau,

naikkhammachandābhiratau vicakkhaṇau|

aggau na sau gacchati jātu khambhanaṃ,

naruttamau aisa hi tassa dhammatā’’ti||

(2) pādatalacak‍kalakkhaṇaṃ

204. ‘‘yampi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau purimaṃ jātiṃ purimaṃ bhavaṃ purimaṃ nikaitaṃ pubbai manussabhūtau  samānau bahujanassa  sukhāvahau ahausi, ubbaigauttāsabhayaṃ apanuditā, dhammikañ‍ca rakkhāvaraṇaguttiṃ saṃvidhātā, saparivārañ‍ca dānaṃ adāsi| sau tassa kammassa kaṭattā upacitattā ussan‍nattā vipulattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati…pai॰… sau tatau cutau itthattaṃ āgatau samānau imaṃ mahāpurisalakkhaṇaṃ paṭilabhati| haiṭṭhāpādatalaisu cak‍kāni jātāni haunti sahassārāni sanaimikāni sanābhikāni sabbākāraparipūrāni suvibhattantarāni|

‘‘sau taina lakkhaṇaina saman‍nāgatau sacai agāraṃ ajjhāvasati, rājā hauti cak‍kavattī…pai॰… rājā samānau kiṃ labhati? mahāparivārau hauti; mahāssa haunti parivārā brāhmaṇagahapatikā  naigamajānapadā gaṇakamahāmattā anīkaṭṭhā dauvārikā amac‍cā pārisaj‍jā rājānau bhaugiyā kumārā| rājā samānau idaṃ labhati| sacai khau pana agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajati, arahaṃ hauti sammāsambuddhau laukai vivaṭṭacchadau| buddhau samānau kiṃ labhati? mahāparivārau hauti; mahāssa haunti parivārā bhikkhū bhikkhuniyau upāsakā upāsikāyau daivā manussā asurā nāgā gandhabbā| buddhau samānau idaṃ labhati’’| aitamatthaṃ bhagavā avauca|

205. tatthaitaṃ vuc‍cati –

‘‘purai puratthā purimāsu jātisu,

manussabhūtau bahunaṃ sukhāvahau|

ubbhaigauttāsabhayāpanūdanau,

guttīsu rakkhāvaraṇaisu ussukau||

‘‘sau  taina kammaina divaṃ samak‍kami,

sukhañ‍ca khiḍḍāratiyau ca anvabhi|

tatau cavitvā punarāgatau idha,

cak‍kāni pādaisu duvaisu vindati||

‘‘samantanaimīni sahassarāni ca,

byākaṃsu vaiyyañ‍janikā samāgatā|

disvā  kumāraṃ satapuñ‍ñalakkhaṇaṃ,

parivāravā haissati sattumaddanau||

tathā hī cak‍kāni samantanaimini,

sacai  na pabbaj‍jamupaiti tādisau|

vattaiti cak‍kaṃ pathaviṃ pasāsati,

tassānuyantādha tassānuyuttā idha (sī॰ pī॰), tassānuyantā idha (syā॰ ka॰) bhavanti khattiyā||

‘‘mahāyasaṃ saṃparivārayanti naṃ,

sacai ca pabbaj‍jamupaiti tādisau|

naikkhammachandābhiratau vicakkhaṇau,

daivāmanussāsurasak‍karakkhasā sattarakkhasā (ka॰) sī॰ syāaṭṭhakathā aulaukaitabbā||

‘‘gandhabbanāgā vihagā catuppadā,

anuttaraṃ daivamanussapūjitaṃ|

mahāyasaṃ saṃparivārayanti na’’nti||

(3-5) āyatapaṇhitāditilakkhaṇaṃ

206. ‘‘yampi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau purimaṃ jātiṃ purimaṃ bhavaṃ purimaṃ nikaitaṃ pubbai manussabhūtau samānau pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau ahausi nihitadaṇḍau nihitasatthau laj‍jī dayāpan‍nau, sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī vihāsi| sau tassa kammassa kaṭattā upacitattā ussan‍nattā vipulattā…pai॰… sau tatau cutau itthattaṃ āgatau samānau imāni tīṇi mahāpurisalakkhaṇāni  paṭilabhati| āyatapaṇhi ca hauti, dīghaṅguli ca brahmujugattau ca|

‘‘sau  taihi lakkhaṇaihi saman‍nāgatau sacai agāraṃ ajjhāvasati, rājā hauti cak‍kavattī…pai॰… rājā samānau kiṃ labhati? dīghāyukau hauti ciraṭṭhitikau, dīghamāyuṃ pālaiti, na sak‍kā hauti antarā jīvitā vauraupaituṃ kainaci manussabhūtaina pac‍catthikaina pac‍cāmittaina| rājā samānau idaṃ labhati… buddhau samānau kiṃ labhati? dīghāyukau hauti  ciraṭṭhitikau, dīghamāyuṃ pālaiti, na sak‍kā hauti antarā jīvitā vauraupaituṃ pac‍catthikaihi pac‍cāmittaihi samaṇaina vā brāhmaṇaina vā daivaina vā māraina vā brahmunā vā kainaci vā laukasmiṃ| buddhau samānau idaṃ labhati’’| aitamatthaṃ bhagavā avauca|

207. tatthaitaṃ vuc‍cati –

‘‘māraṇavadhabhayattanau maraṇavadhabhayattanau (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰), maraṇavadhabhayamattanau (syā॰) viditvā,

paṭiviratau paraṃ māraṇāyahausi|

taina sucaritaina saggamagamā taina sau sucaritaina saggamagamāsi (syā॰),

sukataphalavipākamanubhausi||

‘‘caviya punaridhāgatau samānau,

paṭilabhati idha tīṇi lakkhaṇāni|

bhavati vipuladīghapāsaṇhikau,

brahmāva suju subhau sujātagattau||

‘‘subhujau susu susaṇṭhitau sujātau,

mudutalunaṅguliyassa haunti|

dīghā tībhi  purisavaraggalakkhaṇaihi,

cirayapanāya cirayāpanāya (syā॰) kumāramādisanti||

‘‘bhavati yadi gihī ciraṃ yapaiti,

cirataraṃ pabbajati yadi tatau hi|

yāpayati  ca vasiddhibhāvanāya,

iti dīghāyukatāya taṃ nimitta’’nti||

(6) sattussadatālakkhaṇaṃ

208. ‘‘yampi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau purimaṃ jātiṃ purimaṃ bhavaṃ purimaṃ nikaitaṃ pubbai manussabhūtau samānau dātā ahausi paṇītānaṃ rasitānaṃ khādanīyānaṃ bhaujanīyānaṃ sāyanīyānaṃ laihanīyānaṃ  pānānaṃ| sau tassa kammassa kaṭattā…pai॰… sau tatau cutau itthattaṃ āgatau samānau imaṃ mahāpurisalakkhaṇaṃ paṭilabhati, sattussadau hauti, sattassa ussadā haunti; ubhausu hatthaisu ussadā haunti, ubhausu pādaisu ussadā haunti, ubhausu aṃsakūṭaisu ussadā haunti, khandhai ussadau hauti|

‘‘sau taina lakkhaṇaina saman‍nāgatau sacai agāraṃ ajjhāvasati, rājā hauti cak‍kavattī…pai॰… rājā samānau kiṃ labhati? lābhī hauti paṇītānaṃ rasitānaṃ khādanīyānaṃ bhaujanīyānaṃ sāyanīyānaṃ laihanīyānaṃ pānānaṃ| rājā samānau idaṃ labhati… buddhau samānau kiṃ labhati? lābhī hauti paṇītānaṃ rasitānaṃ khādanīyānaṃ bhaujanīyānaṃ sāyanīyānaṃ laihanīyānaṃ pānānaṃ| buddhau samānau idaṃ labhati’’| aitamatthaṃ  bhagavā avauca|

209. tatthaitaṃ vuc‍cati –

‘‘khaj‍jabhauj‍jamatha laiyya sāyiyaṃ,

uttamaggarasadāyakau ahu|

taina sau sucaritaina kammunā,

nandanai ciramabhippamaudati||

‘‘satta  cussadai idhādhigacchati,

hatthapādamudutañ‍ca vindati|

āhu byañ‍jananimittakauvidā,

khaj‍jabhauj‍jarasalābhitāya naṃ||

‘‘yaṃ gihissapi na taṃ gihissāpi (syā॰) tadatthajautakaṃ,

pabbaj‍jampi ca tadādhigacchati|

khaj‍jabhauj‍jarasalābhiruttamaṃ,

āhu sabbagihibandhanacchida’’nti||

(7-8) karacaraṇamudujālatālakkhaṇāni

210. ‘‘yampi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau purimaṃ jātiṃ purimaṃ bhavaṃ purimaṃ nikaitaṃ pubbai manussabhūtau samānau catūhi saṅgahavatthūhi janaṃ saṅgāhakau ahausi – dānaina paiyyavaj‍jaina piyavācaina (syā॰ ka॰) atthacariyāya samānattatāya| sau tassa kammassa kaṭattā…pai॰… sau tatau cutau itthattaṃ āgatau samānau imāni dvai mahāpurisalakkhaṇāni  paṭilabhati| mudutalunahatthapādau ca hauti jālahatthapādau ca|

‘‘sau taihi lakkhaṇaihi saman‍nāgatau sacai agāraṃ ajjhāvasati, rājā hauti cak‍kavattī…pai॰… rājā samānau kiṃ labhati? susaṅgahitaparijanau hauti, susaṅgahitāssa haunti brāhmaṇagahapatikā naigamajānapadā  gaṇakamahāmattā anīkaṭṭhā dauvārikā amac‍cā pārisaj‍jā rājānau bhaugiyā kumārā| rājā samānau idaṃ labhati… buddhau samānau kiṃ labhati? susaṅgahitaparijanau hauti, susaṅgahitāssa haunti bhikkhū bhikkhuniyau upāsakā upāsikāyau daivā manussā asurā nāgā gandhabbā| buddhau samānau idaṃ labhati’’| aitamatthaṃ bhagavā avauca|

211. tatthaitaṃ vuc‍cati –

‘‘dānampi catthacariyatañ‍ca dānampi ca atthacariyatampi ca (sī॰ pī॰),

piyavāditañ‍ca samānattatañ‍ca piyavadatañ‍ca samānachandatañ‍ca (sī॰ pī॰)|

kariyacariyasusaṅgahaṃ bahūnaṃ,

anavamataina guṇaina yāti saggaṃ||

‘‘caviya punaridhāgatau samānau,

karacaraṇamudutañ‍ca jālinau ca|

atirucirasuvaggudassanaiyyaṃ,

paṭilabhati daharau susu kumārau||

‘‘bhavati  parijanassavau vidhaiyyau,

mahimaṃ āvasitau susaṅgahitau|

piyavadū  hitasukhataṃ jigīsamānau jigiṃ samānau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰),

abhirucitāni guṇāni ācarati||

‘‘yadi ca jahati sabbakāmabhaugaṃ,

kathayati dhammakathaṃ jinau janassa|

vacanapaṭikarassābhippasan‍nā,

sutvāna dhammānudhammamācarantī’’ti||

(9-10) ussaṅkhapādauddhaggalaumatālakkhaṇāni

212. ‘‘yampi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau purimaṃ jātiṃ purimaṃ bhavaṃ purimaṃ nikaitaṃ pubbai manussabhūtau samānau samānau bahunau janassa (sī॰ pī॰) atthūpasaṃhitaṃ dhammūpasaṃhitaṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā ahausi, bahujanaṃ nidaṃsaisi, pāṇīnaṃ hitasukhāvahau dhammayāgī| sau tassa kammassa kaṭattā…pai॰… sau tatau cutau itthattaṃ āgatau samānau imāni dvai mahāpurisalakkhaṇāni paṭilabhati| ussaṅkhapādau ca hauti, uddhaggalaumau ca|

‘‘sau taihi lakkhaṇaihi saman‍nāgatau, sacai agāraṃ ajjhāvasati, rājā hauti cak‍kavattī…pai॰… rājā samānau kiṃ labhati? aggau ca hauti saiṭṭhau ca pāmaukkhau ca uttamau ca pavarau ca kāmabhaugīnaṃ| rājā samānau idaṃ labhati… buddhau samānau kiṃ labhati? aggau ca hauti saiṭṭhau ca pāmaukkhau ca uttamau ca pavarau ca sabbasattānaṃ| buddhau samānau idaṃ labhati’’| aitamatthaṃ bhagavā avauca|

213. tatthaitaṃ vuc‍cati –

‘‘atthadhammasahitaṃ  atthadhammasaṃhitaṃ (ka॰ sī॰ pī॰), atthadhammupasaṃhitaṃ (ka॰) purai giraṃ,

airayaṃ bahujanaṃ nidaṃsayi|

pāṇinaṃ hitasukhāvahau ahu,

dhammayāgamayajī dhammayāgaṃ assaji (ka॰) amaccharī||

‘‘taina  sau sucaritaina kammunā,

suggatiṃ  vajati tattha maudati|

lakkhaṇāni ca duvai idhāgatau,

uttamappamukhatāya uttamasukhatāya (syā॰), uttamapamukkhatāya (ka॰) vindati||

‘‘ubbhamuppatitalaumavā sasau,

pādagaṇṭhirahu sādhusaṇṭhitā|

maṃsalauhitācitā tacautthatā,

uparicaraṇasaubhanā uparijānusaubhanā (syā॰), upari ca pana saubhanā (sī॰ pī॰) ahu||

‘‘gaihamāvasati cai tathāvidhau,

aggataṃ vajati kāmabhauginaṃ|

taina uttaritarau na vij‍jati,

jambudīpamabhibhuyya iriyati||

‘‘pabbajampi  ca anaumanik‍kamau,

aggataṃ vajati sabbapāṇinaṃ|

taina uttaritarau na vij‍jati,

sabbalaukamabhibhuyya viharatī’’ti||

(11) aiṇijaṅghalakkhaṇaṃ

214. ‘‘yampi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau purimaṃ jātiṃ purimaṃ bhavaṃ purimaṃ nikaitaṃ pubbai manussabhūtau samānau sak‍kac‍caṃ vācaitā ahausi sippaṃ vā vij‍jaṃ vā caraṇaṃ vā kammaṃ vā – ‘kiṃ timai khippaṃ vijānaiyyuṃ, khippaṃ paṭipaj‍jaiyyuṃ, na ciraṃ kilissaiyyu’’nti| sau tassa kammassa kaṭattā…pai॰… sau tatau cutau itthattaṃ āgatau samānau  imaṃ mahāpurisalakkhaṇaṃ paṭilabhati| aiṇijaṅghau hauti|

‘‘sau taina lakkhaṇaina saman‍nāgatau sacai agāraṃ ajjhāvasati, rājā hauti cak‍kavattī…pai॰… rājā samānau kiṃ labhati? yāni tāni rājārahāni rājaṅgāni rājūpabhaugāni  rājānucchavikāni tāni khippaṃ paṭilabhati| rājā samānau idaṃ labhati… buddhau samānau kiṃ labhati? yāni  tāni samaṇārahāni samaṇaṅgāni samaṇūpabhaugāni samaṇānucchavikāni, tāni khippaṃ paṭilabhati| buddhau samānau idaṃ labhati’’| aitamatthaṃ bhagavā avauca|

215. tatthaitaṃ vuc‍cati –

‘‘sippaisu vij‍jācaraṇaisu kammaisu kammasu (sī॰ pī॰),

kathaṃ vijānaiyyuṃ vijānaiyya (sī॰ pī॰), vijānaiyyu (syā॰) lahunti icchati|

yadūpaghātāya  na hauti kassaci,

vācaiti khippaṃ na ciraṃ kilissati||

‘‘taṃ kammaṃ katvā kusalaṃ sukhudrayaṃ sukhindriyaṃ (ka॰),

jaṅghā manuñ‍ñā labhatai susaṇṭhitā|

vaṭṭā sujātā anupubbamuggatā,

uddhaggalaumā sukhumattacautthatā||

‘‘aiṇaiyyajaṅghauti tamāhu puggalaṃ,

sampattiyā khippamidhāhu khippamidāhu (?) lakkhaṇaṃ|

gaihānulaumāni yadābhikaṅkhati,

apabbajaṃ khippamidhādhigacchati khippamidādhigacchati (?)||

‘‘sacai  ca pabbaj‍jamupaiti tādisau,

naikkhammachandābhiratau vicakkhaṇau|

anucchavikassa yadānulaumikaṃ,

taṃ vindati khippamanaumavik‍kamau nik‍kamau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰)’’ti||

(12) sukhumacchavilakkhaṇaṃ

216. ‘‘yampi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau purimaṃ jātiṃ purimaṃ bhavaṃ purimaṃ nikaitaṃ pubbai manussabhūtau  samānau samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā upasaṅkamitvā paripucchitā ahausi – ‘‘kiṃ, bhantai, kusalaṃ, kiṃ akusalaṃ, kiṃ sāvaj‍jaṃ, kiṃ anavaj‍jaṃ, kiṃ saivitabbaṃ, kiṃ na saivitabbaṃ, kiṃ mai karīyamānaṃ dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya assa, kiṃ vā pana mai karīyamānaṃ dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya  assā’’ti| sau tassa kammassa kaṭattā…pai॰… sau tatau cutau itthattaṃ āgatau samānau imaṃ mahāpurisalakkhaṇaṃ paṭilabhati| sukhumacchavi  hauti, sukhumattā chaviyā rajaujal‍laṃ kāyai na upalimpati|

‘‘sau taina lakkhaṇaina saman‍nāgatau sacai agāraṃ ajjhāvasati, rājā hauti cak‍kavattī…pai॰… rājā samānau kiṃ labhati? mahāpañ‍ñau hauti, nāssa hauti kauci pañ‍ñāya sadisau vā saiṭṭhau vā kāmabhaugīnaṃ| rājā samānau idaṃ labhati… buddhau samānau kiṃ labhati? mahāpañ‍ñau hauti puthupañ‍ñau hāsapañ‍ñau hāsupañ‍ñau (sī॰ pī॰) javanapañ‍ñau tikkhapañ‍ñau nibbaidhikapañ‍ñau, nāssa hauti kauci pañ‍ñāya sadisau vā saiṭṭhau vā sabbasattānaṃ| buddhau samānau idaṃ labhati’’| aitamatthaṃ bhagavā avauca|

217. tatthaitaṃ  vuc‍cati –

‘‘purai puratthā purimāsu jātisu,

añ‍ñātukāmau paripucchitā ahu|

sussūsitā pabbajitaṃ upāsitā,

atthantarau atthakathaṃ nisāmayi||

‘‘pañ‍ñāpaṭilābhagataina pañ‍ñāpaṭilābhakataina (sī॰ pī॰) ṭīkā aulaukaitabbā kammunā,

manussabhūtau sukhumacchavī ahu|

byākaṃsu uppādanimittakauvidā,

sukhumāni atthāni avaic‍ca dakkhiti||

‘‘sacai na pabbaj‍jamupaiti tādisau,

vattaiti cak‍kaṃ pathaviṃ pasāsati|

atthānusiṭṭhīsu pariggahaisu ca,

na taina saiyyau sadisau ca vij‍jati||

‘‘sacai  ca pabbaj‍jamupaiti tādisau,

naikkhammachandābhiratau vicakkhaṇau|

pañ‍ñāvisiṭṭhaṃ labhatai anuttaraṃ,

pappauti baudhiṃ varabhūrimaidhasau’’ti||

(13) suvaṇṇavaṇṇalakkhaṇaṃ

218. ‘‘yampi, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau purimaṃ jātiṃ purimaṃ bhavaṃ purimaṃ nikaitaṃ pubbai manussabhūtau samānau ak‍kaudhanau ahausi anupāyāsabahulau, bahumpi vuttau samānau nābhisaj‍ji na kuppi na byāpaj‍ji na  patitthīyi, na kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātvākāsi| dātā ca ahausi sukhumānaṃ mudukānaṃ attharaṇānaṃ pāvuraṇānaṃ pāpuraṇānaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) khaumasukhumānaṃ kappāsikasukhumānaṃ kausaiyyasukhumānaṃ kambalasukhumānaṃ| sau tassa kammassa kaṭattā upacitattā…pai॰… sau tatau cutau itthattaṃ āgatau samānau imaṃ mahāpurisalakkhaṇaṃ paṭilabhati| suvaṇṇavaṇṇau hauti kañ‍canasan‍nibhattacau|
