‘‘imai khau, āvusau, taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina tayau dhammā sammadakkhātā| tattha sabbaihaiva saṅgāyitabbaṃ…pai॰… atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussānaṃ|

catuk‍kaṃ

306. ‘‘atthi  khau, āvusau, taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina cattārau dhammā sammadakkhātā| tattha sabbaihaiva saṅgāyitabbaṃ, na vivaditabbaṃ…pai॰… atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussānaṃ| katamai cattārau?

‘‘cattārau satipaṭṭhānā| idhāvusau, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau  satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī…pai॰… cittai cittānupassī…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ|

‘‘cattārau sammappadhānā| idhāvusau, bhikkhu anuppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| uppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ  paggaṇhāti padahati| anuppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| uppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammausāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati|

‘‘cattārau iddhipādā| idhāvusau, bhikkhu chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti| cittasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti| vīriyasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ  iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti| vīmaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti|

‘‘cattāri jhānāni| idhāvusau, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ paṭhamajjhānaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) upasampaj‍ja viharati| vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ dutiyajjhānaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) upasampaj‍ja viharati| pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharati satau ca sampajānau, sukhañ‍ca  kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaiti, yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti – ‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’ti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ tatiyajjhānaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) upasampaj‍ja viharati| sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā, pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā, adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ catutthajjhānaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) upasampaj‍ja viharati|

307. ‘‘catassau samādhibhāvanā| atthāvusau, samādhibhāvanā bhāvitā bahulīkatā  diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārāya saṃvattati| atthāvusau, samādhibhāvanā bhāvitā bahulīkatā ñāṇadassanapaṭilābhāya saṃvattati| atthāvusau samādhibhāvanā bhāvitā bahulīkatā satisampajañ‍ñāya saṃvattati| atthāvusau samādhibhāvanā bhāvitā bahulīkatā āsavānaṃ khayāya saṃvattati|

‘‘katamā  cāvusau, samādhibhāvanā bhāvitā bahulīkatā diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārāya saṃvattati? idhāvusau, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ, āvusau, samādhibhāvanā bhāvitā bahulīkatā diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārāya saṃvattati|

‘‘katamā cāvusau, samādhibhāvanā bhāvitā bahulīkatā ñāṇadassanapaṭilābhāya saṃvattati? idhāvusau, bhikkhu ālaukasañ‍ñaṃ manasi karauti, divāsañ‍ñaṃ adhiṭṭhāti yathā divā tathā rattiṃ, yathā rattiṃ tathā divā| iti vivaṭaina caitasā apariyaunaddhaina sappabhāsaṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti| ayaṃ, āvusau samādhibhāvanā bhāvitā bahulīkatā ñāṇadassanapaṭilābhāya saṃvattati|

‘‘katamā cāvusau, samādhibhāvanā bhāvitā bahulīkatā satisampajañ‍ñāya saṃvattati? idhāvusau, bhikkhunau viditā vaidanā uppaj‍janti, viditā upaṭṭhahanti, viditā abbhatthaṃ gacchanti| viditā sañ‍ñā uppaj‍janti, viditā upaṭṭhahanti, viditā abbhatthaṃ gacchanti| viditā vitak‍kā uppaj‍janti, viditā upaṭṭhahanti, viditā abbhatthaṃ gacchanti| ayaṃ, āvusau, samādhibhāvanā bhāvitā bahulīkatā satisampajañ‍ñāya saṃvattati|

‘‘katamā  cāvusau, samādhibhāvanā bhāvitā bahulīkatā āsavānaṃ khayāya saṃvattati? idhāvusau, bhikkhu pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu udayabbayānupassī viharati| iti rūpaṃ, iti rūpassa samudayau, iti rūpassa atthaṅgamau| iti vaidanā…pai॰… iti sañ‍ñā… iti saṅkhārā… iti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, iti viñ‍ñāṇassa samudayau, iti viñ‍ñāṇassa atthaṅgamau| ayaṃ, āvusau, samādhibhāvanā bhāvitā bahulīkatā āsavānaṃ khayāya saṃvattati|

308. ‘‘catassau  appamañ‍ñā| idhāvusau, bhikkhu maittāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati| tathā dutiyaṃ| tathā tatiyaṃ| tathā catutthaṃ| iti uddhamadhau  tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ maittāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina abyāpajjhaina (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) pharitvā viharati| karuṇāsahagataina caitasā…pai॰… muditāsahagataina caitasā…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati| tathā dutiyaṃ| tathā tatiyaṃ| tathā catutthaṃ| iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina pharitvā viharati|

‘‘cattārau āruppā| arūpā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) idhāvusau, bhikkhu sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ‘anantau ākāsau’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ  upasampaj‍ja viharati| sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati|

‘‘cattāri apassaināni| idhāvusau, bhikkhu saṅkhāyaikaṃ paṭisaivati, saṅkhāyaikaṃ adhivāsaiti, saṅkhāyaikaṃ parivaj‍jaiti, saṅkhāyaikaṃ vinaudaiti|

309. ‘‘cattārau  ariyavaṃsā| idhāvusau, bhikkhu santuṭṭhau hauti itarītaraina cīvaraina, itarītaracīvarasantuṭṭhiyā ca vaṇṇavādī, na ca cīvarahaitu anaisanaṃ appatirūpaṃ āpaj‍jati; aladdhā ca cīvaraṃ na paritassati, laddhā ca cīvaraṃ agadhitau agathitau (sī॰ pī॰) amucchitau anajjhāpan‍nau ādīnavadassāvī nissaraṇapañ‍ñau paribhuñ‍jati; tāya ca pana itarītaracīvarasantuṭṭhiyā naivattānuk‍kaṃsaiti na paraṃ vambhaiti| yau hi tattha dakkhau analasau sampajānau paṭissatau, ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau  – ‘bhikkhu paurāṇai aggañ‍ñai ariyavaṃsai ṭhitau’|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu santuṭṭhau hauti itarītaraina piṇḍapātaina, itarītarapiṇḍapātasantuṭṭhiyā ca vaṇṇavādī, na ca piṇḍapātahaitu anaisanaṃ appatirūpaṃ āpaj‍jati; aladdhā ca piṇḍapātaṃ na paritassati, laddhā ca piṇḍapātaṃ agadhitau amucchitau anajjhāpan‍nau ādīnavadassāvī nissaraṇapañ‍ñau paribhuñ‍jati; tāya ca pana itarītarapiṇḍapātasantuṭṭhiyā  naivattānuk‍kaṃsaiti na paraṃ vambhaiti| yau hi tattha dakkhau analasau sampajānau paṭissatau, ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau – ‘bhikkhu paurāṇai aggañ‍ñai ariyavaṃsai ṭhitau’|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu santuṭṭhau hauti itarītaraina saināsanaina, itarītarasaināsanasantuṭṭhiyā ca vaṇṇavādī, na ca saināsanahaitu anaisanaṃ appatirūpaṃ āpaj‍jati; aladdhā ca saināsanaṃ na paritassati, laddhā ca saināsanaṃ agadhitau amucchitau anajjhāpan‍nau ādīnavadassāvī nissaraṇapañ‍ñau paribhuñ‍jati; tāya ca pana itarītarasaināsanasantuṭṭhiyā naivattānuk‍kaṃsaiti na paraṃ vambhaiti| yau hi tattha dakkhau analasau sampajānau paṭissatau, ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau – ‘bhikkhu paurāṇai aggañ‍ñai ariyavaṃsai ṭhitau’|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu pahānārāmau hauti pahānaratau, bhāvanārāmau hauti bhāvanāratau; tāya ca pana pahānārāmatāya pahānaratiyā bhāvanārāmatāya bhāvanāratiyā naivattānuk‍kaṃsaiti na paraṃ vambhaiti| yau hi tattha dakkhau analasau sampajānau paṭissatau ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau – ‘bhikkhu paurāṇai aggañ‍ñai ariyavaṃsai ṭhitau’|

310. ‘‘cattāri  padhānāni| saṃvarapadhānaṃ pahānapadhānaṃ bhāvanāpadhānaṃ bhāvanāppadhānaṃ (syā॰) anurakkhaṇāpadhānaṃ anurakkhanāppadhānaṃ (syā॰)| katamañ‍cāvusau, saṃvarapadhānaṃ? idhāvusau, bhikkhu cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ  viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati, rakkhati cakkhundriyaṃ, cakkhundriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā… kāyaina  phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ manindriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati, rakkhati manindriyaṃ, manindriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| idaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, saṃvarapadhānaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍cāvusau, pahānapadhānaṃ? idhāvusau, bhikkhu uppan‍naṃ kāmavitak‍kaṃ nādhivāsaiti pajahati vinaudaiti byantiṃ karauti byantī karauti (syā॰ kaṃ॰) anabhāvaṃ gamaiti| uppan‍naṃ byāpādavitak‍kaṃ…pai॰… uppan‍naṃ vihiṃsāvitak‍kaṃ… uppan‍nuppan‍nai pāpakai akusalai dhammai nādhivāsaiti pajahati vinaudaiti byantiṃ karauti anabhāvaṃ gamaiti| idaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, pahānapadhānaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍cāvusau, bhāvanāpadhānaṃ? idhāvusau, bhikkhu satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti… vīriyasambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti… pītisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti… passaddhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti… samādhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| idaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, bhāvanāpadhānaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍cāvusau, anurakkhaṇāpadhānaṃ? idhāvusau, bhikkhu uppan‍naṃ bhadrakaṃ bhaddakaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) samādhinimittaṃ anurakkhati – aṭṭhikasañ‍ñaṃ, puḷuvakasañ‍ñaṃ puḷavakasañ‍ñaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), vinīlakasañ‍ñaṃ, vicchiddakasañ‍ñaṃ, uddhumātakasañ‍ñaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, anurakkhaṇāpadhānaṃ|

‘‘cattāri  ñāṇāni – dhammai ñāṇaṃ, anvayai ñāṇaṃ, pariyai paric‍cai (sī॰ ka॰), paricchaidai (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰) ṭīkā aulaukaitabbā ñāṇaṃ, sammutiyā ñāṇaṃ sammatiñāṇaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰)|

‘‘aparānipi  cattāri ñāṇāni – dukkhai ñāṇaṃ, dukkhasamudayai ñāṇaṃ, dukkhaniraudhai ñāṇaṃ, dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ñāṇaṃ|

311. ‘‘cattāri sautāpattiyaṅgāni – sappurisasaṃsaivau, saddhammassavanaṃ, yaunisaumanasikārau, dhammānudhammappaṭipatti|

‘‘cattāri sautāpan‍nassa aṅgāni| idhāvusau, ariyasāvakau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau, bhagavā’ti| dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – ‘svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau sandiṭṭhikau akālikau aihipassikau aupanaiyyikau aupanayikau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhī’ti| saṅghai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – ‘suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau ujuppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau ñāyappaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau sāmīcippaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau yadidaṃ cattāri purisayugāni aṭṭha purisapuggalā, aisa bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau āhunaiyyau pāhunaiyyau dakkhiṇaiyyau añ‍jalikaraṇīyau anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’ti| ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti akhaṇḍaihi acchiddaihi asabalaihi akammāsaihi bhujissaihi viñ‍ñuppasatthaihi aparāmaṭṭhaihi samādhisaṃvattanikaihi|

‘‘cattāri  sāmañ‍ñaphalāni – sautāpattiphalaṃ, sakadāgāmiphalaṃ, anāgāmiphalaṃ, arahattaphalaṃ|

‘‘catassau  dhātuyau – pathavīdhātu, āpaudhātu, taijaudhātu, vāyaudhātu|

‘‘cattārau āhārā – kabaḷīkārau āhārau auḷārikau vā sukhumau vā, phassau dutiyau, manausañ‍caitanā tatiyā, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ catutthaṃ|

‘‘catassau viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitiyau| rūpūpāyaṃ vā, āvusau, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ tiṭṭhamānaṃ tiṭṭhati rūpārammaṇaṃ rūpāramaṇaṃ (?) rūpappatiṭṭhaṃ nandūpasaicanaṃ vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jati; vaidanūpāyaṃ  vā āvusau…pai॰… sañ‍ñūpāyaṃ vā, āvusau…pai॰… saṅkhārūpāyaṃ vā, āvusau, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ tiṭṭhamānaṃ tiṭṭhati saṅkhārārammaṇaṃ saṅkhārappatiṭṭhaṃ nandūpasaicanaṃ vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jati|

‘‘cattāri agatigamanāni – chandāgatiṃ gacchati, dausāgati gacchati, mauhāgatiṃ gacchati, bhayāgatiṃ gacchati|

‘‘cattārau taṇhuppādā – cīvarahaitu vā, āvusau, bhikkhunau taṇhā uppaj‍jamānā uppaj‍jati; piṇḍapātahaitu vā, āvusau, bhikkhunau taṇhā uppaj‍jamānā uppaj‍jati; saināsanahaitu vā, āvusau, bhikkhunau taṇhā uppaj‍jamānā uppaj‍jati; itibhavābhavahaitu vā, āvusau, bhikkhunau taṇhā uppaj‍jamānā uppaj‍jati|

‘‘catassau  paṭipadā – dukkhā paṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā, dukkhā paṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñā, sukhā paṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā, sukhā paṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñā|

‘‘aparāpi  catassau paṭipadā – akkhamā paṭipadā, khamā paṭipadā, damā paṭipadā, samā paṭipadā|

‘‘cattāri  dhammapadāni – anabhijjhā dhammapadaṃ, abyāpādau dhammapadaṃ, sammāsati dhammapadaṃ, sammāsamādhi dhammapadaṃ|

‘‘cattāri dhammasamādānāni – atthāvusau, dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nadukkhañ‍caiva āyatiñ‍ca dukkhavipākaṃ| atthāvusau, dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nadukkhaṃ āyatiṃ sukhavipākaṃ| atthāvusau, dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nasukhaṃ āyatiṃ dukkhavipākaṃ| atthāvusau, dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nasukhañ‍caiva āyatiñ‍ca sukhavipākaṃ|

‘‘cattārau dhammakkhandhā – sīlakkhandhau, samādhikkhandhau, pañ‍ñākkhandhau, vimuttikkhandhau|

‘‘cattāri balāni – vīriyabalaṃ, satibalaṃ, samādhibalaṃ, pañ‍ñābalaṃ|

‘‘cattāri adhiṭṭhānāni – pañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānaṃ, sac‍cādhiṭṭhānaṃ, cāgādhiṭṭhānaṃ, upasamādhiṭṭhānaṃ|

312. ‘‘cattāri  pañhabyākaraṇāni – cattārau pañhābyākaraṇā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) aikaṃsabyākaraṇīyau pañhau, paṭipucchābyākaraṇīyau pañhau, vibhaj‍jabyākaraṇīyau pañhau, ṭhapanīyau pañhau|

‘‘cattāri  kammāni – atthāvusau, kammaṃ kaṇhaṃ kaṇhavipākaṃ; atthāvusau, kammaṃ suk‍kaṃ suk‍kavipākaṃ; atthāvusau, kammaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kavipākaṃ; atthāvusau, kammaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kavipākaṃ kammakkhayāya saṃvattati|

‘‘cattārau sacchikaraṇīyā dhammā – pubbainivāsau satiyā sacchikaraṇīyau; sattānaṃ cutūpapātau cakkhunā sacchikaraṇīyau; aṭṭha vimaukkhā kāyaina sacchikaraṇīyā; āsavānaṃ khayau pañ‍ñāya sacchikaraṇīyau|

‘‘cattārau aughā – kāmaughau, bhavaughau, diṭṭhaughau, avij‍jaughau|

‘‘cattārau yaugā – kāmayaugau, bhavayaugau, diṭṭhiyaugau, avij‍jāyaugau|

‘‘cattārau  visañ‍ñaugā – kāmayaugavisañ‍ñaugau, bhavayaugavisañ‍ñaugau, diṭṭhiyaugavisañ‍ñaugau, avij‍jāyaugavisañ‍ñaugau|

‘‘cattārau ganthā – abhijjhā kāyaganthau, byāpādau kāyaganthau, sīlabbataparāmāsau kāyaganthau, idaṃsac‍cābhinivaisau kāyaganthau|

‘‘cattāri upādānāni – kāmupādānaṃ kāmūpādānaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) aivamitaraisupi, diṭṭhupādānaṃ, sīlabbatupādānaṃ, attavādupādānaṃ|

‘‘catassau yauniyau – aṇḍajayauni, jalābujayauni, saṃsaidajayauni, aupapātikayauni|

‘‘catassau  gabbhāvak‍kantiyau| idhāvusau, aikac‍cau asampajānau mātukucchiṃ auk‍kamati, asampajānau mātukucchismiṃ ṭhāti, asampajānau mātukucchimhā nikkhamati, ayaṃ paṭhamā gabbhāvak‍kanti| puna caparaṃ, āvusau, idhaikac‍cau  sampajānau mātukucchiṃ auk‍kamati, asampajānau mātukucchismiṃ ṭhāti, asampajānau mātukucchimhā nikkhamati, ayaṃ dutiyā gabbhāvak‍kanti| puna caparaṃ, āvusau, idhaikac‍cau sampajānau mātukucchiṃ auk‍kamati, sampajānau mātukucchismiṃ ṭhāti, asampajānau mātukucchimhā nikkhamati, ayaṃ tatiyā gabbhāvak‍kanti| puna caparaṃ, āvusau, idhaikac‍cau sampajānau mātukucchiṃ auk‍kamati, sampajānau mātukucchismiṃ ṭhāti, sampajānau mātukucchimhā nikkhamati, ayaṃ catutthā gabbhāvak‍kanti|

‘‘cattārau attabhāvapaṭilābhā| atthāvusau, attabhāvapaṭilābhau, yasmiṃ attabhāvapaṭilābhai attasañ‍caitanāyaiva kamati, nau parasañ‍caitanā| atthāvusau, attabhāvapaṭilābhau, yasmiṃ attabhāvapaṭilābhai parasañ‍caitanāyaiva kamati, nau attasañ‍caitanā| atthāvusau, attabhāvapaṭilābhau, yasmiṃ attabhāvapaṭilābhai attasañ‍caitanā caiva kamati parasañ‍caitanā ca| atthāvusau, attabhāvapaṭilābhau, yasmiṃ attabhāvapaṭilābhai naiva attasañ‍caitanā kamati, nau parasañ‍caitanā|

313. ‘‘catassau dakkhiṇāvisuddhiyau| atthāvusau, dakkhiṇā dāyakatau visujjhati nau paṭiggāhakatau| atthāvusau, dakkhiṇā paṭiggāhakatau  visujjhati nau dāyakatau| atthāvusau, dakkhiṇā naiva dāyakatau visujjhati nau  paṭiggāhakatau| atthāvusau, dakkhiṇā dāyakatau caiva visujjhati paṭiggāhakatau ca|

‘‘cattāri  saṅgahavatthūni – dānaṃ, paiyyavaj‍jaṃ piyavaj‍jaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰), atthacariyā, samānattatā|

‘‘cattārau anariyavauhārā – musāvādau, pisuṇāvācā, pharusāvācā, samphappalāpau|

‘‘cattārau ariyavauhārā – musāvādā vairamaṇī vairamaṇi (ka॰), pisuṇāya vācāya vairamaṇī, pharusāya vācāya vairamaṇī, samphappalāpā vairamaṇī|

‘‘aparaipi cattārau anariyavauhārā – adiṭṭhai diṭṭhavāditā, assutai sutavāditā, amutai mutavāditā, aviñ‍ñātai viñ‍ñātavāditā|

‘‘aparaipi  cattārau ariyavauhārā – adiṭṭhai adiṭṭhavāditā, assutai assutavāditā, amutai amutavāditā, aviñ‍ñātai aviñ‍ñātavāditā|

‘‘aparaipi cattārau anariyavauhārā – diṭṭhai adiṭṭhavāditā, sutai assutavāditā, mutai amutavāditā, viñ‍ñātai aviñ‍ñātavāditā|

‘‘aparaipi cattārau ariyavauhārā – diṭṭhai diṭṭhavāditā, sutai sutavāditā, mutai mutavāditā, viñ‍ñātai viñ‍ñātavāditā|

314. ‘‘cattārau puggalā| idhāvusau, aikac‍cau puggalau attantapau hauti attaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau| idhāvusau, aikac‍cau puggalau parantapau  hauti paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau| idhāvusau, aikac‍cau puggalau attantapau ca hauti attaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau, parantapau ca paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau| idhāvusau, aikac‍cau puggalau naiva attantapau hauti na attaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau na parantapau na paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau| sau anattantapau aparantapau  diṭṭhaiva dhammai nicchātau nibbutau sītībhūtau sītibhūtau (ka॰) sukhappaṭisaṃvaidī brahmabhūtaina attanā viharati|

‘‘aparaipi cattārau puggalā| idhāvusau, aikac‍cau puggalau attahitāya paṭipan‍nau hauti nau parahitāya| idhāvusau, aikac‍cau puggalau parahitāya paṭipan‍nau hauti nau attahitāya| idhāvusau, aikac‍cau puggalau naiva attahitāya paṭipan‍nau hauti nau parahitāya| idhāvusau, aikac‍cau puggalau attahitāya caiva paṭipan‍nau hauti parahitāya ca|

‘‘aparaipi cattārau puggalā – tamau tamaparāyanau, tamau jautiparāyanau, jauti tamaparāyanau, jauti jautiparāyanau|

‘‘aparaipi cattārau puggalā – samaṇamacalau, samaṇapadumau, samaṇapuṇḍarīkau, samaṇaisu samaṇasukhumālau|

‘‘imai  khau, āvusau, taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina cattārau dhammā sammadakkhātā; tattha sabbaihaiva saṅgāyitabbaṃ…pai॰… atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussānaṃ|

paṭhamabhāṇavārau niṭṭhitau|

pañ‍cakaṃ

315. ‘‘atthi  khau, āvusau, taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina pañ‍ca dhammā sammadakkhātā| tattha sabbaihaiva saṅgāyitabbaṃ…pai॰… atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussānaṃ| katamai pañ‍ca?

‘‘pañ‍cakkhandhā| rūpakkhandhau vaidanākkhandhau sañ‍ñākkhandhau saṅkhārakkhandhau viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau|

‘‘pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā| rūpupādānakkhandhau rūpūpādānakkhandhau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) aivamitaraisupi vaidanupādānakkhandhau  sañ‍ñupādānakkhandhau saṅkhārupādānakkhandhau viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhau|

‘‘pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā| cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasañhitā rajanīyā, sautaviñ‍ñaiyyā saddā… ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyā gandhā… jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyā phauṭṭhabbā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasañhitā rajanīyā|

‘‘pañ‍ca gatiyau – nirayau, tiracchānayauni, paittivisayau, manussā, daivā|

‘‘pañ‍ca macchariyāni – āvāsamacchariyaṃ, kulamacchariyaṃ, lābhamacchariyaṃ, vaṇṇamacchariyaṃ, dhammamacchariyaṃ|

‘‘pañ‍ca nīvaraṇāni – kāmacchandanīvaraṇaṃ, byāpādanīvaraṇaṃ, thinamiddhanīvaraṇaṃ, uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍canīvaraṇaṃ, vicikicchānīvaraṇaṃ|

‘‘pañ‍ca aurambhāgiyāni sañ‍ñaujanāni – sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi, vicikicchā, sīlabbataparāmāsau, kāmacchandau, byāpādau|

‘‘pañ‍ca  uddhambhāgiyāni sañ‍ñaujanāni – rūparāgau, arūparāgau, mānau, uddhac‍caṃ, avij‍jā|

‘‘pañ‍ca  sikkhāpadāni – pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī, adin‍nādānā vairamaṇī, kāmaisumicchācārā vairamaṇī, musāvādā vairamaṇī, surāmairayamaj‍jappamādaṭṭhānā vairamaṇī|

316. ‘‘pañ‍ca abhabbaṭṭhānāni| abhabbau, āvusau, khīṇāsavau bhikkhu sañ‍cic‍ca pāṇaṃ jīvitā vauraupaituṃ| abhabbau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyituṃ ādātuṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)| abhabbau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivituṃ| abhabbau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu sampajānamusā bhāsituṃ| abhabbau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu san‍nidhikārakaṃ kāmai paribhuñ‍jituṃ, saiyyathāpi pubbai āgārikabhūtau|

‘‘pañ‍ca byasanāni – ñātibyasanaṃ, bhaugabyasanaṃ, raugabyasanaṃ, sīlabyasanaṃ, diṭṭhibyasanaṃ| nāvusau, sattā ñātibyasanahaitu vā bhaugabyasanahaitu vā raugabyasanahaitu vā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍janti| sīlabyasanahaitu vā, āvusau, sattā diṭṭhibyasanahaitu  vā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍janti|

‘‘pañ‍ca sampadā – ñātisampadā, bhaugasampadā, āraugyasampadā, sīlasampadā, diṭṭhisampadā| nāvusau, sattā ñātisampadāhaitu vā bhaugasampadāhaitu vā āraugyasampadāhaitu vā kāyassa bhaidā  paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti| sīlasampadāhaitu vā, āvusau, sattā diṭṭhisampadāhaitu vā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti|

‘‘pañ‍ca ādīnavā dussīlassa sīlavipattiyā| idhāvusau, dussīlau sīlavipan‍nau pamādādhikaraṇaṃ mahatiṃ bhaugajāniṃ nigacchati, ayaṃ paṭhamau ādīnavau dussīlassa sīlavipattiyā| puna caparaṃ, āvusau, dussīlassa sīlavipan‍nassa pāpakau kittisaddau abbhuggacchati, ayaṃ dutiyau ādīnavau dussīlassa sīlavipattiyā| puna caparaṃ, āvusau, dussīlau sīlavipan‍nau  yañ‍ñadaiva parisaṃ upasaṅkamati yadi khattiyaparisaṃ yadi brāhmaṇaparisaṃ yadi gahapatiparisaṃ yadi samaṇaparisaṃ, avisāradau upasaṅkamati maṅkubhūtau, ayaṃ tatiyau ādīnavau dussīlassa sīlavipattiyā| puna caparaṃ, āvusau, dussīlau sīlavipan‍nau sammūḷhau kālaṃ karauti, ayaṃ catutthau ādīnavau dussīlassa sīlavipattiyā| puna caparaṃ, āvusau, dussīlau sīlavipan‍nau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati, ayaṃ pañ‍camau ādīnavau dussīlassa sīlavipattiyā|

‘‘pañ‍ca ānisaṃsā sīlavatau sīlasampadāya| idhāvusau, sīlavā sīlasampan‍nau appamādādhikaraṇaṃ mahantaṃ bhaugakkhandhaṃ adhigacchati, ayaṃ paṭhamau ānisaṃsau sīlavatau sīlasampadāya| puna caparaṃ, āvusau, sīlavatau sīlasampan‍nassa kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggacchati, ayaṃ dutiyau  ānisaṃsau sīlavatau sīlasampadāya| puna caparaṃ, āvusau, sīlavā sīlasampan‍nau yañ‍ñadaiva parisaṃ upasaṅkamati yadi khattiyaparisaṃ yadi brāhmaṇaparisaṃ yadi gahapatiparisaṃ yadi samaṇaparisaṃ, visāradau upasaṅkamati amaṅkubhūtau, ayaṃ tatiyau ānisaṃsau sīlavatau sīlasampadāya| puna caparaṃ, āvusau, sīlavā sīlasampan‍nau asammūḷhau kālaṃ karauti, ayaṃ catutthau ānisaṃsau sīlavatau sīlasampadāya| puna caparaṃ, āvusau, sīlavā sīlasampan‍nau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati, ayaṃ pañ‍camau ānisaṃsau sīlavatau sīlasampadāya|

‘‘caudakaina, āvusau, bhikkhunā paraṃ caudaitukāmaina pañ‍ca dhammai ajjhattaṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā parau caudaitabbau| kālaina vakkhāmi nau akālaina, bhūtaina vakkhāmi nau abhūtaina, saṇhaina vakkhāmi nau pharusaina, atthasaṃhitaina  vakkhāmi nau anatthasaṃhitaina, maittacittaina maittācittaina (katthaci) vakkhāmi nau dausantaraināti| caudakaina, āvusau, bhikkhunā paraṃ caudaitukāmaina imai pañ‍ca dhammai ajjhattaṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā parau caudaitabbau|

317. ‘‘pañ‍ca  padhāniyaṅgāni| idhāvusau, bhikkhu saddhau hauti, saddahati tathāgatassa baudhiṃ – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau, laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti| appābādhau hauti appātaṅkau, samavaipākiniyā  gahaṇiyā saman‍nāgatau nātisītāya nāc‍cuṇhāya majjhimāya padhānakkhamāya| asaṭhau hauti amāyāvī, yathābhūtaṃ attānaṃ āvikattā satthari vā viñ‍ñūsu vā sabrahmacārīsu| āraddhavīriyau viharati akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu| pañ‍ñavā hauti udayatthagāminiyā pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau ariyāya nibbaidhikāya sammādukkhakkhayagāminiyā|

318. ‘‘pañ‍ca suddhāvāsā – avihā, atappā, sudassā, sudassī, akaniṭṭhā|

‘‘pañ‍ca anāgāminau – antarāparinibbāyī, upahac‍caparinibbāyī, asaṅkhāraparinibbāyī, sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyī, uddhaṃsautauakaniṭṭhagāmī|

319. ‘‘pañ‍ca caitaukhilā| idhāvusau, bhikkhu satthari kaṅkhati  vicikicchati nādhimuc‍cati na sampasīdati| yau sau, āvusau, bhikkhu satthari kaṅkhati vicikicchati nādhimuc‍cati na sampasīdati, tassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya, yassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya, ayaṃ paṭhamau caitaukhilau| puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu dhammai kaṅkhati vicikicchati…pai॰… saṅghai kaṅkhati vicikicchati… sikkhāya kaṅkhati vicikicchati… sabrahmacārīsu kupitau hauti anattamanau āhatacittau khilajātau| yau sau, āvusau, bhikkhu  sabrahmacārīsu kupitau hauti anattamanau āhatacittau khilajātau, tassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya, yassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya, ayaṃ pañ‍camau caitaukhilau|

320. ‘‘pañ‍ca  caitasauvinibandhā| idhāvusau, bhikkhu kāmaisu avītarāgau hauti avigatacchandau avigatapaimau avigatapipāsau avigatapariḷāhau  avigatataṇhau| yau sau, āvusau, bhikkhu kāmaisu avītarāgau hauti avigatacchandau avigatapaimau avigatapipāsau avigatapariḷāhau avigatataṇhau, tassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya| yassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya| ayaṃ paṭhamau caitasau vinibandhau| puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu kāyai avītarāgau hauti…pai॰… rūpai avītarāgau hauti…pai॰… puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu yāvadatthaṃ udarāvadaihakaṃ bhuñ‍jitvā saiyyasukhaṃ passasukhaṃ middhasukhaṃ anuyuttau viharati…pai॰… puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu  añ‍ñataraṃ daivanikāyaṃ paṇidhāya brahmacariyaṃ carati – ‘imināhaṃ sīlaina vā vataina vā tapaina vā brahmacariyaina vā daivau vā bhavissāmi daivañ‍ñatarau vā’ti| yau sau, āvusau, bhikkhu añ‍ñataraṃ daivanikāyaṃ paṇidhāya brahmacariyaṃ carati – ‘imināhaṃ sīlaina vā vataina vā tapaina vā brahmacariyaina vā daivau vā bhavissāmi daivañ‍ñatarau vā’ti, tassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya| yassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya  padhānāya| ayaṃ pañ‍camau caitasau vinibandhau|

‘‘pañ‍cindriyāni – cakkhundriyaṃ, sautindriyaṃ, ghānindriyaṃ, jivhindriyaṃ, kāyindriyaṃ|

‘‘aparānipi pañ‍cindriyāni – sukhindriyaṃ, dukkhindriyaṃ, saumanassindriyaṃ, daumanassindriyaṃ, upaikkhindriyaṃ|

‘‘aparānipi pañ‍cindriyāni – saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ|

321. ‘‘pañ‍ca nissaraṇiyā nissāraṇīyā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) ṭīkā aulaukaitabbā dhātuyau| idhāvusau, bhikkhunau kāmai manasikarautau kāmaisu cittaṃ na pakkhandati na pasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuc‍cati| naikkhammaṃ khau panassa manasikarautau naikkhammai cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuc‍cati| tassa taṃ cittaṃ sugataṃ  subhāvitaṃ suvuṭṭhitaṃ suvimuttaṃ visaṃyuttaṃ kāmaihi| yai ca kāmapac‍cayā uppaj‍janti āsavā vighātā pariḷāhā vighātapariḷāhā (syā॰ kaṃ॰), muttau sau taihi, na sau taṃ vaidanaṃ vaidaiti| idamakkhātaṃ kāmānaṃ nissaraṇaṃ|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunau byāpādaṃ manasikarautau byāpādai cittaṃ na pakkhandati na  pasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuc‍cati| abyāpādaṃ khau panassa manasikarautau abyāpādai cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuc‍cati| tassa taṃ cittaṃ sugataṃ subhāvitaṃ suvuṭṭhitaṃ suvimuttaṃ visaṃyuttaṃ byāpādaina| yai ca byāpādapac‍cayā uppaj‍janti āsavā vighātā pariḷāhā, muttau sau taihi, na sau taṃ vaidanaṃ vaidaiti| idamakkhātaṃ byāpādassa  nissaraṇaṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunau vihaisaṃ manasikarautau vihaisāya cittaṃ na pakkhandati na pasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuc‍cati| avihaisaṃ khau panassa manasikarautau avihaisāya cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuc‍cati| tassa taṃ cittaṃ sugataṃ subhāvitaṃ suvuṭṭhitaṃ suvimuttaṃ visaṃyuttaṃ vihaisāya| yai ca vihaisāpac‍cayā uppaj‍janti āsavā vighātā pariḷāhā, muttau sau taihi, na sau taṃ vaidanaṃ vaidaiti| idamakkhātaṃ vihaisāya nissaraṇaṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunau rūpai manasikarautau rūpaisu cittaṃ na pakkhandati na pasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuc‍cati| arūpaṃ khau panassa manasikarautau arūpai cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuc‍cati| tassa taṃ cittaṃ sugataṃ subhāvitaṃ suvuṭṭhitaṃ suvimuttaṃ visaṃyuttaṃ rūpaihi| yai ca rūpapac‍cayā uppaj‍janti āsavā vighātā pariḷāhā, muttau sau taihi, na sau taṃ vaidanaṃ vaidaiti| idamakkhātaṃ rūpānaṃ nissaraṇaṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunau sak‍kāyaṃ manasikarautau sak‍kāyai cittaṃ na pakkhandati na pasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuc‍cati| sak‍kāyaniraudhaṃ khau panassa manasikarautau sak‍kāyaniraudhai cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuc‍cati| tassa taṃ cittaṃ sugataṃ subhāvitaṃ suvuṭṭhitaṃ suvimuttaṃ visaṃyuttaṃ sak‍kāyaina| yai ca sak‍kāyapac‍cayā uppaj‍janti āsavā vighātā pariḷāhā, muttau sau  taihi, na sau taṃ vaidanaṃ vaidaiti| idamakkhātaṃ sak‍kāyassa nissaraṇaṃ|

322. ‘‘pañ‍ca  vimuttāyatanāni| idhāvusau, bhikkhunau satthā dhammaṃ daisaiti añ‍ñatarau vā garuṭṭhāniyau sabrahmacārī| yathā yathā, āvusau, bhikkhunau satthā dhammaṃ daisaiti añ‍ñatarau vā garuṭṭhāniyau sabrahmacārī| tathā tathā sau tasmiṃ dhammai atthapaṭisaṃvaidī ca hauti dhammapaṭisaṃvaidī ca| tassa atthapaṭisaṃvaidinau dhammapaṭisaṃvaidinau pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati, pamuditassa pīti jāyati, pītimanassa kāyau passambhati, passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidaiti, sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati| idaṃ paṭhamaṃ vimuttāyatanaṃ|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunau na haiva khau satthā dhammaṃ daisaiti añ‍ñatarau vā garuṭṭhāniyau sabrahmacārī, api ca khau yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ vitthāraina paraisaṃ daisaiti…pai॰… api ca khau yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ vitthāraina sajjhāyaṃ karauti…pai॰… api ca khau yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ caitasā anuvitak‍kaiti anuvicāraiti manasānupaikkhati…pai॰…  api ca khvassa añ‍ñataraṃ samādhinimittaṃ suggahitaṃ hauti sumanasikataṃ sūpadhāritaṃ suppaṭividdhaṃ pañ‍ñāya| yathā yathā, āvusau, bhikkhunau añ‍ñataraṃ samādhinimittaṃ suggahitaṃ hauti sumanasikataṃ sūpadhāritaṃ suppaṭividdhaṃ pañ‍ñāya tathā tathā sau tasmiṃ dhammai atthapaṭisaṃvaidī ca hauti dhammapaṭisaṃvaidī ca| tassa atthapaṭisaṃvaidinau dhammapaṭisaṃvaidinau pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati, pamuditassa pīti jāyati, pītimanassa kāyau passambhati, passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidaiti, sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati| idaṃ pañ‍camaṃ vimuttāyatanaṃ|

‘‘pañ‍ca  vimuttiparipācanīyā sañ‍ñā – anic‍casañ‍ñā, anic‍cai dukkhasañ‍ñā, dukkhai anattasañ‍ñā, pahānasañ‍ñā, virāgasañ‍ñā|

‘‘imai khau, āvusau, taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina pañ‍ca dhammā sammadakkhātā; tattha sabbaihaiva saṅgāyitabbaṃ…pai॰… atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussānaṃ saṅgitiyapañ‍cakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰)|

chak‍kaṃ

323. ‘‘atthi khau, āvusau, taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina cha dhammā sammadakkhātā; tattha sabbaihaiva saṅgāyitabbaṃ…pai॰… atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussānaṃ| katamai cha?

‘‘cha ajjhattikāni  āyatanāni – cakkhāyatanaṃ, sautāyatanaṃ, ghānāyatanaṃ, jivhāyatanaṃ, kāyāyatanaṃ, manāyatanaṃ|

‘‘cha bāhirāni āyatanāni – rūpāyatanaṃ, saddāyatanaṃ, gandhāyatanaṃ, rasāyatanaṃ, phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ, dhammāyatanaṃ|

‘‘cha  viñ‍ñāṇakāyā – cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ|

‘‘cha phassakāyā – cakkhusamphassau, sautasamphassau, ghānasamphassau, jivhāsamphassau, kāyasamphassau, manausamphassau|

‘‘cha vaidanākāyā – cakkhusamphassajā vaidanā, sautasamphassajā  vaidanā, ghānasamphassajā vaidanā, jivhāsamphassajā vaidanā, kāyasamphassajā vaidanā, manausamphassajā vaidanā|

‘‘cha sañ‍ñākāyā – rūpasañ‍ñā, saddasañ‍ñā, gandhasañ‍ñā, rasasañ‍ñā, phauṭṭhabbasañ‍ñā, dhammasañ‍ñā|

‘‘cha sañ‍caitanākāyā – rūpasañ‍caitanā, saddasañ‍caitanā, gandhasañ‍caitanā, rasasañ‍caitanā, phauṭṭhabbasañ‍caitanā, dhammasañ‍caitanā|

‘‘cha  taṇhākāyā – rūpataṇhā, saddataṇhā, gandhataṇhā, rasataṇhā, phauṭṭhabbataṇhā, dhammataṇhā|

324. ‘‘cha agāravā| idhāvusau, bhikkhu satthari agāravau viharati appatissau; dhammai agāravau viharati appatissau; saṅghai agāravau viharati appatissau; sikkhāya agāravau viharati appatissau; appamādai agāravau viharati appatissau; paṭisanthārai paṭisandhārai (ka॰) agāravau viharati appatissau|

‘‘cha gāravā| idhāvusau, bhikkhu satthari sagāravau viharati sappatissau; dhammai sagāravau viharati sappatissau; saṅghai sagāravau viharati sappatissau; sikkhāya sagāravau viharati sappatissau; appamādai sagāravau viharati sappatissau; paṭisanthārai sagāravau viharati sappatissau|

‘‘cha saumanassūpavicārā| cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā saumanassaṭṭhāniyaṃ rūpaṃ upavicarati; sautaina  saddaṃ sutvā… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā| manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya saumanassaṭṭhāniyaṃ dhammaṃ upavicarati|

‘‘cha  daumanassūpavicārā| cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā daumanassaṭṭhāniyaṃ rūpaṃ upavicarati…pai॰… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya daumanassaṭṭhāniyaṃ dhammaṃ upavicarati|

‘‘cha upaikkhūpavicārā| cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā upaikkhāṭṭhāniyaṃ upaikkhāṭhāniyaṃ (ka॰) rūpaṃ upavicarati…pai॰… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya upaikkhāṭṭhāniyaṃ dhammaṃ upavicarati|

‘‘cha sāraṇīyā dhammā| idhāvusau, bhikkhunau maittaṃ kāyakammaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti sabrahmacārīsu āvi āvī (ka॰ sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) caiva rahau ca| ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau piyakaraṇau garukaraṇau saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunau maittaṃ vacīkammaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti sabrahmacārīsu āvi caiva rahau ca| ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau…pai॰… aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunau maittaṃ manaukammaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti sabrahmacārīsu āvi caiva rahau ca| ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau…pai॰… aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu yai tai lābhā dhammikā dhammaladdhā antamasau pattapariyāpan‍namattampi, tathārūpaihi lābhaihi appaṭivibhattabhaugī hauti sīlavantaihi sabrahmacārīhi sādhāraṇabhaugī| ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau…pai॰… aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu yāni tāni sīlāni akhaṇḍāni acchiddāni asabalāni akammāsāni bhujissāni viñ‍ñuppasatthāni aparāmaṭṭhāni samādhisaṃvattanikāni, tathārūpaisu sīlaisu sīlasāmañ‍ñagatau viharati  sabrahmacārīhi āvi caiva rahau ca| ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau…pai॰… aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu yāyaṃ diṭṭhi ariyā niyyānikā niyyāti tak‍karassa sammā dukkhakkhayāya, tathārūpāya  diṭṭhiyā diṭṭhisāmañ‍ñagatau viharati sabrahmacārīhi āvi caiva  rahau ca| ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau piyakaraṇau garukaraṇau saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

325. cha vivādamūlāni| idhāvusau, bhikkhu kaudhanau hauti upanāhī| yau sau, āvusau, bhikkhu kaudhanau hauti upanāhī, sau sattharipi agāravau viharati appatissau, dhammaipi agāravau viharati appatissau, saṅghaipi agāravau viharati appatissau, sikkhāyapi na paripūrakārī paripūrīkārī (syā॰ kaṃ॰) hauti| yau sau, āvusau, bhikkhu satthari agāravau viharati appatissau, dhammai agāravau viharati appatissau, saṅghai agāravau viharati appatissau, sikkhāya na paripūrakārī, sau saṅghai vivādaṃ janaiti| yau hauti vivādau bahujanaahitāya bahujanaasukhāya anatthāya ahitāya dukkhāya daivamanussānaṃ| aivarūpaṃ cai tumhai, āvusau, vivādamūlaṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā samanupassaiyyātha| tatra tumhai, āvusau, tassaiva pāpakassa vivādamūlassa pahānāya vāyamaiyyātha| aivarūpaṃ cai tumhai, āvusau, vivādamūlaṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā na samanupassaiyyātha| tatra tumhai, āvusau, tassaiva pāpakassa vivādamūlassa āyatiṃ anavassavāya paṭipaj‍jaiyyātha| aivamaitassa pāpakassa vivādamūlassa pahānaṃ hauti| aivamaitassa pāpakassa vivādamūlassa āyatiṃ anavassavau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu makkhī hauti paḷāsī…pai॰… issukī hauti maccharī…pai॰… saṭhau hauti māyāvī… pāpicchau hauti micchādiṭṭhī… sandiṭṭhiparāmāsī  hauti ādhānaggāhī duppaṭinissaggī…pai॰… yau sau, āvusau, bhikkhu sandiṭṭhiparāmāsī hauti ādhānaggāhī duppaṭinissaggī, sau sattharipi agāravau viharati appatissau, dhammaipi agāravau viharati appatissau, saṅghaipi agāravau viharati appatissau, sikkhāyapi na paripūrakārī hauti| yau sau, āvusau, bhikkhu satthari agāravau viharati appatissau, dhammai agāravau viharati appatissau, saṅghai agāravau viharati appatissau, sikkhāya na paripūrakārī, sau saṅghai vivādaṃ janaiti| yau hauti vivādau bahujanaahitāya bahujanaasukhāya anatthāya ahitāya dukkhāya daivamanussānaṃ| aivarūpaṃ cai tumhai, āvusau, vivādamūlaṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā samanupassaiyyātha| tatra tumhai, āvusau, tassaiva pāpakassa vivādamūlassa pahānāya vāyamaiyyātha| aivarūpaṃ cai tumhai, āvusau, vivādamūlaṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā na samanupassaiyyātha| tatra tumhai, āvusau, tassaiva pāpakassa vivādamūlassa āyatiṃ anavassavāya paṭipaj‍jaiyyātha| aivamaitassa pāpakassa vivādamūlassa pahānaṃ hauti| aivamaitassa pāpakassa vivādamūlassa āyatiṃ anavassavau hauti|

‘‘cha dhātuyau  – pathavīdhātu, āpaudhātu, taijaudhātu, vāyaudhātu, ākāsadhātu, viñ‍ñāṇadhātu|

326. ‘‘cha nissaraṇiyā dhātuyau| idhāvusau, bhikkhu aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘maittā hi khau mai caitauvimutti bhāvitā bahulīkatā  yānīkatā vatthukatā  anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā, atha ca pana mai byāpādau cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhatī’ti| sau ‘mā haivaṃ’, tissa vacanīyau, ‘māyasmā aivaṃ avaca, mā bhagavantaṃ abbhācikkhi, na hi sādhu bhagavatau abbhakkhānaṃ, na hi bhagavā aivaṃ vadaiyya| aṭṭhānamaitaṃ, āvusau, anavakāsau, yaṃ maittāya caitauvimuttiyā bhāvitāya bahulīkatāya yānīkatāya vatthukatāya anuṭṭhitāya paricitāya susamāraddhāya| atha ca panassa byāpādau cittaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassati, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| nissaraṇaṃ haitaṃ, āvusau, byāpādassa, yadidaṃ maittā caitauvimuttī’ti|

‘‘idha panāvusau, bhikkhu aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘karuṇā hi khau mai caitauvimutti bhāvitā bahulīkatā yānīkatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā| atha ca pana mai vihaisā cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhatī’ti, sau ‘mā haivaṃ’ tissa vacanīyau ‘māyasmā aivaṃ avaca, mā bhagavantaṃ abbhācikkhi, na hi sādhu bhagavatau abbhakkhānaṃ, na hi bhagavā aivaṃ vadaiyya| aṭṭhānamaitaṃ āvusau, anavakāsau, yaṃ karuṇāya caitauvimuttiyā bhāvitāya bahulīkatāya yānīkatāya vatthukatāya  anuṭṭhitāya paricitāya susamāraddhāya, atha ca panassa vihaisā cittaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassati, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| nissaraṇaṃ haitaṃ, āvusau, vihaisāya, yadidaṃ karuṇā caitauvimuttī’ti|

‘‘idha panāvusau, bhikkhu aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘muditā hi khau mai caitauvimutti bhāvitā bahulīkatā yānīkatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā| atha ca pana mai  arati cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhatī’ti, sau ‘mā haivaṃ’ tissa vacanīyau ‘‘māyasmā aivaṃ avaca, mā bhagavantaṃ abbhācikkhi, na hi sādhu bhagavatau abbhakkhānaṃ, na hi bhagavā aivaṃ vadaiyya| aṭṭhānamaitaṃ, āvusau, anavakāsau, yaṃ muditāya caitauvimuttiyā bhāvitāya bahulīkatāya yānīkatāya vatthukatāya  anuṭṭhitāya paricitāya susamāraddhāya, atha ca panassa arati cittaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassati, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| nissaraṇaṃ haitaṃ, āvusau, aratiyā, yadidaṃ muditā caitauvimuttī’ti|

‘‘idha  panāvusau, bhikkhu aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘upaikkhā hi khau mai caitauvimutti bhāvitā bahulīkatā yānīkatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā| atha ca pana mai rāgau cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhatī’ti| sau ‘mā haivaṃ’ tissa vacanīyau ‘māyasmā aivaṃ avaca, mā bhagavantaṃ abbhācikkhi, na hi sādhu bhagavatau abbhakkhānaṃ, na hi bhagavā aivaṃ vadaiyya| aṭṭhānamaitaṃ, āvusau, anavakāsau, yaṃ upaikkhāya caitauvimuttiyā bhāvitāya bahulīkatāya yānīkatāya vatthukatāya anuṭṭhitāya paricitāya susamāraddhāya, atha ca panassa rāgau cittaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassati naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| nissaraṇaṃ haitaṃ, āvusau, rāgassa, yadidaṃ upaikkhā caitauvimuttī’ti|

‘‘idha panāvusau, bhikkhu aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘animittā hi khau mai caitauvimutti bhāvitā bahulīkatā yānīkatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā| atha ca pana mai nimittānusāri viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hautī’ti| sau ‘mā haivaṃ’ tissa  vacanīyau ‘māyasmā aivaṃ avaca, mā bhagavantaṃ abbhācikkhi, na hi sādhu bhagavatau abbhakkhānaṃ, na hi bhagavā aivaṃ vadaiyya| aṭṭhānamaitaṃ, āvusau, anavakāsau, yaṃ animittāya caitauvimuttiyā bhāvitāya bahulīkatāya yānīkatāya vatthukatāya anuṭṭhitāya paricitāya susamāraddhāya, atha ca panassa nimittānusāri  viñ‍ñāṇaṃ bhavissati, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| nissaraṇaṃ haitaṃ, āvusau, sabbanimittānaṃ, yadidaṃ animittā caitauvimuttī’ti|

‘‘idha panāvusau, bhikkhu aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘asmīti khau mai vigataṃ vighātaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), vigatai (syā॰ ka॰), ayamahamasmīti na samanupassāmi, atha ca pana mai vicikicchākathaṅkathāsal‍laṃ cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhatī’ti| sau ‘mā haivaṃ’ tissa vacanīyau ‘māyasmā aivaṃ avaca, mā bhagavantaṃ abbhācikkhi, na hi sādhu  bhagavatau abbhakkhānaṃ, na hi bhagavā aivaṃ vadaiyya| aṭṭhānamaitaṃ, āvusau, anavakāsau, yaṃ asmīti vigatai vighātai (sī॰ pī॰) ayamahamasmīti asamanupassatau, atha ca panassa vicikicchākathaṅkathāsal‍laṃ cittaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassati, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| nissaraṇaṃ haitaṃ, āvusau, vicikicchākathaṅkathāsal‍lassa, yadidaṃ asmimānasamugghātau’ti|

327. ‘‘cha anuttariyāni – dassanānuttariyaṃ, savanānuttariyaṃ, lābhānuttariyaṃ, sikkhānuttariyaṃ, pāricariyānuttariyaṃ, anussatānuttariyaṃ|

‘‘cha anussatiṭṭhānāni  – buddhānussati, dhammānussati, saṅghānussati, sīlānussati, cāgānussati, daivatānussati|

328. ‘‘cha  satatavihārā| idhāvusau, bhikkhu cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā naiva sumanau hauti na dummanau, upaikkhakau upaikkhakau ca (syā॰ ka॰) viharati satau sampajānau| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā…pai॰… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya naiva sumanau hauti na dummanau, upaikkhakau viharati satau sampajānau|

329. ‘‘chaḷābhijātiyau| idhāvusau, aikac‍cau kaṇhābhijātikau  samānau kaṇhaṃ dhammaṃ abhijāyati| idha panāvusau, aikac‍cau kaṇhābhijātikau samānau suk‍kaṃ dhammaṃ abhijāyati| idha panāvusau, aikac‍cau kaṇhābhijātikau samānau akaṇhaṃ asuk‍kaṃ nibbānaṃ abhijāyati| idha panāvusau, aikac‍cau suk‍kābhijātikau samānau suk‍kaṃ dhammaṃ abhijāyati| idha panāvusau, aikac‍cau suk‍kābhijātikau samānau kaṇhaṃ dhammaṃ abhijāyati| idha panāvusau, aikac‍cau suk‍kābhijātikau samānau  akaṇhaṃ asuk‍kaṃ nibbānaṃ abhijāyati|

‘‘cha nibbaidhabhāgiyā sañ‍ñā nibbaidhabhāgiyasañ‍ñā (syā॰ kaṃ॰) – anic‍casañ‍ñā anic‍cai, dukkhasañ‍ñā dukkhai, anattasañ‍ñā, pahānasañ‍ñā, virāgasañ‍ñā, niraudhasañ‍ñā|

‘‘imai khau, āvusau, taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina cha dhammā sammadakkhātā; tattha sabbaihaiva saṅgāyitabbaṃ…pai॰… atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussānaṃ|

sattakaṃ

330. ‘‘atthi khau, āvusau, taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā  sammāsambuddhaina satta dhammā sammadakkhātā; tattha sabbaihaiva saṅgāyitabbaṃ…pai॰… atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussānaṃ| katamai satta?

‘‘satta  ariyadhanāni – saddhādhanaṃ, sīladhanaṃ, hiridhanaṃ, auttappadhanaṃ, sutadhanaṃ, cāgadhanaṃ, pañ‍ñādhanaṃ|

‘‘satta baujjhaṅgā – satisambaujjhaṅgau, dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau, vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau, pītisambaujjhaṅgau, passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau, samādhisambaujjhaṅgau, upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau|

‘‘satta samādhiparikkhārā – sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappau, sammāvācā, sammākammantau, sammāājīvau, sammāvāyāmau, sammāsati|

‘‘satta asaddhammā – idhāvusau, bhikkhu assaddhau hauti, ahirikau hauti, anauttappī hauti, appassutau hauti, kusītau hauti, muṭṭhassati hauti, duppañ‍ñau hauti|

‘‘satta saddhammā – idhāvusau, bhikkhu saddhau hauti, hirimā hauti, auttappī hauti, bahussutau hauti, āraddhavīriyau hauti, upaṭṭhitassati hauti, pañ‍ñavā hauti|

‘‘satta sappurisadhammā – idhāvusau, bhikkhu dhammañ‍ñū ca hauti atthañ‍ñū ca attañ‍ñū ca mattañ‍ñū ca kālañ‍ñū ca parisañ‍ñū ca puggalañ‍ñū ca|

331. ‘‘satta  niddasavatthūni| idhāvusau, bhikkhu sikkhāsamādānai tibbacchandau hauti, āyatiñ‍ca sikkhāsamādānai avigatapaimau| dhammanisantiyā  tibbacchandau hauti, āyatiñ‍ca dhammanisantiyā avigatapaimau| icchāvinayai tibbacchandau hauti, āyatiñ‍ca icchāvinayai avigatapaimau| paṭisal‍lānai tibbacchandau hauti, āyatiñ‍ca paṭisal‍lānai avigatapaimau| vīriyārambhai tibbacchandau hauti, āyatiñ‍ca vīriyārambhai avigatapaimau| satinaipak‍kai tibbacchandau hauti, āyatiñ‍ca satinaipak‍kai avigatapaimau| diṭṭhipaṭivaidhai tibbacchandau hauti, āyatiñ‍ca diṭṭhipaṭivaidhai avigatapaimau|

‘‘satta sañ‍ñā – anic‍casañ‍ñā, anattasañ‍ñā, asubhasañ‍ñā, ādīnavasañ‍ñā, pahānasañ‍ñā, virāgasañ‍ñā, niraudhasañ‍ñā|

‘‘satta  balāni – saddhābalaṃ, vīriyabalaṃ, hiribalaṃ, auttappabalaṃ, satibalaṃ, samādhibalaṃ, pañ‍ñābalaṃ|

332. ‘‘satta viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitiyau| santāvusau, sattā nānattakāyā nānattasañ‍ñinau, saiyyathāpi manussā aikac‍cai ca daivā aikac‍cai ca vinipātikā| ayaṃ paṭhamā viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti|

‘‘santāvusau, sattā nānattakāyā aikattasañ‍ñinau saiyyathāpi daivā brahmakāyikā paṭhamābhinibbattā| ayaṃ dutiyā viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti|

‘‘santāvusau, sattā aikattakāyā nānattasañ‍ñinau saiyyathāpi daivā ābhassarā| ayaṃ tatiyā viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti|

‘‘santāvusau, sattā aikattakāyā aikattasañ‍ñinau saiyyathāpi daivā subhakiṇhā| ayaṃ catutthī viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti|

‘‘santāvusau, sattā sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ‘anantau ākāsau’ti  ākāsānañ‍cāyatanūpagā| ayaṃ pañ‍camī viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti|

‘‘santāvusau, sattā sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanūpagā| ayaṃ chaṭṭhī viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti|

‘‘santāvusau, sattā sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanūpagā| ayaṃ sattamī viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti|

‘‘satta puggalā dakkhiṇaiyyā – ubhataubhāgavimuttau, pañ‍ñāvimuttau, kāyasakkhi, diṭṭhippattau, saddhāvimuttau, dhammānusārī, saddhānusārī|

‘‘satta anusayā – kāmarāgānusayau, paṭighānusayau, diṭṭhānusayau, vicikicchānusayau, mānānusayau, bhavarāgānusayau, avij‍jānusayau|

‘‘satta  sañ‍ñaujanāni – anunayasañ‍ñaujanaṃ kāmasañ‍ñaujanaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰), paṭighasañ‍ñaujanaṃ, diṭṭhisañ‍ñaujanaṃ, vicikicchāsañ‍ñaujanaṃ, mānasañ‍ñaujanaṃ, bhavarāgasañ‍ñaujanaṃ, avij‍jāsañ‍ñaujanaṃ|

‘‘satta adhikaraṇasamathā – uppan‍nuppan‍nānaṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ samathāya vūpasamāya sammukhāvinayau dātabbau, sativinayau dātabbau, amūḷhavinayau dātabbau, paṭiñ‍ñāya kāraitabbaṃ, yaibhuyyasikā, tassapāpiyasikā, tiṇavatthārakau|

‘‘imai khau, āvusau, taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina satta dhammā sammadakkhātā; tattha sabbaihaiva saṅgāyitabbaṃ…pai॰… atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussānaṃ|

dutiyabhāṇavārau niṭṭhitau|

aṭṭhakaṃ

333. ‘‘atthi  khau, āvusau, taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina aṭṭha dhammā sammadakkhātā; tattha sabbaihaiva saṅgāyitabbaṃ…pai॰… atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussānaṃ| katamai aṭṭha?

‘‘aṭṭha micchattā – micchādiṭṭhi, micchāsaṅkappau, micchāvācā, micchākammantau, micchāājīvau, micchāvāyāmau micchāsati, micchāsamādhi|

‘‘aṭṭha  sammattā – sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappau, sammāvācā, sammākammantau, sammāājīvau, sammāvāyāmau, sammāsati, sammāsamādhi|

‘‘aṭṭha puggalā dakkhiṇaiyyā – sautāpan‍nau, sautāpattiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipan‍nau; sakadāgāmī, sakadāgāmiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipan‍nau; anāgāmī, anāgāmiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipan‍nau; arahā, arahattaphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipan‍nau|

334. ‘‘aṭṭha  kusītavatthūni| idhāvusau, bhikkhunā kammaṃ kātabbaṃ hauti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘kammaṃ khau mai kātabbaṃ bhavissati, kammaṃ khau pana mai karauntassa kāyau kilamissati, handāhaṃ nipaj‍jāmī’ti! sau nipaj‍jati na vīriyaṃ ārabhati appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya| idaṃ paṭhamaṃ kusītavatthu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunā kammaṃ kataṃ hauti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khau kammaṃ akāsiṃ, kammaṃ khau pana mai karauntassa  kāyau kilantau, handāhaṃ nipaj‍jāmī’ti! sau nipaj‍jati na vīriyaṃ ārabhati…pai॰… idaṃ dutiyaṃ kusītavatthu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunā maggau gantabbau hauti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘maggau khau mai gantabbau bhavissati, maggaṃ khau pana mai gacchantassa kāyau kilamissati, handāhaṃ nipaj‍jāmī’ti! sau nipaj‍jati na vīriyaṃ ārabhati… idaṃ tatiyaṃ kusītavatthu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunā maggau gatau hauti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khau maggaṃ agamāsiṃ, maggaṃ khau pana mai gacchantassa kāyau kilantau, handāhaṃ nipaj‍jāmī’ti! sau nipaj‍jati na vīriyaṃ ārabhati… idaṃ catutthaṃ kusītavatthu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya carantau na labhati lūkhassa vā paṇītassa vā bhaujanassa yāvadatthaṃ pāripūriṃ| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khau gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya  carantau nālatthaṃ lūkhassa vā paṇītassa vā bhaujanassa yāvadatthaṃ pāripūriṃ, tassa mai kāyau kilantau akammañ‍ñau, handāhaṃ nipaj‍jāmī’ti! sau nipaj‍jati na vīriyaṃ ārabhati… idaṃ pañ‍camaṃ kusītavatthu|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya carantau labhati lūkhassa vā paṇītassa vā bhaujanassa yāvadatthaṃ pāripūriṃ| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khau gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya carantau alatthaṃ lūkhassa vā paṇītassa vā bhaujanassa yāvadatthaṃ pāripūriṃ, tassa mai kāyau garukau akammañ‍ñau, māsācitaṃ mañ‍ñai, handāhaṃ nipaj‍jāmī’ti! sau nipaj‍jati na vīriyaṃ ārabhati… idaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ kusītavatthu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunau uppan‍nau hauti appamattakau ābādhau| tassa aivaṃ hauti  – ‘uppan‍nau khau mai ayaṃ appamattakau ābādhau; atthi kappau nipaj‍jituṃ, handāhaṃ nipaj‍jāmī’ti! sau nipaj‍jati na vīriyaṃ ārabhati… idaṃ sattamaṃ kusītavatthu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu gilānā vuṭṭhitau gilānavuṭṭhitau (saddanīti) a॰ ni॰ 6.16 nakulapitusuttaṭīkā passitabbā hauti aciravuṭṭhitau gailañ‍ñā| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khau gilānā vuṭṭhitau aciravuṭṭhitau gailañ‍ñā, tassa mai kāyau dubbalau akammañ‍ñau, handāhaṃ nipaj‍jāmī’ti! sau nipaj‍jati na vīriyaṃ ārabhati appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya| idaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ kusītavatthu|

335. ‘‘aṭṭha ārambhavatthūni| idhāvusau, bhikkhunā kammaṃ kātabbaṃ hauti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘kammaṃ khau mai kātabbaṃ bhavissati, kammaṃ khau pana mai karauntaina na sukaraṃ buddhānaṃ sāsanaṃ manasi kātuṃ, handāhaṃ vīriyaṃ ārabhāmi appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya, asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāyā’ti! sau vīriyaṃ ārabhati appattassa pattiyā, anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya| idaṃ paṭhamaṃ ārambhavatthu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunā kammaṃ  kataṃ hauti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khau kammaṃ akāsiṃ, kammaṃ khau panāhaṃ karauntau nāsakkhiṃ buddhānaṃ sāsanaṃ manasi kātuṃ, handāhaṃ vīriyaṃ ārabhāmi…pai॰… sau  vīriyaṃ ārabhati… idaṃ dutiyaṃ ārambhavatthu|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunā maggau gantabbau hauti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘maggau khau mai gantabbau bhavissati, maggaṃ khau pana mai gacchantaina na sukaraṃ buddhānaṃ sāsanaṃ manasi kātuṃ| handāhaṃ vīriyaṃ ārabhāmi…pai॰… sau vīriyaṃ ārabhati… idaṃ tatiyaṃ ārambhavatthu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunā maggau gatau hauti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khau maggaṃ agamāsiṃ, maggaṃ khau panāhaṃ gacchantau nāsakkhiṃ buddhānaṃ sāsanaṃ manasi kātuṃ, handāhaṃ vīriyaṃ ārabhāmi…pai॰… sau vīriyaṃ ārabhati… idaṃ catutthaṃ ārambhavatthu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya carantau na labhati lūkhassa vā paṇītassa vā bhaujanassa yāvadatthaṃ pāripūriṃ| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khau gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya carantau nālatthaṃ lūkhassa vā paṇītassa vā bhaujanassa yāvadatthaṃ pāripūriṃ, tassa mai kāyau lahukau kammañ‍ñau, handāhaṃ vīriyaṃ ārabhāmi…pai॰… sau vīriyaṃ ārabhati… idaṃ pañ‍camaṃ ārambhavatthu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya carantau labhati lūkhassa vā paṇītassa vā bhaujanassa yāvadatthaṃ pāripūriṃ| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khau gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya carantau alatthaṃ lūkhassa vā paṇītassa vā bhaujanassa yāvadatthaṃ pāripūriṃ, tassa mai kāyau balavā kammañ‍ñau, handāhaṃ vīriyaṃ ārabhāmi…pai॰… sau vīriyaṃ ārabhati… idaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ ārambhavatthu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunau uppan‍nau hauti appamattakau ābādhau| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘uppan‍nau khau mai ayaṃ appamattakau ābādhau, ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ mai ābādhau pavaḍḍhaiyya, handāhaṃ vīriyaṃ ārabhāmi…pai॰… sau vīriyaṃ ārabhati… idaṃ  sattamaṃ ārambhavatthu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu gilānā vuṭṭhitau hauti aciravuṭṭhitau gailañ‍ñā| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khau gilānā vuṭṭhitau aciravuṭṭhitau gailañ‍ñā, ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ mai ābādhau pac‍cudāvattaiyya, handāhaṃ vīriyaṃ ārabhāmi appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāyā’’ti! sau vīriyaṃ ārabhati appattassa pattiyā  anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya| idaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ ārambhavatthu|

336. ‘‘aṭṭha dānavatthūni| āsaj‍ja dānaṃ daiti, bhayā dānaṃ daiti, ‘adāsi mai’ti dānaṃ daiti, ‘dassati mai’ti dānaṃ daiti, ‘sāhu dāna’nti dānaṃ daiti, ‘ahaṃ pacāmi, imai na pacanti, nārahāmi pacantau apacantānaṃ dānaṃ na dātu’nti dānaṃ daiti, ‘idaṃ mai dānaṃ dadatau kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggacchatī’ti dānaṃ daiti| cittālaṅkāra-cittaparikkhāratthaṃ dānaṃ daiti|

337. ‘‘aṭṭha dānūpapattiyau| idhāvusau, aikac‍cau dānaṃ daiti samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā an‍naṃ pānaṃ vatthaṃ yānaṃ mālāgandhavilaipanaṃ saiyyāvasathapadīpaiyyaṃ| sau yaṃ daiti taṃ pac‍cāsīsati pac‍cāsiṃsati (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)| sau passati  khattiyamahāsālaṃ vā brāhmaṇamahāsālaṃ vā gahapatimahāsālaṃ vā pañ‍cahi kāmaguṇaihi samappitaṃ samaṅgībhūtaṃ paricārayamānaṃ| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa  bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā khattiyamahāsālānaṃ vā brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ vā gahapatimahāsālānaṃ vā sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti! sau taṃ cittaṃ dahati, taṃ cittaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, taṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti, tassa taṃ cittaṃ hīnai vimuttaṃ uttari abhāvitaṃ tatrūpapattiyā saṃvattati| tañ‍ca khau sīlavatau vadāmi nau dussīlassa| ijjhatāvusau, sīlavatau caitaupaṇidhi visuddhattā|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, idhaikac‍cau dānaṃ daiti samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā an‍naṃ pānaṃ…pai॰… saiyyāvasathapadīpaiyyaṃ| sau yaṃ daiti taṃ pac‍cāsīsati| tassa sutaṃ hauti – ‘cātumahārājikā cātummahārājikā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) daivā dīghāyukā vaṇṇavantau sukhabahulā’’ti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā cātumahārājikānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’’nti! sau taṃ cittaṃ dahati, taṃ cittaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, taṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti, tassa taṃ cittaṃ hīnai vimuttaṃ uttari abhāvitaṃ tatrūpapattiyā saṃvattati| tañ‍ca khau sīlavatau vadāmi nau dussīlassa| ijjhatāvusau, sīlavatau caitaupaṇidhi visuddhattā|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, āvusau, idhaikac‍cau dānaṃ daiti samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā an‍naṃ pānaṃ…pai॰… saiyyāvasathapadīpaiyyaṃ| sau yaṃ daiti taṃ pac‍cāsīsati| tassa sutaṃ hauti – ‘tāvatiṃsā daivā…pai॰… yāmā daivā…pai॰… tusitā daivā  …pai॰… nimmānaratī daivā…pai॰… paranimmitavasavattī daivā dīghāyukā vaṇṇavantau sukhabahulā’ti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā paranimmitavasavattīnaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’’nti! sau taṃ cittaṃ dahati, taṃ cittaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, taṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti, tassa taṃ cittaṃ hīnai vimuttaṃ uttari abhāvitaṃ tatrūpapattiyā saṃvattati| tañ‍ca khau sīlavatau vadāmi nau dussīlassa| ijjhatāvusau, sīlavatau caitaupaṇidhi visuddhattā|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, idhaikac‍cau dānaṃ daiti samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā an‍naṃ pānaṃ vatthaṃ yānaṃ mālāgandhavilaipanaṃ saiyyāvasathapadīpaiyyaṃ| sau yaṃ daiti taṃ pac‍cāsīsati| tassa sutaṃ hauti – ‘brahmakāyikā daivā dīghāyukā vaṇṇavantau sukhabahulā’ti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā brahmakāyikānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti! sau taṃ cittaṃ dahati, taṃ cittaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, taṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti, tassa taṃ cittaṃ hīnai vimuttaṃ uttari abhāvitaṃ tatrūpapattiyā saṃvattati| tañ‍ca khau sīlavatau  vadāmi nau dussīlassa; vītarāgassa nau sarāgassa| ijjhatāvusau, sīlavatau caitaupaṇidhi vītarāgattā|

‘‘aṭṭha  parisā – khattiyaparisā, brāhmaṇaparisā, gahapatiparisā, samaṇaparisā, cātumahārājikaparisā, tāvatiṃsaparisā, māraparisā, brahmaparisā|

‘‘aṭṭha laukadhammā – lābhau ca, alābhau ca, yasau ca, ayasau ca, nindā ca, pasaṃsā ca, sukhañ‍ca, dukkhañ‍ca|

338. ‘‘aṭṭha abhibhāyatanāni| ajjhattaṃ rūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati parittāni suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇāni, ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī’ti aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ paṭhamaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ  rūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati appamāṇāni suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇāni, ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī’ti – aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ dutiyaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati parittāni suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇāni, ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī’ti aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ tatiyaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati appamāṇāni suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇāni, ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī’ti aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ catutthaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati nīlāni nīlavaṇṇāni nīlanidassanāni nīlanibhāsāni| saiyyathāpi nāma umāpupphaṃ nīlaṃ nīlavaṇṇaṃ nīlanidassanaṃ nīlanibhāsaṃ, saiyyathā vā pana taṃ vatthaṃ bārāṇasaiyyakaṃ ubhataubhāgavimaṭṭhaṃ nīlaṃ nīlavaṇṇaṃ nīlanidassanaṃ nīlanibhāsaṃ| aivamaiva aivamaivaṃ (ka॰) ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati nīlāni  nīlavaṇṇāni nīlanidassanāni nīlanibhāsāni, ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī’ti aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ pañ‍camaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati pītāni pītavaṇṇāni pītanidassanāni pītanibhāsāni| saiyyathāpi nāma kaṇikārapupphaṃ  kaṇṇikārapupphaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) pītaṃ pītavaṇṇaṃ  pītanidassanaṃ pītanibhāsaṃ, saiyyathā vā pana taṃ vatthaṃ bārāṇasaiyyakaṃ ubhataubhāgavimaṭṭhaṃ pītaṃ pītavaṇṇaṃ pītanidassanaṃ pītanibhāsaṃ| aivamaiva ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati pītāni pītavaṇṇāni pītanidassanāni pītanibhāsāni, ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī’ti aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati lauhitakāni lauhitakavaṇṇāni lauhitakanidassanāni lauhitakanibhāsāni| saiyyathāpi nāma bandhujīvakapupphaṃ lauhitakaṃ lauhitakavaṇṇaṃ lauhitakanidassanaṃ lauhitakanibhāsaṃ, saiyyathā vā pana taṃ vatthaṃ bārāṇasaiyyakaṃ ubhataubhāgavimaṭṭhaṃ lauhitakaṃ lauhitakavaṇṇaṃ lauhitakanidassanaṃ lauhitakanibhāsaṃ| aivamaiva ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau  bahiddhā rūpāni passati lauhitakāni lauhitakavaṇṇāni lauhitakanidassanāni lauhitakanibhāsāni, ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī’ti aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ sattamaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati audātāni audātavaṇṇāni audātanidassanāni audātanibhāsāni| saiyyathāpi nāma ausadhitārakā audātā audātavaṇṇā audātanidassanā audātanibhāsā, saiyyathā vā pana taṃ vatthaṃ bārāṇasaiyyakaṃ ubhataubhāgavimaṭṭhaṃ audātaṃ audātavaṇṇaṃ audātanidassanaṃ audātanibhāsaṃ| aivamaiva ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati audātāni audātavaṇṇāni audātanidassanāni audātanibhāsāni, ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī’ti aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ|

339. ‘‘aṭṭha vimaukkhā| rūpī rūpāni passati| ayaṃ paṭhamau vimaukkhau|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī bahiddhā  rūpāni passati| ayaṃ dutiyau vimaukkhau|

‘‘subhantaiva adhimuttau hauti| ayaṃ tatiyau vimaukkhau|

‘‘sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ‘anantau ākāsau’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ catutthau vimaukkhau|

‘‘sabbasau  ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ pañ‍camau vimaukkhau|

‘‘sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ chaṭṭhau vimaukkhau|

‘‘sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ sattamau vimaukkhau|

‘‘sabbasau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma sañ‍ñāvaidayita niraudhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ aṭṭhamau vimaukkhau|

‘‘imai  khau, āvusau, taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina aṭṭha dhammā sammadakkhātā; tattha sabbaihaiva saṅgāyitabbaṃ…pai॰… atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussānaṃ|

navakaṃ

340. ‘‘atthi khau, āvusau, taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā  sammāsambuddhaina nava dhammā sammadakkhātā; tattha sabbaihaiva saṅgāyitabbaṃ…pai॰… atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussānaṃ| katamai nava?

‘‘nava āghātavatthūni| ‘anatthaṃ mai acarī’ti āghātaṃ bandhati; ‘anatthaṃ mai caratī’ti āghātaṃ bandhati; ‘anatthaṃ mai carissatī’ti āghātaṃ bandhati; ‘piyassa mai manāpassa anatthaṃ acarī’ti āghātaṃ bandhati…pai॰… anatthaṃ caratīti āghātaṃ bandhati…pai॰… anatthaṃ carissatīti āghātaṃ bandhati; ‘appiyassa mai amanāpassa atthaṃ acarī’ti āghātaṃ bandhati…pai॰… atthaṃ caratīti āghātaṃ bandhati…pai॰… atthaṃ carissatīti āghātaṃ bandhati|

‘‘nava āghātapaṭivinayā| ‘anatthaṃ mai acari acarīti (syā॰ ka॰) aivaṃ ‘‘carati carissati’’ padaisupi, taṃ kutaittha labbhā’ti āghātaṃ paṭivinaiti; ‘anatthaṃ mai  carati, taṃ kutaittha labbhā’ti āghātaṃ paṭivinaiti; ‘anatthaṃ mai carissati, taṃ kutaittha labbhā’ti āghātaṃ paṭivinaiti; ‘piyassa mai manāpassa anatthaṃ acari…pai॰… anatthaṃ carati…pai॰… anatthaṃ carissati, taṃ kutaittha labbhā’ti āghātaṃ paṭivinaiti; ‘appiyassa mai amanāpassa atthaṃ acari…pai॰… atthaṃ carati…pai॰… atthaṃ carissati, taṃ kutaittha labbhā’ti āghātaṃ paṭivinaiti|

341. ‘‘nava sattāvāsā| santāvusau, sattā nānattakāyā nānattasañ‍ñinau, saiyyathāpi manussā aikac‍cai ca daivā aikac‍cai ca vinipātikā| ayaṃ paṭhamau sattāvāsau|

‘‘santāvusau, sattā nānattakāyā aikattasañ‍ñinau, saiyyathāpi daivā brahmakāyikā paṭhamābhinibbattā| ayaṃ dutiyau sattāvāsau|

‘‘santāvusau, sattā aikattakāyā nānattasañ‍ñinau, saiyyathāpi daivā ābhassarā| ayaṃ tatiyau  sattāvāsau|

‘‘santāvusau, sattā aikattakāyā aikattasañ‍ñinau, saiyyathāpi daivā subhakiṇhā| ayaṃ catutthau sattāvāsau|

‘‘santāvusau, sattā asañ‍ñinau appaṭisaṃvaidinau, saiyyathāpi daivā asañ‍ñasattā asañ‍ñisattā (syā॰ kaṃ॰)| ayaṃ pañ‍camau sattāvāsau|

‘‘santāvusau, sattā sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ‘anantau ākāsau’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanūpagā| ayaṃ chaṭṭhau sattāvāsau|

‘‘santāvusau, sattā sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanūpagā| ayaṃ sattamau sattāvāsau|

‘‘santāvusau, sattā sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñcaññāyatanūpagā| ayaṃ aṭṭhamau sattāvāsau|

‘‘santāvusau, sattā sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma samatik‍kamma santamaitaṃ paṇītamaitanti (syā॰ kaṃ॰) naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanūpagā| ayaṃ navamau sattāvāsau|

342. ‘‘nava akkhaṇā asamayā brahmacariyavāsāya|  idhāvusau, tathāgatau ca laukai uppan‍nau hauti arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau, dhammau ca daisiyati aupasamikau parinibbānikau sambaudhagāmī sugatappavaiditau| ayañ‍ca puggalau nirayaṃ upapan‍nau hauti| ayaṃ paṭhamau akkhaṇau asamayau brahmacariyavāsāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, tathāgatau ca laukai uppan‍nau hauti arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau, dhammau ca daisiyati aupasamikau parinibbānikau sambaudhagāmī sugatappavaiditau| ayañ‍ca puggalau  tiracchānayauniṃ upapan‍nau hauti| ayaṃ dutiyau akkhaṇau asamayau brahmacariyavāsāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ…pai॰… paittivisayaṃ upapan‍nau hauti| ayaṃ tatiyau akkhaṇau asamayau brahmacariyavāsāya|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ…pai॰… asurakāyaṃ upapan‍nau hauti| ayaṃ catutthau akkhaṇau asamayau brahmacariyavāsāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ…pai॰… añ‍ñataraṃ dīghāyukaṃ daivanikāyaṃ upapan‍nau hauti| ayaṃ pañ‍camau akkhaṇau asamayau brahmacariyavāsāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ…pai॰… pac‍cantimaisu janapadaisu pac‍cājātau hauti milakkhaisu milakkhakaisu (syā॰ kaṃ॰) milakkhūsu (ka॰) aviñ‍ñātāraisu, yattha natthi gati bhikkhūnaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ upāsakānaṃ upāsikānaṃ| ayaṃ chaṭṭhau akkhaṇau asamayau brahmacariyavāsāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ…pai॰… majjhimaisu janapadaisu pac‍cājātau hauti| sau ca hauti micchādiṭṭhikau viparītadassanau – ‘natthi din‍naṃ, natthi yiṭṭhaṃ, natthi hutaṃ, natthi sukataduk‍kaṭānaṃ sukaṭa duk‍kaṭānaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipākau, natthi ayaṃ laukau, natthi parau laukau, natthi  mātā, natthi pitā, natthi sattā aupapātikā, natthi laukai samaṇabrāhmaṇā sammaggatā sammāpaṭipan‍nā yai imañ‍ca laukaṃ  parañ‍ca laukaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaintī’ti| ayaṃ sattamau akkhaṇau asamayau brahmacariyavāsāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ…pai॰… majjhimaisu janapadaisu pac‍cājātau hauti| sau ca hauti duppañ‍ñau jaḷau aiḷamūgau, nappaṭibalau subhāsitadubbhāsitānamatthamañ‍ñātuṃ| ayaṃ aṭṭhamau akkhaṇau asamayau brahmacariyavāsāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, tathāgatau ca laukai na katthaci nakārau na dissati uppan‍nau hauti arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau, dhammau ca na daisiyati aupasamikau parinibbānikau sambaudhagāmī sugatappavaiditau| ayañ‍ca puggalau majjhimaisu janapadaisu pac‍cājātau hauti, sau ca hauti pañ‍ñavā ajaḷau anaiḷamūgau, paṭibalau subhāsita-dubbhāsitānamatthamañ‍ñātuṃ| ayaṃ navamau akkhaṇau asamayau brahmacariyavāsāya|

343. ‘‘nava anupubbavihārā| idhāvusau, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja  viharati| vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| pītiyā ca virāgā…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sukhassa ca pahānā  …pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā…pai॰… ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma  ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sabbasau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati|

344. ‘‘nava anupubbaniraudhā| paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nassa kāmasañ‍ñā niruddhā hauti| dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nassa vitak‍kavicārā niruddhā haunti| tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nassa pīti niruddhā hauti| catutthaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nassa assāsapassāsā niruddhā haunti| ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ  samāpan‍nassa rūpasañ‍ñā niruddhā hauti| viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samāpan‍nassa ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñā niruddhā hauti| ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samāpan‍nassa viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñā niruddhā hauti| naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ  samāpan‍nassa ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñā niruddhā hauti| sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpan‍nassa sañ‍ñā ca vaidanā ca niruddhā haunti|

‘‘imai khau, āvusau, taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina nava dhammā sammadakkhātā| tattha sabbaihaiva saṅgāyitabbaṃ…pai॰… atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussānaṃ|

dasakaṃ

345. ‘‘atthi khau, āvusau, taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina dasa dhammā sammadakkhātā| tattha sabbaihaiva saṅgāyitabbaṃ…pai॰… atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussānaṃ| katamai dasa?

‘‘dasa nāthakaraṇā dhammā| idhāvusau, bhikkhu sīlavā hauti| pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharati ācāragaucarasampan‍nau, aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu| yaṃpāvusau, bhikkhu  sīlavā  hauti, pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharati, ācāragaucarasampan‍nau, aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu| ayampi dhammau nāthakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu bahussutau hauti sutadharau sutasan‍nicayau| yai  tai dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhaikalyāṇā pariyausānakalyāṇā sātthā sabyañ‍janā sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti, tathārūpāssa dhammā bahussutā haunti dhatā (ka॰ sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) dhātā vacasā paricitā manasānupaikkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā, yaṃpāvusau, bhikkhu bahussutau hauti…pai॰… diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā| ayampi dhammau nāthakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu kalyāṇamittau hauti kalyāṇasahāyau kalyāṇasampavaṅkau| yaṃpāvusau, bhikkhu kalyāṇamittau hauti kalyāṇasahāyau kalyāṇasampavaṅkau| ayampi dhammau nāthakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu suvacau hauti sauvacassakaraṇaihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau khamau  padakkhiṇaggāhī anusāsaniṃ| yaṃpāvusau, bhikkhu suvacau hauti…pai॰… padakkhiṇaggāhī anusāsaniṃ| ayampi dhammau nāthakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu yāni tāni sabrahmacārīnaṃ uc‍cāvacāni kiṃkaraṇīyāni, tattha dakkhau hauti analasau tatrupāyāya vīmaṃsāya saman‍nāgatau, alaṃ kātuṃ alaṃ saṃvidhātuṃ| yaṃpāvusau, bhikkhu yāni tāni sabrahmacārīnaṃ…pai॰… alaṃ saṃvidhātuṃ| ayampi dhammau nāthakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu dhammakāmau hauti piyasamudāhārau, abhidhammai abhivinayai uḷārapāmauj‍jau uḷārapāmuj‍jau (sī॰ pī॰), auḷārapāmauj‍jau (syā॰ kaṃ॰)| yaṃpāvusau, bhikkhu dhammakāmau hauti…pai॰… uḷārapāmauj‍jau uḷārapāmuj‍jau (sī॰ pī॰), auḷārapāmauj‍jau (syā॰ kaṃ॰)| ayampi dhammau nāthakaraṇau|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu santuṭṭhau hauti itarītaraihi cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraihi| yaṃpāvusau, bhikkhu santuṭṭhau hauti…pai॰… parikkhāraihi| ayampi dhammau nāthakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu āraddhavīriyau viharati akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya, thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu| yaṃpāvusau, bhikkhu āraddhavīriyau viharati…pai॰… anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu| ayampi dhammau nāthakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu satimā hauti paramaina satinaipak‍kaina saman‍nāgatau cirakatampi cirabhāsitampi saritā anussaritā| yaṃpāvusau, bhikkhu satimā hauti…pai॰… saritā anussaritā| ayampi dhammau nāthakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu pañ‍ñavā hauti, udayatthagāminiyā pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau ariyāya nibbaidhikāya sammādukkhakkhayagāminiyā| yaṃpāvusau, bhikkhu pañ‍ñavā hauti…pai॰… sammādukkhakkhayagāminiyā| ayampi dhammau nāthakaraṇau|

346. dasa kasiṇāyatanāni| pathavīkasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti, uddhaṃ adhau tiriyaṃ advayaṃ  appamāṇaṃ| āpaukasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti…pai॰… taijaukasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti… vāyaukasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti… nīlakasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti… pītakasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti…  lauhitakasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti… audātakasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti… ākāsakasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti… viñ‍ñāṇakasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti, uddhaṃ adhau tiriyaṃ advayaṃ appamāṇaṃ|

347. ‘‘dasa  akusalakammapathā – pāṇātipātau, adin‍nādānaṃ, kāmaisumicchācārau, musāvādau, pisuṇā vācā, pharusā vācā, samphappalāpau, abhijjhā, byāpādau, micchādiṭṭhi|

‘‘dasa  kusalakammapathā – pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī, adin‍nādānā vairamaṇī, kāmaisumicchācārā vairamaṇī, musāvādā vairamaṇī, pisuṇāya vācāya vairamaṇī, pharusāya vācāya vairamaṇī, samphappalāpā vairamaṇī, anabhijjhā, abyāpādau, sammādiṭṭhi|

348. ‘‘dasa ariyavāsā| idhāvusau, bhikkhu pañ‍caṅgavippahīnau hauti, chaḷaṅgasaman‍nāgatau, aikārakkhau, caturāpassainau, paṇun‍napac‍caikasac‍cau, samavayasaṭṭhaisanau, anāvilasaṅkappau, passaddhakāyasaṅkhārau, suvimuttacittau, suvimuttapañ‍ñau|

‘‘kathañ‍cāvusau, bhikkhu pañ‍caṅgavippahīnau hauti? idhāvusau, bhikkhunau kāmacchandau pahīnau hauti, byāpādau pahīnau hauti, thinamiddhaṃ pahīnaṃ hauti, uddhac‍cakukuc‍caṃ pahīnaṃ hauti, vicikicchā pahīnā hauti| aivaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu pañ‍caṅgavippahīnau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍cāvusau, bhikkhu chaḷaṅgasaman‍nāgatau hauti? idhāvusau, bhikkhu cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā naiva sumanau hauti na dummanau, upaikkhakau viharati satau sampajānau| sautaina saddaṃ  sutvā…pai॰… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya naiva sumanau hauti na dummanau, upaikkhakau viharati satau sampajānau| aivaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu chaḷaṅgasaman‍nāgatau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍cāvusau, bhikkhu aikārakkhau hauti? idhāvusau, bhikkhu satārakkhaina caitasā saman‍nāgatau hauti| aivaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu aikārakkhau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍cāvusau, bhikkhu caturāpassainau hauti? idhāvusau, bhikkhu saṅkhāyaikaṃ paṭisaivati, saṅkhāyaikaṃ adhivāsaiti, saṅkhāyaikaṃ parivaj‍jaiti, saṅkhāyaikaṃ vinaudaiti| aivaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu caturāpassainau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍cāvusau, bhikkhu paṇun‍napac‍caikasac‍cau hauti? idhāvusau, bhikkhunau yāni tāni puthusamaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ puthupac‍caikasac‍cāni, sabbāni tāni nun‍nāni haunti paṇun‍nāni cattāni vantāni muttāni pahīnāni paṭinissaṭṭhāni| aivaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu paṇun‍napac‍caikasac‍cau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍cāvusau, bhikkhu samavayasaṭṭhaisanau hauti? idhāvusau, bhikkhunau kāmaisanā pahīnā hauti, bhavaisanā pahīnā hauti, brahmacariyaisanā paṭippassaddhā| aivaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu samavayasaṭṭhaisanau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍cāvusau, bhikkhu anāvilasaṅkappau hauti? idhāvusau, bhikkhunau kāmasaṅkappau pahīnau hauti, byāpādasaṅkappau pahīnau hauti, vihiṃsāsaṅkappau pahīnau hauti| aivaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu anāvilasaṅkappau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍cāvusau, bhikkhu passaddhakāyasaṅkhārau hauti? idhāvusau, bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| aivaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu passaddhakāyasaṅkhārau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍cāvusau, bhikkhu suvimuttacittau hauti? idhāvusau, bhikkhunau rāgā cittaṃ vimuttaṃ hauti, dausā cittaṃ vimuttaṃ hauti, mauhā cittaṃ vimuttaṃ hauti| aivaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu suvimuttacittau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍cāvusau, bhikkhu suvimuttapañ‍ñau hauti? idhāvusau, bhikkhu ‘rāgau mai pahīnau ucchin‍namūlau  tālāvatthukatau anabhāvaṃkatau  āyatiṃ anuppādadhammau’ti pajānāti| ‘dausau mai pahīnau ucchin‍namūlau tālāvatthukatau anabhāvaṃkatau āyatiṃ anuppādadhammau’ti pajānāti| ‘mauhau mai pahīnau ucchin‍namūlau tālāvatthukatau anabhāvaṃkatau āyatiṃ anuppādadhammau’ti pajānāti| aivaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu suvimuttapañ‍ñau hauti|

‘‘dasa asaikkhā dhammā – asaikkhā sammādiṭṭhi, asaikkhau sammāsaṅkappau, asaikkhā sammāvācā, asaikkhau sammākammantau, asaikkhau sammāājīvau, asaikkhau sammāvāyāmau, asaikkhā sammāsati, asaikkhau sammāsamādhi, asaikkhaṃ sammāñāṇaṃ, asaikkhā sammāvimutti|

‘‘imai khau, āvusau, taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina dasa dhammā sammadakkhātā| tattha sabbaihaiva saṅgāyitabbaṃ na  vivaditabbaṃ, yathayidaṃ brahmacariyaṃ addhaniyaṃ assa  ciraṭṭhitikaṃ, tadassa bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya laukānukampāya atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussāna’’nti|

349. atha khau bhagavā uṭṭhahitvā āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ āmantaisi – ‘sādhu sādhu, sāriputta, sādhu khau tvaṃ, sāriputta, bhikkhūnaṃ saṅgītipariyāyaṃ abhāsī’ti| idamavaucāyasmā sāriputtau, samanuñ‍ñau satthā ahausi| attamanā tai bhikkhū āyasmatau sāriputtassa bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

saṅgītisuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dasamaṃ|



11. dasuttarasuttaṃ

350. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā campāyaṃ viharati gaggarāya paukkharaṇiyā tīrai mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ pañ‍camattaihi bhikkhusataihi| tatra khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘āvusau bhikkhavai’’ti! ‘‘āvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau sāriputtassa pac‍cassausuṃ| āyasmā sāriputtau aitadavauca –

‘‘dasuttaraṃ pavakkhāmi, dhammaṃ nibbānapattiyā|

dukkhassantakiriyāya, sabbaganthappamaucanaṃ’’||

aikau dhammau

351. ‘‘aikau, āvusau, dhammau bahukārau, aikau dhammau bhāvaitabbau, aikau dhammau pariñ‍ñaiyyau, aikau dhammau pahātabbau, aikau dhammau hānabhāgiyau, aikau dhammau visaisabhāgiyau, aikau dhammau duppaṭivijjhau, aikau dhammau uppādaitabbau, aikau dhammau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau, aikau dhammau sacchikātabbau|

(ka) ‘‘katamau aikau dhammau bahukārau? appamādau kusalaisu dhammaisu| ayaṃ aikau dhammau bahukārau|

(kha) ‘‘katamau aikau dhammau bhāvaitabbau? kāyagatāsati sātasahagatā| ayaṃ aikau dhammau bhāvaitabbau|

(ga) ‘‘katamau  aikau dhammau pariñ‍ñaiyyau? phassau sāsavau upādāniyau| ayaṃ aikau dhammau pariñ‍ñaiyyau|

(gha) ‘‘katamau  aikau dhammau pahātabbau? asmimānau| ayaṃ aikau dhammau pahātabbau|

(ṅa) ‘‘katamau aikau dhammau hānabhāgiyau? ayaunisau manasikārau| ayaṃ aikau dhammau hānabhāgiyau|

(ca) ‘‘katamau aikau dhammau visaisabhāgiyau? yaunisau manasikārau| ayaṃ aikau dhammau visaisabhāgiyau|

(cha) ‘‘katamau  aikau dhammau duppaṭivijjhau? ānantarikau caitausamādhi| ayaṃ aikau dhammau duppaṭivijjhau|

(ja) ‘‘katamau aikau dhammau uppādaitabbau? akuppaṃ ñāṇaṃ| ayaṃ aikau dhammau uppādaitabbau|

(jha) ‘‘katamau aikau dhammau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau? sabbai sattā āhāraṭṭhitikā| ayaṃ aikau dhammau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau|

(ña) ‘‘katamau aikau dhammau sacchikātabbau? akuppā caitauvimutti| ayaṃ aikau dhammau sacchikātabbau|

‘‘iti imai dasa dhammā bhūtā tacchā tathā avitathā anañ‍ñathā sammā tathāgataina abhisambuddhā|

dvai dhammā

352. ‘‘dvai dhammā bahukārā, dvai dhammā bhāvaitabbā, dvai dhammā pariñ‍ñaiyyā, dvai dhammā pahātabbā, dvai dhammā hānabhāgiyā, dvai dhammā visaisabhāgiyā, dvai dhammā duppaṭivijjhā, dvai dhammā uppādaitabbā, dvai dhammā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā, dvai dhammā  sacchikātabbā|

(ka) ‘‘katamai dvai dhammā bahukārā? sati ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca| imai dvai dhammā bahukārā|

(kha) ‘‘katamai dvai dhammā bhāvaitabbā? samathau ca vipassanā ca| imai dvai dhammā bhāvaitabbā|

(ga) ‘‘katamai dvai dhammā pariñ‍ñaiyyā? nāmañ‍ca rūpañ‍ca| imai dvai dhammā pariñ‍ñaiyyā|

(gha) ‘‘katamai  dvai dhammā pahātabbā? avij‍jā ca bhavataṇhā ca| imai dvai dhammā pahātabbā|

(ṅa) ‘‘katamai dvai dhammā hānabhāgiyā? dauvacassatā ca pāpamittatā ca| imai dvai dhammā hānabhāgiyā|

(ca) ‘‘katamai dvai dhammā visaisabhāgiyā? sauvacassatā ca kalyāṇamittatā ca| imai dvai dhammā visaisabhāgiyā|

(cha) ‘‘katamai  dvai dhammā duppaṭivijjhā? yau ca haitu yau ca pac‍cayau sattānaṃ saṃkilaisāya, yau ca haitu yau ca pac‍cayau sattānaṃ visuddhiyā| imai dvai dhammā duppaṭivijjhā|

(ja) ‘‘katamai dvai dhammā uppādaitabbā? dvai ñāṇāni – khayai ñāṇaṃ, anuppādai ñāṇaṃ| imai dvai dhammā uppādaitabbā|

(jha) ‘‘katamai dvai dhammā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā? dvai dhātuyau – saṅkhatā ca dhātu asaṅkhatā ca dhātu| imai dvai dhammā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā|

(ña) ‘‘katamai  dvai dhammā sacchikātabbā? vij‍jā ca vimutti ca| imai dvai dhammā sacchikātabbā|

‘‘iti  imai vīsati dhammā bhūtā tacchā tathā avitathā anañ‍ñathā sammā tathāgataina abhisambuddhā|

tayau dhammā

353. ‘‘tayau dhammā bahukārā, tayau dhammā bhāvaitabbā…pai॰… tayau dhammā sacchikātabbā|

(ka) ‘‘katamai tayau dhammā bahukārā? sappurisasaṃsaivau, saddhammassavanaṃ, dhammānudhammappaṭipatti| imai tayau dhammā bahukārā|

(kha) ‘‘katamai tayau dhammā bhāvaitabbā? tayau samādhī – savitak‍kau savicārau samādhi, avitak‍kau vicāramattau samādhi, avitak‍kau avicārau samādhi| imai tayau dhammā bhāvaitabbā|

(ga) ‘‘katamai  tayau dhammā pariñ‍ñaiyyā? tissau vaidanā – sukhā vaidanā, dukkhā vaidanā, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā| imai tayau dhammā pariñ‍ñaiyyā|

(gha) ‘‘katamai tayau dhammā pahātabbā? tissau taṇhā – kāmataṇhā, bhavataṇhā, vibhavataṇhā| imai tayau dhammā pahātabbā|

(ṅa) ‘‘katamai tayau dhammā hānabhāgiyā? tīṇi akusalamūlāni – laubhau  akusalamūlaṃ, dausau akusalamūlaṃ, mauhau akusalamūlaṃ| imai tayau dhammā hānabhāgiyā|

(ca) ‘‘katamai  tayau dhammā visaisabhāgiyā? tīṇi kusalamūlāni – alaubhau kusalamūlaṃ, adausau kusalamūlaṃ, amauhau kusalamūlaṃ| imai tayau dhammā visaisabhāgiyā|

(cha) ‘‘katamai tayau dhammā duppaṭivijjhā? tissau nissaraṇiyā dhātuyau – kāmānamaitaṃ nissaraṇaṃ yadidaṃ naikkhammaṃ, rūpānamaitaṃ nissaraṇaṃ yadidaṃ arūpaṃ, yaṃ khau pana kiñ‍ci bhūtaṃ saṅkhataṃ paṭic‍casamuppan‍naṃ, niraudhau tassa nissaraṇaṃ| imai tayau dhammā duppaṭivijjhā|

(ja) ‘‘katamai  tayau dhammā uppādaitabbā? tīṇi ñāṇāni – atītaṃsai ñāṇaṃ, anāgataṃsai ñāṇaṃ, pac‍cuppan‍naṃsai ñāṇaṃ| imai tayau dhammā uppādaitabbā|

(jha) ‘‘katamai tayau dhammā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā? tissau dhātuyau – kāmadhātu, rūpadhātu, arūpadhātu| imai tayau dhammā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā|

(ña) ‘‘katamai tayau dhammā sacchikātabbā? tissau vij‍jā – pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇaṃ vij‍jā, sattānaṃ cutūpapātai ñāṇaṃ vij‍jā, āsavānaṃ khayai ñāṇaṃ vij‍jā| imai tayau dhammā sacchikātabbā|

‘‘iti  imai tiṃsa dhammā bhūtā tacchā tathā avitathā anañ‍ñathā sammā tathāgataina abhisambuddhā|

cattārau dhammā

354. ‘‘cattārau  dhammā bahukārā, cattārau dhammā bhāvaitabbā…pai॰… cattārau dhammā sacchikātabbā|

(ka) ‘‘katamai cattārau dhammā bahukārā? cattāri cak‍kāni – patirūpadaisavāsau, sappurisūpanissayau sappurisupassayau (syā॰ kaṃ॰), attasammāpaṇidhi, pubbai ca katapuñ‍ñatā| imai cattārau dhammā bahukārā|

(kha) ‘‘katamai cattārau dhammā bhāvaitabbā? cattārau satipaṭṭhānā – idhāvusau, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai… dhammaisu  dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| imai cattārau dhammā bhāvaitabbā|

(ga) ‘‘katamai cattārau dhammā pariñ‍ñaiyyā? cattārau āhārā – kabaḷīkārau kavaḷīkārau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) āhārau auḷārikau  vā sukhumau vā, phassau dutiyau, manausañ‍caitanā tatiyā, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ catutthaṃ| imai cattārau dhammā pariñ‍ñaiyyā|

(gha) ‘‘katamai cattārau dhammā pahātabbā? cattārau aughā  – kāmaughau, bhavaughau, diṭṭhaughau, avij‍jaughau| imai cattārau dhammā pahātabbā|

(ṅa) ‘‘katamai cattārau dhammā hānabhāgiyā? cattārau yaugā – kāmayaugau, bhavayaugau, diṭṭhiyaugau, avij‍jāyaugau| imai cattārau dhammā hānabhāgiyā|

(ca) ‘‘katamai cattārau dhammā visaisabhāgiyā? cattārau visañ‍ñaugā – kāmayaugavisaṃyaugau, bhavayaugavisaṃyaugau, diṭṭhiyaugavisaṃyaugau, avij‍jāyaugavisaṃyaugau| imai cattārau dhammā visaisabhāgiyā|

(cha) ‘‘katamai  cattārau dhammā duppaṭivijjhā? cattārau samādhī – hānabhāgiyau samādhi, ṭhitibhāgiyau samādhi, visaisabhāgiyau samādhi, nibbaidhabhāgiyau samādhi| imai cattārau dhammā duppaṭivijjhā|

(ja) ‘‘katamai cattārau dhammā uppādaitabbā? cattāri ñāṇāni – dhammai ñāṇaṃ, anvayai ñāṇaṃ, pariyai ñāṇaṃ, sammutiyā ñāṇaṃ| imai cattārau dhammā uppādaitabbā|
