(jha) ‘‘katamai cattārau dhammā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā? cattāri ariyasac‍cāni – dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ, dukkhasamudayaṃ dukkhasamudayau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) ariyasac‍caṃ, dukkhaniraudhaṃ dukkhaniraudhau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) ariyasac‍caṃ, dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasac‍caṃ| imai cattārau dhammā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā|

(ña) ‘‘katamai cattārau dhammā sacchikātabbā? cattāri sāmañ‍ñaphalāni – sautāpattiphalaṃ, sakadāgāmiphalaṃ, anāgāmiphalaṃ, arahattaphalaṃ| imai cattārau dhammā sacchikātabbā|

‘‘iti  imai cattārīsadhammā bhūtā tacchā tathā avitathā anañ‍ñathā sammā tathāgataina abhisambuddhā|

pañ‍ca dhammā

355. ‘‘pañ‍ca  dhammā bahukārā…pai॰… pañ‍ca dhammā sacchikātabbā|

(ka) ‘‘katamai pañ‍ca dhammā bahukārā? pañ‍ca padhāniyaṅgāni – idhāvusau, bhikkhu saddhau hauti, saddahati tathāgatassa baudhiṃ – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti| appābādhau hauti appātaṅkau samavaipākiniyā gahaṇiyā saman‍nāgatau nātisītāya nāc‍cuṇhāya majjhimāya padhānakkhamāya| asaṭhau hauti amāyāvī yathābhūtamattānaṃ āvīkattā satthari vā viñ‍ñūsu vā sabrahmacārīsu| āraddhavīriyau viharati akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya, thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu| pañ‍ñavā hauti udayatthagāminiyā pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau ariyāya nibbaidhikāya sammā dukkhakkhayagāminiyā| imai pañ‍ca dhammā bahukārā|

(kha) ‘‘katamai  pañ‍ca dhammā bhāvaitabbā? pañ‍caṅgikau sammāsamādhi – pītipharaṇatā, sukhapharaṇatā, caitaupharaṇatā, ālaukapharaṇatā, pac‍cavaikkhaṇanimittaṃ pac‍cavaikkhaṇānimittaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰)| imai pañ‍ca dhammā bhāvaitabbā|

(ga) ‘‘katamai pañ‍ca dhammā pariñ‍ñaiyyā? pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā saiyyathīdaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) – rūpupādānakkhandhau, vaidanupādānakkhandhau, sañ‍ñupādānakkhandhau, saṅkhārupādānakkhandhau viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhau| imai pañ‍ca dhammā pariñ‍ñaiyyā|

(gha) ‘‘katamai pañ‍ca dhammā pahātabbā? pañ‍ca nīvaraṇāni – kāmacchandanīvaraṇaṃ, byāpādanīvaraṇaṃ, thinamiddhanīvaraṇaṃ, uddhac‍cakukuc‍canīvaraṇaṃ, vicikicchānīvaraṇaṃ| imai pañ‍ca dhammā pahātabbā|

(ṅa) ‘‘katamai pañ‍ca dhammā hānabhāgiyā? pañ‍ca caitaukhilā – idhāvusau, bhikkhu satthari kaṅkhati vicikicchati nādhimuc‍cati na sampasīdati| yau sau, āvusau, bhikkhu satthari kaṅkhati vicikicchati nādhimuc‍cati na sampasīdati, tassa  cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya| yassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya| ayaṃ paṭhamau caitaukhilau| puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu dhammai kaṅkhati vicikicchati…pai॰… saṅghai kaṅkhati vicikicchati…pai॰… sikkhāya kaṅkhati vicikicchati…pai॰… sabrahmacārīsu kupitau hauti anattamanau āhatacittau khilajātau, yau sau, āvusau, bhikkhu sabrahmacārīsu  kupitau hauti anattamanau āhatacittau khilajātau, tassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya| yassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya| ayaṃ pañ‍camau caitaukhilau| imai pañ‍ca dhammā hānabhāgiyā|

(ca) ‘‘katamai pañ‍ca dhammā visaisabhāgiyā? pañ‍cindriyāni – saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ| imai pañ‍ca dhammā visaisabhāgiyā|

(cha) ‘‘katamai pañ‍ca dhammā duppaṭivijjhā? pañ‍ca nissaraṇiyā dhātuyau – idhāvusau, bhikkhunau kāmai manasikarautau kāmaisu cittaṃ na pakkhandati na pasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuc‍cati| naikkhammaṃ khau panassa manasikarautau naikkhammai cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuc‍cati| tassa taṃ cittaṃ sugataṃ subhāvitaṃ suvuṭṭhitaṃ suvimuttaṃ visaṃyuttaṃ kāmaihi| yai ca kāmapac‍cayā uppaj‍janti āsavā vighātā pariḷāhā, muttau sau taihi| na sau taṃ vaidanaṃ vaidaiti| idamakkhātaṃ kāmānaṃ nissaraṇaṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunau byāpādaṃ manasikarautau byāpādai cittaṃ na pakkhandati na pasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuc‍cati| abyāpādaṃ khau panassa manasikarautau abyāpādai cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuc‍cati| tassa taṃ cittaṃ sugataṃ subhāvitaṃ suvuṭṭhitaṃ suvimuttaṃ visaṃyuttaṃ byāpādaina| yai ca byāpādapac‍cayā uppaj‍janti āsavā vighātā pariḷāhā, muttau sau taihi| na  sau taṃ vaidanaṃ vaidaiti| idamakkhātaṃ byāpādassa nissaraṇaṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunau vihaisaṃ manasikarautau vihaisāya cittaṃ na pakkhandati na pasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuc‍cati| avihaisaṃ khau panassa manasikarautau avihaisāya cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuc‍cati| tassa taṃ cittaṃ sugataṃ subhāvitaṃ suvuṭṭhitaṃ suvimuttaṃ visaṃyuttaṃ vihaisāya| yai ca vihaisāpac‍cayā uppaj‍janti āsavā vighātā pariḷāhā, muttau sau taihi| na sau taṃ vaidanaṃ vaidaiti| idamakkhātaṃ vihaisāya nissaraṇaṃ|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunau rūpai manasikarautau rūpaisu cittaṃ na pakkhandati na pasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuc‍cati| arūpaṃ khau panassa manasikarautau arūpai cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuc‍cati| tassa taṃ cittaṃ sugataṃ subhāvitaṃ suvuṭṭhitaṃ suvimuttaṃ visaṃyuttaṃ rūpaihi| yai ca rūpapac‍cayā uppaj‍janti āsavā vighātā pariḷāhā, muttau sau taihi| na sau taṃ vaidanaṃ vaidaiti| idamakkhātaṃ rūpānaṃ nissaraṇaṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunau sak‍kāyaṃ manasikarautau sak‍kāyai cittaṃ na pakkhandati na pasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuc‍cati| sak‍kāyaniraudhaṃ khau panassa manasikarautau sak‍kāyaniraudhai cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuc‍cati| tassa taṃ cittaṃ sugataṃ subhāvitaṃ suvuṭṭhitaṃ suvimuttaṃ visaṃyuttaṃ sak‍kāyaina| yai ca sak‍kāyapac‍cayā uppaj‍janti āsavā vighātā pariḷāhā, muttau sau taihi| na sau taṃ vaidanaṃ vaidaiti| idamakkhātaṃ sak‍kāyassa nissaraṇaṃ| imai pañ‍ca dhammā  duppaṭivijjhā|

(ja) ‘‘katamai pañ‍ca dhammā uppādaitabbā? pañ‍ca ñāṇikau sammāsamādhi – ‘ayaṃ samādhi pac‍cuppan‍nasukhau caiva āyatiñ‍ca sukhavipākau’ti pac‍cattaṃyaiva ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍jati| ‘ayaṃ samādhi ariyau nirāmisau’ti pac‍cattañ‍ñaiva  ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍jati| ‘ayaṃ samādhi akāpurisasaivitau’ti pac‍cattaṃyaiva ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍jati| ‘ayaṃ samādhi santau paṇītau paṭippassaddhaladdhau aikaudibhāvādhigatau, na sasaṅkhāraniggayhavāritagatau’ti na ca sasaṅkhāraniggayha vāritavatauti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), na sasaṅkhāraniggayhavārivāvatau (ka॰), na sasaṅkhāraniggayhavāriyādhigatau (?) pac‍cattaṃyaiva ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍jati| ‘sau khau panāhaṃ imaṃ samādhiṃ satauva samāpaj‍jāmi satau vuṭṭhahāmī’ti pac‍cattaṃyaiva ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍jati| imai pañ‍ca dhammā uppādaitabbā|

(jha) ‘‘katamai pañ‍ca dhammā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā? pañ‍ca vimuttāyatanāni – idhāvusau, bhikkhunau satthā dhammaṃ daisaiti añ‍ñatarau vā garuṭṭhāniyau sabrahmacārī| yathā  yathā, āvusau, bhikkhunau satthā dhammaṃ daisaiti, añ‍ñatarau vā garuṭṭhāniyau sabrahmacārī, tathā tathā sau bhikkhu (syā॰ kaṃ॰) tasmiṃ dhammai atthappaṭisaṃvaidī ca hauti dhammapaṭisaṃvaidī ca| tassa atthappaṭisaṃvaidinau dhammapaṭisaṃvaidinau pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati, pamuditassa pīti jāyati, pītimanassa kāyau passambhati, passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidaiti, sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati| idaṃ paṭhamaṃ vimuttāyatanaṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunau na haiva khau satthā dhammaṃ daisaiti, añ‍ñatarau vā garuṭṭhāniyau sabrahmacārī, api ca khau yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ  vitthāraina paraisaṃ daisaiti yathā  yathā, āvusau, bhikkhu yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ vitthāraina paraisaṃ daisaiti| tathā tathā sau tasmiṃ dhammai atthappaṭisaṃvaidī ca hauti dhammapaṭisaṃvaidī ca| tassa atthappaṭisaṃvaidinau dhammapaṭisaṃvaidinau pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati, pamuditassa pīti jāyati, pītimanassa kāyau passambhati, passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidaiti, sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati| idaṃ dutiyaṃ vimuttāyatanaṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunau na haiva khau satthā dhammaṃ daisaiti, añ‍ñatarau vā garuṭṭhāniyau sabrahmacārī, nāpi yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ vitthāraina paraisaṃ daisaiti| api ca khau, yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ vitthāraina sajjhāyaṃ karauti| yathā yathā, āvusau, bhikkhu yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ vitthāraina sajjhāyaṃ karauti tathā tathā sau tasmiṃ dhammai atthappaṭisaṃvaidī ca hauti dhammapaṭisaṃvaidī ca| tassa atthappaṭisaṃvaidinau dhammapaṭisaṃvaidinau pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati, pamuditassa pīti jāyati, pītimanassa kāyau passambhati, passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidaiti, sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati| idaṃ tatiyaṃ vimuttāyatanaṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunau na haiva khau satthā dhammaṃ daisaiti, añ‍ñatarau vā garuṭṭhāniyau sabrahmacārī, nāpi yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ vitthāraina paraisaṃ daisaiti, nāpi yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ vitthāraina sajjhāyaṃ karauti| api ca khau, yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ caitasā anuvitak‍kaiti anuvicāraiti manasānupaikkhati| yathā yathā, āvusau, bhikkhu yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ caitasā anuvitak‍kaiti anuvicāraiti manasānupaikkhati tathā tathā sau tasmiṃ dhammai atthappaṭisaṃvaidī ca hauti dhammapaṭisaṃvaidī ca| tassa atthappaṭisaṃvaidinau dhammapaṭisaṃvaidinau pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati, pamuditassa pīti jāyati, pītimanassa kāyau passambhati, passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidaiti, sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati| idaṃ catutthaṃ vimuttāyatanaṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunau na haiva khau satthā dhammaṃ daisaiti, añ‍ñatarau vā garuṭṭhāniyau sabrahmacārī, nāpi yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ vitthāraina paraisaṃ daisaiti, nāpi yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ vitthāraina sajjhāyaṃ karauti, nāpi yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ caitasā anuvitak‍kaiti anuvicāraiti manasānupaikkhati; api ca khvassa añ‍ñataraṃ samādhinimittaṃ suggahitaṃ hauti sumanasikataṃ sūpadhāritaṃ suppaṭividdhaṃ pañ‍ñāya| yathā yathā, āvusau, bhikkhunau añ‍ñataraṃ samādhinimittaṃ suggahitaṃ hauti sumanasikataṃ sūpadhāritaṃ suppaṭividdhaṃ  pañ‍ñāya tathā tathā sau tasmiṃ dhammai atthappaṭisaṃvaidī ca hauti dhammappaṭisaṃvaidī ca| tassa atthappaṭisaṃvaidinau dhammappaṭisaṃvaidinau pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati, pamuditassa pīti jāyati, pītimanassa kāyau passambhati, passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidaiti, sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati| idaṃ pañ‍camaṃ vimuttāyatanaṃ| imai pañ‍ca dhammā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā|

(ña) ‘‘katamai pañ‍ca dhammā sacchikātabbā? pañ‍ca dhammakkhandhā – sīlakkhandhau, samādhikkhandhau, pañ‍ñākkhandhau, vimuttikkhandhau, vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhau| imai pañ‍ca dhammā sacchikātabbā|

‘‘iti imai pañ‍ñāsa dhammā bhūtā tacchā tathā avitathā anañ‍ñathā sammā tathāgataina abhisambuddhā|

cha dhammā

356. ‘‘cha dhammā bahukārā…pai॰… cha dhammā sacchikātabbā|

(ka) ‘‘katamai cha dhammā bahukārā? cha sāraṇīyā dhammā| idhāvusau, bhikkhunau maittaṃ kāyakammaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ  hauti sabrahmacārīsu āvi caiva rahau  ca, ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau piyakaraṇau garukaraṇau saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunau maittaṃ vacīkammaṃ…pai॰… aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunau maittaṃ manaukammaṃ…pai॰… aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu yai tai lābhā dhammikā dhammaladdhā antamasau pattapariyāpan‍namattampi, tathārūpaihi lābhaihi appaṭivibhattabhaugī hauti sīlavantaihi sabrahmacārīhi sādhāraṇabhaugī, ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau…pai॰… aikībhāvāya  saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu, yāni tāni sīlāni akhaṇḍāni acchiddāni asabalāni akammāsāni bhujissāni viñ‍ñuppasatthāni aparāmaṭṭhāni samādhisaṃvattanikāni, tathārūpaisu  sīlaisu sīlasāmañ‍ñagatau viharati sabrahmacārīhi āvi caiva rahau ca, ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau…pai॰… aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu yāyaṃ diṭṭhi ariyā niyyānikā niyyāti tak‍karassa sammā dukkhakkhayāya, tathārūpāya diṭṭhiyā diṭṭhi sāmañ‍ñagatau viharati sabrahmacārīhi āvi caiva rahau ca, ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau piyakaraṇau garukaraṇau, saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā aikībhāvāya saṃvattati| imai cha dhammā bahukārā|

(kha) ‘‘katamai cha dhammā bhāvaitabbā? cha anussatiṭṭhānāni – buddhānussati, dhammānussati, saṅghānussati, sīlānussati, cāgānussati, daivatānussati| imai cha dhammā bhāvaitabbā|

(ga) ‘‘katamai cha dhammā pariñ‍ñaiyyā? cha ajjhattikāni āyatanāni – cakkhāyatanaṃ, sautāyatanaṃ, ghānāyatanaṃ, jivhāyatanaṃ, kāyāyatanaṃ, manāyatanaṃ| imai cha dhammā pariñ‍ñaiyyā|

(gha) ‘‘katamai cha dhammā pahātabbā? cha taṇhākāyā – rūpataṇhā, saddataṇhā, gandhataṇhā, rasataṇhā, phauṭṭhabbataṇhā, dhammataṇhā| imai cha dhammā pahātabbā|

(ṅa) ‘‘katamai  cha dhammā hānabhāgiyā? cha agāravā – idhāvusau, bhikkhu satthari agāravau viharati appatissau| dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai… sikkhāya… appamādai… paṭisanthārai agāravau viharati appatissau| imai cha dhammā hānabhāgiyā|

(ca) ‘‘katamai cha dhammā visaisabhāgiyā? cha gāravā – idhāvusau, bhikkhu satthari sagāravau viharati sappatissau dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai… sikkhāya… appamādai… paṭisanthārai sagāravau viharati sappatissau| imai cha dhammā visaisabhāgiyā|

(cha) ‘‘katamai cha dhammā duppaṭivijjhā? cha nissaraṇiyā dhātuyau – idhāvusau, bhikkhu aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘maittā hi khau mai, caitauvimutti bhāvitā bahulīkatā yānīkatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā, atha ca pana mai byāpādau cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhatī’ti| sau ‘mā haivaṃ’ tissa vacanīyau ‘māyasmā aivaṃ avaca, mā bhagavantaṃ abbhācikkhi| na hi  sādhu bhagavatau abbhakkhānaṃ, na hi bhagavā aivaṃ vadaiyya| aṭṭhānamaitaṃ āvusau anavakāsau yaṃ maittāya caitauvimuttiyā bhāvitāya bahulīkatāya yānīkatāya vatthukatāya anuṭṭhitāya paricitāya susamāraddhāya| atha ca panassa byāpādau cittaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassatīti, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| nissaraṇaṃ haitaṃ, āvusau, byāpādassa, yadidaṃ maittācaitauvimuttī’ti|

‘‘idha panāvusau, bhikkhu aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘karuṇā  hi khau mai caitauvimutti bhāvitā bahulīkatā yānīkatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā| atha ca pana mai vihaisā cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhatī’ti| sau – ‘mā haivaṃ’ tissa vacanīyau, ‘māyasmā aivaṃ avaca, mā bhagavantaṃ abbhācikkhi…pai॰… nissaraṇaṃ haitaṃ, āvusau, vihaisāya, yadidaṃ karuṇācaitauvimuttī’ti|

‘‘idha panāvusau, bhikkhu aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘muditā hi khau mai caitauvimutti bhāvitā…pai॰… atha ca pana mai arati cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhatī’ti| sau – ‘mā haivaṃ’ tissa vacanīyau ‘māyasmā aivaṃ avaca…pai॰… nissaraṇaṃ haitaṃ, āvusau aratiyā, yadidaṃ muditācaitauvimuttī’ti|

‘‘idha  panāvusau, bhikkhu aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘upaikkhā hi khau mai caitauvimutti  bhāvitā…pai॰… atha ca pana mai rāgau cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhatī’ti| sau – ‘mā haivaṃ’ tissa vacanīyau ‘māyasmā aivaṃ avaca…pai॰… nissaraṇaṃ haitaṃ, āvusau, rāgassa yadidaṃ upaikkhācaitauvimuttī’ti|

‘‘idha panāvusau, bhikkhu aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘animittā hi khau mai caitauvimutti bhāvitā…pai॰… atha ca pana mai nimittānusāri viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hautī’ti| sau – ‘mā haivaṃ’ tissa vacanīyau ‘māyasmā aivaṃ avaca…pai॰… nissaraṇaṃ haitaṃ, āvusau, sabbanimittānaṃ yadidaṃ animittā caitauvimuttī’ti|

‘‘idha panāvusau, bhikkhu aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘asmīti khau mai vigataṃ, ayamahamasmīti na  samanupassāmi, atha ca pana mai vicikicchākathaṃkathāsal‍laṃ cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhatī’ti| sau – ‘mā haivaṃ’ tissa vacanīyau ‘māyasmā aivaṃ avaca, mā bhagavantaṃ abbhācikkhi, na  hi sādhu bhagavatau abbhakkhānaṃ, na hi bhagavā aivaṃ vadaiyya| aṭṭhānamaitaṃ, āvusau, anavakāsau yaṃ asmīti vigatai ayamahamasmīti asamanupassatau| atha ca panassa vicikicchākathaṃkathāsal‍laṃ cittaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassati, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| nissaraṇaṃ haitaṃ, āvusau, vicikicchākathaṃkathāsal‍lassa, yadidaṃ asmimānasamugghāṭau’ti| imai cha dhammā duppaṭivijjhā|

(ja) ‘‘katamai  cha dhammā uppādaitabbā? cha satatavihārā| idhāvusau, bhikkhu cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā naiva sumanau hauti na dummanau, upaikkhakau viharati satau sampajānau| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā…pai॰… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya naiva sumanau hauti na dummanau, upaikkhakau viharati satau sampajānau| imai cha dhammā uppādaitabbā|

(jha) ‘‘katamai cha dhammā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā? cha anuttariyāni – dassanānuttariyaṃ, savanānuttariyaṃ, lābhānuttariyaṃ, sikkhānuttariyaṃ, pāricariyānuttariyaṃ, anussatānuttariyaṃ| imai cha dhammā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā|

(ña) ‘‘katamai cha dhammā sacchikātabbā? cha abhiñ‍ñā – idhāvusau, bhikkhu anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhauti – aikaupi hutvā bahudhā hauti, bahudhāpi hutvā aikau hauti| āvibhāvaṃ tiraubhāvaṃ| tiraukuṭṭaṃ tiraupākāraṃ tiraupabbataṃ asaj‍jamānau gacchati saiyyathāpi ākāsai| pathaviyāpi ummuj‍janimuj‍jaṃ karauti saiyyathāpi udakai| udakaipi abhij‍jamānai gacchati saiyyathāpi pathaviyaṃ| ākāsaipi pal‍laṅkaina kamati saiyyathāpi pakkhī sakuṇau| imaipi candimasūriyai aivaṃmahiddhikai aivaṃmahānubhāvai pāṇinā parāmasati parimaj‍jati| yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaiti|

‘‘dibbāya sautadhātuyā visuddhāya atik‍kantamānusikāya ubhau saddai suṇāti dibbai ca mānusai ca, yai dūrai santikai ca|

‘‘parasattānaṃ  parapuggalānaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāti jānāti (syā॰ kaṃ॰), sarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ sarāgaṃ cittanti pajānāti  …pai॰… avimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ avimuttaṃ cittanti pajānāti|

‘‘sau anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, saiyyathidaṃ aikampi jātiṃ…pai॰… iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati|

‘‘dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti …pai॰…

‘‘āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| imai cha dhammā sacchikātabbā|

‘‘iti imai saṭṭhi dhammā bhūtā tacchā tathā avitathā anañ‍ñathā sammā tathāgataina abhisambuddhā|

satta dhammā

357. ‘‘satta  dhammā bahukārā…pai॰… satta dhammā sacchikātabbā|

(ka) ‘‘katamai satta dhammā bahukārā? satta ariyadhanāni – saddhādhanaṃ, sīladhanaṃ, hiridhanaṃ, auttappadhanaṃ, sutadhanaṃ, cāgadhanaṃ, pañ‍ñādhanaṃ| imai satta dhammā bahukārā|

(kha) ‘‘katamai  satta dhammā bhāvaitabbā? satta sambaujjhaṅgā – satisambaujjhaṅgau, dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau, vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau, pītisambaujjhaṅgau, passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau, samādhisambaujjhaṅgau, upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau| imai satta dhammā bhāvaitabbā|

(ga) ‘‘katamai satta dhammā pariñ‍ñaiyyā? satta viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitiyau – santāvusau, sattā nānattakāyā nānattasañ‍ñinau, saiyyathāpi manussā aikac‍cai ca daivā aikac‍cai ca vinipātikā| ayaṃ paṭhamā viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti|

‘‘santāvusau, sattā nānattakāyā aikattasañ‍ñinau, saiyyathāpi daivā brahmakāyikā paṭhamābhinibbattā| ayaṃ dutiyā viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti|

‘‘santāvusau, sattā aikattakāyā nānattasañ‍ñinau, saiyyathāpi daivā ābhassarā| ayaṃ tatiyā viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti|

‘‘santāvusau, sattā aikattakāyā aikattasañ‍ñinau, saiyyathāpi daivā subhakiṇhā| ayaṃ catutthī viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti|

‘‘santāvusau, sattā sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā…pai॰… ‘anantau ākāsau’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanūpagā| ayaṃ pañ‍camī viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti|

‘‘santāvusau, sattā sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanūpagā| ayaṃ chaṭṭhī viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti|

‘‘santāvusau, sattā sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanūpagā| ayaṃ sattamī viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti| imai satta dhammā pariñ‍ñaiyyā|

(gha) ‘‘katamai satta dhammā pahātabbā? sattānusayā – kāmarāgānusayau, paṭighānusayau, diṭṭhānusayau, vicikicchānusayau, mānānusayau, bhavarāgānusayau, avij‍jānusayau| imai satta dhammā pahātabbā|

(ṅa) ‘‘katamai satta dhammā hānabhāgiyā? satta asaddhammā – idhāvusau, bhikkhu assaddhau hauti, ahirikau hauti, anauttappī hauti, appassutau  hauti, kusītau hauti, muṭṭhassati hauti, duppañ‍ñau hauti| imai satta dhammā hānabhāgiyā|

(ca) ‘‘katamai satta dhammā visaisabhāgiyā? satta saddhammā – idhāvusau, bhikkhu saddhau hauti, hirimā hirikau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) hauti, auttappī hauti, bahussutau hauti, āraddhavīriyau hauti, upaṭṭhitassati hauti, pañ‍ñavā hauti| imai satta dhammā visaisabhāgiyā|

(cha) ‘‘katamai  satta dhammā duppaṭivijjhā? satta sappurisadhammā – idhāvusau, bhikkhu dhammañ‍ñū ca hauti atthañ‍ñū ca attañ‍ñū ca mattañ‍ñū ca kālañ‍ñū ca parisañ‍ñū ca puggalañ‍ñū ca| imai satta dhammā duppaṭivijjhā|

(ja) ‘‘katamai satta dhammā uppādaitabbā? satta sañ‍ñā – anic‍casañ‍ñā, anattasañ‍ñā, asubhasañ‍ñā, ādīnavasañ‍ñā, pahānasañ‍ñā, virāgasañ‍ñā, niraudhasañ‍ñā| imai satta dhammā uppādaitabbā|

(jha) ‘‘katamai satta dhammā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā? satta niddasavatthūni – idhāvusau, bhikkhu sikkhāsamādānai tibbacchandau hauti, āyatiñ‍ca sikkhāsamādānai avigatapaimau| dhammanisantiyā tibbacchandau hauti, āyatiñ‍ca  dhammanisantiyā avigatapaimau| icchāvinayai tibbacchandau hauti, āyatiñ‍ca icchāvinayai avigatapaimau| paṭisal‍lānai tibbacchandau hauti, āyatiñ‍ca paṭisal‍lānai avigatapaimau| vīriyārambhai tibbacchandau hauti, āyatiñ‍ca vīriyārambhai avigatapaimau| satinaipak‍kai tibbacchandau hauti, āyatiñ‍ca satinaipak‍kai avigatapaimau| diṭṭhipaṭivaidhai tibbacchandau hauti, āyatiñ‍ca diṭṭhipaṭivaidhai avigatapaimau| imai satta dhammā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā|

(ña) ‘‘katamai satta dhammā sacchikātabbā? satta khīṇāsavabalāni – idhāvusau, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau anic‍catau sabbai saṅkhārā yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhā haunti| yaṃpāvusau, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau anic‍catau sabbai saṅkhārā yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhā haunti, idampi khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau balaṃ hauti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma khīṇāsavau bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayaṃ paṭijānāti – ‘khīṇā mai āsavā’ti|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, āvusau, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau aṅgārakāsūpamā kāmā yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhā haunti| yaṃpāvusau…pai॰… ‘khīṇā mai āsavā’ti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau vivaikanin‍naṃ cittaṃ hauti vivaikapauṇaṃ vivaikapabbhāraṃ vivaikaṭṭhaṃ naikkhammābhirataṃ byantībhūtaṃ sabbasau āsavaṭṭhāniyaihi  dhammaihi| yaṃpāvusau…pai॰… ‘khīṇā mai āsavā’ti|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, āvusau, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau cattārau satipaṭṭhānā bhāvitā haunti subhāvitā| yaṃpāvusau…pai॰… ‘khīṇā mai āsavā’ti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau pañ‍cindriyāni bhāvitāni haunti subhāvitāni| yaṃpāvusau…pai॰… ‘khīṇā mai āsavā’ti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau satta baujjhaṅgā bhāvitā haunti subhāvitā| yaṃpāvusau…pai॰… ‘khīṇā mai āsavā’ti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvitau hauti subhāvitau| yaṃpāvusau, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau  maggau bhāvitau hauti subhāvitau, idampi khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau balaṃ hauti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma khīṇāsavau bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayaṃ paṭijānāti – ‘khīṇā mai āsavā’ti| imai satta dhammā sacchikātabbā|

‘‘itimai sattati dhammā bhūtā tacchā tathā avitathā anañ‍ñathā sammā tathāgataina abhisambuddhā|

paṭhamabhāṇavārau niṭṭhitau|

aṭṭha dhammā

358. ‘‘aṭṭha dhammā bahukārā…pai॰… aṭṭha dhammā sacchikātabbā|

(ka) ‘‘katamai aṭṭha dhammā bahukārā? aṭṭha haitū aṭṭha pac‍cayā ādibrahmacariyikāya pañ‍ñāya appaṭiladdhāya paṭilābhāya paṭiladdhāya bhiyyaubhāvāya  vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā saṃvattanti| katamai aṭṭha? idhāvusau, bhikkhu satthāraṃ satthāraṃ vā (syā॰ ka॰) upanissāya viharati añ‍ñataraṃ vā garuṭṭhāniyaṃ sabrahmacāriṃ, yatthassa tibbaṃ hirauttappaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti paimañ‍ca gāravau  ca| ayaṃ paṭhamau haitu paṭhamau pac‍cayau ādibrahmacariyikāya pañ‍ñāya appaṭiladdhāya paṭilābhāya| paṭiladdhāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā saṃvattati|

‘‘taṃ khau pana satthāraṃ upanissāya viharati añ‍ñataraṃ vā garuṭṭhāniyaṃ sabrahmacāriṃ, yatthassa tibbaṃ hirauttappaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti paimañ‍ca gāravau ca| tai kālaina kālaṃ upasaṅkamitvā paripucchati paripañhati – ‘idaṃ, bhantai, kathaṃ? imassa kau atthau’ti? tassa tai āyasmantau avivaṭañ‍caiva vivaranti, anuttānīkatañ‍ca uttānī anuttānikatañ‍ca uttāniṃ (ka॰) karaunti, anaikavihitaisu ca kaṅkhāṭṭhāniyaisu dhammaisu kaṅkhaṃ paṭivinaudainti| ayaṃ dutiyau haitu dutiyau pac‍cayau ādibrahmacariyikāya pañ‍ñāya appaṭiladdhāya paṭilābhāya, paṭiladdhāya bhiyyaubhāvāya, vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā saṃvattati|

‘‘taṃ khau pana dhammaṃ sutvā dvayaina vūpakāsaina sampādaiti – kāyavūpakāsaina ca cittavūpakāsaina ca| ayaṃ tatiyau haitu tatiyau pac‍cayau ādibrahmacariyikāya pañ‍ñāya appaṭiladdhāya paṭilābhāya, paṭiladdhāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu sīlavā hauti, pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharati ācāragaucarasampan‍nau, aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu| ayaṃ catutthau haitu catutthau pac‍cayau ādibrahmacariyikāya pañ‍ñāya appaṭiladdhāya paṭilābhāya, paṭiladdhāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu bahussutau hauti sutadharau sutasan‍nicayau| yai tai dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhaikalyāṇā pariyausānakalyāṇā sātthā sabyañ‍janā kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti, tathārūpāssa dhammā bahussutā haunti dhātā  vacasā  paricitā manasānupaikkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā| ayaṃ pañ‍camau haitu pañ‍camau pac‍cayau ādibrahmacariyikāya pañ‍ñāya appaṭiladdhāya paṭilābhāya, paṭiladdhāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu āraddhavīriyau viharati akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya, thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu| ayaṃ chaṭṭhau  haitu chaṭṭhau pac‍cayau ādibrahmacariyikāya pañ‍ñāya appaṭiladdhāya paṭilābhāya, paṭiladdhāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu satimā hauti paramaina satinaipak‍kaina saman‍nāgatau| cirakatampi cirabhāsitampi saritā anussaritā| ayaṃ sattamau haitu sattamau pac‍cayau ādibrahmacariyikāya pañ‍ñāya appaṭiladdhāya paṭilābhāya, paṭiladdhāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu, udayabbayānupassī viharati – ‘iti rūpaṃ iti rūpassa samudayau iti rūpassa atthaṅgamau; iti vaidanā iti vaidanāya samudayau iti vaidanāya atthaṅgamau; iti sañ‍ñā iti sañ‍ñāya samudayau iti sañ‍ñāya atthaṅgamau; iti saṅkhārā iti saṅkhārānaṃ samudayau iti saṅkhārānaṃ atthaṅgamau; iti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ iti viñ‍ñāṇassa samudayau iti viñ‍ñāṇassa atthaṅgamau’ti| ayaṃ aṭṭhamau haitu aṭṭhamau pac‍cayau ādibrahmacariyikāya  pañ‍ñāya appaṭiladdhāya paṭilābhāya, paṭiladdhāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā saṃvattati| imai aṭṭha dhammā bahukārā|

(kha) ‘‘katamai aṭṭha dhammā bhāvaitabbā? ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappau, sammāvācā, sammākammantau, sammāājīvau, sammāvāyāmau, sammāsati, sammāsamādhi| imai aṭṭha dhammā bhāvaitabbā|

(ga) ‘‘katamai aṭṭha dhammā pariñ‍ñaiyyā? aṭṭha laukadhammā – lābhau ca, alābhau ca, yasau ca, ayasau ca, nindā ca, pasaṃsā ca, sukhañ‍ca, dukkhañ‍ca| imai aṭṭha dhammā pariñ‍ñaiyyā|

(gha) ‘‘katamai  aṭṭha dhammā pahātabbā? aṭṭha micchattā  – micchādiṭṭhi, micchāsaṅkappau, micchāvācā, micchākammantau, micchāājīvau, micchāvāyāmau, micchāsati, micchāsamādhi| imai aṭṭha dhammā pahātabbā|

(ṅa) ‘‘katamai aṭṭha dhammā hānabhāgiyā? aṭṭha kusītavatthūni| idhāvusau, bhikkhunā kammaṃ kātabbaṃ hauti, tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘kammaṃ khau mai kātabbaṃ bhavissati, kammaṃ khau pana  mai karauntassa kāyau kilamissati, handāhaṃ nipaj‍jāmī’ti| sau nipaj‍jati, na vīriyaṃ ārabhati appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya| idaṃ paṭhamaṃ kusītavatthu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunā kammaṃ kataṃ hauti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khau kammaṃ akāsiṃ, kammaṃ khau pana mai karauntassa kāyau kilantau, handāhaṃ nipaj‍jāmī’ti| sau nipaj‍jati, na vīriyaṃ ārabhati…pai॰… idaṃ dutiyaṃ kusītavatthu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunā maggau gantabbau hauti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘maggau khau mai gantabbau bhavissati, maggaṃ khau pana mai gacchantassa kāyau kilamissati, handāhaṃ nipaj‍jāmī’ti| sau nipaj‍jati, na vīriyaṃ ārabhati…pai॰… idaṃ tatiyaṃ kusītavatthu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunā maggau gatau hauti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khau maggaṃ agamāsiṃ, maggaṃ khau pana mai gacchantassa kāyau kilantau, handāhaṃ nipaj‍jāmī’ti| sau nipaj‍jati, na vīriyaṃ ārabhati…pai॰… idaṃ catutthaṃ kusītavatthu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya carantau na labhati lūkhassa vā paṇītassa vā bhaujanassa yāvadatthaṃ pāripūriṃ| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khau gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya carantau nālatthaṃ lūkhassa vā paṇītassa vā bhaujanassa yāvadatthaṃ pāripūriṃ, tassa mai kāyau kilantau akammañ‍ñau, handāhaṃ nipaj‍jāmī’ti…pai॰… idaṃ pañ‍camaṃ kusītavatthu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya carantau labhati lūkhassa vā paṇītassa vā bhaujanassa yāvadatthaṃ pāripūriṃ| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khau gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya carantau alatthaṃ lūkhassa  vā paṇītassa vā bhaujanassa yāvadatthaṃ pāripūriṃ, tassa mai kāyau garukau akammañ‍ñau, māsācitaṃ mañ‍ñai, handāhaṃ nipaj‍jāmī’ti| sau nipaj‍jati…pai॰… idaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ kusītavatthu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunau uppan‍nau hauti appamattakau ābādhau, tassa aivaṃ hauti  – ‘uppan‍nau khau mai ayaṃ appamattakau ābādhau atthi kappau nipaj‍jituṃ, handāhaṃ nipaj‍jāmī’ti| sau nipaj‍jati…pai॰… idaṃ sattamaṃ kusītavatthu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu gilānāvuṭṭhitau hauti aciravuṭṭhitau gailañ‍ñā| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khau gilānāvuṭṭhitau aciravuṭṭhitau gailañ‍ñā| tassa mai kāyau dubbalau akammañ‍ñau, handāhaṃ nipaj‍jāmī’ti| sau nipaj‍jati…pai॰… idaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ kusītavatthu| imai aṭṭha dhammā hānabhāgiyā|

(ca) ‘‘katamai aṭṭha dhammā visaisabhāgiyā? aṭṭha ārambhavatthūni| idhāvusau, bhikkhunā kammaṃ kātabbaṃ hauti, tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘kammaṃ khau mai kātabbaṃ bhavissati, kammaṃ khau pana mai karauntaina na sukaraṃ buddhānaṃ sāsanaṃ manasikātuṃ, handāhaṃ vīriyaṃ ārabhāmi appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa  sacchikiriyāyā’ti| sau vīriyaṃ ārabhati appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya| idaṃ paṭhamaṃ ārambhavatthu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunā kammaṃ kataṃ hauti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khau kammaṃ akāsiṃ, kammaṃ khau panāhaṃ karauntau nāsakkhiṃ buddhānaṃ sāsanaṃ manasikātuṃ, handāhaṃ vīriyaṃ ārabhāmi…pai॰… idaṃ dutiyaṃ ārambhavatthu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunā maggau gantabbau hauti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘maggau khau mai gantabbau bhavissati, maggaṃ khau pana mai gacchantaina na sukaraṃ buddhānaṃ sāsanaṃ manasikātuṃ, handāhaṃ vīriyaṃ ārabhāmi…pai॰… idaṃ tatiyaṃ ārambhavatthu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunā maggau gatau hauti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khau maggaṃ agamāsiṃ, maggaṃ khau panāhaṃ gacchantau nāsakkhiṃ buddhānaṃ sāsanaṃ manasikātuṃ, handāhaṃ vīriyaṃ ārabhāmi…pai॰… idaṃ catutthaṃ ārambhavatthu|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya carantau na  labhati lūkhassa vā paṇītassa vā bhaujanassa yāvadatthaṃ pāripūriṃ| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khau gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya carantau nālatthaṃ lūkhassa vā paṇītassa vā bhaujanassa yāvadatthaṃ pāripūriṃ, tassa mai kāyau lahukau kammañ‍ñau, handāhaṃ vīriyaṃ ārabhāmi…pai॰… idaṃ pañ‍camaṃ ārambhavatthu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya carantau labhati lūkhassa vā paṇītassa vā bhaujanassa yāvadatthaṃ pāripūriṃ| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khau gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya carantau alatthaṃ lūkhassa vā paṇītassa vā bhaujanassa yāvadatthaṃ pāripūriṃ| tassa mai kāyau balavā kammañ‍ñau, handāhaṃ vīriyaṃ ārabhāmi…pai॰… idaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ ārambhavatthu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunau uppan‍nau hauti appamattakau ābādhau| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘uppan‍nau khau mai ayaṃ appamattakau ābādhau ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ vij‍jati, yaṃ mai ābādhau pavaḍḍhaiyya, handāhaṃ vīriyaṃ ārabhāmi…pai॰… idaṃ  sattamaṃ ārambhavatthu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu gilānā vuṭṭhitau hauti aciravuṭṭhitau gailañ‍ñā| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khau gilānā vuṭṭhitau aciravuṭṭhitau gailañ‍ñā, ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ vij‍jati, yaṃ mai ābādhau pac‍cudāvattaiyya, handāhaṃ vīriyaṃ ārabhāmi appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāyā’ti| sau vīriyaṃ ārabhati appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya| idaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ ārambhavatthu| imai aṭṭha dhammā visaisabhāgiyā|

(cha) ‘‘katamai aṭṭha dhammā duppaṭivijjhā? aṭṭha akkhaṇā asamayā brahmacariyavāsāya| idhāvusau, tathāgatau ca laukai uppan‍nau hauti arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau, dhammau ca daisiyati aupasamikau parinibbānikau sambaudhagāmī sugatappavaiditau| ayañ‍ca puggalau nirayaṃ upapan‍nau hauti| ayaṃ paṭhamau akkhaṇau asamayau brahmacariyavāsāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, tathāgatau ca laukai uppan‍nau hauti arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau, dhammau ca daisiyati aupasamikau parinibbānikau sambaudhagāmī  sugatappavaiditau, ayañ‍ca puggalau tiracchānayauniṃ upapan‍nau hauti| ayaṃ dutiyau akkhaṇau asamayau brahmacariyavāsāya|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ…pai॰… paittivisayaṃ  upapan‍nau hauti| ayaṃ tatiyau akkhaṇau asamayau brahmacariyavāsāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ…pai॰… añ‍ñataraṃ dīghāyukaṃ daivanikāyaṃ upapan‍nau hauti| ayaṃ catutthau akkhaṇau asamayau brahmacariyavāsāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ…pai॰… pac‍cantimaisu janapadaisu pac‍cājātau hauti milakkhaisu aviñ‍ñātāraisu, yattha natthi gati bhikkhūnaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ upāsakānaṃ upāsikānaṃ| ayaṃ pañ‍camau akkhaṇau asamayau brahmacariyavāsāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ…pai॰… ayañ‍ca puggalau majjhimaisu janapadaisu pac‍cājātau hauti, sau ca hauti micchādiṭṭhikau viparītadassanau – ‘natthi din‍naṃ, natthi yiṭṭhaṃ, natthi hutaṃ, natthi sukataduk‍kaṭānaṃ kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipākau, natthi ayaṃ laukau, natthi parau laukau, natthi mātā, natthi pitā, natthi sattā aupapātikā, natthi laukai samaṇabrāhmaṇā sammaggatā sammāpaṭipan‍nā yai imañ‍ca laukaṃ parañ‍ca laukaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaintī’ti| ayaṃ chaṭṭhau akkhaṇau asamayau brahmacariyavāsāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ…pai॰… ayañ‍ca puggalau majjhimaisu janapadaisu pac‍cājātau hauti, sau ca hauti duppañ‍ñau jaḷau aiḷamūgau, nappaṭibalau subhāsitadubbhāsitānamatthamañ‍ñātuṃ| ayaṃ sattamau akkhaṇau asamayau brahmacariyavāsāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ…pai॰… ayañ‍ca puggalau majjhimaisu janapadaisu pac‍cājātau hauti, sau ca hauti pañ‍ñavā ajaḷau anaiḷamūgau, paṭibalau subhāsitadubbhāsitānamatthamañ‍ñātuṃ| ayaṃ aṭṭhamau akkhaṇau asamayau brahmacariyavāsāya| imai aṭṭha dhammā duppaṭivijjhā|

(ja) ‘‘katamai aṭṭha dhammā uppādaitabbā? aṭṭha mahāpurisavitak‍kā – appicchassāyaṃ dhammau, nāyaṃ dhammau mahicchassa| santuṭṭhassāyaṃ dhammau, nāyaṃ dhammau asantuṭṭhassa| pavivittassāyaṃ dhammau, nāyaṃ dhammau saṅgaṇikārāmassa| āraddhavīriyassāyaṃ dhammau, nāyaṃ dhammau kusītassa| upaṭṭhitasatissāyaṃ dhammau, nāyaṃ dhammau muṭṭhassatissa| samāhitassāyaṃ dhammau, nāyaṃ dhammau asamāhitassa| pañ‍ñavatau pañ‍ñāvatau (sī॰ pī॰) ayaṃ dhammau, nāyaṃ dhammau duppañ‍ñassa| nippapañ‍cassāyaṃ dhammau, nāyaṃ dhammau papañ‍cārāmassāti nippapañ‍cārāmassa ayaṃ dhammau nippapañ‍caratinau, nāyaṃ dhammau papañ‍cārāmassa papañ‍caratinauti (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) aṅguttaraipi tathaiva dissati| aṭṭhakathāṭīkā pana aulaukaitabbā imai aṭṭha dhammā uppādaitabbā|

(jha) ‘‘katamai aṭṭha dhammā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā? aṭṭha abhibhāyatanāni – ajjhattaṃ rūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati parittāni suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇāni, ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī’ti – aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ paṭhamaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ rūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati appamāṇāni suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇāni, ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī’ti – aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ dutiyaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati parittāni suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇāni, ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī’ti aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ tatiyaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati appamāṇāni suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇāni, ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī’ti aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ catutthaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati nīlāni nīlavaṇṇāni nīlanidassanāni nīlanibhāsāni| saiyyathāpi nāma umāpupphaṃ nīlaṃ nīlavaṇṇaṃ nīlanidassanaṃ nīlanibhāsaṃ| saiyyathā vā pana taṃ vatthaṃ bārāṇasaiyyakaṃ ubhataubhāgavimaṭṭhaṃ nīlaṃ nīlavaṇṇaṃ nīlanidassanaṃ nīlanibhāsaṃ, aivamaiva ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati nīlāni nīlavaṇṇāni nīlanidassanāni nīlanibhāsāni, ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī’ti aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ pañ‍camaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati pītāni pītavaṇṇāni pītanidassanāni pītanibhāsāni| saiyyathāpi nāma kaṇikārapupphaṃ pītaṃ pītavaṇṇaṃ pītanidassanaṃ pītanibhāsaṃ| saiyyathā vā pana taṃ vatthaṃ bārāṇasaiyyakaṃ  ubhataubhāgavimaṭṭhaṃ pītaṃ pītavaṇṇaṃ pītanidassanaṃ pītanibhāsaṃ, aivamaiva ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati pītāni pītavaṇṇāni pītanidassanāni pītanibhāsāni, ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī’ti aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ  arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati lauhitakāni lauhitakavaṇṇāni lauhitakanidassanāni lauhitakanibhāsāni| saiyyathāpi nāma bandhujīvakapupphaṃ lauhitakaṃ lauhitakavaṇṇaṃ lauhitakanidassanaṃ lauhitakanibhāsaṃ, saiyyathā vā pana taṃ vatthaṃ bārāṇasaiyyakaṃ ubhataubhāgavimaṭṭhaṃ lauhitakaṃ lauhitakavaṇṇaṃ lauhitakanidassanaṃ lauhitakanibhāsaṃ, aivamaiva ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati lauhitakāni lauhitakavaṇṇāni lauhitakanidassanāni lauhitakanibhāsāni, ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī’ti aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ sattamaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati audātāni audātavaṇṇāni audātanidassanāni audātanibhāsāni| saiyyathāpi nāma ausadhitārakā audātā audātavaṇṇā audātanidassanā audātanibhāsā, saiyyathā vā pana taṃ vatthaṃ bārāṇasaiyyakaṃ ubhataubhāgavimaṭṭhaṃ audātaṃ audātavaṇṇaṃ audātanidassanaṃ audātanibhāsaṃ, aivamaiva ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati audātāni audātavaṇṇāni audātanidassanāni audātanibhāsāni, ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī’ti aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ| imai aṭṭha dhammā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā|

(ña) ‘‘katamai  aṭṭha dhammā sacchikātabbā? aṭṭha vimaukkhā – rūpī rūpāni passati| ayaṃ paṭhamau vimaukkhau|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati| ayaṃ dutiyau vimaukkhau|

‘‘subhantaiva adhimuttau hauti| ayaṃ tatiyau vimaukkhau|

‘‘sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ‘anantau ākāsau’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ catutthau vimaukkhau|

‘‘sabbasau  ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ pañ‍camau vimaukkhau|

‘‘sabbasau  viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ chaṭṭhau vimaukkhau|

‘‘sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ sattamau vimaukkhau|

‘‘sabbasau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ aṭṭhamau vimaukkhau| imai aṭṭha dhammā sacchikātabbā|

‘‘iti imai asīti dhammā bhūtā tacchā tathā avitathā anañ‍ñathā sammā tathāgataina abhisambuddhā|

nava dhammā

359. ‘‘nava dhammā bahukārā…pai॰… nava dhammā sacchikātabbā|

(ka) ‘‘katamai  nava dhammā bahukārā? nava yaunisaumanasikāramūlakā dhammā, yaunisaumanasikarautau pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati, pamuditassa pīti jāyati, pītimanassa kāyau passambhati, passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidaiti, sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati, samāhitai cittai yathābhūtaṃ jānāti passati, yathābhūtaṃ jānaṃ passaṃ nibbindati, nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati, virāgā vimuc‍cati| imai nava dhammā bahukārā|

(kha) ‘‘katamai nava dhammā bhāvaitabbā? nava pārisuddhipadhāniyaṅgāni – sīlavisuddhi pārisuddhipadhāniyaṅgaṃ, cittavisuddhi pārisuddhipadhāniyaṅgaṃ, diṭṭhivisuddhi pārisuddhipadhāniyaṅgaṃ, kaṅkhāvitaraṇavisuddhi pārisuddhipadhāniyaṅgaṃ, maggāmaggañāṇadassana – visuddhi pārisuddhipadhāniyaṅgaṃ, paṭipadāñāṇadassanavisuddhi pārisuddhipadhāniyaṅgaṃ, ñāṇadassanavisuddhi pārisuddhipadhāniyaṅgaṃ, pañ‍ñāvisuddhi pārisuddhipadhāniyaṅgaṃ, vimuttivisuddhi pārisuddhipadhāniyaṅgaṃ| imai nava dhammā bhāvaitabbā|

(ga) ‘‘katamai nava dhammā pariñ‍ñaiyyā? nava sattāvāsā – santāvusau, sattā nānattakāyā  nānattasañ‍ñinau, saiyyathāpi manussā aikac‍cai ca daivā aikac‍cai ca vinipātikā| ayaṃ paṭhamau sattāvāsau|

‘‘santāvusau, sattā nānattakāyā aikattasañ‍ñinau, saiyyathāpi daivā brahmakāyikā paṭhamābhinibbattā| ayaṃ dutiyau sattāvāsau|

‘‘santāvusau, sattā aikattakāyā  nānattasañ‍ñinau, saiyyathāpi daivā ābhassarā| ayaṃ tatiyau sattāvāsau|

‘‘santāvusau, sattā aikattakāyā aikattasañ‍ñinau, saiyyathāpi daivā subhakiṇhā| ayaṃ catutthau sattāvāsau|

‘‘santāvusau, sattā asañ‍ñinau appaṭisaṃvaidinau, saiyyathāpi daivā asañ‍ñasattā| ayaṃ pañ‍camau sattāvāsau|

‘‘santāvusau, sattā sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ‘anantau ākāsau’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanūpagā| ayaṃ chaṭṭhau sattāvāsau|

‘‘santāvusau, sattā sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanūpagā| ayaṃ sattamau sattāvāsau|

‘‘santāvusau, sattā sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanūpagā| ayaṃ aṭṭhamau sattāvāsau|

‘‘santāvusau, sattā sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanūpagā| ayaṃ navamau sattāvāsau| imai nava dhammā pariñ‍ñaiyyā|

(gha) ‘‘katamai nava dhammā pahātabbā? nava taṇhāmūlakā  dhammā – taṇhaṃ paṭic‍ca pariyaisanā, pariyaisanaṃ paṭic‍ca lābhau, lābhaṃ paṭic‍ca vinicchayau, vinicchayaṃ paṭic‍ca chandarāgau, chandarāgaṃ paṭic‍ca ajjhausānaṃ, ajjhausānaṃ paṭic‍ca pariggahau, pariggahaṃ paṭic‍ca macchariyaṃ, macchariyaṃ paṭic‍ca ārakkhau, ārakkhādhikaraṇaṃ ārakkhādhikaraṇaṃ paṭic‍ca (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰) daṇḍādānasatthādānakalahaviggahavivādatuvaṃtuvaṃpaisuñ‍ñamusāvādā anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti| imai nava dhammā pahātabbā|

(ṅa) ‘‘katamai  nava dhammā hānabhāgiyā? nava āghātavatthūni – ‘anatthaṃ mai acarī’ti āghātaṃ bandhati, ‘anatthaṃ mai caratī’ti āghātaṃ bandhati, ‘anatthaṃ mai carissatī’ti āghātaṃ bandhati; ‘piyassa mai manāpassa anatthaṃ acarī’ti āghātaṃ bandhati…pai॰… ‘anatthaṃ caratī’ti āghātaṃ bandhati…pai॰… ‘anatthaṃ carissatī’ti āghātaṃ bandhati; ‘appiyassa mai amanāpassa atthaṃ acarī’ti āghātaṃ bandhati…pai॰… ‘atthaṃ caratī’ti āghātaṃ bandhati…pai॰… ‘atthaṃ carissatī’ti āghātaṃ bandhati| imai nava dhammā hānabhāgiyā|

(ca) ‘‘katamai nava dhammā visaisabhāgiyā? nava āghātapaṭivinayā – ‘anatthaṃ mai acari, taṃ kutaittha labbhā’ti āghātaṃ paṭivinaiti; ‘anatthaṃ mai carati, taṃ kutaittha labbhā’ti āghātaṃ paṭivinaiti; ‘anatthaṃ mai carissati, taṃ kutaittha labbhā’ti āghātaṃ paṭivinaiti; ‘piyassa mai manāpassa anatthaṃ acari…pai॰… anatthaṃ carati…pai॰… anatthaṃ carissati, taṃ kutaittha labbhā’ti āghātaṃ paṭivinaiti; ‘appiyassa mai amanāpassa atthaṃ acari…pai॰… atthaṃ carati…pai॰… atthaṃ carissati, taṃ kutaittha labbhā’ti āghātaṃ  paṭivinaiti| imai nava dhammā visaisabhāgiyā|

(cha) ‘‘katamai nava dhammā duppaṭivijjhā? nava nānattā – dhātunānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati phassanānattaṃ, phassanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati vaidanānānattaṃ, vaidanānānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sañ‍ñānānattaṃ, sañ‍ñānānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati saṅkappanānattaṃ, saṅkappanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati chandanānattaṃ, chandanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati pariḷāhanānattaṃ, pariḷāhanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati pariyaisanānānattaṃ, pariyaisanānānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati lābhanānattaṃ| imai nava dhammā duppaṭivijjhā|

(ja) ‘‘katamai nava dhammā uppādaitabbā? nava sañ‍ñā – asubhasañ‍ñā, maraṇasañ‍ñā, āhāraipaṭikūlasañ‍ñā, sabbalaukaianabhiratisañ‍ñā anabhiratasañ‍ñā (syā॰ ka॰), anic‍casañ‍ñā, anic‍cai dukkhasañ‍ñā, dukkhai  anattasañ‍ñā, pahānasañ‍ñā, virāgasañ‍ñā| imai nava dhammā uppādaitabbā|

(jha) ‘‘katamai nava dhammā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā? nava anupubbavihārā – idhāvusau, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ  pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| pītiyā ca virāgā  …pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā…pai॰… ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sabbasau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| imai nava dhammā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā|

(ña) ‘‘katamai nava dhammā sacchikātabbā? nava anupubbaniraudhā – paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nassa kāmasañ‍ñā niruddhā hauti, dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nassa vitak‍kavicārā niruddhā haunti, tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nassa pīti niruddhā hauti, catutthaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nassa assāsapassāssā niruddhā haunti, ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samāpan‍nassa rūpasañ‍ñā niruddhā hauti, viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samāpan‍nassa ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñā niruddhā hauti, ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samāpan‍nassa viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñā  niruddhā hauti, naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samāpan‍nassa ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñā niruddhā hauti, sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpan‍nassa sañ‍ñā ca vaidanā ca niruddhā haunti| imai nava dhammā sacchikātabbā|

‘‘iti imai navuti dhammā bhūtā tacchā tathā avitathā anañ‍ñathā sammā tathāgataina abhisambuddhā|

dasa dhammā

360. ‘‘dasa  dhammā bahukārā…pai॰… dasa dhammā sacchikātabbā|

(ka) ‘‘katamai dasa dhammā bahukārā? dasa nāthakaraṇādhammā – idhāvusau, bhikkhu sīlavā hauti, pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharati ācāragaucarasampan‍nau, aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu, yaṃpāvusau, bhikkhu sīlavā hauti…pai॰… sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu| ayampi dhammau nāthakaraṇau|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu bahussutau  …pai॰… diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā, yaṃpāvusau, bhikkhu bahussutau…pai॰… ayampi dhammau nāthakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu kalyāṇamittau hauti kalyāṇasahāyau kalyāṇasampavaṅkau| yaṃpāvusau, bhikkhu…pai॰… kalyāṇasampavaṅkau| ayampi dhammau nāthakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu suvacau hauti sauvacassakaraṇaihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau, khamau padakkhiṇaggāhī anusāsaniṃ| yaṃpāvusau, bhikkhu…pai॰… anusāsaniṃ| ayampi dhammau nāthakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu yāni tāni sabrahmacārīnaṃ uc‍cāvacāni kiṃkaraṇīyāni tattha dakkhau hauti analasau tatrupāyāya vīmaṃsāya saman‍nāgatau, alaṃ kātuṃ, alaṃ saṃvidhātuṃ| yaṃpāvusau, bhikkhu…pai॰… alaṃ saṃvidhātuṃ| ayampi dhammau nāthakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu dhammakāmau hauti piyasamudāhārau abhidhammai abhivinayai uḷārapāmauj‍jau| yaṃpāvusau, bhikkhu…pai॰… uḷārapāmauj‍jau| ayampi dhammau nāthakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu santuṭṭhau hauti itarītaraihi cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraihi| yaṃpāvusau, bhikkhu  …pai॰… ayampi dhammau nāthakaraṇau|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu āraddhavīriyau viharati…pai॰… kusalaisu dhammaisu| yaṃpāvusau, bhikkhu…pai॰… ayampi dhammau nāthakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu satimā hauti, paramaina satinaipak‍kaina saman‍nāgatau, cirakatampi cirabhāsitampi saritā anussaritā| yaṃpāvusau, bhikkhu…pai॰… ayampi dhammau nāthakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu pañ‍ñavā hauti udayatthagāminiyā pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau, ariyāya nibbaidhikāya sammā dukkhakkhayagāminiyā| yaṃpāvusau, bhikkhu…pai॰… ayampi dhammau nāthakaraṇau| imai dasa dhammā bahukārā|

(kha) ‘‘katamai  dasa dhammā bhāvaitabbā? dasa kasiṇāyatanāni – pathavīkasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti uddhaṃ adhau tiriyaṃ advayaṃ appamāṇaṃ| āpaukasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti…pai॰… taijaukasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti… vāyaukasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti… nīlakasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti… pītakasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti… lauhitakasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti… audātakasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti… ākāsakasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti… viñ‍ñāṇakasiṇamaikau sañ‍jānāti uddhaṃ adhau tiriyaṃ advayaṃ appamāṇaṃ| imai dasa dhammā bhāvaitabbā|

(ga) ‘‘katamai dasa dhammā pariñ‍ñaiyyā? dasāyatanāni – cakkhāyatanaṃ, rūpāyatanaṃ, sautāyatanaṃ, saddāyatanaṃ, ghānāyatanaṃ, gandhāyatanaṃ, jivhāyatanaṃ, rasāyatanaṃ, kāyāyatanaṃ, phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ| imai dasa dhammā pariñ‍ñaiyyā|

(gha) ‘‘katamai dasa dhammā pahātabbā? dasa micchattā – micchādiṭṭhi, micchāsaṅkappau, micchāvācā, micchākammantau, micchāājīvau, micchāvāyāmau, micchāsati, micchāsamādhi, micchāñāṇaṃ, micchāvimutti| imai dasa dhammā pahātabbā|

(ṅa) ‘‘katamai dasa dhammā hānabhāgiyā? dasa akusalakammapathā – pāṇātipātau, adin‍nādānaṃ, kāmaisumicchācārau, musāvādau, pisuṇā vācā, pharusā vācā, samphappalāpau, abhijjhā, byāpādau, micchādiṭṭhi| imai dasa dhammā hānabhāgiyā|

(ca) ‘‘katamai  dasa dhammā visaisabhāgiyā? dasa kusalakammapathā – pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī, adin‍nādānā vairamaṇī, kāmaisumicchācārā vairamaṇī, musāvādā vairamaṇī, pisuṇāya vācāya vairamaṇī, pharusāya vācāya vairamaṇī, samphappalāpā vairamaṇī, anabhijjhā, abyāpādau, sammādiṭṭhi| imai dasa dhammā visaisabhāgiyā|

(cha) ‘‘katamai dasa dhammā duppaṭivijjhā? dasa ariyavāsā – idhāvusau, bhikkhu pañ‍caṅgavippahīnau hauti, chaḷaṅgasaman‍nāgatau, aikārakkhau, caturāpassainau, paṇun‍napac‍caikasac‍cau, samavayasaṭṭhaisanau, anāvilasaṅkappau, passaddhakāyasaṅkhārau, suvimuttacittau, suvimuttapañ‍ñau|

‘‘kathañ‍cāvusau, bhikkhu pañ‍caṅgavippahīnau hauti? idhāvusau, bhikkhunau kāmacchandau pahīnau hauti, byāpādau pahīnau hauti, thinamiddhaṃ pahīnaṃ hauti, uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ pahīnaṃ hauti, vicikicchā pahīnā hauti| aivaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu pañ‍caṅgavippahīnau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍cāvusau, bhikkhu chaḷaṅgasaman‍nāgatau hauti? idhāvusau, bhikkhu cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā naiva sumanau hauti na dummanau, upaikkhakau viharati satau sampajānau| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā…pai॰… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya naiva sumanau hauti na dummanau, upaikkhakau viharati satau sampajānau| aivaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu chaḷaṅgasaman‍nāgatau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍cāvusau, bhikkhu aikārakkhau hauti? idhāvusau, bhikkhu satārakkhaina caitasā saman‍nāgatau hauti| aivaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu aikārakkhau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍cāvusau, bhikkhu caturāpassainau hauti? idhāvusau, bhikkhu saṅkhāyaikaṃ paṭisaivati, saṅkhāyaikaṃ adhivāsaiti, saṅkhāyaikaṃ parivaj‍jaiti, saṅkhāyaikaṃ vinaudaiti| aivaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu caturāpassainau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍cāvusau, bhikkhu paṇun‍napac‍caikasac‍cau hauti? idhāvusau, bhikkhunau yāni  tāni puthusamaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ  puthupac‍caikasac‍cāni, sabbāni tāni nun‍nāni haunti paṇun‍nāni cattāni vantāni muttāni pahīnāni paṭinissaṭṭhāni| aivaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu paṇun‍napac‍caikasac‍cau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍cāvusau, bhikkhu samavayasaṭṭhaisanau hauti? idhāvusau, bhikkhunau kāmaisanā pahīnā hauti, bhavaisanā pahīnā hauti, brahmacariyaisanā paṭippassaddhā| aivaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu samavayasaṭṭhaisanau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍cāvusau, bhikkhu anāvilasaṅkappā hauti? idhāvusau, bhikkhunau kāmasaṅkappau pahīnau hauti, byāpādasaṅkappau pahīnau hauti, vihiṃsāsaṅkappau pahīnau hauti| aivaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu anāvilasaṅkappau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍cāvusau, bhikkhu passaddhakāyasaṅkhārau hauti? idhāvusau, bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| aivaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu passaddhakāyasaṅkhārau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍cāvusau, bhikkhu suvimuttacittau hauti? idhāvusau, bhikkhunau rāgā cittaṃ vimuttaṃ hauti, dausā cittaṃ vimuttaṃ hauti, mauhā cittaṃ vimuttaṃ hauti| aivaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu suvimuttacittau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍cāvusau, bhikkhu suvimuttapañ‍ñau hauti? idhāvusau, bhikkhu ‘rāgau mai pahīnau ucchin‍namūlau tālāvatthukatau anabhāvaṃkatau āyatiṃ anuppādadhammau’ti pajānāti| ‘dausau mai pahīnau…pai॰… āyatiṃ anuppādadhammau’ti pajānāti| ‘mauhau mai pahīnau  …pai॰… āyatiṃ anuppādadhammau’ti pajānāti| aivaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu suvimuttapañ‍ñau hauti| imai dasa dhammā duppaṭivijjhā|

(ja) ‘‘katamai dasa dhammā uppādaitabbā? dasa sañ‍ñā – asubhasañ‍ñā, maraṇasañ‍ñā, āhāraipaṭikūlasañ‍ñā, sabbalaukaianabhiratisañ‍ñā, anic‍casañ‍ñā, anic‍cai dukkhasañ‍ñā, dukkhai anattasañ‍ñā, pahānasañ‍ñā, virāgasañ‍ñā, niraudhasañ‍ñā| imai dasa dhammā uppādaitabbā|

(jha) ‘‘katamai  dasa dhammā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā? dasa nij‍jaravatthūni – sammādiṭṭhissa micchādiṭṭhi nij‍jiṇṇā hauti| yai ca micchādiṭṭhipac‍cayā anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, tai cassa nij‍jiṇṇā haunti| sammāsaṅkappassa micchāsaṅkappau…pai॰… sammāvācassa micchāvācā… sammākammantassa micchākammantau… sammāājīvassa micchāājīvau…  sammāvāyāmassa micchāvāyāmau… sammāsatissa micchāsati… sammāsamādhissa micchāsamādhi… sammāñāṇassa micchāñāṇaṃ nij‍jiṇṇaṃ hauti| sammāvimuttissa  micchāvimutti  nij‍jiṇṇā hauti| yai ca micchāvimuttipac‍cayā anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, tai cassa nij‍jiṇṇā haunti| imai dasa dhammā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā|

(ña) ‘‘katamai  dasa dhammā sacchikātabbā? dasa asaikkhā dhammā – asaikkhā sammādiṭṭhi, asaikkhau sammāsaṅkappau, asaikkhā sammāvācā, asaikkhau sammākammantau, asaikkhau sammāājīvau, asaikkhau sammāvāyāmau, asaikkhā sammāsati, asaikkhau sammāsamādhi, asaikkhaṃ sammāñāṇaṃ, asaikkhā sammāvimutti| imai dasa dhammā sacchikātabbā|

‘‘iti imai satadhammā bhūtā tacchā tathā avitathā anañ‍ñathā sammā tathāgataina abhisambuddhā’’ti| idamavaucāyasmā sāriputtau| attamanā tai bhikkhū āyasmatau sāriputtassa bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

dasuttarasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ aikādasamaṃ|


pāthikavaggau pāṭikavaggau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) niṭṭhitau|

tassuddānaṃ –

pāthikau  ca pāṭikañ‍ca (syā॰ kaṃ॰) udumbaraṃ pāṭikaudumbarīcaiva (sī॰ pī॰), cak‍kavatti aggañ‍ñakaṃ|

sampasādanapāsādaṃ  sampasādañ‍ca pāsādaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), mahāpurisalakkhaṇaṃ||

siṅgālāṭānāṭiyakaṃ, saṅgīti ca dasuttaraṃ|

aikādasahi suttaihi, pāthikavaggauti vuc‍cati||

pāthikavaggapāḷi niṭṭhitā|


tīhi vaggaihi paṭimaṇḍitau sakalau

dīghanikāyau samattau|
